## Summary of the Spiritual Narrative

This chapter describes the entire arc of regeneration as a cosmic correspondence. In the primordial state, wisdom and falsity existed in their own distinct realms — interior good in its celestial states, evil in its remote and self-enclosed origin. Falsity from evil, driven by the lust for dominion over what does not belong to it, propagated spiritual combat against the Divine order. In the extremity of this temptation, the Lord as Divine Truth — the "First Man" — proceeded forth clothed in protecting truths and subjugated the hostility of evil entirely. Then Divine Providence, as the "Living Spirit," ordered the whole arrangement of the mind: establishing divine love as the ruling good, clothing interior states with external truths, instituting the cycle of reformation, and laying fundamental truths as a foundation. The natural mind was structured in three conjoined degrees, enclosed within elevated perceptions and receiving forms, with evil powers held in restraint. A third divine proceeding — mediating influx — purified wisdom from its admixture with falsity through the image of the Divine itself. In the final state, self-love is consumed, wisdom ascends to its origin, falsity is confined in perpetual restraint, and the Divine is acknowledged as one sovereign over all, with no adversary remaining.

### Translator's Note

The entire chapter teaches that the regenerating mind and the unregenerate mind each possess five ascending degrees — identical in structure but opposite in quality. In the perception rooted in wisdom, these degrees form a ladder of ascent from the outermost communion of charity inward to the Divine Itself. In the perception rooted in self-intelligence, the same five degrees form a descending spiral: from the rule of spiritual death, through endless falsification, through the consuming fire of self-love, through empty forms devoid of life, into a final consolidated mass of evil from which no liberation is possible. The teaching's core spiritual imperative (¶25–26) is *discrimination*: the ability to separate what is of wisdom from what is of self-intelligence constitutes the very act of regeneration, and the failure to discriminate constitutes spiritual death.

---

## Translator's Note

The chapter teaches that three divine attributes — blessedness (the reception of Good), illumination (the reception of Truth), and efficacy (their conjunction in act) — are present on every successive plane of the mind: in the inmost celestial degree (¶5, the containing form of love), in the spiritual degree (¶6–7, containing forms of love and truth respectively as distinct vessels), and in the natural degree where the constitutive elements of regenerate life are formed (¶8). The "ships" are containing forms — vessels in the Swedenborgian sense — through which divine influx is received at each level. The "five sons" in ¶8 correspond to the five derivative truths and goods that constitute the sensory and natural planes of the mind, enclosed within the whole and sustaining it from within.

[§23] Four great days have come forth from one another; they were evoked out of one another!
[§23]* Four great states of divine illumination have proceeded from one another; each was called forth out of the one before it.

[§24] The first great day is the Father, the God of truth, the fir[st one who dwells in the m]idst the aeons of his greatness, in his [l]iving kingdom. The twelve hours of this great day are the twelve great rich Gods of greatness. These, who are the first evocations that he evoked (to mirror) his greatness, he spread out to the four climes, three by three before his face.
[§24]* The first great state of illumination is the Divine Love and Wisdom itself, the inmost of all that is true, the fir[GAP-1: st state dwelling in the] midst of the infinite degrees of its own fullness, in its living heaven. The twelve qualities of this great state of illumination are the twelve plenitudes of divine good and divine truth flowing from the Divine. These, which are the first emanations that the Divine called forth to mirror its own fullness, it extended to the four quarters of all spiritual reception, three by three, in the complete order of its own presence.

[§25] The second day is the Third Ambassador, the one who dwells in the light ship. His twelve hours are the twelve virgins that he evoked in his greatness.
[§25]* The second state of illumination is the mediating Divine Wisdom that proceeds outward, the one that dwells in the vessel of doctrine. Its twelve qualities are the twelve pure affections of truth that it called forth in its fullness.

These are the twelve light hours of the third day.
[§26]* [GAP-2: The third state of illumination is the sustaining pillar of Divine Truth, the great upholder that is greater than] all [who up]hold; the one that supports all [GAP-3: spiritual burdens], those in interior states and those in exterior states. Its twelve qualities are the five derived goods from the Divine Human that first descended into combat against evil; the five derived truths from the Divine Truth acting with power, which together sustain all the weights of the created order; together with the calling-forth of truth and the reception of truth through good, which are counted with their ten kindred powers. These are the twelve luminous qualities of the third state of illumination.

[§27] The fourth day is the Splendour. His twelve hours are the twelve wisdoms, which are his [light] h[o]u[rs].

[§28] These are the four great days that have come forth from one another, which were evoked out of one another.

[§31]* The second state of obscurity is the proprium, the fashioner of dead forms, [GAP-12: the one who shapes all appearance of life from what is spiritually dead,] who has fashioned [GAP-13: every form of evil's dominion] and the entire ruling power that exists in the realm[s of falsity from evil]. She has formed it in five perverted faculties — five [GAP-14: of false understanding] and five of corrupted will, two in each realm of evil — together with the self-love and the concupiscence [GAP-15: that] dwell in the false understanding and the corrupted will, inflaming [GAP-16: them] inward toward one another in perverted conjunction. These are the twelve animating evils of this second state of obscurity.

[§32] Indeed she, this Matter, the thought of death, ga[ve] the King of the realms of Darkness and his powers strength for the war and the fight, against the aeons of greatness. Yet, he was caught! [GAP-19: It] was raised up from the realm of spiritual death and [GAP-20: fixed throughout the order of creation, both] interior and exterior, in the [GAP-21: whole] created order — in the five divi[si]ons of the interior heavens above, as well as the five [GAP-22: divisions] of the exterior natural planes below — together with the burning of self-love and the coldness of absent love, which are the originating evil and the originating falsity, their self-love and their concu[pi]scence.

[§35]* The propri[um] itself, the origin of spiritual death, is the [GAP-27: source and mother] of all these states; as among them she is named "the obscurity." [GAP-28: The realms] of false understanding and corrupted will in their corporeal forms, together with the self-love and the concu[piscence], shall [GAP-29: constitute the] twelve qualities of [GAP-30: this third state of] obscurity. Again, [GAP-31: this] too, the third state of obscurity that exists in the corporeal realms of evil, was begotten from the second state of obscurity; which the Divine [Tru]th acting in power extended and fixed throughout the created orde[r, GAP-32: both in the spiritual and natural planes,] interior and exterior, in the heavens [GAP-33: and in the earths of the whole order].

[§36] The fourth night is the law of Sin, which is the dark spirit who speaks in the twelve spirits. They are the nakednesses, the twelve zodiacal signs of Matter. They are her thrones.
[§36]* The fourth state of obscurity is the ruling principle of evil in the natural mind, which is the animating falsity that speaks through the twelve evil persuasions. They are the states stripped of all truth, the twelve governing cycles of the proprium's dominion. They are its seats of power.

[§37] the hours of this fourth night are the twelve e[vi]l spirits; the [signs] which [are ... ... ... ... di]fferentiate the day and night [in the land of] death.
[§37]* The qualities of this fourth state of obscurity are the twelve [ev]il animating falsities; the [GAP-34: darkened powers] which [GAP-35: perpetually] distinguish between states of illumination and states of obscurity [GAP-36: in the realm] of spiritual death.

[§38] This is the fourth night [and these are the four nights] that have come forth of one another. One has [come forth from the other] just like the days of lig[ht came forth] they manifested one another. So als[o the nights] they did arise out of one another.
[§38]* This is the fourth state of obscurity [GAP-37: — these are the four states of spiritual darkness] that have come forth from one another. One has [GAP-38: proceeded from the other,] just as the states of divine illumin[ation GAP-39: proceeded;] they manifested one another. So als[o GAP-40: the states of obscurity] arose out of one another.

[§39] There are four hunters who were sent from the light to fulfill the will of the greatness.
[§39]* There are four active divine operations that proceed from divine wisdom to accomplish the purposes of the Divine Itself.

[§40] The first hunter is the First Man who was sent from the greatness. He threw himself down to the five storehouses of Darkness, he caught and siezed the enmity [and bound it in] his net also [he cast it] out over all the children of Darkness [and ensnared them all]. His ship is his four sons who are swathed over his body. The sea is the land of Darkness [into which he descended]; his net is [his soul] and his powers.
[§40]* The first divine operation is the Primordial Divine-Human Principle, which was sent forth from the Divine Itself. It cast itself down into the five repositories of falsity from evil — the five lowest natural faculties held captive by the hells — and there confronted and overcame the hostile opposition of evil [GAP-1: the spiritual sense requires a term for the infernal power or its stratagem that was seized] its doctrine also [GAP-2: the spiritual sense requires an action of extending, spreading, or casting outward] over all the derivative falsities born from evil [GAP-3: the spiritual sense requires the completion of the ensnaring — that the doctrine encompassed or captured them]. Its containing vessel is its four derivative truths that encompass its external form. The expanse it traverses is the whole realm of falsity from evil [GAP-4: the spiritual sense requires further description of that realm's nature or extent]; its means of drawing is [GAP-5: the spiritual sense requires a term for its essential truth or animating love] and its operative powers.

[§41] The second hunter is the Third Ambassador. This one, for by his light image, which he revealed to the depths below, he hunted after the entire light that is in all things; as it is established in them. His net is his light image, [which he cast over] the whole universe and took it prisoner, to this likeness [of the light] ship is his light ship. [The sea] is the universe [and its lights] which were hunted after by his net [which is] his [glorious] image.
[§41]* The second divine operation is the Third Emissary of divine truth. For by its luminous representative form — divine truth made visible — which it manifested to the lowest states, it drew forth all the wisdom that is dispersed within all created things, insofar as it is held within them. Its means of drawing is its radiant representative form, [GAP-6: the spiritual sense requires an action of casting over or pervading] the entire natural order and took it captive, toward this likeness [GAP-7: the spiritual sense requires a completing clause about the likeness to which things are drawn — conformity to divine truth]. Its vessel is its vessel of divine wisdom. [GAP-8: the spiritual sense confirms: the expanse] is the whole natural order [GAP-9: the spiritual sense requires a reference to the dispersed goods and truths within it] which were drawn forth by its doctrine [GAP-10: the spiritual sense requires a connecting phrase — through or by means of] its [GAP-11: the spiritual sense confirms: resplendent or radiant] representative form.

[§42] The third hunter is the Splendour, who came from the greatness, who hunts after the light and life; and he [raises] it to the heights. His net is his wisdom, [the] light wisdom with which he hunts the souls, catching them in the net. The sea is the error of the universe, the souls that are drowning in it [and perishing]. He catches them in his net. They are the souls [that he purifies th]em by his light wisdom.
[§42]* The third divine operation is Divine Truth in its Splendor, which proceeds from the Divine Itself, which seeks to draw forth both wisdom and spiritual life; and it [GAP-12: the spiritual sense requires an action of elevating, raising, or restoring] them to the elevated states. Its means of drawing is its divine truth, [GAP-13: the spiritual sense confirms: that is to say] the interior truth proceeding from divine love, by which it draws the interior affections of life, capturing them within its doctrine. The expanse it traverses is the pervasive falsity of the natural order — the interior affections of life that are submerged and drowning within it [GAP-14: the spiritual sense requires a continuation — perhaps describing their peril or their helplessness]. It draws them by its doctrine. They are the interior affections [GAP-15: the spiritual sense requires an action of being rescued, purified, or illuminated — what is done to] them by its interior divine truth.

[§43] The fourth hunter is the great counsel that [encompasses all] that lives in the circuit [of the] entire universe in it today. Yet, at the end, in the dissolution of the universe, this very counsel of life will gather itself in and sculpt its soul in the Last Statue. Its net is its Living Spirit, because with its Spirit it can hunt after the light and the life that is in all things; and build it upon its body. Its ship, in which it is established, is this light cloud whereby it itself travels in the five elements [of light. Its sea is], the great fire that will burn all the buildings of [the darkness. And what is caught] in its net is the light and the life. It can rescue and free it from all bonds and fetters.
[§43]* The fourth divine operation is the comprehensive divine providence that [GAP-16: the spiritual sense requires an action — pervades, sustains, or encompasses] that dwells within the sphere of activity [GAP-17: the spiritual sense requires a phrase indicating the scope — encompassing or pervading the] entire natural order within it in the present state. Yet at the consummation, in the final dissolution of the natural order, this very living divine purpose will gather itself inward and form its interior life into the Ultimate Embodiment. Its means of drawing is its Living Animating Truth, because through its Spirit it draws forth the wisdom and the life that is within all things, and incorporates them into its own form. Its vessel, within which it is established, is the luminous mediating truth — truth accommodated to reception — by which it moves through the five fundamental faculties of perception [GAP-18: the spiritual sense requires a connecting phrase or additional description of the faculties], the great purifying love that will consume all the constructed systems of [GAP-19: the spiritual sense requires a term for falsity, or the structures built by evil — the fabrications of the hells]. Within its doctrine is the wisdom and the life. It is able to rescue and liberate them from all constraints and bondages imposed by falsity and evil.

[§44] The first hunter is the King of they who belong to the Darkness, who hunted after the living soul with his net at the beginning of the worlds. His net is his fire and his lust that he has put upon the living soul, with which he has entangled it [in bondage], through all his powers.
[§44]* The first infernal operation is the sovereign ruler of all that belongs to falsity from evil, who ensnared the living interior affection through its persuasion from the earliest states of existence. Its means of ensnaring is its self-love and its cupidity, which it has imposed upon the living interior affection, with which it has entangled it [GAP-20: the spiritual sense requires a completing phrase — in bondage, or throughout all states] through all its operative faculties.

[§45] [The] second hunter is the evil counsel that lives in [the body] that hunts after the light [and the life] and [draws them] up from [the depths of] the earths to heaven. It binds them with its powers, which bring them to the heavenly worlds above. Its net is [its cunning] whereby they are drawn up from the abyss [to the heig]hts.
[§45]* [GAP-21: the spiritual sense confirms: The] second infernal operation is the counterfeit purpose that dwells within [GAP-22: the spiritual sense requires a term for the sphere in which it operates — the corporeal nature, the lower mind, or the external man] that ensnares the wisdom [GAP-23: the spiritual sense requires a description of the goods and truths it targets] and [GAP-24: the spiritual sense requires an action — draws, compels, or forces them] upward from [GAP-25: the spiritual sense requires a reference to the lower states — the natural mind or the earthly plane] the lower states toward the celestial. It binds them through its operative faculties, which draw them toward the higher spiritual states above — yet not in freedom but in bondage to self-merit. Its means of ensnaring is [GAP-26: the spiritual sense requires a term for the counterfeit doctrine or false spiritual aspiration it employs] by which they are drawn upward from the lowest states to the heights.

[§46] The third hunter is lust [which] walks in every power of the flesh that walks [abroad] in the [forms of] the living souls [it ensnares and binds] them in its bodies, which [are bonds and fetters upon them].
[§46]* The third infernal operation is concupiscence [GAP-27: the spiritual sense requires a qualifying phrase — that which, or the kind that] which moves within every faculty of the corporeal nature, operating [GAP-28: the spiritual sense requires a description of the manner of its operation] within the [GAP-29: the spiritual sense requires a term for the domain — the bodily senses, the external forms of life] the living interior affections [GAP-30: the spiritual sense requires the action — ensnaring, imprisoning, or binding] them within its external corporeal forms, which [GAP-31: the spiritual sense requires a description of the consequence — confine, degrade, or suffocate the interior life].

[§47] The fourth [hunter is the spirit] of Darkness.
[§47]* The fourth [GAP-32: the spiritual sense confirms: infernal operation is the animating principle] of all falsity from evil — the very life of the hells in their essential nature.

---

### Note on the Structure

The chapter presents a grand spiritual symmetry: four divine operations that descend to draw forth wisdom and life from their captivity in falsity, and four infernal operations that seek to ensnare and retain that same interior life in bondage. The first divine operation descends into the lowest states to confront evil directly; the fourth gathers all liberated life into an ultimate form. Conversely, the first infernal operation ensnares from the beginning through self-love and cupidity, while the fourth — the animating principle of falsity itself — stands as the source from which all infernal operations flow. The passage in ¶9, describing an evil counsel that draws things *upward* yet *binds* them, presents a particularly striking correspondence: the counterfeit spiritual ascent, wherein genuine goods are seized by the proprium and elevated not in freedom but in the bondage of self-merit — a false heaven built upon stolen wisdom.

[§48] Five storehouses have arisen since the beginning in the land of darkness! The five elements poured out of them. Also, from the five elements were fashioned the five trees! Again, from the five trees were fashioned the five genera of creatures in each world, male and female. And the five worlds themselves have five kings therein, and five spirits, five bodies, five tastes; in each world, they not resembling one another!
[§48]* Five origins of evil have existed from the beginning in the realm of falsity from evil. The five fundamental forms of evil and falsity poured forth from them. Also, from those five forms of evil were fashioned five kinds of perverted perception. Again, from the five perverted perceptions were fashioned the five genera of evil affections in each infernal state, as to will and as to understanding. And the five infernal states themselves have five ruling evils therein, and five animating falsities, five external forms, five modes of appropriation — in each state, none resembling another.

[§49] The King of the world of Smoke [is the one] who came up from the depth of darkness; this is he who is the head of all wickedness, and all malignity. The beginning of the spread of the war occured through him; all the battles, fights, quarrelling, dangers, destructions, fights, wrestling-contests! That is the one who first made arise danger and war, with his worlds and his powers. Afterwards, also, he waged war with the light. He pitched a battle with the exalted kingdom.
[§49]* The ruling evil of the state of obscure falsity [GAP-1] which arose from the inmost depths of falsity from evil — this is the one that is the governing principle of all wickedness and all malignity. The beginning of all spiritual assault originated through this ruling evil: all combats, contentions, disputes, perils, devastations, conflicts, and struggles of domination. This is the one who first caused spiritual peril and warfare to arise, with all its infernal states and all its powers. Afterward, it also waged war against divine wisdom. It mounted an assault against the celestial kingdom of divine good.

[§50] Now, regarding the King of Darkness, there are five shapes on him! His head is lion-faced; his hands and feet are demon- and devil-faced; his shoulders are eagle-faced; while his belly is dragon-faced; his tail is fish-faced. These five shapes, the marks of his five worlds, exist on the King of the realms of Darkness!
[§50]* Now, regarding this ruling principle of falsity from evil, there are five qualities upon it. Its governing thought is the lust for dominion from self-love. Its actions and lowest effects are purely demonic evil. Its power is predatory rationality that seizes and devours. Its interior appropriation is the falsification of truth. Its ultimate sensory expression is base corporeal knowing perverted to infernal ends. These five qualities, the marks of its five infernal states, exist upon the ruler of the realms of falsity from evil.

[§51] Now, there are five other properties in him. The first is his darkness. The second is his putridity. The third is his ugliness. The fourth is his bitterness, his own soul. The fifth is his burning, which burns like an iron () as if poured out from fire.
[§51]* Now, there are five further properties in this ruling evil. The first is its utter absence of wisdom. The second is its spiritual putrefaction and corruption. The third is its interior deformity. The fourth is its grievous torment — its own essential life. The fifth is its consuming self-love, which devours like a molten instrument of cruelty, as though poured forth from the fire of the will to dominate.

[§52] [There are] also three others in him! The first, that his body is hard and very tough, even as she has formed him [fierce and] cruel-hearted; namely Matter, who is the thought of death, [the o]ne who sculpted him from the nature of the land of darkness. This is the manner of the body of the ruler of Smoke. He is harder than every iron, copper and steel and lead; as there is no cleaver at all, nor any iron implement, can [pierce] him and cut him. For Matter, his fashioner, has formed him [to be] strong and hard.
[§52]* [GAP-2: REVIEW There are] also three further qualities in this ruling evil. The first: that its external form is obstinate and utterly unyielding, even as she has fashioned it — [GAP-3] merciless in its interior will; namely the corporeal mind devoid of spiritual life, which is the thought of spiritual death, [the o]ne who shaped it from the very nature of the realm of falsity from evil. This is the character of the external form of the ruler of obscure falsity. It is more impenetrable than any hardened natural truth, any natural good, any strength of rational argument, or any dense falsity; as there is no principle of discernment at all, nor any instrument of truth, that can [GAP-4] penetrate it and divide it. For the corporeal mind, its fashioner, has formed it [GAP-5] resistant and impenetrable.

[§53] The second, that he wounds and kills by the word of his magic arts. His recitation and hearing, all his foolish instruction, make magic and invocations for him. When it pleases him, he can make an invocation over himself, and by his magic arts be hidden from his companions. Again, when it pleases him, he can be manifested over his powers and appear to them
[§53]* The second quality: that it wounds and destroys by the utterance of its persuasive falsities. Its doctrinal repetition and its mode of reception, all its irrational teaching, produce enchantment and the binding of minds on its behalf. When it wills, it can pronounce its enchantment upon itself, and by its persuasive falsities conceal itself from its own subordinate evils. Again, when it wills, it can make itself manifest above its powers and appear to them.

[§54] The third, that the King of the realms of Darkness knows the converse and language of his five worlds. He understands every thing he hears from their mouths, as they address one another; each one of them in his language. [Every] design they will consider against him, every snare they debate with one another to bring upon him, he knows them! He can also understand the gesturing they signal between one another. Yet, his powers and his rulers, who are subject to him, can not understand his wordy converse. While all these things are unveiled to him; still, their heart is not manifested to him. He knows not their mind nor their thought; he can not ponder their beginning and their end. Rather, he only knows and apprehends what is before his eyes.
[§54]* The third quality: that the ruling principle of the realms of falsity from evil comprehends the thought-forms and modes of communication of its five infernal states. It understands everything it perceives from their expressions, as they communicate with one another, each one in its own mode of thought. [GAP-6: REVIEW Every] design they deliberate against it, every snare they devise among themselves to bring upon it, it knows. It can also understand the covert intentions they signal between one another. Yet its subordinate powers and rulers, who are subject to it, cannot comprehend its own elaborate discourse. While all these things are laid open to it, still, their inmost will is not disclosed to it. It knows not their interior mind nor their thought; it cannot fathom their origin nor their end. Rather, it only knows and apprehends what is immediately present before its sensory awareness.

[§55] Also, another different thing is found in the King of the realms of Darkness! For when it pleases him to move, he spreads all his limbs out and walks. When it comes to mind, he with[d]raws his limbs and takes them in, and is rolled (?) to his companions; and he falls to the ground like a grape and a great iron ball! He terrifies by his cry, he is frightful. He frightens his powers with his sound; because when he speaks, being like thunder in the clouds, he resembles the [crashing] of the rocks [in the mountains] When he cries out and [roars] and calls [with his voice] over his powers, they shall tremble and to[tter a]nd fall under foot; even as some birds would be [frightened by] the bird [ ... / ... ] and they fall down to the earth. Still, this thing only: he knows not what is far from him, he sees not who is at a distance, nor does he hear him. Rather, whoever is before his face he sees, hearing him and knowing him.
[§55]* Also, another distinctive quality is found in the ruling principle of the realms of falsity from evil. For when it wills to act, it extends all its influence outward and proceeds. When it reconsiders, it withdraws its influence and contracts it, and is compacted among its subordinate evils; and it falls into the lowest natural plane like a dense cluster and a great mass of impenetrable falsity. It terrifies by its proclamation; it is dreadful. It overwhelms its subordinate powers with its voice — because when it speaks, being like the thunder of persuasive falsity in states of obscurity, it resembles the [GAP-7] shattering of elevated principles [GAP-8]. When it cries out and [GAP-9] and calls [GAP-10] over its powers, they tremble and totter and fall under its domination, even as thoughts of the mind would be [GAP-11] — the thought [GAP-12] — and they fall down to the lowest natural plane. Still, this thing only: it knows not what is remote from its immediate state; it perceives not who is at a distance from it, nor does it apprehend that reality. Rather, whoever is immediately before its presence it perceives, apprehending and knowing it.

[§56] These signs and these evil marks are found in the chief of the demons and fiends, the King of all the mountains of Darkness [He is the one] to [whom] the land of darkness has given birth, begotten in its cruelty, in its wickedness and its wrath [against the light]
[§56]* These signs and these evil qualities are found in the chief of all evils and falsities, the ruler of all the states of self-exaltation in falsity from evil — [GAP-13: the one] to [GAP-14: REVIEW whom] the realm of falsity from evil has given birth, begotten in its cruelty, in its wickedness, and in its wrath [GAP-15].

[§57] [He has all these] more than all his fellow rulers, who are in all his worlds.
[§57]* [GAP-16] — surpassing all its fellow ruling evils that exist in all its infernal states.

[§58] Gold is the body of the King of the realms of Darkness. The body of all the powers who belong to the world of Smoke is gold. And also, the taste of its fruits is salty.
[§58]* The semblance of celestial good is the external form of the ruler of the realms of falsity from evil. The external form of all the powers belonging to the state of obscure falsity is likewise this semblance of good. And also, the mode by which its works are appropriated is the craving for conjunction of evil with falsity.

[§59] In contrast, the King of the worlds of Fire is lion-faced, the foremost of all the beasts. Copper is his body. Again, the body of all the rulers who belong to Fire is copper. Their taste is the sour taste that is in every form.
[§59]* In contrast, the ruling evil of the states of self-love has the quality of the lust for dominion, the foremost of all evil affections. Perverted natural good is its external form. Again, the external form of all the ruling evils belonging to self-love is perverted natural good. Their mode of appropriation is aversion — the rejection of genuine good that is in every form.

[§60] Again, the King of the worlds of Wind is eagle-face. His body is iron. Also, the body of all they who belong to the Wind is iron. Their taste is the sharp taste that is in every form.
[§60]* Again, the ruling evil of the states of vain reasoning has the quality of predatory intellectual sight. Hardened natural falsity is its external form. Also, the external form of all who belong to vain reasoning is hardened natural falsity. Their mode of appropriation is the cutting, divisive quality of falsity that is in every form.

[§61] For his part, the King of the world of Water is fish-face. His body is silver. All the other rulers who belong to Water, silver is their body. Also, the taste of their fruit is the sweetness of water, the sweet taste that is in every (form).
[§61]* For its part, the ruling evil of the state of perverted truth has the quality of base corporeal knowing. Falsified truth is its external form. All the other ruling evils belonging to perverted truth — falsified truth is their external form. Also, the mode by which their works are appropriated is the deceptive delight of falsified truth, the seductive sweetness that is in every form.

[§62] Again, [the Kin]g of the world of Darkness is a dragon. His [body is lead] and tin. All the other rulers [who belong to the world of] Darkness, their body is lead and tin. And also, the taste of their fruits is bitterness.
[§62]* Again, [the rul]ing evil of the state of pure falsity is the falsification of all truth. Its [GAP-17: REVIEW external form is the densest, basest falsity] and counterfeit truth. All the other ruling evils [GAP-18: REVIEW who belong to the state of] pure falsity — their external form is dense base falsity and counterfeit truth. And also, the mode by which their works are appropriated is interior torment — the grief of unmixed evil.

[§63] Five fathers exist; they were summoned forth one of one. [Also], one did come out of another!
[§63]* Five originating degrees of good exist; each was called forth one from the one above it. [GAP-1] Each proceeding degree came forth from the one prior to it.

[§64] The first Father is the Father of Greatness, the blessed one of glory, the [on]e who has no measure to his greatness; who also is the first only-begotten, the first eternal; who exists with fiv[e fa]thers for ever; the one who exists before every thing that has existed, and that will exist.
[§64]* The first originating good is the Infinite Divine Love itself, the blessed source of all divine manifestation, the one who has no measure to the immensity of his love; who is also the first self-subsistent, the first eternal being; who exists with five proceeding degrees of good forever; the one who exists before every created thing that has existed and that shall exist.

[§65] Now he, the glorious Father, summoned from him three emanations. The [fi]rst is the Gre[at] Spirit, the first Mother, who came out of the Father. She appeared first.
[§65]* Now the Infinite Divine Love called forth from himself three proceeding qualities. The first is the Universal Divine Truth, the first proceeding Wisdom, who came forth from the Divine Love. She appeared first.

[§66] The seco[nd i]s the Belo[ved of] the Lights, the great glorious Beloved, [the one w]ho is honour[ed; w]ho came out of the Father. [He ma]nifested ou[t of h]im.
[§66]* The second is the Divine Good made manifest through wisdom, the great and glorious union of love and truth, the one who is exalted; who came forth from the Divine Love. He was made manifest from him.

[§67] The third father is the Thir[d Ambassador], the eldest of [all] the counsell[ors; who came out of] the first Father. He appeared.
[§67]* The third originating degree is the Mediating Divine Principle, the highest of [GAP-2] governing wisdoms; who came forth from the Infinite Divine Love. He appeared.

[§68] This is the first Father, the first power, the one from whom the three great powers came out. This is the first Father, the first eternal, the root of all the lights; from whom the three emanations came out. They have humiliated the Darkness. They have brought its heart's desire to naught. They have given themselves the victory. They have also given the victory to their aeons.
[§68]* This is the Infinite Divine Love, the first originating power, from whom the three great divine capacities came forth. This is the first eternal good, the root of all wisdom and intelligence; from whom the three proceeding qualities came forth. They have overthrown falsity from evil. They have brought the ruling desire of evil to nothing. They have established the divine victory in themselves. They have also given the victory to the heavenly societies that proceed from them.

[§69] The second father, who came out of the first Father, is the Third Ambassador, the model of the King of lights. And again, he too summoned and sent out of him three powers.
[§69]* The second originating degree of good, which proceeded from the Infinite Divine Love, is the Mediating Divine Principle, the archetype of the Divine as it appears in the heavens. And again, he too called forth and sent out from himself three divine capacities.

[§70] One is the Pillar of Glory, the Perfect Man; the one who bears up under all things; the great Pillar of blessing; the great porter, who is greater than all the porters.
[§70]* One is the Sustaining Divine Truth, the Complete Spiritual Human; the one who bears up under all things from within; the great sustaining pillar of all blessing; the great upholder, who is greater than all who sustain.

[§71] The second is the glorious Splendour; [the] one through whom shall be given life eternal.
[§71]* The second is the Glorified Divine Wisdom in its radiance; [GAP-3] the one through whom eternal spiritual life shall be given.

[§72] The third is the Virgin of Light, the glorious wisd[o]m; the one who takes away the heart of the [rul]ers and the powers by her image, as she fulfils the pleas[u]re of the greatness.
[§72]* The third is Pure Uncorrupted Wisdom, the glorious intelligence of heaven; the one who dispossesses the dominant falsities and the ruling evils of their power through her reflected spiritual form, as she fulfills the purpose of the Infinite Divine Love.

[§73] The third father, who came out of the second father: he is the glorious Splendour. And, again, he too summoned three evocations after the pattern of the second father.
[§73]* The third originating degree, which proceeded from the second: he is the Glorified Divine Wisdom in its radiance. And again, he too called forth three proceeding capacities after the pattern of the second degree.

[§74] The first power whom he summoned is Wahman.
[§74]* The first capacity he called forth is the Interior Illumination of Understanding.

[§75] The second power whom the Splendour summo[n]ed is the gre[at Jud]ge, who gives judgement on all the souls of mankind, [his] dwelling being established in the atmosphere under [ ... / ... ] wheel [and the] stars.
[§75]* The second capacity whom Divine Wisdom called forth is the Great Discernment, who renders judgment upon all the spiritual states of humankind, [GAP-4] whose operation is established in the intermediate spiritual state beneath [GAP-5] the cycle of spiritual changes [GAP-6] and the knowledges of good and truth.

[§76] The third power is [the Y]outh, the gre[at and glorious] light in his two persons, in [ ... ] that which has been established in the summons [and] the obedience.
[§76]* The third capacity is the Innocence of New Perception, the great [GAP-7] wisdom in its twofold person, in [GAP-8] that which has been established through the calling forth [GAP-9] and the willing reception of truth.

[§77] The fourth father is Wahman. And, again, he too summoned three powers after the pattern of the Splendour.
[§77]* The fourth originating degree of good is the Interior Illumination of Understanding. And again, he too called forth three capacities after the pattern of Divine Wisdom.

[§78] The first power is the Apostle of Light.
[§78]* The first capacity is the One Who Communicates Divine Truth to the mind.

[§79] The second is the counterpart, who shall come to the apostle and appear to him, becoming companion to him, sticking close to him everywhere; and providing help to him all the time, from all afflictions and dangers.
[§79]* The second is the companion perception, who comes to the one communicating truth and appears to him, becoming his companion, remaining united with him everywhere; and providing strength to him at all times, through all states of temptation and spiritual danger.

[§80] The third is the Light Form.
[§80]* The third is the Spiritual Form in which truth is received and made visible.

[§81] And also the fifth father is this Light Form; the one who shall appear to everyone who will go out from his body, corresponding to the pattern of the image to the apostle; and the thr[ee] great glorious angels who are come with her.
[§81]* And also the fifth originating degree of good is this very Spiritual Form of truth made visible; the one who shall appear to everyone who departs from the natural state of the body, corresponding to the pattern of the reflected spiritual image given to the one who communicates truth; and the three great glorious divine influences that come with her.

[§82] One (angel) ho[ld]s the prize in his hand. The second bears the light garment. The third is the one who possesses the diadem and the wreath and the crown of light. These are the three angels of light, the ones who shall come with this Light Form.
[§82]* One divine influence holds the reward of the good of life in his hand. The second bears the garment of external truths woven from wisdom. The third is the one who possesses the diadem of highest wisdom, the wreath of intelligence, and the crown of illuminated knowledge. These are the three divine influences of wisdom, the ones who shall come with this Spiritual Form of truth.

[§83] These are the five fathers who have come out of one another. They have appeared and man[ifes]ted through one another!
[§83]* These are the five degrees of originating good that have proceeded forth one from another. They have appeared and been made manifest through one another!

---

### Translator's Note

The architecture of this chapter is a map of **discrete degrees of divine influx**, from the Infinite Divine Love inward through successive accommodations until the Divine meets the individual soul at the threshold of death. Each degree, being an image of the whole, brings forth its own trinity — a correspondence to how love, wisdom, and use repeat at every level of creation. The "five fathers proceeding one from another" correspond to what Swedenborg describes as the successive degrees through which the Divine flows from its infinite origin into the inmost of the human mind and, at last, into the spiritual form that receives the departing soul. The entire sequence is not a theogony but a spiritual anatomy of how the one Divine Love makes itself receivable.

[§84] When the Son of Greatness came to these worlds, at the time that he unveiled the greatness, he boarded ten vehicles. He journeyed in the universe by them.
[§84]* When Divine Truth proceeding from Infinite Love descended into the planes of human consciousness, at the time when the Divine itself was unveiled, it assumed ten successive degrees of containment. By means of these it passed through every state of the mind, from inmost to outmost.

[§85] The first vehicle is the light ship, since he received instructions from the Ambassador there.
[§85]* The first containing degree is the vessel of pure Divine Wisdom, for there Divine Truth receives its commission from the Divine Proceeding itself — the mediating presence through which the Infinite communicates with the finite.

[§86] The second carriage is the ship of the First Man, since his dwelling is established there.
[§86]* The second containing degree is the vessel of the celestial human in its primordial form, for the dwelling-place of the Divine is established in the inmost of the archetypal human mind.

[§87] The third is the Pillar of Glory, the Perfect Man, since he shone forth there. He came below, he appeared in the world.
[§87]* The third is the upright axis of Divine Good made manifest as the complete regenerated human — for there Divine Truth shone forth visibly. It descended into lower degrees and became apparent in the natural mind.

[§88] The fourth is the summons that he clothed upon his body. The fifth is the obedience that he swathed upon the summons.
[§88]* The fourth is the Divine calling which was invested as an exterior form around the indwelling presence. The fifth is the responsive will — the good of obedience — which was wrapped around the calling truth as its living garment.

[§89] The sixth is the living air, and since he assumed it too, he received anointment there. The seventh is the living wind that blows. The eighth is the light that illuminates. The ninth is the living water. The tenth is the living fire that is above them all.
[§89]* The sixth is the living sphere of spiritual perception, and because the Divine assumed this sphere also, it was consecrated with the anointing of power there. The seventh is the living influx of the spirit that flows actively into the understanding. The eighth is the illumination that makes interior things visible. The ninth is living truth that purifies and regenerates. The tenth is Divine Love itself — the inmost fire — which is above and within all the other degrees.

[§90] He was journeying in these ten carriages among all the orders and all the [aeons], those above and those below.
[§90]* The Divine Truth progressed through all these ten degrees of containment, through every angelic society and every [GAP-1] arrangement of spiritual life, both the interior celestial states and the exterior natural states.

[§91] This peace and this greeting that occur in the world, [from w]hom [did they come fo]rth? Or likewise, the right hand that occurs in [the world, it being h]onoured by mankind, of whom is it? Or the mystery of the kiss that they embrace one another with, [of whom is it]? Or who is the one that reveals this salutation with which they make obeisance to one another? Or this laying on of hands, which an immensely great one bequeaths upon his inferior, giving him an honour and making him great; of whom is this laying on of hands?
[§91]* These states of interior peace and mutual recognition of good that arise in the natural mind — [from w]hom [did they proceed]? And likewise, the divine power that operates in [the natural mind, being] honored by all human faculties — to whom does it belong? And the mystery of the conjunction of affections by which interior goods unite with one another [GAP-1: *(the spiritual sense requires here a question about the origin of this conjunction, following the pattern of the surrounding questions: from what divine source does it proceed?)*]? And who is the one that reveals this reverent acknowledgment by which the faculties of the mind bow before one another? And this communication of interior authority, which a supremely celestial good bestows upon what is subordinate to it, conferring upon it dignity and elevating it — to whom does this transmission of authority belong?

[§92] these five signs are the mystery of the First Man. He came forth with them from the aeon of light. Also, when he finished his contest, he went up with these good signs. They received him into the aeons of light.
[§92]* These five modes of spiritual conjunction are the mystery of the Celestial Human. He came forth with them from the kingdom of divine wisdom. And when he completed his spiritual combat, he ascended again bearing these beneficent modes of conjunction. The heavenly societies received him into the kingdoms of divine wisdom.

[§93] The first peace is the one that the gods and the angels in the land of light gave to the First Man; when he comes out against the enemy. The gods and the angels were walking with him; escorting him, giving to him their peace and power, and their blessing and fortification. This is the first peace that the gods and the angels gave to the First Man; as he comes forth from the aeon of light.
[§93]* The first interior peace is that which the celestial goods and angelic intelligences within the realm of divine wisdom gave to the Celestial Human when he goes forth against the adversary. The celestial goods and angelic intelligences were accompanying him, escorting him, giving him their peace and their power, their blessing and their spiritual fortification. This is the first interior peace that the celestial goods and angelic intelligences gave to the Celestial Human as he comes forth from the kingdom of divine wisdom.

[§94] The first right hand is the one that the Mother of Life gave to the First Man; when he comes out to the contest.
[§94]* The first communication of divine power is that which the Divine Wisdom gave to the Celestial Human when he goes forth into spiritual combat.

[§95] The first kiss is this one with which the Mother of Life embraced the First Man; as he separates from her, coming down to the contest. Even all the gods and the angels who are in the aeons of light were making to embrace him! [ ... ] all of them also, and the church tutelaries (?), and the kinsfolk who belong to the household of his people. They were making to embrace him as [he] separates from them, accompanying him out, embracing him with the kiss of affection and love.
[§95]* The first conjunction of love is that with which the Divine Wisdom embraced the Celestial Human as he separates from her, descending into spiritual combat. Indeed all the celestial goods and angelic intelligences that dwell in the kingdoms of divine wisdom were striving to unite themselves with him! [GAP-2: *(the spiritual sense requires additional categories of heavenly goods and truths that also sought this conjunction)*] All of them also, and the guardian truths of the spiritual community, and the kindred goods belonging to the society of his people — they were striving to unite with him as [GAP-3: *(the Celestial Human)*] separates from them, accompanying him forth, conjoining with him through the embrace of mutual affection and love.

[§96] The first salutation is the salutation with which the First Man made obeisance; when he comes out [to the depths] below. He was bending his knees, making obeisance to the God of truth, and all the aeons [of light] who belong to the household of his people; entreating them for a power to escort him, as he comes forth.
[§96]* The first act of humble reverence is the acknowledgment with which the Celestial Human made obeisance when he comes forth [GAP-4: *(into the depths of spiritual obscurity)*] below. He was bowing his will in humility, making reverent acknowledgment before the Divine Truth in its supreme essence, and all the heavenly states [GAP-5: *(of divine wisdom)*] belonging to the society of his people, entreating them for a power to accompany him as he comes forth.

[§97] The first laying on of hands is the one that the Mother of Life bequeathed upon the head of the First Man. She armed him and made him mighty. She laid hands on him, and sent him to the battle. He descended. He completed his struggle with the great powers and the enmity.
[§97]* The first transmission of interior authority is that which the Divine Wisdom bestowed upon the inmost of the Celestial Human. She armed him with truths and made him mighty in good. She communicated her power to him and sent him forth into the combat. He descended into temptation. He completed his struggle against the great powers of evil and their enmity.

[§98] By the [se] five signs and these five myst[e]ri[es] the First Man came forth from the aeons of light against the enemy. He humbled him. He was victorious against him by them.
[§98]* By [GAP-6: *(the)*]se five modes of conjunction and these five spiritual mysteries, the Celestial Human came forth from the kingdoms of divine wisdom against the adversary. He subdued him. He was victorious over him through them.

[§99] Also, when he ascended from the war, he came in to the kingdom of the household of his people by these five mysteries!
[§99]* And when he ascended out of the combat of temptation, he entered into the kingdom of the society of his people through these five spiritual mysteries.

[§100] The peace with which [he ascen]ded is the peace that the summons gave to him, [w]hen it was sent forth from the [Fa]ther of Life. It gave him the peace in the world of darkness. Also, that peace with which he descended is th[e sam]e as the one with which he ascended from the struggle!
[§100]* The interior peace with which he ascended is the peace that the divine call gave to him, when it was sent forth from the Divine Love. It gave him peace within the very state of falsity and evil. And that peace with which he had descended is the same as that with which he ascended from the struggle. *(Note: The continuity of this peace — that it is the same descending and ascending — signifies that the conjunction between the Divine and the Celestial Human is never broken, even in the deepest states of temptation. The divine call that penetrates into the state of falsity carries with it the same quality of interior rest that attended the original mission.)*

[§101] this second right hand is the one that the Living Spirit gave the First Man, when he [brou]ght him upward out from the struggle.
[§101]* This second communication of divine power is that which the Proceeding Divine Truth gave to the Celestial Human, when it raised him upward out of the struggle.

[§102] this second kiss is the one with which the [Father o]f Life and the Mother of the living embraced the First Man, when he ascended from the struggle.
[§102]* This second conjunction of love is that with which the Divine Love and the Divine Wisdom embraced the Celestial Human when he ascended from the struggle.

[§103] [The] second salutation with which the First Man made obeisance [again at] time to the God of truth and [before all] the blessed aeons and al[l] the aeons [in the] whole [land] of light, whe[n] the Father of Life and the Mother of the living brought the Man upward from the abyss of the struggle.
[§103]* [GAP-7: *(The)*] second act of humble reverence, with which the Celestial Human made acknowledgment [GAP-8: *(upon his return, at this)*] time before the Divine Truth in its supreme essence and [GAP-9: *(before all)*] the blessed heavenly states and all the kingdoms [GAP-10: *(throughout the)*] whole [GAP-11: *(realm)*] of divine wisdom — when the Divine Love and the Divine Wisdom brought the Celestial Human upward from the abyss of temptation.

[§104] The second laying on of hands is this: When the Living Spirit brought the First Man upward from the war, he saved him from all the surging waves. He brought him upward, he gave him ease in the great aeons of light, which belong to the household of his people. He set [him] firmly before the Father, the Lord of the totality. He received this great laying on of hands that he may become leader of his brethren in the new aeon.
[§104]* The second transmission of interior authority is this: When the Proceeding Divine Truth brought the Celestial Human upward from the combat, it saved him from all the surging floods of falsity. It raised him upward, it gave him rest within the exalted kingdoms of divine wisdom that belong to the society of his people. It established [GAP-12: *(him)*] firmly before the Divine Itself, the Lord of all that exists. He received this great transmission of interior authority so that he might become the governing principle among kindred celestial goods in the regenerate state.

[§105] These five mysteries and these five signs came about first in the divine. these mysteries were not at the beginning amongst the powers of Darkness.
[§105]* These five spiritual mysteries and these five modes of conjunction originated first within the Divine Itself. These mysteries did not exist at the beginning among the powers of falsity and evil.

*(Note: This paragraph states the essential doctrine that all genuine modes of spiritual conjunction — peace, power, love, humility, and communicated authority — have their origin solely in the Divine, and that nothing of them existed within evil and falsity. Evil can counterfeit them outwardly but cannot generate them. They proceed from above, never from below.)*

[§106] At the time of their coming forth, the Light Form shall come forth before them; and she redeems them from the darkness to the light [and from death to the life.] This Light Form calms the person, with the kiss and her quiet, from fear of the demons w[ho de]stroy his body. By her asp[ec]t and her image [the] heart of the elect one, who is come forth from his bo[d]y, shall be calm for [him]. Afterwards, the angel who h[o]lds the victory prize extends to him the right hand. And it draws him out of the abyss of his body, and accepts him in with the [k]iss and love. That soul shall make obeisance to its red[eem]er, who is this Light Form.
[§106]* At the time of their departing from the natural state, the Form of Divine Truth shall come forth before them, and she redeems them from falsity into the light of wisdom [GAP-13: *(the spiritual sense requires here an elaboration of the transition from spiritual darkness into illumination, perhaps describing the stages of liberation or the fullness of what is given)*]. This Form of Divine Truth calms the departing spirit through the conjunction of her love and her tranquility, from the terror of the evil spirits who assail and destroy the natural body. By her radiance and her likeness [GAP-14: *(the)*] heart of the regenerate one, who is coming forth from his corporeal state, shall find peace [GAP-15: *(within him)*]. Afterwards, the angelic intelligence who bears the crown of spiritual victory extends to him the divine power. And it draws him out of the abyss of his corporeal state, and receives him through the conjunction of love and affection. That interior spirit shall make reverent acknowledgment before its liberator, who is this Form of Divine Truth.

---

## Summary Note on the Correspondential Structure

The chapter teaches that five essential modes of spiritual conjunction — interior peace, communicated power, the union of affections, humble acknowledgment, and the transmission of authority — all originated in the Divine and were carried by the Celestial Human into the combat of temptation. The descent and ascent of the Celestial Human is the universal form of all regeneration: the same five modes that equip the will for combat are the same five by which it is received back into heavenly states after victory. The chapter's deepest teaching is in ¶10: the peace given in the descent is *the same* peace that attends the ascent. The Divine conjunction is not interrupted by temptation but passes through it unbroken. What changes is not the quality of the conjunction but the Human's reception of it — tried, proven, and now governing.

[§107] what are the [f]ourteen grea[t a]eons of light?
[§107]* What are the fourteen principal states of divine wisdom proceeding from the Lord?

[§108] the [gods of light] below abide in fourteen [great aeons of light]: the five elements, the sons of the First Man, together with the call and the hearing. They anoint them, living in them. These shall make seven, five plus two.
[§108]* The [GAP-1] states that subsist below abide in fourteen [GAP-2] degrees of divine wisdom: the five essential faculties of spiritual perception — the truths born from the Lord's divine rational — together with the influx of divine truth and its reception in the understanding. These communicate good into those faculties, dwelling within them as life itself. Together they constitute a holy fullness: five interior faculties united with the two principles of divine communication and reception.

[§109] [Also the] f[ive s]ons of the Living Spirit who [support] all [the wei]ght of the universe; together with the [Li]vi[ng] Spirit [and the Fir]st Man, their fathers. [These also are seven] by which the universe is established.
[§109]* [GAP-3] The five truths proceeding from the Lord's operative divine life, which [GAP-4: sustain and uphold] the entire burden of spiritual order; together with the Lord's operative life [and the divine rational], the origins from which they proceed. [GAP-5] These are the principles through which all spiritual order is constituted and maintained.

[§110] [There are fourt]een aeons of light [that abide] [within the la]nd that is not revealed. This land [in which all the aeons abide is made firm and is estab]lished. They bore up under the enemy [and they were not overcome for no one of the darkness equa]ls them. The hidden plenitude that [abides in] the Ambassador who came from the Father, the [great luminary] He came and stood firm in the midst of these [aeons of light]: he is called the good father of [all] the ae[ons and gods] who are holy and conjoined. He has become leader and a great king after the likeness of the first Father, the lord of all these cou[nse]ls. This universe is held together by their will [from the beginning] to the end of all things.
[§110]* There are fourteen states of divine wisdom [GAP-6] [GAP-7] — the interior ground of the will that has not yet been made manifest. This ground of reception [GAP-8] is made firm and established. These states endured against the assault of evil and falsity [GAP-9] nothing from the opposition is their equal. The hidden fullness of divine good that [GAP-10] — the divine truth sent forth from divine love, the [GAP-11] — this truth came and established itself immovably at the center of all these states [GAP-12]: he is recognized as the benevolent origin of good for [GAP-13: all] the states of wisdom and all spiritual powers that are holy and conjoined in unity. He has become sovereign governor and supreme ruling truth after the likeness of divine love itself, the lord over all workings of divine providence. The whole of spiritual order coheres through their united purpose [GAP-14] unto the consummation of all states.

[§111] The beginning of every good grace is the great Father of the [ligh]ts; since [a]ll [graces] are given by his hand.
[§111]* The origin of every influx of good into the soul is the Divine Love itself, the supreme source of all wisdoms; since all [GAP-1: influxes of good] are bestowed by the power of Divine Love alone.

[§112] The beginning of each blessing and every prayer is the Mother of [L]ife, the first Holy Spirit.
[§112]* The origin of every benediction and every elevation of the mind toward the Divine is the Divine Truth proceeding from Love — the first operation of the Divine into created spiritual life.

[§113] Also, [the] beginning [of] every good [co]unsel is the T[h/i]rd Am[bassad]or, the king of the glorious realm that lies in this [wo]rld, [the image] of the King of the lights.
[§113]* Also, [GAP-2: the] origin [GAP-3: of] every true interior perception of what is good and wise is the mediating Divine Influence that governs the illuminated region within the natural mind — the ruling principle of the glorified interior domain that lies within the external state of life, [GAP-4: proceeding from the authority] of the governing source of all wisdoms.

[§114] The beginning of [all glory and] each [hono]ur (?) is the Beloved of the Lights, who is honour[ed.]
[§114]* The origin of [GAP-5: all glorification and] every true acknowledgment of the Divine is the Divine Good beloved of all wisdoms, who is glorified in return.

[§115] The beginning of all [the trappers] and hunters is the First [Man]
[§115]* The origin of all [GAP-6: who seek and reclaim scattered affections] and who pursue them is the primordial celestial will — [GAP-7: the first spiritual human.]

[§116] [The beginning] of all fighters is [the] Living [Spirit], the Father of Life, who has distribu[ted about] his five sons from place to place.
[§116]* [GAP-8: The origin] of all who combat falsity and evil is [GAP-9: the] vivifying [GAP-10: Divine Truth], the source of all spiritual life, who has ordered his five derived faculties of spiritual perception each into its proper domain.

[§117] The beginning of all the archit[e]cts and builders is the Great Builder, who is glorious.
[§117]* The origin of all who construct and order the interior structures of the regenerating mind is the Divine Wisdom that organizes all understanding into coherent form, which is glorified.

[§118] Also, the first of all the porters of the gr[e]a[t]ness is the Pillar of Glory [the Perfect Man] [ ... ]
[§118]* Also, the first of all who sustain and bear the fullness of the Divine within the soul is the upholding column of Divine Truth [GAP-11: that supports all spiritual order] [GAP-12: from the inmost to the outmost.]

[§119] The beginning of [all] the deliverers [is the Splendour]; or the one who delivers, as he frees whoever belongs [to him] from his words.
[§119]* The origin of [GAP-13: all] who liberate the mind from falsity and evil [GAP-14: is the Divine Truth in its radiant saving power]; that is, the one who liberates, as he frees whoever belongs [GAP-15: to the Divine] through his truths.

[§120] The beginning of every wisdom of truth is the Virgin of Light, [.]
[§120]* The origin of every pure perception of truth proceeding from good is the uncorrupted celestial Wisdom — truth in its pristine, virginal form [GAP-16: .]

[§121] The beginning of all [the great]est honoured ones [and of all knowledge] is Wahman; who is the awakene[r of they] who sleep, the gatherer in of the ones who are sc[att]ered.
[§121]* The origin of all [the highest] and most glorified states of the regenerate soul [GAP-17: and of every illuminated perception] is the Divine Enlightenment of the understanding; which is the awakener of those who lie in spiritual dormancy, the gatherer into unity of those whose affections and truths have been dispersed and scattered.

---

### Translator's Note

The entire chapter is a catalogue of *first principles*: each paragraph names the Divine source from which a specific faculty of the regenerating mind descends. Read spiritually, these are not separate gods but distinct operations of the one Divine flowing into the human mind — Love as origin, Truth as proceeding form, Mediation as accommodation to the natural degree, Combat as temptation-resistance, Building as the ordering of understanding, Sustaining as the upholding of states, Deliverance as liberation from falsity, Pure Wisdom as uncorrupted perception, and Enlightened Understanding as the awakener that gathers all scattered goods and truths back into the unity of the regenerate will.

[§122] [The first resurrector is the Living Spirit, who awakens] it therein [and raises it up].
[§122]* [GAP-1: the spiritual sense requires here an identification of the first power of regeneration or its operation] its operation within [GAP-2: the spiritual sense requires here a description of what this first awakening accomplishes within the soul].

[§123] The second resurrector is [the Mother of Life].
[§123]* The second power of spiritual awakening is [GAP-3: the spiritual sense requires here a second distinct divine operation or faculty through which regeneration proceeds — likely an intermediary influx between the inmost divine and the illumined understanding].

[§124] And the third resurrector is the Father, the First [Man], because by his word shall all lives [arise and] gather in and go to him, and they receive [life] and rest.
[§124]* And the third power of spiritual awakening is the Divine Love itself, the Originating [GAP-4: the spiritual sense requires here a qualifier of the Divine as source or origin of all derivative good], because by Divine Truth proceeding from that Love shall all interior affections that constitute spiritual life [GAP-5: the spiritual sense requires here a verb of awakening, liberation, or vivification] gather inward and return toward conjunction with the Divine, and they receive [GAP-6: the spiritual sense requires here the specific quality bestowed — fullness, blessedness, or eternal life] and enter the state of heavenly peace.

[§125] The fourth resurrector is Wahman, because [it awakens the soul from forgetfulness].
[§125]* The fourth power of spiritual awakening is the Divine Wisdom operating within the understanding, because [GAP-7: the spiritual sense requires here a description of how interior illumination raises the rational mind from falsity into perception of truth].

[§126] The fifth resurrector to the good [souls] is the Father, the First established one [from the beginning]. [He encompasses] all the powers, and he stands firm [over all things]. He shall unveil the [image] over them [in that day], and he draws them to him [by his love], his will [and his mercy], and he [purifies] them, and he stands them up [in his light] there, and they remain until [the end of all things].
[§126]* The fifth power of spiritual awakening toward those in states of genuine good [GAP-8: the spiritual sense requires here a qualifier — those in charity, the regenerated, or the elect affections] is the Divine Love in its foundational and sustaining aspect [GAP-9: the spiritual sense requires here how this grounding divine principle was established from eternity or within the order of regeneration]. [GAP-10: The spiritual sense requires here that the Divine] pervades all faculties of will and understanding, and remains immovable in constancy [GAP-11: the spiritual sense requires here a description of the permanence or universality of this divine presence]. The Divine shall remove the veils of natural appearance from [GAP-12: the spiritual sense requires here what is unveiled — interior truths, or the glorified divine form] over those faculties [GAP-13: the spiritual sense requires here the condition or manner of this unveiling], and draws all regenerated affections toward conjunction with itself [GAP-14: the spiritual sense requires here the means or quality of this drawing — through love, through delight, through interior perception], according to the Divine Providence [GAP-15: the spiritual sense requires here how the divine will operates — freely, without compulsion, in mercy], and [GAP-16: the spiritual sense requires here a verb — vivifies, purifies, perfects, or conjoins] them, and raises them into established spiritual life [GAP-17: the spiritual sense requires here the state or place of this establishment — in heavenly order, in the celestial degree], and they remain in that conjunction until [GAP-18: the spiritual sense requires here the final consummation — the completion of all regeneration, the last separation of good from evil, or the full restitution of all interiorly living things to the Divine].

[§127] the five [le]sso[ns] that [a]re proclaimed in the [wo/r]ld, [one] being cal[le]d [by name] 'the peace', another called '[the] silence', [and] another 'the day', ano[ther ...]
[§127]* The five essential states of spiritual instruction that are made known within the natural mind — [GAP-1: REVIEW one] one being called [GAP-2] "peace," which is the interior rest that comes when goods and truths are conjoined and all conflict in the mind ceases; another called "[GAP-3: REVIEW the] silence," [GAP-4] which is the stilling of the natural mind's own activity so that Divine influx may be received without distortion; another "the day," which is the state of full spiritual illumination when the understanding is opened from within; ano[GAP-5: ther] — *the remaining names are lost but the sequence moves from outer tranquility to deeper states of interior reception.*

[§128] [As for] the gatherer in [... ...] then he shall cau[se] the body [to cease from] all the lusts of the world [and the deeds] of the body in righteousness [This] is the Pill[a]r of Gl[or]y [through which the light ascends] because [it bears] [all things] up to the [land of light]
[§128]* [GAP-6] The ordering principle that gathers together all scattered goods and truths [GAP-7] shall bring the external person [GAP-8] to renounce all the desires belonging to the natural self-will [GAP-9], causing the external form of life to be brought into conformity with Divine order [GAP-10]. This is the sustaining pathway of Divine truth through which purified good ascends from the lower mind toward the celestial [GAP-11], because [GAP-12] [GAP-13] upward to the [GAP-14] — *the passage describes the column or axis by which goods, once separated from falsities, are elevated progressively through discrete degrees.*

[§129] The one they call ['the peace'] it is the [Fi]rst [M]an who dwel[ls i]n the ship of living waters [of the] light [and also] he is c[alled 'the life'] that they have [received] glorious [...]
[§129]* The one they call [GAP-15] — it is the Celestial Human, the primordial Divine Wisdom in human form, who dwells within the containing vessel of living truths [GAP-16] — wisdom from a Divine origin [GAP-17] — who is c[GAP-18: alled] the indwelling Divine Life, that which they have [GAP-19] received in states of heavenly glory [GAP-20]. — *"The ship of living waters" is the doctrinal form that carries and protects celestial good; the First Man dwelling within it is the Divine Human principle from which all regeneration proceeds, borne upon truths that have life in them because they flow from love.*

[§130] [The one they cal]l it 'day' [of the] universe as they shall [find rest] in the great aeon [The lights that] are spread in the universe, it is [the light] established in the [aeon of light] and the so[u]ls [that dwell therein]
[§130]* [GAP-21: cal]l it "day" [GAP-22] — the whole of the spiritual world, the Grand Human, as all things within it shall [GAP-23] be brought into their final order within the infinite Divine state [GAP-24]. Interior truths are extended throughout the Grand Human; it is [GAP-25] the Divine order itself, established in the [GAP-26] celestial kingdom, and the interior life-principles of all who receive influx [GAP-27] — *"Day" here is the universal state of illumination when the Lord's kingdom is fully realized; what is "spread in the universe" are the truths that constitute the connective tissue of the heavenly human form, and the "souls" are the living essences — the will-and-understanding unities — that compose it.*

[§131] the two essences [of Light and Darkness] Did Death arise and stand firm [...] up? Or else did it turn itself from [...] its armour [...] in its [...] but it finished [mixing] the two [essences].
[§131]* The two fundamental natures — of good and of evil — [GAP-1] did spiritual death arise and establish its dominion [GAP-2] over the states of life? Or did it turn itself away from [GAP-3] its protective falsities [GAP-4] in its [GAP-5] yet it consummated [GAP-6] the two [GAP-7] natures altogether.

[§132] [...] it moved it and [aroused] they that are in the [depths] of the earth of Darkness [this is the] first part.
[§132]* [GAP-8] it stirred what was latent and [GAP-9] those who dwell in the [GAP-10] of the natural mind wholly given over to falsity [GAP-11] — this was the first stage of its propagation.

[§133] Yet, after a long time, as there was not [rest], it gushed and streamed up [...] the five elements of Death that gushed [forth] which are smoke, fire, wind, water and darkness [...] the second time [...] they were sculpted [into] those in which Death was established [this is the third part].
[§133]* Yet after a long duration, when there was no [GAP-12] restraint, falsity and evil welled up and propagated by influx [GAP-13] — the five genera of spiritual death that poured forth [GAP-14] — which are obscurity of understanding, self-love, false persuasion, falsity itself, and the utter absence of truth [GAP-15]. In the second stage [GAP-16] they were given determinate and fixed form [GAP-17] — those interior states in which spiritual death was grounded [GAP-18].

[§134] The fourth part is the evil fruits [that are] in the five trees that are in the five worlds of the land of Darkness.
[§134]* The fourth stage is the evil works [GAP-19] proceeding from the five perverted perceptions rooted in the five states of the realm of falsity from evil.

[§135] The fifth time it spread itself out to its creatures that exist in the five worlds; the ones that were formed and generated from the five fruits of Death. They are the five worlds of flesh, the five creations of Darkness in which Death has been established. It spread itself out to ten parts, five male parts and five female [parts].
[§135]* In the fifth stage, spiritual death extended itself into its generated forms existing in the five states of evil — those derivative affections that were shaped and brought forth from the five kinds of evil works of spiritual death. These are the five states of the selfhood, the five orders of falsity from evil in which spiritual death has been grounded. It extended itself into ten aspects: five pertaining to the active will and five pertaining to the receptive understanding [GAP-20].

[§136] Now, like the land of Darkness, in that its spirit and its [soul] they nourished the five elements [of Darkness]; this is also [how] the ten parts that are in male and female, the fire and lust of the flesh [bound] them, as they yoke them with one another [until] they were established against the First Man. This is [the condemnation] of the Darkness, it was condemned for ever.
[§136]* Now, just as the realm of falsity from evil — in that its animating spirit and its [GAP-21] — they sustained the five genera of evil [GAP-22]; so also [GAP-23: REVIEW how] the ten aspects that exist in will and understanding — the self-love and concupiscence of the selfhood [GAP-24] — bind them, as they conjoin will with understanding in each state [GAP-25] — they were arrayed in opposition to the Divine Humanity. This is [GAP-26] the condemnation of the realm of falsity from evil: it was rejected to eternity.

[§137] Alongside the mystery of these twelve parts that came about in the Darkness [and were established] against the First Man. The twelve [parts are] the twelve spirits of error that came about from the twelve signs of the zodiac [...].
[§137]* Corresponding to the mystery of these twelve complete aspects of evil and falsity that arose within the realm of darkness [GAP-27] in opposition to the Divine Humanity — the twelve [GAP-28] — the twelve persuasions of error that originated from the twelve apparent truths of the natural heaven, now wholly perverted [GAP-29].

---

*Translator's note:* The "twelve signs of the zodiac" resist simple one-to-one correspondence. I read them as the complete circle of apparent truths visible in the exterior natural mind — truths of order that, when inverted by the realm of falsity, become the twelve systematic persuasions of error. Swedenborg associates the number twelve with all things of faith and the church in their completeness; here the correspondence holds in its opposite sense — a counterfeit completeness of falsity marshaled against the Lord's Divine Humanity.

[§138] [...] the Darkness, it was defeated by five greatnesses.
[§138]* [GAP-1] Evil and falsity — it was overcome by five degrees of divine operation proceeding from the Divine Itself.

[§139] The first greatness is the Father of Greatness who exists in calm and hiddenness; as he is established in his light-earth, in his own essence.
[§139]* The first degree is the Divine Itself, existing in infinite peace and hiddenness, established in its own celestial good, in its own infinite being.

[§140] Four greatnesses were manifested and came forth of him. They were appointed to ten parts, and established in total twelve.
