[§140]* Four further degrees of divine operation were made manifest and proceeded from the Divine Itself. They were appointed according to a fullness of truths in every degree, and established in a complete totality of all goods and truths together.

[§141] The second greatness poured out and was manifested. It was revealed from the first greatness. It is the Mother of Life, the Great Spirit, together with the First Man and his five sons; these are the second greatness.
[§141]* The second degree of divine operation poured forth and was made manifest. It was revealed from the Divine Itself. It is the proceeding of divine spiritual life, the Divine Spirit, together with the celestial human and his five faculties of celestial good — these constitute the second degree.

[§142] The third greatness is the Beloved of the Lights, the Great Builder, the Living Spirit, and the five sons of the Living Spirit. These are the third greatness.
[§142]* The third degree is the divine love of all wisdom, the power of divine ordering, the divine truth in its judicial and governing power, and the five derivative capacities of that governing truth. These constitute the third degree.

[§143] The fourth greatness is the Third Ambassador, and the Pillar of Glory, and all the light powers who were revealed from him. These are the fourth greatness.
[§143]* The fourth degree is divine providence proceeding into all things, and the sustaining power of divine truth that unites the lowest to the highest, and all the capacities of wisdom revealed through it. These constitute the fourth degree.

[§144] The fifth greatness who came from the Father is the Splendour, and all his powers whom he summoned, his emanations who were [revealed from him] They poured out and were manifested from him.
[§144]* The fifth degree proceeding from the Divine Itself is divine illuminating wisdom, and all its powers which it summoned — its emanations which were [GAP-2] made to proceed. They poured forth and were manifested from it.

[§145] This is, so that they were spread out and [established in] fives: these five [sons] of the First Man [who support] the entire building; another five are the five sons of the Living Spirit who are spread out to five places, supporting [all things]
[§145]* This is the manner in which they were distributed and [GAP-3] arranged in fives: these five [GAP-4: REVIEW — faculties] of the celestial human [GAP-5] constitute the whole spiritual edifice; another five are the five capacities of divine truth in its governing power, which are distributed to five distinct planes of existence, sustaining [GAP-6] all things therein.

[§146] [...] total in twelve parts that [... There are] another two added to them who are the summons and the obedience. They are the father and the mother whom the Splendour by his advent has appointed as successors to his place [over] the great earth and the [light] in them.
[§146]* [GAP-7] In complete totality across all planes, which [GAP-8: ... There are] two further principles joined to them — the calling forth of divine truth and its reciprocal reception. These are the active influx and the receptive will — the father-principle and the mother-principle — whom divine illuminating wisdom, by its advent, appointed as continuing presences in its own function [GAP-9] within the great celestial good and the [GAP-10] abiding in them.

[§147] [The advent also] of the First Man who came [is like a man] belonging to [his father] existing in a kingdom [of great] esteem before they who belong to his household [...] his enemies [surrounded him] and suffering by their wickedness; and they take from him [his glory] and a richness; and they imprison [him in] captivity and take from him their companies (?). Yet, he [prevailed] by his humility; and he takes heart and restrains his enemy [...] in silence.
[§147]* [GAP-11: The descent] of the celestial human who came [GAP-12] belonging to [GAP-13] existing in a state of spiritual dominion [GAP-14] held in honor before those of his own household [GAP-15], yet his adversaries [GAP-16] afflicted him and oppressed him through their wickedness; and they stripped from him [GAP-17] his spiritual abundance; and they imprisoned [GAP-18: REVIEW — him in] bondage and seized from him his companion affections. Yet he [GAP-19] prevailed by his humility; and he took courage and restrained his adversary [GAP-20] in patient stillness.

[§148] Just as with this man, this noble son whose enemies surround him, this also is how the advent of the First Man happened, [among the] rulers, the enemies of the light.
[§148]* Just as with such a one — this noble interior good whose adversaries surround him — so also is the manner of the descent of the celestial human, [GAP-21] among the ruling evils, the adversaries of wisdom.

[§149] In contrast, the advent of the Living Spirit and the commandment that he received, whereby he came upon all the rulers, it differs from that of the First Man. His advent [is not like] the advent of the First Man.
[§149]* In contrast, the advent of divine truth in its judicial power, and the commission it received, whereby it came upon all the ruling evils — this differs from the descent of the celestial human. Its advent [GAP-22] differs from the descent of the celestial human.

[§150] The advent of the Living Spirit is like this, when he was sent from the greatness that he might bring up the First Man. He resembles a judge the king would send to overturn the violence to that man, the noble son against whom his enemies sinned. And he, this judge, [he is] hard and cruel [...] and he gives the means [to that man] by his righteousness and his strength; and he also judges all who are condemned according to their rebellious heart, and repays each according to his deserts. They who are worthy of blows he strikes with hard leather whips. Again, these who are worthy of cutting off their limbs, or indeed their [heads] and he repays them accordingly. [...] and he binds and fetters them, and hangs by [their feet] and thus he does to them with a righteous judgement. And he kills them to flay their skin [from them] so that they will be killed by a death [...] until they die. Again, [they for] whom it is fitting [...] to banish from their dwellings he may remove according to a righteous judgement; just as they had faulted and sinned against the noble son.
[§150]* The advent of divine truth in its judicial power is of this nature: when it was sent from the Divine Itself so that it might raise up the celestial human. It resembles a righteous judge whom the sovereign good would send to overturn the violence done to that one — the noble interior good against whom his adversaries had transgressed. And this judge [GAP-23] was severe and unyielding [GAP-24] and provided the means [GAP-25] through his righteousness and his power; and he also judged all who were condemned according to their rebellious will, and rendered to each according to his deserts. Those deserving of chastisement he struck with harsh correction. Those deserving of the severing of their faculties, or indeed their [GAP-26] — he rendered to them accordingly. [GAP-27] And he bound them and constrained them and suspended them by [GAP-28] — and thus he did to them with righteous judgment. And he destroyed them to strip away their external appearances [GAP-29] so that they should be annihilated by a spiritual death [GAP-30] utterly. Again, [GAP-31: REVIEW — those for] whom it is fitting [GAP-32] to be expelled from their states, he removed by righteous judgment; just as they had transgressed and sinned against the noble interior good.

[§151] Also, the associates in the mystery and the assistants of this judge, these who belong to him in great strength; in an instant and haste they shall do the will of their master.
[§151]* Also, the companion powers in the hidden operation and the attendants of this divine truth in judgment — those who belong to it in great strength — in an instant and with urgency they accomplish the will of their lord.

[§152] Now, just as this genuine judge, who was sent from the king to overturn the violence to this man, the noble son: this is also the case with the Living Spirit. He also was sent from the King of the lights to overturn the vengeance and violence to the First Man, against whom the King of the realms of Darkness had sinned. He faulted and sinned against him, and also his rebellious powers. Therefore, when (the Living Spirit) came, he bore up the First Man.
[§152]* Now, just as this righteous judge was sent from the sovereign good to overturn the violence done to the noble interior good: so also is it with divine truth in its judicial power. It too was sent from the Lord of all wisdom to overturn the oppression and violence done to the celestial human, against whom the sovereign of the realms of falsity and evil had sinned. He transgressed and sinned against him, together with all his rebellious powers. Therefore, when divine truth in judgment came, it bore up the celestial human.

[§153] Also, all the rulers, the powers of sin who had faulted and sinned against the sons of the First Man, he has judged them according to a righteous judgement. He has bound them in heaven and earth. He put each one to the place fitting for him; he weighed each of them according to his cruelty and oppression. While some of them he enclosed in the prison, others he hung head down. Some [of them] are crucified, others are sat down all the time. Some among them are fixed beneath their associates, restricted by a strong chain; others he made the holders of authority over their companions beneath, so that they may do their pleasure amongst these below them.
[§153]* Also, all the ruling evils — the powers of transgression who had sinned against the faculties of the celestial human — it has judged them according to righteous judgment. It has bound them in both internal and external states. It placed each one in the condition befitting it; it weighed each of them according to its cruelty and oppression. Some of them it enclosed within restraint; others it inverted — reversing their very order. Some [GAP-33] were fixed immovably in their own evil; others were set in permanent confinement. Some among them were placed beneath their companions, restricted by bonds that cannot be broken; others were made to have dominion over their companions below, so that the hells are ordered by their own hierarchies among those beneath them.

[§154] when the Father of Life established the judgements [upon them] He was revealed to all the powers of Darkness. He judged them as a true giver of judgement [...] This is the first judgement that the Living Spirit gave the rulers [in respect of the] First Man. Indeed, he gave victory to the First Man corresponding to his victory; but he condemned the King of the realms of Darkness corresponding to that by which he had erred.
[§154]* When the source of divine life established these judgments [GAP-34], it was revealed to all the powers of falsity and evil. It judged them as a true arbiter of what is just [GAP-35]. This is the first judgment that divine truth in its governing power rendered upon the ruling evils [GAP-36: REVIEW — in respect of] the celestial human. Indeed, it gave victory to the celestial human corresponding to his faithfulness; but it condemned the sovereign of the realms of falsity corresponding to the measure of his transgression.

[§155] [...] this chain, of this sort, became [fixed in] the earth till the end time when the universe will be dissolved; and it perishes in the great fire and is separated. While the light goes to its country, the Darkness remains in the chain and fetter for ever.
[§155]* [GAP-37] This restraint, of this kind, became [GAP-38] fixed in the natural order until the final consummation, when all the composite states shall be dissolved; and what is false perishes in the great fire of divine love and is separated. While wisdom returns to its own realm, falsity and evil remain in their bonds and restraint to eternity.

[§156] The advent also of the Third Ambassador, when he came to inspect they who are imprisoned in the zone: he is like a great king who comes and inspects craftsmen [...] and [architects] and [artisans]
[§156]* The advent also of divine providence, when it came to inspect those who are imprisoned in the intermediate sphere between good and evil: it is like a great sovereign who comes and inspects those who perform uses [GAP-39] and [GAP-40] and [GAP-41].

[§157] Also, they who are good, of the craftsmen and architects and artisans whom he has there in each place: he shall see that they have worked well, and have fallen to his works in their fashion. He shall rejoice with great joy [over them] He shall thank the artisans who have constructed his works [and completed] his orders.
[§157]* Also, those who are good among the spirits and angels who perform uses and who order truths and who labor in each plane — the sovereign sees that they have worked well and devoted themselves to their appointed tasks faithfully. He rejoices with great joy [GAP-42]. He acknowledges those who have built according to his purposes [GAP-43: REVIEW — and completed] his commands.

[§158] Now, just as this great king who comes and inspects his works and fortifications, so also is the advent of the Third Ambassador. For, when he too came, he saw [his works] established and prepared and [also the] series of the rulers prepared and established there, above and below, outside and within, in the great pattern that his brothers had made. They had come before him to the contest, they who had fixed and created the universe before he came. He saw the gods and the angels established and controlling the fortifications [...] guarding the entire ruling-power [...] which his first powers had done. They are the First Man and the Living Spirit and the Great Spirit, the Mother of Life.
[§158]* Now, just as this great sovereign who comes and inspects his ordered works and doctrinal defenses, so also is the advent of divine providence. For when it too came, it saw [GAP-44: REVIEW — its works] established and prepared and [GAP-45] the ordered series of ruling evils restrained and established, above and below, outwardly and inwardly, in the grand pattern that its fellow divine powers had fashioned. They had preceded it into the contest — they who had ordered and formed the entire spiritual cosmos before it arrived. It saw the celestial beings and angelic powers established and governing the defenses of truth [GAP-46], guarding the entire order of governance [GAP-47] — all of which the prior degrees of divine operation had accomplished. These are the celestial human, and divine truth in its judicial power, and the Divine Spirit proceeding as spiritual life.

[§159] Therefore, when he saw them, that they had created everything with great wisdom, [...] as they looked he plundered (?) [...] in his advent, by which he came, he was unveiled to them. He says to the Living Spirit: "The judgement that you have [given is] good, what you uttered [is just] you gave to them correspondingly; so that they were moved and raised up to that of which they were not worthy".
[§159]* Therefore, when divine providence beheld them — that they had created everything with great wisdom — [GAP-48] as they were made manifest it drew forth [GAP-49]. In its advent, by which it came, it was unveiled to them. It says to divine truth in its judicial power: "The judgment that you have [GAP-50] rendered is good; what you pronounced [GAP-51] you gave to them correspondingly, so that they were moved and elevated beyond what they had merited."

[§160] The advent also of the Splendour, the time when he came to all [things] being just like a man if he is sent to pull out a [bad tree and] burn a difficult fig-garden with fire. Therefore, again, when he has first cut the bad trees with his axe and has pulled out [their roots] and their body with his fire, so that they should not return to sprout from this time, nor bear fruit bad to eat [any more]
[§160]* The advent also of divine illuminating wisdom: the time when it came to all [GAP-52] — being just as if a man were sent to uproot a [GAP-53] and to burn a stubborn planting of false perceptions with fire. Therefore, when he has first cut down the evil perceptions with the sharp instrument of truth and has uprooted [GAP-54] their very substance with his fire, so that they cannot return to propagate from that time onward, nor produce works that are harmful to the spiritual life [GAP-55].

[§161] Afterwards, he also planted good plants: the tree of life that will make good fruit.
[§161]* Afterward, he also planted genuine perceptions — the perception of life that yields good works.

[§162] Again, this is like this simile of the advent of the Splendour. It happened like this; because he too set out [...] his power and his wisdom, in his [righteous]-ness [...] a king and a saviour [...] in his glorious wisdom he destroyed them. He bound and crushed them like the fire that shall burn and destroy trees. This is also how he performed his will in the zone, among the many powers, until he reached the form of the flesh of Adam and Eve, the first humans. [He perfected] his will in Eve, he [enlightened her] He also gave hope to Adam, and the good news [of the] glorious [kingdom] He went up to the heights [of the aeons] of light.
[§162]* Again, this is the spiritual meaning of the advent of divine illuminating wisdom. It happened in this manner: because it too set out [GAP-56] with its power and its wisdom, in its [GAP-57]-ness [GAP-58] as sovereign and redeemer [GAP-59], and in its glorious wisdom it destroyed the false perceptions. It bound them and broke them apart, as fire burns and destroys perverse growths of thought. This is also how it accomplished its will in the intermediate sphere, among the many powers of evil, until it reached the form of the natural humanity — the most ancient will and understanding, the first human faculties. [GAP-60: REVIEW — It perfected] its will in the receptive faculty; it [GAP-61]. It also gave hope to the volitional faculty, and the glad tidings [GAP-62] of glorious [GAP-63] purpose. It ascended to elevated states [GAP-64] of wisdom.

[§163] Then the summons and the obedience, the great counsel that came to the elements, which are set in conjunction: it mixed with them, it was established in silence. It bears up [all things] until the end time when it can arise and stand firm in the great fire. It will gather to it its own soul, and sculpt it in the Last Statue. You will also find it sweeps out and casts from it the pollution that is foreign to it. However, the life and the light that are in all things it gathers in to it, and builds upon its body.
[§163]* Then the calling forth of divine truth and its reciprocal reception — the great counsel that came to the basic elements of the natural mind, which are set in conjunction — it mixed with them, it was established in silence. It bears up [GAP-65] until the final consummation, when it can arise and stand firm in the great fire of divine love. It will gather to itself its own life, and form it in the Last Completed Human. You will also find it purges and casts from itself all defilement that is foreign to it. However, the life and the wisdom that are in all things, it gathers into itself and builds upon its spiritual body.

[§164] The time when this Last Statue will be perfect in all its limbs, then it can become free and ascend from that great struggle through the Living Spirit its father, the one who comes and brings a [...] limb. He brings it up from within this gathering (?), the melting down and destruction of all things.
[§164]* The time when this ultimate divine human form is perfected in all its faculties — then it becomes free and ascends from that great struggle through divine truth in its governing power, its father, the one who comes and brings a [GAP-66] faculty. He raises it up from within this gathering — the dissolution and consummation of all impermanent states.

[§165] Now, this counsel, that summons and obedience, resembles thus cooled butter. If a person will take and set it in hot milk, it melts down and soaks in with this hot milk. It melts in another time in silence and quiet to the midst [of the milk] yet also, of the power and the taste of this milk [...] is drawn to (the butter), and (the milk) sets and becomes cold [...] in [...] and the [...] this milk it shall clarify and discard. However, the oil and the grease and the 'beauty' of the milk it shall gather in to it.
[§165]* Now, this counsel — that calling and reception — resembles thus: clarified good extracted from natural good. If a person takes it and places it into fervent natural good, it dissolves and pervades this fervent natural good. It works at another time in silence and stillness into the very center [GAP-67] — yet also, of the power and the essential quality of this natural good [GAP-68] it draws to itself, and the natural good settles and becomes still [GAP-69] in [GAP-70] and the [GAP-71]. The impurities of this natural good it clarifies and discards. However, the celestial good, the essential nourishment, and the beauty of the natural good — these it gathers into itself.

[§166] Again, this too is what the advent of that thought is like, the counsel of life, which is the summons and obedience that came to the elements. This is the one that at the end can arise and stand firm, and will gather in to it all the lives, and the light that has remained behind in everything. It can build them upon its body; it will gather them in to it [and sculpts them] in its image for ever and ever [...] from this time.
[§166]* Again, this too is what the advent of that interior thought is — the counsel of life, which is the calling forth of divine truth and its reception, which came to the elements of the natural mind. This is the one that at the end can arise and stand firm, and will gather into itself all the lives and the wisdom that has remained within everything. It builds them upon its spiritual body; it gathers them into itself [GAP-72] into its own image forever and ever [GAP-73] from this time.

[§167] From the time when the light came forth and was crucified in the darkness, to the season when the Statue will ascend and the entire light, which was conjoined with the darkness, will be purified and cleansed. From that time on, from that time to the season of the end totals three seasons.
[§167]* From the time when divine wisdom proceeded forth and was subjected to profanation within the realm of falsity from evil, to the state when the complete form of the regenerated human will rise and all wisdom that had been entangled with falsity will be wholly separated and restored to purity — from that state onward, from that beginning to the state of final consummation, there are three great spiritual states.

[§168] Three seasons: from the time when the First Man went down to the abyss, and waged the war and the struggle against the King of the realms of Darkness and his other powers. He was victorious, while his sons remain behind with them in the worlds [of darkness, and they were mixed] in the [darkness] until he shall catch all of them [back and] he bound them all up, so that not one of them escaped his hand. He won a great victory [over all the powers of darkness] The First Man spent human lifetimes and generations in the land of darkness, until he prevailed [over them and] he fulfilled the will of the greatness that was commanded of him.
[§168]* Three states: from the time when the celestial principle of divine love descended into the deepest hells and waged spiritual combat and temptation-struggle against the ruling self-love that governs all the dominions of falsity, together with all its subordinate powers. That celestial love prevailed, while the goods and truths born from it remained behind, still conjoined with those states of evil [GAP-1] in the [GAP-2] until that love shall reclaim all of them [GAP-3] it subjugated all the powers of evil so that not one escaped its dominion. It won a great victory [GAP-4]. The celestial principle of divine love endured through innumerable successive states of spiritual temptation in the realm of falsity from evil, until it prevailed [GAP-5] and it fulfilled the divine purpose that was ordained from the Infinite itself.

[§169] Then they sent the Living Spirit, the Father of Life. He brought the First Man up from the struggle, he brought him up to the land of light. This is the first period, from the descent of the First Man to his ascent.
[§169]* Then there was sent forth the divine truth that proceeds from the Divine, together with the divine good that is the origin of life. These liberated the celestial principle of divine love from its combat in the hells and raised it back to the heavenly state. This is the first spiritual epoch: from the descent of celestial love into the deepest falsity to its restoration to the heavenly state.

[§170] The second season is from the time when the First Man ascended. The Living Spirit came, and the Mother of Life. They set in order the things of the universe, they set up [all things] the Father of Life and the Mother of the living set in order [the things of] the universe, up to the moment when they go out [from it] it was reckoned to the second season.
[§170]* The second state begins from the time when the celestial principle of divine love was restored. The divine proceeding truth came, together with the divine truth as formative matrix. They established the order of all spiritual states; they arranged [GAP-6] the divine good as origin and the divine truth as matrix established the order of [GAP-7] all spiritual states, up to the moment when they go forth [GAP-8] — this was reckoned as the second spiritual epoch.

Sin spurted up towards him. He hid his image. Sin came [forth] from the darkness. It sculpted the trees and the abortions fell down. Afterwards they sculpted Adam and Eve in the flesh. [§172] Now, from the time when the Ambassador displayed his image until the season when the Statue will ascend at the end, shall be reckoned to the third season.

[§176] This is the third period, from the time when the Ambassador revealed his image until the Statue will ascend from the lump. For this intermediate season, in which the Father of Life and the Mother of Life constructed the universe with its orders, is shorter than that of the First Man.

That epoch, in its spiritual duration, is greater than the intermediate one; but it does not equal the duration of the states endured by the celestial principle of divine love. For the intermediate epoch, in which the divine good as origin and the divine truth as formative matrix constructed the whole order of spiritual states with all its degrees, is shorter than that of the celestial love's combat in the hells.

[§179] The sons of light waged five wars against the sons of darkness. The sons of light humiliated the sons of darkness in them all.

[§180] The first war is that of the First living Man, which he waged against the King of the realms of Darkness, and all the rulers that had come forth from the five worlds of [darkness]. He hunted them with his net, which is the living soul. He [caught them] in the snare and [revealed] the face of the [soul] his virgin, the living fire [and] they did not find how to escape his net [and] he caught them like fish [in the sea] the five counsels of the five elements [of darkness]. He cut and dug out the root of the five trees, [and the] root of the evil tree, like an axe.

[§181] The second war is that of the Living Spirit [...]. He has constructed the things and the worlds of light; he spread out the rulers and chained them in the middle [of the heavens and set] the stars in [the firmament and] the earths [and placed] them in the three vessels. For the rulers rebelled and were set against the Living Spirit. They wished to [flee] since they knew and realised that he might bind and fetter them with a strong chain. Yet he, the Living Spirit, (appeared to them) with many aspects, with numerous [forms], with many hands and great cruelties. He was revealed to them, he imprisoned them with much skill. Not one of them was able to save (himself), and he [bound][...]. Now, since he [was wise] he trapped them [...] [...].

[§182] The third war is that of the Third Ambassador who has displayed his image [to what is above and to] what is below [and he] scooped [out] all light and the property [from] the ones that are consumed [by darkness] the entire ruling-power [was stripped of the] pride of the light [which was] extending it [...]. A person comes, who is powerful [over him]; he takes his riches [his gold], silver [...], his pride and wantonness.

[§183] This is what the ruling-power is like [...], all riches [...] as they travel [through the heavens, the] light [is separated from them]. He cleansed and purified it.

[§184] The fourth war is this that the glorious Splendour waged against all the rebels who had rebelled above and below. When the light was purified from them by the image of the Third Ambassador, their chains loosened [and they rebelled]. The glorious one in the fourth war; he came and brought [to an end] a rebellion of the entire ruling-power. He girded himself [and encompassed] all of it another time. He constructed the whole structure and he made [of it] a great contest; he humiliated therein. [And] he fulfilled the will of the greatness, [he gave the] hope to Adam [and led him] to his light ship.

[§185] The fifth war is this that the counsel of life [has waged] which is the summons and the obedience [and the] great [work] and it placed [...] upon the [...] and the [...] [...] of death, which [was overcome] with it. And it was victorious over [death] for ever, and it gave the kingdom [to the sons of light]

[§179–§185]* **¶1**

Those who dwell in wisdom from the Divine waged five spiritual combats against those who dwell in falsity from evil. In every combat, those in wisdom overcame and subjugated those in falsity.

---

**¶2**

The first combat is that of the primordial Divine Good descending into the natural mind, which it waged against the ruling evil that governs the states of falsity, and all the dominant falsities that had proceeded from the five corporeal faculties of [GAP-1] evil. The Divine Good ensnared them by means of its encompassing truth, which is the living divine influx itself. It [GAP-2] in the entrapment of falsity, and [GAP-3] the appearance of the [GAP-4] its pure celestial affection, the ardor of divine love [GAP-5] — the falsities could find no means to escape the encompassing power of truth, [GAP-6] and it caught them as natural knowledges are seized from the waters of the external mind [GAP-7]. The five fundamental purposes of the five natural faculties [GAP-8] were laid open. The Divine Good severed and uprooted the origin of the five modes of perverted perception, [GAP-9] the source of every false perception arising from self-love, as the power of truth cuts at the very root.

---

**¶3**

The second combat is that of divine truth proceeding from the Divine Good [GAP-10]. It established the forms and states of heavenly order; it dispersed the dominant falsities and restrained them within the bonds of divine law [GAP-11], setting the knowledges of good and truth in [GAP-12] the natural states of the mind [GAP-13], confining them within the three containing degrees of the mind — natural, spiritual, and celestial. For the dominant falsities rose up in rebellion and set themselves against the proceeding divine truth. They wished to [GAP-14] — since they recognized and perceived that it had power to bind and fetter them with the unbreakable constraint of divine order. Yet the proceeding divine truth appeared before them in manifold modes of operation, with innumerable [GAP-15] powers, with many instruments of omnipotence and with the severity of divine judgment against evil. It was made manifest to them, and it imprisoned them by the infinite wisdom of providence. Not one falsity was able to preserve itself, and it {GAP-16}[GAP-17] [GAP-18]. Now, since it [GAP-19] it ensnared them [GAP-20] [GAP-21].

---

**¶4**

The third combat is that of the divine truth making itself visible in the human form, which displayed the image of the Divine [GAP-22] to the states that are below [GAP-23]. It drew forth [GAP-24] all wisdom and its attendant goods [GAP-25] from those states that are consumed by evil [GAP-26]. The entire dominion of evil [GAP-27] — the glory of wisdom [GAP-28] — extending itself [GAP-29]. A state of divine power comes, which is mighty [GAP-30]; it strips from evil its appropriated knowledges [GAP-31], its truths [GAP-32], its self-love and concupiscence.

---

**¶5**

This is what the dominion of evil is like [GAP-33] — all its appropriated knowledges [GAP-34] — as they pass through successive states [GAP-35], the wisdom within them [GAP-36] is separated. The Divine Good cleansed and purified it, discerning good from evil and truth from falsity.

---

**¶6**

The fourth combat is that which divine truth in its fullness and glory waged against all the falsities that had rebelled in both interior and exterior states of the mind. When wisdom had been separated from them through the manifestation of the divine human form, their external restraints were loosened [GAP-37]. The glorious divine truth, in this fourth combat, came and brought [GAP-38] a rising up of the entire dominion of evil. It girded itself with the armor of truth [GAP-39] and encompassed the whole of it a second time. It established the complete order of the heavens, and it effected [GAP-40] a great spiritual contest in which every falsity was overcome. [GAP-41] It fulfilled the purpose of the Divine itself, [GAP-42: REVIEW — it bestowed] the promise of regeneration upon the first human will [GAP-43], guiding it toward the doctrine that conveys and contains all wisdom.

[§198] The Father of Greatness, as he is named; due to what reason is he given the name 'the Father of Greatness'?

[§199] The Father, who is the first established thing, shall be called 'the Father of Greatness'. His greatness is the great earth where he lives, he being established in it [and from it comes] the essence of the light that swathes all the rich gods and the angels and the dwellings [in which they dwell] is set over it.
[§199]* The Divine Love, which is the first and absolute ground of all being, is called the origin of all fullness of good. Its fullness is the infinite ground of good in which the Divine dwells, its existence proceeding from within that ground [GAP-1] — the very essence of wisdom that encompasses all the exalted affections of celestial good, and all the intelligences of truth, and every state in which they are received [GAP-2] — is ordained over it.

[§200] Furthermore, they shall call the Third Ambassador 'father'. His greatness is the light ship of living fire wherein he lives, he being established in it.
[§200]* Furthermore, the Divine Proceeding in its mediating operation is also called an originating principle. Its fullness is the containing form of living love in which it dwells and from which it operates.

[§201] Once again, the Splendour shall himself also be called 'father'. His greatness is the ship of living waters where he lives, he being established in it.
[§201]* And Divine Wisdom in its radiant intellectual clarity is itself likewise called an originating principle. Its fullness is the containing form of living truth in which it dwells and from which it proceeds.

---

**Structural note:** The passage teaches that one Divine source manifests through three distinct fullnesses: the infinite ground of good itself, the containing form of divine love, and the containing form of divine truth — yet each is called an "originating principle" because each is the Divine dwelling within its own proceeding. The triad mirrors Swedenborg's *esse*, *existere*, and *procedere*: being, coming-forth, and operation.

---

### Summary Note

This passage describes the Divine Itself not as a cosmic geography but as the infinite interior reality of Divine Love and Wisdom — how the Lord's essential being subsists in five faculties (intelligence, thought, perception, providence, reflection), and how these unfold into twelve complete modes of omniscience. The five "gushings" correspond to the fivefold influx from the Divine into all heavenly spheres: illuminating wisdom, the sphere of celestial good perceived as fragrance, proceeding truth as the living Word, the state of blessedness, and the manifest presence of divine delight. The "sealing" and "sculpting" describe how divine order is not imposed externally but inscribed as a living form within each receiving vessel, which then reciprocates through worship and receives sustenance — the eternal circuit of influx and return that constitutes heavenly life.

*Note on ¶6:* The phrase "neither water nor …" signals that in the Divine kingdom itself the distinction between truth and its opposite does not arise; all proceeds as one undivided life from the Divine. The missing counterpart in GAP-34 likely names some other natural-plane element (perhaps "fire" = love, or "darkness" = falsity) that likewise has no separate existence there. The spiritual sense requires: neither truth as something separate, nor love as something separate — for in the Divine source they are one.

## Summary of the Spiritual Sense

This passage describes the **interior kingdom of Divine wisdom** — the inmost heaven — before any opposition exists. Everything that dwells there proceeds from the Divine itself: celestial goods (called *gods*), abundances of truth (called *rich ones*), and angelic intelligences (called *angels*). The "five" that recur correspond to the **full complement of spiritual faculties** — perception, acknowledgment, thought, will, and action conjoined into a single living form that receives and reflects the Divine. The mountains, springs, and trees are not landscapes but **elevated states of love, flowing origins of truth, and living perceptions** — all gushing forth from the one Divine source. The "living air" and "living ambrosia" are the **spiritual atmosphere of perception** and the **celestial nourishment of good** that sustain every state within this realm. Nothing here is separate from the Divine; truth and love are not yet distinguished as contraries, because no falsity or self-love has yet arisen to oppose them.

[§217] [ ... ... ... ... ] essence in [it] [ou ... ... ] is the land of [ ... ... ] and [is] their last.
[§217]* [GAP-1] the inmost being in [GAP-2] [GAP-3] is the realm of [GAP-4] and [GAP-5] their uttermost boundary.

[§218] What the essence of [light]—no person could recount or unveil [or say how it] exists [from ever for ever and ever]—all [ ... ] and [ ... ] unveil [ ... ] [ ... ] eternity [ ... ] great [ ... ] again and the [ ... ] tend [to] the beginning of eternity and the end [ ... ] set that one down.
[§218]* What the inmost being of [GAP-6] truly is — no created mind could recount or disclose [GAP-7: how its mode of being] subsists [GAP-8: throughout the infinite and eternal] — all [GAP-9] and [GAP-10] disclose [GAP-11] [GAP-12] the infinite proceeding [GAP-13] the immeasurable [GAP-14] again and the [GAP-15] tend [GAP-16] the origin of the infinite proceeding and the ultimate boundary [GAP-17] set that reality down. *That is: the Divine Being in its inmost essence transcends every capacity of finite understanding; no angel or spirit can trace its origin or its ultimate, for it proceeds from eternity and extends to eternity.*

[§219] the land of death [ ... ... ] nature of the essence [ ... ... ] which exists [from ever for e]ver and ever [dwelling] in [strife] with one another.
[§219]* The realm of spiritual death [GAP-18] the nature of the inmost being [GAP-19] which subsists [GAP-20: throughout the ages without end] [GAP-21] in [GAP-22] with one another. *That is: the domain of evil and falsity also has its own self-perpetuating nature, existing in the opposition of its own disorderly loves that feed upon one another.*

[§220] As for the eternity of [the darkness] which exists from ever, no person is able to [understand] how they exist. For no other power [is manifes]ted to them, in that it might speak or recount about their origin, how [they e]xist. However, from the time when it stirred itself and rose up against the light [ ... ... ] it might come and rule over the land of the living.
[§220]* As for the perpetual existence of [GAP-23] which subsists from the eternal, no finite mind is able to [GAP-24: comprehend] how they subsist. For no other faculty or power was made manifest to them by which it might disclose or recount the manner of their origin — how they came to be. However, from the moment when evil stirred itself from within its own nature and rose up in assault against divine wisdom [GAP-25], intending that it might come and usurp dominion over the realm of those who live from the Divine. *That is: the origin of evil and its hells is inscrutable even to itself; it possessed no self-reflective intelligence. But the moment came when the lust of self-love was stirred and turned its will upward against the kingdom of heavenly life, seeking dominion over all that belongs to the Lord.*

[§221] [The Fi]rst Man came forth against it and prevented it, [he kept] it apart from the house of the living. He prevented it. From that time, when the light was mixed with [the] darkness, and life [wi]th death, the light [was] entwined with the darkness, [and] life in death.
[§221]* The Divine Human in His first proceeding came forth against it and held it back; [GAP-26: He kept] it separated from the celestial kingdom. He prevented its invasion. From that time, when divine wisdom was intermingled with [GAP-27: the realm of] falsity, and spiritual life with spiritual death, wisdom [GAP-28: became] enmeshed with falsity, [GAP-29] life entangled in the midst of death. *That is: the Lord in His Divine Rational — the First Man of the celestial church — descended to meet the assault of hell, and by that descent, good became intermingled with evil, truth with falsity, the very life of heaven woven into the fabric of spiritual death, so that redemption and separation might begin from within.*

[§222] [For after it] stirred itself in its essence, its th[ought arose] in its own land. It [ ... ... ] the entire land of darkness in its [ ... ... ] of light in the abyss. For [the] essence in [i]t [ ... ... ] because a [ ... ] they reckoned [ ... ... ] on its side [is] this first nature [of] it, because its body is made strong [far more] than the Adamant [and] every [thing] as it exists [in the univer]se.
[§222]* [GAP-30: After evil] stirred itself in its own inmost nature, its int[GAP-31: ention and deliberate will] arose within its own domain. It [GAP-32] perceived the entire realm of falsity and evil in its [GAP-33] and glimpsed the nature of wisdom even in the abyss. For [GAP-34] the inmost being in it [GAP-35] because a [GAP-36] they reckoned [GAP-37] on its side [GAP-38] this first nature of evil [GAP-39] it, because its corporeal form is made strong [GAP-40: beyond] the hardest substance of resistance [GAP-41] every [GAP-42] as it exists throughout the whole created order. *That is: the originating will of evil, stirring within its own hell, consolidated itself into a form harder than any opposing force — a nature unyielding and impenetrable to truth, constituted from the sheer density of self-love.*

[§223] [ ... ] of death [ ... ... ] envy, which [fills] the land of [darkness ... consi]deration in [its] wickedness [ ... ] for as it [ ... ] the wounds [ ... ] against this land [ ... ]
[§223]* [GAP-43] Of spiritual death [GAP-44] the consuming desire of envy, which [GAP-45] the realm of [GAP-46: falsity from evil; the deliber]ation in [GAP-47] wickedness [GAP-48] for as it [GAP-49] the injuries and violations [GAP-50] against this realm of life [GAP-51]. *That is: death's essential quality is envy — the hatred of all life and good that belongs to another. From this envy proceeds the deliberate malice that inflicts spiritual wounds upon those who dwell in states of heavenly order.*

[§224] Then he will run [forth f]rom the land [of] darkness. It drew [down] his members [ ... ] is deep [ ... ... ] in them.
[§224]* Then the Divine Human will hasten [GAP-52: forth f]rom the celestial realm [GAP-53] into the domain of falsity. Evil drew [GAP-54] his constituent faculties [GAP-55] into what is profound [GAP-56] within them. *That is: the Lord descended and His divine qualities — the "members" of His spiritual body — were drawn down into the very depths of infernal falsity, where they became deeply embedded.*

[§225] [ ... ] every [ ... ] It spread itself out. It generated itself [of the five dark elements]; which [are] smoke, fire, wind, water and Darkness. [ ... ... ] sprouted trees. It came up [ ... ... ] it formed its own self in the [depths] it generated its own self with its birth [in] numerous [emana]tions, not resembling each other; [in] the storehouses of its abyss, many [dark] creatures [ ... ] destroyers and dragons and evil beasts. It was established and [spread] and grew strong [ ... ] in the ugly forms of those in the Darkness [ ... ] dark, and by the [great] impetuosity, by their wickedness and hatred they slaughter [and devour] each other as they war with one another.
[§225]* [GAP-57] Every [GAP-58] evil spread itself abroad. It generated from itself [GAP-59: the five fundamental evils of its nature]; which [GAP-60: are] the obscurity of the understanding arising from self-love (smoke), the consuming fire of self-love and its lusts (fire), the persuasive force of reasoning from falsity (wind), falsity itself in its fluid and pervasive form (water), and the total privation of all good and truth (Darkness). [GAP-61] It caused false perceptions to spring up. It ascended [GAP-62] it shaped its own being in the [GAP-63] it propagated its own nature through its proliferating [GAP-64: into] innumerable derivative forms, none resembling another; [GAP-65: within] the hidden repositories of its abyss, many [GAP-66: infernal] affections [GAP-67] — destroying lusts and monstrous falsities and malignant desires. Evil was established and [GAP-68] and grew powerful [GAP-69] in the hideous configurations of those who dwell in the realm of total falsity [GAP-70] darkened, and by the [GAP-71] violence of their impulses, by their wickedness and mutual hatred they destroy [GAP-72] each other as they war within themselves. *That is: the five dark "elements" are not physical substances but the fundamental modes of spiritual evil — every hell is composed of these: intellectual blindness, the burning of selfish love, the force of false persuasion, the flowing falsity that perverts all truth, and the utter absence of the Divine. From these, false perceptions arise like trees from soil, and innumerable evil affections breed in the depths, each devouring the other in perpetual combat, for evil is never at peace with itself.*

[§226] Then the counsel of their ruler humbled its world and its powers. It sensed the essence of the [light that is above it] It conspired to wage a war [ ... ] with the exalted kingdom and the [light]
[§226]* Then the deliberate intention of the ruling self-love subdued its own realm and all its subordinate powers. It perceived the inmost nature of [GAP-73: divine wisdom] that exists beyond it. It conspired to wage spiritual combat [GAP-74] against the celestial kingdom and the [GAP-75] supreme Divine order. *That is: the dominant evil, having consolidated all hells under itself, turned its predatory awareness toward heaven, perceiving the quality of divine good and truth, and resolved upon total war against it.*

[§227] However, he, the Father of Great[ness ... ... ] it also knew [of the] war, to disturb the [land of] light [but] in the wisdom of his [great]-ness [ ... ] he transformed himself against it [ ... ... ] against it.
[§227]* However, He — the Divine Itself, the Father of Immeasurable [GAP-76: Love and Wisdom] — He also knew [GAP-77] of the impending assault, that it sought to disturb the [GAP-78: realm of] divine wisdom [GAP-79] yet in the providence of His [GAP-80] infinite [GAP-81] He transformed His own proceeding against it [GAP-82] to meet it and overcome it. *That is: the Infinite Divine, perceiving the assault from the hells, did not act by sheer omnipotence — which would have annihilated the freedom upon which all spiritual life depends — but transformed Himself, accommodating His power through wisdom, descending through mediating forms to engage evil on its own ground.*

[§228] Indeed, at that time [ ... ... ... ] go against [ ... ] God [ ... ]

[§229] [And fr]om its [essence] poured it out [ ... ] in the elements: in the smoke, the fire, the wind, the water and the Darkness. It came up from the [great] depth, it being set in the trees [ ... ... ] against it.
[§229]* [GAP-86: Fr]om its own [GAP-87] it poured itself out [GAP-88] into the fundamental evils: into the obscurity of understanding, the fire of self-love, the force of false persuasion, the flowing falsity, and the total privation. It rose up from the [GAP-89: immense] depth of hell, establishing itself within the false perceptions [GAP-90] arrayed against the Divine. *That is: evil diffused its nature through every mode of spiritual corruption and ascended from the deepest hells, taking root in every false perception as in trees growing upward to challenge heaven.*

[§230] Also, at the time when it generated itself [and] it formed the fruits, it still being set in them. They summoned the five garments, the sons of the Fir[st] Man.
[§230]* Also, at the time when evil generated itself further [GAP-91] it produced its characteristic works — still being established within those works as their animating will. Then the five exterior truths were summoned — the proceeding faculties of the Divine Human. *That is: when evil bore its fruits — its deeds and effects in the world — the Lord's response was to clothe Himself in five garments of truth, which are the divine qualities that descend into engagement with evil. These are the "sons" or emanating powers of the Divine Rational, each truth serving as armor and instrument of combat.*

[§231] And at the time when it generated its own self [f]rom the fruits through its offspring, they fell upon the earth and [drew] strength from the elements and were nourished by them. [ ... ... ] wickedness, together with cruelty [dwell] in the ruler, the leader of all the demons. He hum[bled] his worlds and his powers; he gathered them all into him. He made himself ready for the battle; he set himself to the war with that light earth, and the great King who dwells in it.
[§231]* And at the time when evil propagated its own nature from its works through its successive offspring, they descended onto the plane of natural existence and [GAP-92] drew sustenance and strength from the fundamental evils and were nourished by them. [GAP-93] wickedness, together with cruelty [GAP-94] resided in the ruling self-love, the chief of all infernal spirits. He subjugated his realms and his subordinate powers; he gathered them all into himself. He made himself ready for the spiritual combat; he set himself to the war against that realm of divine wisdom, and the great King — the Lord Himself — who dwells within it. *That is: all hells were drawn under a single ruling evil — the love of dominion from self-love — which consolidated every falsity and every malice into one infernal will, directed against the Divine kingdom and the Lord who is its life.*

[§232] At that very moment the First Man approached his garments, the gods [who sh]ine forth. He spoke with them, that he might entrust to them a [mystery] he [reve]aled to them and taught them about everything that [would come upon] them. He assumed them. He arranged them [ ... ... ] He draped them over one another upon his limbs [ ... ... ] danger, it [ ... ... ] the heights of the earth [ ... ... ] with all its powers [from the beg]inning, it being immeasurable [ ... ... ]
[§232]* At that very moment the Divine Human drew near to His exterior truths — the celestial intelligences who radiate the Divine light. He communed with them, that He might entrust to them a [GAP-95] sacred charge; He [reve]aled to them and instructed them concerning everything that [GAP-96] pertained to them. He assumed these truths into Himself. He arranged them in order [GAP-97]. He clothed them one upon another over His own divine faculties [GAP-98] against the peril; it [GAP-99] the exalted states of the celestial realm [GAP-100] with all its powers [GAP-101: from the beg]inning, it being without measure or limit [GAP-102]. *That is: the Lord, preparing for the descent into temptation-combat, took upon Himself successive layers of truth — each garment corresponding to a degree of accommodation by which the Infinite could engage the finite hells. He ordered these truths from inmost to outmost upon His own being, each faculty clothed and armed, so that the Divine life — immeasurable in itself — could enter the field of spiritual warfare against the whole consolidated power of evil.*

---

*Note on method:* Throughout this translation, "the First Man" is read as the Divine Human — the Lord's own Rational proceeding into combat with the hells, which is Swedenborg's spiritual sense of every narrative in which a divine figure descends to engage evil. The five dark elements correspond to the five genera of infernal falsity and evil love. The five garments correspond to the five degrees of divine truth by which the Lord clothed Himself for the work of redemption. The "land" in every instance is a spiritual state, not a place.

[§233] [...] a great ju[dgement,] now also a ti[me came] that they sculpt[ed him, and] he was revealed in the Father, the first established thing.
[§233]* [GAP-1] A great [GAP-2] act of divine discernment — and also a [GAP-3] spiritual state — in which [GAP-4] was formed — the Divine Wisdom was made manifest within the Divine Love, the first reality brought into spiritual existence.

It is possible to enumerate the particular truths of the natural mind through each complete state, [GAP-15] the innumerable truths of the entire spiritual universe; but the duration of the state that the Divine Wisdom spent within the Divine Love, [GAP-16: REVIEW the] first reality brought into being — one will not be able to make a reckoning of it.

When [GAP-17: REVIEW they had] need of her, she was summoned and proceeded forth from the Divine Love [GAP-18: REVIEW of the Infinite]. She beheld all the heavenly states of wisdom proceeding from the Divine.

[§239] This is how the Man spent a long time being anointed in the Mother, set fast in her inner storehouses [in quiet and silence]; from when the First Man went down to the contest, till the time when the [Statue (?) co]mes i[nto being,] this time [in which] he appeared [...]
[§239]* This is how the Celestial Human spent an extended state being invested with divine good within the Divine Truth of Life, established within her innermost degrees [GAP-26]; from when the Celestial Human descended into spiritual combat, until the state when the [fully realized divine form] comes [GAP-27] into being — this state [GAP-28] in which it was made manifest [GAP-29].

Yet, [at the] time when they were awaited, they were called and came forth.

The five divine faculties spent this entire duration being invested with divine good within the Celestial Human. Yet, [GAP-31: REVIEW at the] state when they were awaited, they were summoned and proceeded forth.

*The image of the bird resists full translation; my best reading is that it corresponds to a swift act of spiritual perception or thought — the instantaneity of understanding that belongs to Divine Truth when proceeding from the Divine.*

They were set before it, as it willed that [GAP-50]-ness [GAP-51] descend, that they would govern within them [GAP-52] one another. It ordered and distributed them [GAP-53] as its derivative powers. It divested itself of them [GAP-54] and [GAP-55] it girded and bound itself to [GAP-56] in a [GAP-57] state of stillness upon the boundary of the natural mind [GAP-58].

[§245] Again, he too, the First Man, spent a long period and [time] until he gave power to his sons; until he assu[m]ed them. After he came to wear them he stood fast in the heights between the borders. Again, he spent a duration and time like this, corresponding to such amount and measure as the raindrops and dew and falls of mist that come down in the universe. This is how he spent this duration also, standing between the borders, until the hour when he cast himself down to the contest!
[§245]* Again, the Celestial Human also spent an extended period and [GAP-59: REVIEW duration] until it communicated power to its derivative faculties; until it took them upon itself. After it came to be invested with them, it stood firm in the heights between the boundaries of the spiritual states. ### Summary of the Spiritual Sense

This chapter teaches that the Divine Love contains infinite states within itself — states of wisdom, of proceeding truth, of the celestial human form — all existing in potential within the innermost degrees of the Divine in peace and stillness, beyond all finite reckoning. No enumeration of particular truths in the natural mind, nor of knowledges in elevated states, nor of the contents of all containing forms in the spiritual universe, can approach the infinity of what is held within the Divine Love before it proceeds outward.

The Divine Truth of Life, the Celestial Human, and the five essential divine faculties each dwelt immeasurably within their origins before being summoned into active existence. Their being "invested with divine good" in stillness before proceeding into spiritual combat describes the infinite preparation within the Divine that precedes every act of redemption.

Correspondingly, Evil Desire — the proprium in its root — also unfolded through stages: from its fundamental constituents, into perverse perceptions, then into evil works, and finally into manifested evil affections across its infernal realms. When these evil affections perceived the realm of wisdom and its Sovereign, spiritual combat was provoked.

Five great spiritual states are then enumerated: the initial combat and mixing of good with evil; the ordering of all spiritual reality; the duration of that ordered state; the dissolution through consuming self-love; and the formation of the fully realized Divine Human, wherein wisdom returns to its proper state and evil is sealed in eternal restraint. A sixth encompasses the whole — from evil's first stirring to its final binding — which, being rooted in eternity on both sides, admits no finite measure.

[§265] The [Ambas]sador [came] from his mind; the Beloved of the Lights [from his thought]; the Mother of Life from his insight; [Jesus] the Beloved from his counsel; the Virgin of Lig[ht f]rom his consideration.
[§265]* The Divine Emissary of truth [GAP-1] proceeded from the Lord's inmost understanding; the Divine Love united with wisdom [GAP-2] proceeded from His interior rational faculty; the receptacle that gives birth to all spiritual life proceeded from His interior perception; [GAP-3] the Divine Love in human form proceeded from His providential deliberation; and pure innocence united with truth proceeded from His reflective contemplation.

[§266] So in this way were summone[d these five] fathers. These five advents came from the five limbs [of the Father]. One came from another and was manifested in its season.
[§266]* In this manner were called forth these five originating principles of Divine order. These five successive influxes of the Divine proceeded from the five essential faculties [GAP-4] of the Lord's own Being. Each proceeded from the one before it in discrete degrees, and each was made manifest according to its proper state of reception and the order of Divine providence.

---

**Translator's note on the series:** The five faculties — inmost understanding, rational thought, interior perception, deliberative wisdom, reflective contemplation — describe a descending series of discrete degrees within the Divine Mind itself. Each "emanation" is the form that Divine life assumes as it proceeds outward through these degrees. The passage teaches that every governing principle of spiritual order originates not from a separate being but from a distinct capacity within the one Divine Life, each unfolding from within the prior, as love passes through successive degrees of wisdom into manifestation. The natural language of "fathers" and "births" corresponds to the spiritual reality that each degree is an originating cause to the degree below it — fathering it, as it were — while remaining one life throughout.

[§267] The First [Man] is like a great distinguished man whose treasure has been taken away from him. He came to pursue his treasure. This is how the [First] Man too came after his sons from the heights, so that he might redeem them and retrieve his treasure, which had been scattered amongst his enemies.
[§267]* The primordial Divine Wisdom [GAP-1] is as a great and exalted good whose interior treasures of love and truth have been seized by the power of falsity from evil. This good went forth to reclaim what belonged to it. In the same manner the [GAP-2] Divine Wisdom descended after its own truths-from-good out of the celestial degree, so that it might liberate them and recover its own spiritual riches, which had been dispersed and scattered among the falsities and evils that opposed it.

[§268] [He is like a man] whose two sons were taken away from him by [the enemy. He] came out toward them to redeem them. This is how the First Man [yearns and] shines in the heights because of his two sons, who were separated from him. They are his living soul that exist in two powers, which makes four.
[§268]* [GAP-3: It is as a celestial good] whose two essential faculties — the will and the understanding — were seized from it by [GAP-4: the power of falsity and evil. This good] went forth toward them to redeem them. In the same manner the primordial Divine Wisdom [GAP-5: yearns and] shines in the celestial degree on account of its two faculties, which had been severed from their origin. These are its living spiritual life that subsists in two powers — the will toward good and the understanding of truth — which, having each an interior and an exterior aspect, become four.

And the Divine Proceeding within it is as a wise] ordering intelligence that adorns and sets in right order [GAP-8] all the truths that serve as protection and armament in the combat against falsity from evil.

[§270] The Living Spirit is like a combatant who might go towards a king's son who has been taken prisoner of war, and he redeems him from the hands of his enemies.
[§270]* The Divine Truth Proceeding is as a warrior who goes forth toward the offspring of the Divine Love itself — an interior good that has been taken captive by falsity through spiritual combat — and liberates it from the dominion of the opposing evils and falsities.

[§271] [The Amba]ssador too is like a great man [who goes forth] because of the treasure [in the] hand of his prisoners [so as to redeem] these light powers [and to bring them together with the] sons and their soul, so that they would [be set free, and that the soul might] be brought forth in the land wherein it was imprisoned.
[§271]* The Divine Mediation likewise is as an exalted good [GAP-9: that goes forth] on account of the spiritual riches [GAP-10: held in the] power of those in bondage to evil [GAP-11: so as to recover] these faculties of wisdom and perception [GAP-12: together with the] truths-from-good and their living spiritual life, so that they would [GAP-13: be freed, and that living good might] be brought forth from the state of captivity in which it had been confined.

[§272] Five forms exist in his body, corresponding to (the archetype) of the five creations that exist in the five worlds of darkness.
[§272]* Five genera of evil subsist within the constitution of this sovereign falsity, corresponding to the archetypal pattern of the five origins of evil and falsity that subsist within the five fundamental states of opposition to the Divine.

[§273] Indeed, his head is lion-faced, which came about from the world of fire. His wings and his shoulders are eagle-faced, after the likeness of the children of the wind. His hands and his feet are daemons, after the likeness of the children of the world of smoke. His belly is dragon-faced, after the likeness of the world of darkness. His tail is the form of the fish, which belongs to the world of the children of water. These five shapes are in him, and they came from the five creations of the five worlds of darkness.
[§273]* Indeed, its ruling love is the love of dominion from self-love, which arose from the state of infernal love. Its power of thought and its capacity to elevate itself are the faculty of reasoning from falsity, after the likeness of the offspring of persuasion. Its power to act and its capacity to advance into outmost effects are evil affections, after the likeness of the offspring of the state of confused obscurity. Its interior appetites are sensual reasoning against truth, after the likeness of the state of complete privation of good. Its lowest and ultimate expression is perverted natural knowledge, which belongs to the state of the offspring of falsity itself. These five genera of evil are in it, and they arose from the five origins of evil within the five fundamental states of opposition to the Divine.

Also, should he wish, he shall plunge down to the waters like the children of water. Again, should he wish, he shall crawl on his belly like the children of darkness. These five shapes are in him.

[§281] Also, when he wishes, he shall send out frost, and his entire body freezes like a [mountain of] snow.

[§282] The third: Whenever his powers are stood before him, he shall look at them and understand; for [whatever ex]ists in their heart he shall understand, what is in their heart and their face so long as they are stood before him. When they retreat before him and distance themselves from him, he shall not understand what is in their heart. There is no life in him whatsoever, but his 'life' is the bile of anger that is apparent on his face and in his fear. Shall a [power behold him, it is] destruction that is establ[ished be]fore him.
[§282]* The third: Whenever its subordinate forces stand in its presence, it shall observe them and perceive their states; for whatever exists within their interior will it shall perceive — what is in their will and their outward manifestation, so long as they stand within its presence. When they withdraw from it and distance themselves from its sphere, it shall not perceive what is within their will. There is no genuine spiritual life in it whatsoever, but its semblance of "life" is the agitation of self-love that is apparent in its outward aspect and in the terror it inspires. **A note on the overall form:** This chapter describes the anatomy of the sovereign evil — the ruling falsity from self-love in its fullest organization. The "body" is not a body but the total constitution of evil will organized into five genera, each corresponding to a distinct mode of spiritual opposition: infernal love (dominion), persuasive reasoning (falsity elevated), confused evil (obscure action), sensual appetite (privation made active), and perverted natural knowledge (falsity in ultimates). The three additional qualities describe its capacity for concealment and deception, its imperviousness to truth, and its hardened will. The final three describe how it can inflame with self-love, extinguish all charity, and perceive subordinate evils — but only externally, through presence, never interiorly. It has no life from the Divine; its animation is solely the reactive force of rage and self-love simulating vitality.

[§283] Twelve judges exist; the great mighty givers of judgement! They were sent and came from the exalted greatness to this place. They were assigned to different works according to the command that the Judge, who belongs to the greatness, entrusted to all these judges. Now he, the great Judge, he who transcends all the judges, who exists and is established in the aeons of greatness; he is the Father of Greatness.
[§283]* Twelve principles of divine discernment exist — great and sovereign orderings of spiritual judgment. They proceeded forth from the Infinite Divine itself into the created spiritual world. Each was appointed to a distinct work of ordering according to the mandate that the one supreme Discernment, which belongs to the Divine itself, entrusted to all these subordinate orderings. Now that supreme Discernment, which transcends every particular act of judgment, which exists and is established in the infinite states of the Divine — that is the Divine Love itself, the origin of all things.

[§284] The first judge is the First Man, he who humiliated the ruler of Darkness in the beginning. He judged him according to a judgement of righteousness; for he had exalted himself against a kingdom that was not his own.
[§284]* The first principle of discernment is the Primal Good sent forth, which overcame the ruling persuasion of falsity from evil in the beginning. It judged that falsity according to a judgment of divine order, for falsity from evil had exalted itself against a dominion that did not belong to it — seeking to usurp governance over the realm of good and truth.

[§285] The second judge is the Living Spirit, he who came and bore up the Man. He too made a judgement on all the powers of Darkness; which had wounded the garments of the First Man.
[§285]* The second principle of discernment is the Divine Truth proceeding, which came and raised up the Primal Good from its subjugation. It likewise rendered judgment upon all the powers of falsity from evil, which had damaged and torn the external truths that clothed the Primal Good.

[§286] The third judge is the Mother of Life, the Great Spirit, she who beautified and set up the heavenly part of the universe, according to her pleasure; which [pleased] her. She judged the rulers above; she fettered and set them firmly in the place that is fitting for them.
[§286]* The third principle of discernment is the Divine Proceeding that gives life — the Great Influx — she who adorned and established the celestial degree of the spiritual world according to her own will, which [GAP-1] her. She judged the false persuasions dwelling in the higher rational; she restrained them and fixed them firmly in the state that properly contains them.

[§287] The fourth judge is the Great Builder, he who built the new aeon of joy. He judged, according to a judgement of righteousness, the storehouses of the enemy; that death may not well up from this time on. He has constructed a prison for the enemy. Also, upon the crown of the building he has constructed a throne for the First Man and all the fathers of light; they who engaged the struggle with the evil one and were victorious over him.
[§287]* The fourth principle of discernment is the Great Ordering Wisdom, which constructed the new state of celestial blessedness. It judged, according to a judgment of divine order, the repositories of evil — so that spiritual death may no longer well up from that time forward. It has built a hell to confine evil permanently. Also, at the inmost summit of this new order, it has established supreme authority for the Primal Good and for all the originating principles of wisdom — they who engaged in the struggle against evil and prevailed over it.

[§288] The fifth judge is the great King of Honour, he who dwells and is established in the seventh firmament. He is the judge of all the firmaments who gives a true judgement, according to a judgement of righteousness, upon all the powers and all the kingdoms of the firmaments.
[§288]* The fifth principle of discernment is the great Sovereignty of Divine Glory, which dwells and is established in the inmost degree of the rational mind. It is the discernment governing all degrees of the understanding, rendering true judgment according to divine order upon all the powers and all the dominions within the successive levels of the rational faculty.

[§289] The sixth judge is [the Th]ird Ambassador, he who came and displayed his image. He purified the light [from the mixture] which his own [image released] also of the enemy, he has [judged them]
[§289]* The sixth principle of discernment is [the Th]ird Manifestation of Divine Truth Going Forth, which came and displayed its own form openly. It purified wisdom [GAP-2] which its own [GAP-3] — the purification extending even to what belonged to falsity from evil — it has [GAP-4] separated and reclaimed what was genuine from what was corrupt.

[§290] The seventh judge is [the Column of Glory, he who judges] the worlds [and all in them; as he gives the victory to] they who are steadfast; but he condemns the ones who are convicted [in their sin. But for the righteous] he shall become for them the steps of the way (?) [of life]; but the convicted he sweeps and casts out to the depths.
[§290]* The seventh principle of discernment is [GAP-5] the states of spiritual life [GAP-6 — as it grants confirmation in good to] those who are steadfast in truth; but it condemns those who are confirmed in evil [GAP-7]. It becomes for those progressing in good the successive degrees of the path of regeneration [GAP-8]; but those confirmed in evil it sweeps away and casts out into the hells.

[§291] The eighth judge is the Splendour, the father of all the apostles; because after the Ambassador had displayed his image and the light was taken [from them] a mass of rulers rose up from above and below. Then they sent the Splendour. He came [ ... ... ] he descended and made a judgement in the firmaments. He separated the steadfast from the convicted.
[§291]* The eighth principle of discernment is the Divine Wisdom made radiant — the origin of all truths sent forth into the world — because after the Manifestation of Divine Truth had displayed its form and wisdom was received [GAP-9], a great uprising of false persuasions arose from the interior and exterior degrees alike. Then the Divine Wisdom Itself was sent forth. It came [GAP-10], it descended through all degrees of the understanding and rendered judgment within them. It separated those confirmed in good from those confirmed in evil.

[§292] The ninth judge is the Virgin of Light, [she who took the heart] of the powers by her image, gathering her own ones into her. She makes a judgement on the ruler of the moist, and the ruler of the dry.
[§292]* The ninth principle of discernment is the Pure Affection for Truth, [GAP-11: REVIEW — she who captivated the interiors] of the powers of falsity through her own manifest beauty, gathering those things genuinely her own back into herself. She renders judgment upon the falsity that corrupts truth, and upon the evil that is barren of all truth.

[§293] The tenth judge is the Judge who dwells in the atmosphere, judging all mankind. He makes a separation between they who are good and they who are evil, setting apart the righteous from the sinners.
[§293]* The tenth principle of discernment is the Judgment that operates in the intermediate state between the interior and the exterior — the world of spirits — discerning all who have lived in the natural world. It makes a separation between those who are in good and those who are in evil, setting apart the regenerate from those who have rejected regeneration.

[§294] The eleventh judge is Wahman, he who shall come and appear in the world. And he unveils [all that is hidden] and separates light from darkness, and sets the truth apart from lawlessness.
[§294]* The eleventh principle of discernment is the Divine Truth entering the understanding — the enlightenment that comes and appears within the mind of the person in the world. And it unveils [GAP-12] what was hidden, and separates wisdom from falsity, and sets genuine order apart from the disorder of self-will.

[§295] The twelfth judge is the great counsel, which is the summons and the obedience, which is appointed in the elements. It is the one that makes a separation between the good and the evil ones. At the end also it can gather itself together and sculpt its own self in the Last Statue. And it separates light from darkness.
[§295]* The twelfth principle of discernment is the great Divine Providence, which is at once the calling forth and the willing response, which is appointed within the foundational principles of all spiritual life. It is the one that makes a final separation between good and evil. At the end also, it gathers all that has been scattered into unity and forms the completed image of the regenerated human in its final wholeness. And it separates wisdom from falsity forever.

[§296] These are the twelve great and mighty judges who were sent. They came from the great Judge, he who transcends all the judges. He is the Father that is hidden and immeasurable, the one who exists; who is established in the aeons of greatness upon the great throne of honour, which is the King of Honour [who is established] in his kingdom.
[§296]* These are the twelve great and sovereign principles of divine discernment that were sent forth. They came from the one supreme Discernment, which transcends all particular judgments. That is the Divine Love which is hidden and immeasurable — the One who Is — who is established in the infinite states of the Divine upon the supreme state of governance and glory, which is the Sovereignty of Divine Glory [GAP-13] within His own dominion.

[§297] Eighteen [great thrones for all the fathers] exist and are appointed, which are established in eighteen places! While there are nine among them [appointed in] the outer aeons, they exist [i]n all the worlds of the damage.
[§297]* Eighteen [GAP-1: governing principles of authority over all successive states of the Lord's kingdom] exist and are appointed, established in eighteen distinct degrees of spiritual life. Nine among them [GAP-2: are ordained within] the interior heavenly states, while nine exist throughout the states where spiritual life has suffered injury and corruption.

[§298] The first throne is the throne of the Father, the God of [trut]h, the King of the aeons of greatness, the one who exists; who is established [in himself] he alone, in his own essen[c]e, [dwelling] apart. This is the first throne [of all] the one that surpasses all the thrones.
[§298]* The first governing principle is the sovereignty of the Divine Itself, the God of divine truth, the supreme dominion over all interior states of celestial life — the One who Is; who is established [GAP-3: in absolute self-subsistence], He alone, in His own essence, [GAP-4: dwelling in unapproachable infinity] apart from all that proceeds from Him. This is the first governing principle [GAP-5: of all spiritual order], the one that surpasses every other sovereignty.

[§299] The second throne is that of the [gl]orious Mother of Life. She is the origin of all the emanations that have come to this world.
