[§299]* The second governing principle is that of the Divine Love that gives life — the glorified origin of all influx that has ever proceeded into the states of this world's spiritual existence.

[§300] The third throne is constructed for the father, the First Man. He is the one who humiliated the entire first enmity, which existed in the worlds of Darkness.
[§300]* The third governing principle is established for the most interior celestial state — the primordial human will united to the Divine. This is the state that humbled and overcame the entire original opposition of falsity from evil, which had dominion in the realms of spiritual darkness.

[§301] The fourth throne is that of the Beloved of the Lights, the great honoured Beloved of the aeons of greatness.
[§301]* The fourth governing principle belongs to the Divine Love cherished within all illumination — the great and honored affection of good within the interior states of celestial life.

[§302] The fifth throne is that of the glorious Great Builder; the great architect who built the new aeon for a newness, for [a resting place] for [the] fathers of light; also for a place of binding and a prison for the enemy and his powers.
[§302]* The fifth governing principle is that of the Divine Ordering Intelligence — the great architect of providence who constructed a new state of spiritual life as a renewal, as [GAP-6: a dwelling place of good] for [GAP-7: all] the originating states of wisdom; and also as a means of restraint and confinement for the power of evil and all its derivative falsities.

[§303] [The sixth thro]n[e] is that of the Living Spirit, the [glorious] mighty one, he who bore up the First Man out from the [land of Darkness]; who also set in order and constructed the world [s ...] both for a cleansing of the light, [and] for a binding [of the] powers of the enmity.
[§303]* The sixth governing principle is that of the Divine Operative Power — the [GAP-8: glorified] mighty agency that raised up the most interior celestial state out of the [GAP-9: depths of falsity and evil]; who also set in order and constructed the external states of spiritual life [GAP-10: as an arrangement] both for a purification of wisdom from its contaminations, [GAP-11: and] for a binding [GAP-12: of the] powers of opposition and hostility.

[§304] The seventh th[rone is] that of the Third Ambassador, the king of the zone, also the master of all advices.
[§304]* The seventh governing principle is that of the Divine Mediating Principle — the sovereign of the intermediate sphere, and the master of all counsels of wisdom by which the interiors communicate with the exteriors.

[§305] The eighth throne is that of the Splendour, who [is] the releaser and redeemer [of] all souls.
[§305]* The eighth governing principle is that of Divine Truth in its radiance, who [GAP-13: is] the liberator and redeemer [GAP-14: of] all the interiors of the human mind.

[§306] The ninth throne is the throne of the great M[ind, he to whom] all the churches shall gather; and all the [life] that will be purified from the world returns to him.
[§306]* The ninth governing principle is that of the Divine Understanding — the universal intelligence to whom all forms of the church shall be gathered; and all the [GAP-15: spiritual life] that will be purified from the natural state returns to this intelligence as to its source.

[§307] These are the nine thrones of ho[no]u[r] appointed in the outer aeo[ns], <which are established> for the fathers of the glorious realm; [in the kingdom of the King of] Honour.
[§307]* These are the nine governing principles of the celestial order, ordained in the interior heavenly states and established for the originating degrees of the Lord's glorified kingdom — [GAP-16: within the dominion of the sovereignty of] divine honor.

[§308] In contrast, these are the other nine thrones that are appointed in this world of the zone.
[§308]* In contrast, these are the other nine governing principles that are ordained and operative within this intermediate sphere of spiritual conflict.

[§309] Three thrones in the ship of [the d]ay: one is that of the Ambassador, the second i[s that of] the Great Spirit, the third is that of the Living Spirit.
[§309]* Three governing principles reside within the doctrine of enlightenment: one belongs to the Divine Mediating Principle, the second to the Divine Operative Power, the third to the Divine Power that rescues and orders.

[§310] Once again, there are another three in the ship that [belongs to the night]: the first is the throne of the Splendour, [the second is that of] the First Man, the third is tha[t of the Virgin of] Light. These six thrones are established in the two ships.
[§310]* Again, there are another three within the doctrine that [GAP-17: operates in states of spiritual obscurity]: the first is the sovereignty of Divine Truth in its radiance, [GAP-18: the second is that of] the most interior celestial will, the third is that of the pure affection of truth in its innocence. These six governing principles are established within the two doctrinal vessels — the one that carries the mind through illuminated states and the one that carries the mind through states of darkness.

[§311] Also, the seventh throne is established in the seventh firmament. The great King of Honour sits upon it.
[§311]* The seventh governing principle is established in the highest degree of the rational mind — the interior firmament that separates heavenly perception from natural thought. The great sovereignty of divine honor presides upon it.

[§312] The eighth throne is established in the atmosphere. The Judge of truth sits upon it, he who judges all mankind. Three paths shall be distinguished before him: one to death, one to life, on[e] to the mingling.
[§312]* The eighth governing principle is established in the external natural mind. The Divine Truth that judges sits upon it — the truth that discriminates in all human willing and thinking. Three ways of life are distinguished before this judgment: one leading to spiritual death, one leading to spiritual life, and one leading to the mixed state where good and evil remain intermingled and undetermined.

[§313] The ninth throne appointed and established is for the [a]postle.
[§313]* The ninth governing principle, ordained and established in this sphere, belongs to the one sent forth — the apostolic function of divine teaching that enters directly into natural life.

[§314] Th[ese] are the [nin]e great [th]ro[nes of honour] that are established in this world of [the zone] of the glorious realm, the one that the [... ...] honour them [... ...] and the [...].
[§314]* These are the nine great governing [GAP-19: principles of spiritual authority] that are established in this sphere of [GAP-20: spiritual conflict and restoration, mirroring the interior order] of the Lord's glorified kingdom, the one that the [GAP-21: successive states of regeneration] honor [GAP-22: through reception of influx from the interior degrees] and the [GAP-23: conjunction of good and truth in the external life of the church].

[§315] when the Living Spirit came he brought the three garments; that of wind, that of fire, and that of water. He assumed them from [the heights] he has [clothed himself] in them in those three garments [and through them] he established [them] for all the things of [this univers]e, both above and below. He arranged them in the [form in which] he was displayed. Afterwards [he completed] his works [and] the [image was revealed, and]e stripped them off before [the ri]ghteous ones.

[§316] Now, when he was about to loose [and release them,] divested himself of them from their (clasp) and he [was no more]n them, and thus these three g[arme]nts are arranged [and extend] from below the edges of the zone as they clothe i[t al]l [reaching] up [to what is] above [and reaching] up [to what is] above them strips them of the zone from that place at its topmost point; and it is placed below them and ra[ised u]p to the heights above, from which it had been sent o[u]t at their beginning.

[§315–§316]* ## ¶1

When the living Divine influx descended from the Lord, it bore with it three coverings of external truth: the truth of rational understanding, the truth of love's activity, and the truth of natural knowledge. The Divine appropriated these coverings from [GAP-1] the celestial origin; it [GAP-2] clothed itself within them, within those three external truths, [GAP-3] so that the Divine might operate through successive degrees. It set into Divine order [GAP-4: REVIEW — these truths as governing forms] for all states of existence both interior and exterior, celestial and natural. It disposed them according to [GAP-5] the form through which the Divine was made manifest. Thereafter, [GAP-6] when the Divine purpose was accomplished, its goods of use [GAP-7] and the [GAP-8] interior reality was laid bare — it divested itself of these external coverings before those who live in the good of charity.

## ¶2

Now, when the Divine influx was about to release [GAP-9: these external truths from their fixed containment, it] separated itself from them by dissolving their binding conjunction, and it [GAP-10: withdrew its presence from] within them, and thus these three coverings of external truth are disposed in order [GAP-11] extending from beneath the outermost boundary of the containing sphere, inasmuch as they encompass all states [GAP-12] ascending [GAP-13] toward what is interior [GAP-14] ascending [GAP-15] — what is inmost and above them strips away the containing boundary from its highest and most interior degree; and what had descended into the lowest natural state is received beneath these coverings and elevated back to the celestial heights from which the Divine had originally proceeded at the very beginning of all influx.

---

### Interpretive Note

The chapter describes a complete arc of Divine operation: the Lord clothes Himself in successive external truths — rational thought, love's warmth, factual knowledge — in order to establish order through all degrees of creation. Having accomplished this ordering, He reveals the interior Divine reality to the regenerate by removing the external coverings. The "zone" (containing boundary) corresponds to the limiting sphere that separates interior states from exterior — what Swedenborg calls the *limbus* or boundary between the spiritual and natural degrees. The garments descend so that all things may be set in order; then they are removed and what had descended is elevated back to its celestial origin, completing the circuit of influx and return that constitutes all Divine providence in created states.

[§317] In which limb of [the liv]i[ng] sou[l] did [the] summons descend to the Man, who exists in the [a]eon?
[§317]* Through which faculty of the spiritual life [GAP-1] did the Divine awakening descend into the Divine Human, who dwells in the eternal spiritual state?

[§318] Indeed, the First Man dispersed himself and he [was in every] limb of the living soul [and every part] of the living soul; he has [suffered and] he did not know [... ...] but the blessed, glorious Man came [in] secret; in his image; in his shape; in his love; in his holy virgin, she who is the virgin of light, the soul of the father.
[§318]* Indeed, the Primordial Divine Human distributed His own life, and He [GAP-2] became present in every faculty of the spiritual life [GAP-3] of that spiritual life itself; He has [GAP-4] yet He did not perceive [GAP-5] — but the blessed and glorious Divine Human came [GAP-6] by an interior and hidden way: in His own likeness; in His own form; in His own love; in His holy Divine Truth, she who is the truth proceeding from wisdom, the inmost life of the Divine Good itself.

[§319] After the fashion of this fleshly body: as the root of all the limbs hang upon the head, so that should one of the person's limbs be cut off, while the head exists he has hope for [healing] but if his head should be cut off, the entire body will die and he is lost.
[§319]* After the manner of the natural body of the external man: as the root of all the subordinate affections and faculties depends upon the ruling love, so that if one particular affection should be severed from a person, while the ruling love remains he retains the hope of [GAP-7] restoration — but if his ruling love itself should be destroyed, the entire spiritual life perishes and he is lost.

[§320] This is also the case for the Firs[t] Ma[n]. He is the head, while his sons attach to it the limbs [of] his soul. Or, conversely, like the living air, on which all flesh entirely lives as it breathes and [lives] therein. His head is placed on the body of the Pillar of Glory in the heights of the universe.
[§320]* So it is also with the Primordial Divine Human. He is the ruling love, while the goods and truths proceeding from Him attach to it as the derivative faculties [GAP-8] of His spiritual life. Or, understood conversely, He is like the Divine influx itself, upon which all natural and corporeal life wholly depends as it receives and [GAP-9] lives by that influx. His governing principle is set upon the ordered form of the Divine Proceeding through all the degrees of the universal spiritual world, from the highest heaven to the lowest.

[§321] [This i]s also the case [for] the father, the blessed Man; as he is like the head that is upon the body and the air that upon the Pillar is set i[n every place] being made strong by his light virgin, [she] who is his soul that he clothes [with light]
[§321]* So it is also [GAP-10] with the Divine Good, the blessed Divine Human; for He is like the ruling love that governs the whole external life, and like the Divine influx that upon the Divine Order is established i[GAP-11]n every degree — being made strong by His luminous Divine Truth, [GAP-12] who is His inmost life, which He invests with external truths [GAP-13] as garments.

[§322] [For there are] t[h]ose [five]: the mind, thought, insight, counsel and c[on]sideration that he produced and sent forth from hi[m to] do his will. He sprang and travelled behind them. [These are the limbs] of his living soul, which is entwined among the rebels; as they are like the limbs of its body, and [are spread through the] universe.
[§322]* [GAP-14] These [GAP-15] are the interior faculties: understanding, reflection, interior perception, deliberation, and discernment — which He brought forth and sent out from Himself to accomplish His Divine purpose. He Himself arose and followed after them. [GAP-16] These are the faculties of His own spiritual life, which had become entangled among the evils and falsities of the rebellious natural will; for they are like the members of its embodied form, and [GAP-17] they extend throughout the whole spiritual world.

[§323] And when they were sent, at [that ti]m[e] he was found with the Virgin of [Lig]ht and [he] stood up, asking and entreating for a p[o]wer. [And the Father] gave him peace and a ki[ss of love and] he [gave] him goo[d] tidings [concerning the] Man. The Man him[s]elf gave [his power to] his limbs and gathered his soul i[nto one and] he built it in its place like this tower [of light]
[§323]* And when those faculties had been sent forth, at that very moment He was found in conjunction with Divine Truth, and [GAP-18] He arose, seeking and imploring for the Divine power from above. [GAP-19] The Divine Good gave Him peace and the conjunction [GAP-20] of good with truth — He [GAP-21] communicated to Him the joyful perception [GAP-22] of the restored Divine Human. The Divine Human Himself then gave [GAP-23] His own vital influx to His derivative faculties, and gathered His spiritual life i[GAP-24]nto one — He built it in its proper order like a structure of truth raised upon truth [GAP-25], an organized habitation of wisdom.

[§324] [And he] shaped it and beautified it skillfully [and he] for[med it anew and] the voices that he sent [forth that] he might sink in and quench [the fire and restore the] so[u]l that was crushed by the enemy. They were gathered in. They came, set firm once more, in the image of their father.
[§324]* [GAP-26] He ordered it and perfected it with Divine skill [GAP-27] — forming [GAP-28] it anew — and the Divine truths that He sent forth [GAP-29] penetrated inward to subdue and extinguish [GAP-30] every falsity, restoring the spiritual life that had been crushed and subjugated by evil. All the scattered faculties were gathered back into unity. They returned, and were established once more in permanence, reformed according to the likeness of the Divine Good from which they had first proceeded.

[§325] The Living Spirit has done seven [wo]rks with his strength.
[§325]* The Divine Power proceeding from the Lord's love and wisdom accomplished seven complete operations of spiritual regeneration through its omnipotence.

[§326] [The fi]rst: He brought the First Man up from the contest, the way a pearl is [brought] up from the sea.
[§326]* The first: It raised up the celestial state of innocence out of the combat of spiritual temptation, as a precious interior truth of faith is [GAP-1] drawn up out of the overwhelming mass of merely natural knowledge and falsity.

[§327] [The second: He spread about the one who had rebelled, he crucified][h]im in every body.
[§327]* [GAP-2: The second: It laid open and exposed the self-love that had turned against divine order, and rendered it powerless] throughout every external form and state of the natural mind.

[§328] The third: He [tr]ampled, piled up, and pierced the essences of death.
[§328]* The third: It subjugated, confined, and penetrated the inmost root-principles of spiritual death — evil and falsity in their very essences — so that they could no longer operate freely.

[§329] [The f]ourth: He constructed the ships of light.
[§329]* The fourth: It formed the celestial and spiritual vessels that carry divine wisdom and truth through all the successive states of the mind.

[§330] The fi[f]th: He evoked his five sons, and distributed them about. They [took] possession of the zone, they bore up under all the universe's weight. Also, he evoked three living words. He set [them] upon the th[re]e vessels, another one upon the giant. [Again, he evoked the] summons, that it might be mixed with the five shining men.
[§330]* The fifth: It called forth its five essential faculties of spiritual perception and distributed them throughout the ordered arrangement of the regenerating mind. They [GAP-3] assumed governance over that ordered structure, and they sustained the entire burden of maintaining spiritual equilibrium. Further, it called forth three essential divine truths proceeding as living utterance. It set [GAP-4] these upon the three containing forms that receive and convey those truths, and another upon the great sustaining spiritual power. [GAP-5: Further, it called forth] the divine invitation to return, so that it might be conjoined with the five illuminated capacities of interior perception.

[§331] The sixth: When he had constructed the zone he aro[se] and s[e]nt forth a multitude of powers and many [angel]s; so that they would bound the circuit of the z[o]ne on every si[de], un[t]il he finished [co]nstructing the works. And at the end [of] all the [wor]ks he to[ok s]ome of them into his storehouses, and he appointed others at the watch-posts.
[§331]* The sixth: When it had established the ordered arrangement of spiritual states, it arose and sent forth a multitude of spiritual agencies and many truths vivified by good, so that they would guard and complete the boundary of that spiritual order on every side, until it had finished constructing all the operations of regeneration. And at the completion [GAP-6] of all those spiritual works, it gathered some of those agencies into the interior repositories of good and truth within the will and understanding, and it appointed others to states of perpetual spiritual vigilance.

[§332] The [sev]enth: A[t the] time when the Ambass[ador dis]pl[aye]d his glorious imag[e], the L[iving] Spirit [established] many [go]ds and angels. They [ ... ] Th[ey to]ok hold of it, that the entire building [might] not [ ... / ... ] one another against [the] light, which he had [spread]
[§332]* The seventh: At the time when the Divine Emissary manifested the beauty of the celestial human form, the Divine Power proceeding from love and wisdom [GAP-7] ordained many governing goods and ministering truths. They [GAP-8: …] They took hold of that form, so that the entire spiritual edifice of the regenerate mind [GAP-9: …] not [GAP-10: …] one another against [GAP-11] the wisdom, which it had [GAP-12: …]

*Note on ¶8: The gaps resist confident reading, but the spiritual sense requires that in this seventh work the whole structure of the regenerated mind is secured — goods and truths are stationed so that nothing within the edifice can turn against the divine wisdom that pervades it. GAP-8 through GAP-12 likely describe the agencies grasping and sustaining the spiritual form to prevent any relapse of the mind's faculties into opposition against that wisdom.*

[§333] Also another gr[ea]t and glorious work he will enact at the e[nd] is [the] Last [Statue], which he will bring up to the aeons of [light]; and he [goes] in, and [he ta]kes hold, and he smites [the darkness], and [he] builds, and he [ ... ], and he [gathers]hem.
[§333]* Also, another great and glorious operation it will accomplish at the final state of regeneration: it will bring forth [GAP-13] the ultimate perfected [GAP-14] spiritual human form and raise it up into the celestial states of [GAP-15] divine wisdom and love. And it [GAP-16: …] enters in, and it takes hold, and it strikes down [GAP-17: …] all remaining resistance, and [GAP-18] it builds, and it [GAP-19: …], and it [GAP-20: …] brings all things into their final heavenly order.

*Note on ¶9: The "Last Statue" — the ultimate perfected form — corresponds to the fully regenerated human being in its complete spiritual body, elevated into the inmost heaven. The sequence of verbs (entering, grasping, smiting, building) describes the final acts of the Lord's power: entering the inmost will, taking hold of the whole person, destroying what remains of hereditary evil, and constructing the eternal spiritual form. The gaps likely name the specific evils destroyed and the specific goods established in this consummation.*

[§334] The Living Spirit constructed five great things from veins and skull[s in the] hard [bodies].
[§334]* The Lord's operative wisdom — the Divine proceeding as active spiritual power — established five essential structural orderings of the spiritual world out of the channels through which interior life flows and out of the outermost intellectual containments within the most resistant [GAP-1: dense and unyielding natural states of the external mind].

[§335] The first is the disc that is placed upon [the sh]oulder of the Porter.
[§335]* The first is the foundational ground of natural truth that rests upon the sustaining power of the Divine principle that upholds all things.

[§336] The se[con]d are the arches of [the] pillar that is before him.
[§336]* The second is the spanning connective truths of [GAP-2: the] fundamental supporting doctrine that stands in the presence of that sustaining power.

[§337] [The th]ird is the [mou]ld of the fo[r]m for the gehennas.
[§337]* The third is the determining pattern that gives boundary and fixed form to the hells — the ordering structure imposed upon states of evil and falsity so that they cannot spread beyond their allotted limits.

[§338] The fourth are the [seven] pillars that he has erected in the great sea, in [the] seven parts of the universe.
[§338]* The fourth is the [GAP-3: fullness of] supporting truths that the Lord has established within the vast general plane of natural knowledge, throughout [GAP-4: the] complete extent of all spiritual states in their ordered provinces.

[§339] The fifth are the four walls that surround the vessels.
[§339]* The fifth is the fourfold enclosure of protecting truths that surrounds on every side the recipient forms of the mind — the containing structures through which good and truth are received and held.

---
*Note:* The overall passage describes how the Lord's active wisdom constructed the entire spiritual architecture — the ground that supports, the truths that connect, the boundaries that constrain hell, the doctrines that sustain throughout all states, and the protective enclosures around every vessel of reception.

[§340] The Ambassador performed ten works by his advent.
[§340]* Divine truth in its emissary function accomplished ten complete spiritual operations by its coming into the mind.

[§341] The first: The Great Builder came forth to build the new aeon.
[§341]* The first: The ordering power of Divine Wisdom came forth to construct a new interior state — a new dispensation of the spiritual mind.

[§342] The second: The ships moved through the heights in the heavens.
[§342]* The second: The containing forms of celestial good and spiritual truth moved through the highest interior degrees of understanding — love and faith in their pure vessels began to operate through the inmost heavens of the mind.

[§343] The third: The wheels begin to send the life to earth and the upper worlds.
[§343]* The third: The cyclical operations of providence began to transmit spiritual life downward into the natural mind and upward into the celestial degrees.

[§344] The fourth: [The zodiac was set in order]
[§344]* The fourth: [GAP-1: ...] *(The spiritual sequence — building, conveying, circulating — requires here an operation concerning the ordering or arrangement of these transmissions; most likely the establishment of distinct degrees or the fixing of the forms through which influx proceeds.)*

[§345] The fifth is the doors of the ships, that they were opened up [to receive the light]
[§345]* The fifth concerns the receiving capacities of the vessels of good and truth — that they were opened [GAP-2: ...] — the interior forms by which love and faith carry redeemed life were made accessible, so that what had been held captive within falsity could be drawn upward.

[§346] The sixth is the doors too [of the depths below, that they were opened to receive the waste]
[§346]* The sixth concerns the receiving capacities likewise [GAP-3: ... ...that they were opened to take in what is spiritually rejected] — the same containing forms were opened on their lower aspect to receive and remove spent falsities and consumed evils from the interiors of the mind.

[§347] The seventh is [the sin], which he had separated from the rulers by cause of the Third Ambassador; it fell upon the earth.
[§347]* The seventh is [GAP-4: REVIEW — the separated evil], which had been distinguished and cast out from the governing affections by the operation of divine truth; it precipitated downward into the natural and external degree of the mind. *(The spiritual sense requires that what the ruling loves had harbored interiorly — hidden evil — was now exposed and exteriorized, falling into the outermost plane where it could be recognized and confronted.)*

[§348] The eighth: Night and day were separated [from one another by the turning of the sphere]
[§348]* The eighth: States of spiritual obscurity and states of spiritual illumination were distinguished from one another [GAP-5: ... ...] *(The separation of night and day corresponds to the mind's newfound ability to discern falsity from truth, darkness of ignorance from the light of understanding — likely the gap specifies the manner or further effect of this discernment.)*

[§349] The ninth: The abortions [fell] to the ground.
[§349]* The ninth: The malformed and abortive conceptions of spiritual life — those quasi-goods and distorted affections that evil produces when exposed to the light of truth — [GAP-6: REVIEW — precipitated] into the natural plane of the mind. *(These are not true births of the spirit but monstrous forms generated when the governing evils, confronted by divine truth, release what they cannot hold; they fall into the external mind as recognizable distortions.)*

[§350] The tenth: Transmigrations came about in the zone.
[§350]* The tenth: Successive changes of state and transfers of affection came about throughout the natural degree of life — the external mind entered into the condition of continual alternation, where loves pass through form after form, unable to rest until they are either elevated toward interior good or dissipated.

---

**Structural note:** The ten operations describe a complete sequence of spiritual regeneration viewed from the side of influx: first the new state is established (1), then the vessels of good and truth begin operating (2–3), the cycles of providence are set in motion (4), the interior forms are opened both to receive redeemed life upward (5) and to expel falsity downward (6), hidden evil is exteriorized (7), truth and falsity are distinguished (8), malformed spiritual productions fall to the natural plane (9), and finally the natural mind enters into the condition of successive states — the perpetual alternation that characterizes life in the external degree before full regeneration (10).

[§351] At the time when the Ambassador displayed his image, four great works were revealed that are very excellent and outstanding.
[§351]* At the time when the Divine Emissary made perceptible the form of Divine Wisdom to the understanding, four complete spiritual operations were disclosed, each surpassing in excellence and eminence all that had been known.

[§352] The first is his image that he displayed in a glorious image.
[§352]* The first is the form of Divine Wisdom itself, made manifest in the splendor of celestial light — the Lord's own Truth as it appears to the interior sight of the regenerating mind.

[§353] The second is all the doors of the firmaments that were opened at that time. They open [before him] and shut behind him.
[§353]* The second is that all passages of communication between the successive degrees of the mind were opened at that moment. They open [GAP-1: *the spiritual sense here requires the opening to occur before or ahead of the Divine influx as it descends through the degrees*] and close behind the influx once it has passed through — for each discrete degree receives the Divine proceeding and, having transmitted it inward, seals itself so that the communication moves only forward into what is more interior.

[§354] The third is the light and the releasing that was purified by his image. That light is more than the light that he shall [gather]
[§354]* The third is the wisdom and the liberation of good from bondage to falsity, which was purified through the perception of Divine Truth. That wisdom exceeds the wisdom that the Divine shall [GAP-2: *the spiritual sense requires a future or ongoing gathering — a collecting of scattered goods and truths not yet liberated*].

[§355] The fourth: He has [made apparent], by the perfection of people, his light ship. He has [perfected] the living soul that shone forth in perfection and fullness over the worlds of enmity.
[§355]* The fourth: The Divine has [GAP-3: REVIEW *made apparent*] — through the perfection of regenerate minds — the containing vessel of celestial wisdom, the doctrine that carries purified good back toward its origin. The Divine has [GAP-4: *the spiritual sense requires an act of vivifying, filling, or perfecting*] the living good united to truth, which radiated in completeness and fullness over all the states of spiritual enmity and opposition where evil and falsity had ruled.

[§356] the wheel that exists in front [of the King of Honour who dwells in] the seventh firmament [...], the root of the rulers above and below in a great and cruel fetter; as the entire will of the great King of Honour is placed in [it]. Also, their entire enmity, which is in the firmaments, is [bound] and ensnared in it.
[§356]* The universal ordering of Divine Providence that stands before [GAP-1: the Divine Good governing through truth, which dwells in] the complete fullness of the interior heavens [GAP-2: ...], the hidden origin of all dominant persuasions in higher and lower degrees of the mind, held fast in a great and unyielding restraint of divine order; for the entire purpose of the Divine Good that governs [GAP-3: is invested in this ordering]. Likewise, all the opposition of evil and falsity that pervades the separated states of understanding is [GAP-4: ...] and held captive within it.

[§357] It exists in a blaze of splendour [with] ornamentation and great beauty. Now, there are twelve seals in it. The seal between i[s] every firmament and the rulers who are there [... ...] are in it; for should one of them wish to escape, he will be bound [by the seal]. If he should wish to cause a trick in his bond [it will be] apparent and known in [that] wheel [before him]. The spheres and stars and [the] leader of all the powers exist there, and can be known by it, in front of the King of Honour.
[§357]* This ordering exists in the radiance of Divine Wisdom [GAP-5: clothed in] the forms of heavenly arrangement and the beauty of spiritual perfection. Now, there are twelve constraining truths within it — truths of faith in their fullness. Each constraining truth stands between every separated state of understanding, and the dominant persuasions that dwell there [GAP-6: ... ...] are bound within it; for should any false persuasion seek to break free from divine order, it will be held fast [GAP-7: ...]. Should it attempt deception against its restraint, [GAP-8: ... it will be] made manifest and laid open in [GAP-9: that] ordering of Providence [GAP-10: ...]. All knowledges of good and truth, all insights of the heavens, and [GAP-11: the] ruling intelligence over every spiritual power exist there and are discerned through it, before the Divine Good that governs.

[§358] The twelf[t]h seal that is in it is its five shining men that are conjoined and enmeshed through the entire zone; because every place where they are cramped, and every place where they have space, is displayed by that wheel. For the root of all the firmaments above and below, and that of their rulers, is ensnared and fixed in it.
[§358]* The twelfth and completing constraining truth within this ordering consists of five illuminated faculties of perception, which are joined together and interwoven throughout the entire boundary of external life; because every state in which these perceptions are constricted, and every state in which they have freedom, is made visible through this ordering of Providence. For the hidden origin of all separated states of understanding, higher and lower, together with that of their dominant persuasions, is held captive and fixed within it.

[§359] The observation [of the wheel] is set right at all times opposite the great King o[f Honour ...] so that fear, wh[ich comes from it] will assume all the powers of mixture [... ...] their root affixed there, so that they cannot make an esc[a]pe from the fetter of their bond.
[§359]* The spiritual vigilance [GAP-12: ...] is maintained at all times before the Divine Good that governs [GAP-13: ... ...], so that the holy dread wh[GAP-14: ich ...] will subdue all the powers where good and evil are commingled [GAP-15: ... ...] their hidden origin fastened there, so that they cannot break free from the restraint of their divinely imposed bond.

[§360] That wheel rese[m]bles, in this respect, a chain in front of the chief guard, for when [...] in th[ose fetters] in the [great cha]in.
[§360]* This ordering of Providence resembles, in this respect, a connected series of truths set before the presiding principle of spiritual watchfulness, for when [GAP-16: ...] in th[GAP-17: ose ...] in the [GAP-18: ... connected series of constraining] truths.

[§361] This is also the case for all the rulers who are in the firmaments, and every power of the zone: If they should wish to escape, they shall be recognised and revealed by that wheel. [For] the wheel is like a great mirror, for [the di]scrimination of all things [that exist is] in it.
[§361]* This is likewise the case for all dominant persuasions that inhabit the separated states of understanding, and every power operating through the boundary of external life: should they seek to break free, they shall be recognized and exposed through this ordering of Providence. [GAP-19: For] this ordering is like a great capacity for self-reflection through Divine Truth, by which [the di]scernment of all interior states [GAP-20: ... is] contained within it.

[§362] When he built and constructed the worlds, he suspended the zones on their zones, and within another zone also. The first zone is the five powers of darkness, males and females.
[§362]* When the Divine established and formed the discrete degrees of spiritual order, He arranged each degree so that it depended upon the degree above it, and also upon a still more interior degree. The outermost degree consists of the five complete genera of falsity from evil, in both their intellectual and volitional forms.

[§363] This is why he hung one zone on a zone! He hung the five powers of darkness on the five shining men, the sons of the Man; because all the enmity is entwined and bound in them.
[§363]* This is why one degree of spiritual order was made to depend upon the degree above it. The five genera of falsity from evil were made subject to the five genera of radiant divine truth proceeding from the Divine Human — because all opposition arising from evil is held restrained and subjugated within these truths. Each genus of falsity is bound by its corresponding genus of wisdom.

[§364] Also, within the zone are the five sons of the [Living] Spirit; because the entire edifice is suspended from them, it being [that they also he]ard (?) him too, the oth[ers that were set] after them.
[§364]* Also, within the interior degree dwell the five essential powers of the [GAP-1: REVIEW — the living divine] Proceeding; because the entire structure of spiritual order is sustained by them, it being [GAP-2: — the spiritual sense requires: that each degree perceives and receives influx from the degree above — he] received from him also, the oth[GAP-3: er degrees of order — each successive degree receiving influx] after them.

[§365] And this is the one who came to [rule over the fi]rmaments above [them all] which is in one zone, is the great [King of Honour] who e[xists in his honour] within the [very midst] of the universe.
[§365]* And this is the One who descended into [GAP-4: — spiritual sense requires: the governing function over — the interior rational] boundaries above [GAP-5: — all subordinate degrees], which exists as one sovereign degree, namely the great [GAP-6: REVIEW — the Divine Good governing in its celestial glory] who ex[GAP-7: ists in sovereign dominion] within the [GAP-8: — inmost life] of the whole of creation.

[§366] There exist three great mighty days in all; and also two great and bitter deaths.
[§366]* There exist three great and mighty spiritual states in all; and also two great and bitter forms of spiritual death.

[§367] What are these three great days? Or again, what are these two deaths that are bitter?
[§367]* What are these three great states? Or again, what are these two forms of death that are bitter?

[§368] The first day is our father, the Man. From the time when the First Man went down to the abyss of darkness and drowned in it; and the Living Spirit was sent, who went and bore him up. After the Man had ascended, the Father of Life and the Great Spirit set things in order, and the worlds above and below.
[§368]* The first state is the celestial principle itself, the Divine Human. From the time when the celestial principle descended into the deepest conditions of falsity from evil and was overwhelmed by them; and the Divine Truth proceeding from the Divine Love was sent forth, which went down and drew that principle upward. After the celestial principle had been elevated, the Divine Love and the Divine Wisdom established all things in order — the interiors and the exteriors of the whole spiritual world.

[§369] Furthermore, the glorious Ambassador came and dwelt in the ship of the day. He displayed his glorious image, and he purified the light out of all things. He walked in the heights of the heavens. Till the time when the world will be dissolved, all things destroyed, the great fire released, and the Last Statue ascends. These three deeds were begun on this first day, when they sent the First Man with his five sons.
[§369]* Furthermore, the Divine Rational manifested and took its dwelling in the doctrinal vessel of that illuminated state. It displayed the fullness of the Divine likeness, and it separated wisdom from all its admixture with falsity throughout every created thing. It proceeded through the highest celestial degrees. Until the time when the old state of the church is dissolved, all its falsities laid bare, the final love of judgment released, and the completed form of the regenerated Grand Man ascends into fullness. These three great workings were begun in this first state, when the celestial principle was sent forth with its five faculties of spiritual perception into confrontation with evil.

[§370] In contrast, [the first death] is the darkness that mixed with the five sons of the First Man.
[§370]* In contrast, [GAP-1: the first spiritual death] is the condition in which falsity from evil commingled with the five perceptive faculties of the celestial principle.

[§371] The second day is the time when the fathers of light, who were victorious in the struggle, will sit on their thrones in the new aeon and dwell on the new earth. And they reign in the new aeon till the Father unveils his image above them.
[§371]* The second state is the time when those who dwell in wisdom, who were victorious in the combats of temptation, will be established upon their states of governance in the regenerated spiritual world and will dwell in the renewed external of the mind. And they reign in the regenerated life until the Divine itself unveils its own likeness above them.

[§372] The length of the sojourn in the new aeon equals the measure of the first day spent in affliction. They will reign like this and rejoice in the new aeon.
[§372]* The measure of the sojourn in the regenerated state is equal to the measure of the first state passed in spiritual affliction and temptation. They will reign in this manner and experience blessedness in the regenerated life.

[§373] The great third day [is the time] when the Father will unveil over them his image [ ... ... ] and he raises them up [to himself] and he receives them into his hidden treasury [of light] and he gives [to them] his soul. He can pour out upon them [without measure] his ambrosia and his sweet perfume, which takes away all affliction that they have seen with their eyes. [It is a] fullness and a joy eternal! When his grace would sate them, he gives to them his [garments of light] and they are made anew in them.
[§373]* The great third state [GAP-2: is the time] when the Divine itself will unveil over them its own likeness [GAP-3: in fullness beyond comprehension] and raises them up [GAP-4: into itself] and receives them into the inmost hidden treasury of the Divine memory [GAP-5: where nothing of falsity can enter] and gives [GAP-6: to each one the fullness of] its own life. The Divine can pour out upon them [GAP-7: without measure] its celestial delight and its sweet perception of good, which removes all suffering that they have known through their understanding. [GAP-8: There is] fullness and an eternal joy! When the Divine grace would satiate them completely, it gives to them its [GAP-9: own likeness renewed] and they are made new within those gifts.

[§374] Also, he can summon them once more in peace and silence, and perfect their stature in same measure as his first aeons. And he sets each power in its aeon, in its dwelling-place. They will dwell in the new aeon in their aeons!
[§374]* Also, the Divine can gather them once more into peace and interior silence, and bring their spiritual form to the same fullness of stature as the first celestial degrees. And it establishes each faculty in its proper degree, in its own state of life. They will dwell in the regenerated life, each within their proper degrees!

[§375] Now, when they dwell in their aeons, they will become rich beyond measure, for ever, beyond time. From then on they will not count that season amongst them; nor the number of the days, nor the hours, generation after generation, for ever and ever. The Father will not be hidden from them, from this time on. Rather, he has accomplished (his) unveiling to them [for ever] he being revealed to them for ever.
[§375]* Now, when they dwell in their proper degrees, they will become rich in wisdom and good beyond all measure, eternally, beyond any succession of states. From then on they will not reckon any passage of time among them, nor the number of the states, nor the changes, from generation to generation, eternally. The Divine will not be hidden from them from this time onward. Rather, it has accomplished its full unveiling to them [GAP-10: in perpetuity] — the Divine being revealed to them forever.

[§376] These are the three great days, [which the Father of Greatness has given number to in the reckoning].
[§376]* These are the three great spiritual states, [GAP-11: to which the Divine itself has assigned their order in the providence of all things].

[§377] In contrast, the two deaths are these.
[§377]* In contrast, the two spiritual deaths are these.

[§378] The first death is from the time when the light fell to the darkness, and was mixed in with the rulers of Darkness; until the time when the light will become pure, and be separated from the darkness in that great fire. The remainder left behind there can build and add to that Last Statue.
[§378]* The first spiritual death extends from the time when wisdom fell into falsity and was commingled with the ruling evils of the hells, until the time when wisdom will become pure and will be separated from falsity in the final love of judgment. Whatever remains behind of the good, however fragmented, can build up and add to the completed form of the Grand Man — the Last Statue of regenerated humanity.

[§379] Now, the second death is the death in which the souls of sinful men shall die; when [they will] be stripped of the shining light that illuminates the world. And also they are separated from the living air, from which they receive [living] breath; and they are deprived of this living soul, which [sustains all things] in the world; and they are separated and swept away from this Last Statue [and they have no portion in it] and bound on [ ... ... ] the female will become [the source of] their torture and their affliction; for their deeds [have cast them into] those three places; because they have blasphemed and despised the Holy Spirit since [ev]ery generation of the world.
[§379]* Now, the second spiritual death is the death in which the interiors of those who lived from evil will perish — when [GAP-12: they will] be stripped of the shining truth that illuminates the understanding. And also they are separated from the living influx of heaven, from which they received [GAP-13: living] spiritual respiration; and they are deprived of the living good itself, which [GAP-14: sustained all things] in the world; and they are severed and swept away from the completed Grand Man [GAP-15: having no part in it] and bound upon [GAP-16: the falsity from their own evil] — the will turned inward upon itself will become [GAP-17: the very source of] their torment and their affliction; for their works [GAP-18: have consigned them to] those three infernal regions; because they have profaned and rejected the Divine proceeding through every successive state of the church.

[§380] These are the two deaths. The first death is temporal; [but] the second death is eternal. It is the sec[ond deat]h!
[§380]* These are the two spiritual deaths. The first spiritual death is temporal — it has a boundary, and ends when purification is complete; [GAP-19: but] the second spiritual death is eternal. It is the second death!

[§381] Three things would be fashioned by the Light through necessity. For there can be no other way nor other result, to allow them to stay without coming about. For the Darkness, the enemy, must be bound by them; he who stood up against the Light from the beginning. Due to this, these things have no (other) result, to allow them to stay without coming about.
[§381]* Three outcomes must be brought into being by Divine wisdom acting from necessity. There exists no other means and no other end — these things cannot be left unaccomplished. For falsity arising from evil — the adversarial love of self that set itself against the Divine from the very origin — must be permanently restrained through these three orderings. Therefore these outcomes admit no alternative; they cannot remain undone.

[§382] Now, the first thing is the new luminous earth, which was set and built on top of this earth [that is beneath] it, namely that alien body [that was] built upon it.
[§382]* Now, the first outcome is the new state of the regenerated external mind, luminous with truth from good, which is established and formed upon the old natural mind [GAP-1: ...] it, namely that selfhood foreign to the Divine [GAP-2: ...] formed upon it.

*Note: "alien body" corresponds to the proprium — the selfhood that is not from the Divine but from the inherited natural will — upon which the regenerated external must be built as upon a foundation that is subjugated.*

[§383] The second thing is this: They will [fashion] and they [will build] the new earth, the coffin [and the grave] of the dark fire, the companion of the enemy.
[§383]* The second outcome is this: They will [GAP-3: ...] and they [GAP-4: ...] the new regenerated external mind, the containing form that encloses what is spiritually dead [GAP-5: ...] of infernal self-love, the inseparable companion of the adversarial will.

[§384] The third thing is the souls of all the sinners who have been condemned by their deeds. They were [judged, and there came] over them woe; they have been condemned according to the wicked deeds they had committed, and due to this they will be crucified on the enemy, he who had raised himself up against the greatness.
[§384]* The third outcome concerns the affections of all who confirmed themselves in evil, who have been judged according to the life that flowed from their ruling love. They were [GAP-6: ...] over them lamentation; they have been judged according to the wicked intentions they had enacted into life, and therefore they will be permanently fixed upon their own self-love — that adversarial will which had exalted itself against the Divine itself.

[§385] They will seal up this final lump when all the likenesses and images of every shape will be nailed in it. Also, those will be bound by this last fetter for eternity, and be placed as a footstool and a mat and a rag for this coffin [of the Darkness] which is the prison of the enemy. It will be placed, being a boundary-marker and a great void between the two, between the Darkness and the enemy, so that they cannot reach one another, nor can they hear their voices; rather they will become sterile as they are separated from each other for ever and ever.
[§385]* They will seal this final mass of confirmed evil when all the appearances, false persuasions, and phantasms of every form have been permanently affixed within it. Also, those confirmed in evil will be restrained by this ultimate Divine ordering for eternity, and be reduced to the lowest and most external containment — as a subjugated base, a trampled surface, and a cast-off remnant — for this enclosure of spiritual death [GAP-7: ...] which is the confinement of the adversarial will. It will be set in place as a permanent boundary and a great emptiness of separation between the two natures — between falsity and the self-love that generated it — so that they can no longer reach one another, nor can the persuasions of the one influx into the other; rather they will become utterly barren, being severed from all mutual communication forever and without end.

*Note on ¶6's final image: The separation of "Darkness" from "the enemy" — falsity from the self-love that produced it — corresponds to the ultimate state of hell described by Swedenborg in which evils and their falsities, once separated from all capacity to act in concert, become inert. The "great void" is the complete absence of spiritual communication — not empty space, but the extinction of all influx between them.*

[§386] The Darkness itself, the enemy, has received three bad blows with three wars; and worse dangers from the light in those three wars.
[§386]* Falsity from evil — the selfhood that opposes the Divine — has been overcome through three spiritual combats, three progressive states of judgment; and in each successive combat, divine wisdom inflicted upon it a deeper devastation than the last.

[§387] The first: (The enemy) was brought out to the middle and separated from his dark earth, whence he had departed. He was vanquished in that first war, caught and [bound] by the living soul.
[§387]* The first: The selfhood was drawn out from its interior concealment into the open and separated from the natural mind in its state of falsity, the ground from which it had arisen. It was overcome in that first spiritual combat, seized and [GAP-1] restrained by the divine life indwelling the human spirit.

[§388] The second blow is the time when he will be dissolved and melted, and he perishes in this great fire. He is destroyed and overthrown, from the images that are all by which he exists. And he will be gathered in [to the] fetter corresponding to his archetype, and be made like he was from the first beginning.
[§388]* The second devastation is the state in which falsity from evil is dissolved and its coherence destroyed, and it perishes in the consuming fire of divine love. It is destroyed and overthrown — stripped of every phantasy and false appearance through which it sustained its existence. And it will be gathered back [GAP-2] into the constraint proper to its essential form, and reduced to the condition it possessed from its first origin.

[§389] The third blow that will befall the enemy is the 'rolling back' [at the] end when everything will be separated, and male will be divided [from] female. Now, the male will be bound in the lump, but the female [will be thrown] in to the tomb. For it will be divided [and sealed by a great] stone in their midst, for generation upon generation, for ever and ever.
[§389]* The third devastation that will befall the selfhood is the final reversal [GAP-3] at the ultimate judgment, when all things will be separated, and the intellectual element of evil will be divided [GAP-4] from the volitional element. Now, the understanding that served evil will be bound into the congealed mass of falsity, but the will of evil [GAP-5] will be cast into spiritual death. For they will be divided [GAP-6] by an immovable ultimate truth set between them, for age upon age, unto eternity.

[§390] This is the way that the binding of the enemy will come about, in a fetter that is burdensome and strong, one from which there is never escape; because [they have achieved] his binding and bound him for ever. And they have achieved his being set apart, and he has been set apart for ever.
[§390]* This is the manner in which the total restraint of the selfhood opposed to the Divine will be accomplished — a constraint absolute and unbreakable, from which there is no release — because [GAP-7] the powers of divine order have accomplished its binding and confined it for eternity. And they have accomplished its complete separation, and it has been set apart forever.

[§391] these three vessels that [were hidden] and were discharged from above, and how they were discharged and separated from all the rulers who are arranged in the heavens. [This is] the manner in which the three vessels have been discharged, while the rulers were flourishing in their body.
[§391]* These three containing forms that [GAP-1] and were expelled from the interior mind, and how they were separated out from all the dominant false persuasions that had established themselves in the interior states of understanding. [GAP-2] The manner in which these three containing forms — of falsified truth, of falsity itself, and of infernal desire — were expelled, while the ruling persuasions still persisted in the fullness of their external life.

[§392] when these vessels were discharged, they were discharged in [great] slaughter, but those [who separated] these vessels from them.
[§392]* When these containing forms were expelled, they were driven out through [GAP-3] violent spiritual destruction, but those [GAP-4] separated these containing forms from them.

[§393] They are like the form of a man who has fallen into a purulent sickness. Other wounds and diseases are in his body, and even bile and other poisons are in his inner limbs. He achieves good health by a wise doctor, who gives him one remedy to drink that causes his purulent sickness to break out above, and gives him another medicine to drink that causes his sickness to discharge below, and he gives him yet another medicine to drink that cures his wounds for him [and purifies him] calmly and silently.
[§393]* They are like the form of a mind that has fallen into deep corruption of understanding. Other distortions and disorders dwell within it, and even bitter hostility and hidden evils pervade its interior will. It is restored to spiritual health by Divine Wisdom, who administers one truth to be received inwardly that causes the corruption to break forth through the higher faculties, and administers another truth to be received that causes the disorder to be expelled through the lower natural mind, and administers yet another truth that heals the distortions [GAP-5] gently and without disturbance.

[§394] Through the skill of a wise doctor, with his effective medicines to consume that take away all sicknesses in this manner and dissolve all magic, the doctors shall [heal, and] they work cures through the aroma of the medicine, other wounds [that are in] what is hidden, they at last tear them out from [within, and are] manifested above and below [and purified through] the aroma of edible medicines that were placed upon them.
[§394]* Through the operation of Divine Wisdom, by means of effective truths received into the understanding that remove all spiritual disorders in this manner and dissolve every enchantment of falsity, the healers shall [GAP-6] — they accomplish reformation through the perception of truth; other distortions [GAP-7] what is concealed within, they at last tear them out from [GAP-8] made manifest in the interior and in the exterior mind [GAP-9 — through] the perception of truths received and applied to those disordered states.

[§395] This is also what these vessels of water, of darkness and of fire are like, which are hidden in the body of all the rulers above; while the Living Spirit is like the wise doctor. These three healing drugs are the three garments that the Living Spirit assumes upon his body: of wind, of water, and of fire. Through them he has set in order the things below. For by those three garments he has been displayed to the rulers who are above, with hard-heartedness, with shaking and fear, with derangement; from them he discharged the three vessels, those of water, of darkness and of fire.
[§395]* This is also what these containing forms of falsified truth, of falsity, and of infernal desire are like, which lie hidden within the life of all the dominant persuasions of the interior mind; while the Living Wisdom of Providence is like the divine healer. These three restorative truths are the three external forms of doctrine that the Living Wisdom assumes upon itself: the doctrine of understanding, the doctrine of truth, and the doctrine of love. Through them Providence has set in order the things of the natural mind. For by those three external doctrines it was made manifest to the ruling persuasions of the interior states, producing in them obstinacy, then agitation and dread, then disorientation; and from them it expelled the three containing forms — those of falsified truth, of falsity, and of infernal desire.

[§396] The case of these vessels is just like a child who is in his mother's womb and is swathed by seven garments. The first is the marrow, the second is the bones, the third is the tendons, the fourth is the flesh, the fifth the veins, the sixth the blood, the seventh the skin of the body that covers him. The other garment also [is the covering that wraps him, and the ninth is that which encloses him] this child. The marrow and the bones, the sinews and flesh, the veins and blood and skin, together with the garment that she wears and the linen that swathes her, total nine. Together with the nine of the child, these and the others, they shall complete and make eighteen garments. Now, you shall find the soul of this child is entwined within all these.
[§396]* The condition of these containing forms is just like a new spiritual life forming within the natural mind, enveloped by seven successive coverings of the natural order. The first is the inmost natural good, the second is the structural framework of truth, the third is the connective truths that bind understanding together, the fourth is the substantial good of natural life, the fifth the channels through which truth flows, the sixth the vivifying spiritual truth that circulates throughout, the seventh the outermost natural truth that covers everything. The further covering also [GAP-10 — within the containing state of the natural mind. That containing state itself is just like] this new spiritual life. The inmost good and the structural truths, the connective truths and natural good, the channels and vivifying truths and outermost covering, together with the external doctrine it wears and the outermost appearances that enwrap it, total nine. Together with the nine coverings of the new spiritual life within, these and those complete eighteen layers of external truth. And you shall find the essential life of this new spiritual being is interwoven throughout all of these.

[§397] And look see: When the smell of the action of the demon, which brings about the stench of birth, will reveal the child; immediately [it] shall [open up] all [these things] and it emerges from a crack that has become visible, while no tear nor wound occurs in the body of the child. In the same way as this child opens up the womb of his mother, so is it also with these vessels of water, of darkness and of fire. The actions that open up his mother to the child, and she shakes him out and reveals him; these are the three living garments that the Living Spirit assumed on his body.
[§397]* And perceive this: When the perception of the activity of evil, which brings about the corruption attending natural generation, reveals the new spiritual life; immediately [GAP-11] shall [GAP-12] all [GAP-13: REVIEW these things] and it emerges through an opening that has become apparent, while no rupture nor injury occurs in the life of the new spiritual being. In the same way as this new life opens up the containing state of the natural mind, so is it also with these containing forms of falsified truth, falsity, and infernal desire. The operations that open up the natural mind to release the new spiritual life, causing it to shake forth and reveal it — these are the three living doctrines that the Living Wisdom of Providence assumed upon itself.

[§398] For by the aspect of the garment of water [in which he was revealed to them, he shook them and discharged the vessel of water] from the rulers; he moved [the waters, for] they are the poison and death with which their veins and entrails are filled.
[§398]* For by the aspect of the doctrine of truth [GAP-14] from the ruling persuasions; it moved forth [GAP-15] — they are the corruptions and spiritual death with which the channels and interior faculties of those persuasions are filled.

[§399] By the aspect of the garment of wind, in which he was revealed [to them] he has swept away and erased the entire destructive shadow and dirt, discharged it down to the earth. Afterwards he swept it out and confined it in the vessel of darkness that surrounds the universe.
[§399]* By the aspect of the doctrine of understanding, through which it was revealed [GAP-16] — it swept away and erased the entire destructive obscurity and contamination of falsity, discharging it down to the natural mind. Afterwards it swept it out and confined it within the containing form of falsity that surrounds the entire system of the mind.

[§400] Also, by the garment of living fire, through its splendour and vigour, he discharged the fire of the darkness from all the rulers and threw it down upon this earth. He swept it another time from the earth and confined it in the vessel that surrounds all the worlds, and they call it the wall of great fire.
[§400]* Also, by the doctrine of living love, through its radiance and power, it expelled the fire of self-love from all the ruling persuasions and cast it down upon the natural mind. It swept it a second time from the natural and confined it within the containing form that surrounds all the states of the mind, and they call it the wall of the great fire of love — the ultimate boundary.

[§401] Now, those three garments, through which he established these three things, he did not peel them off all at once; but he stripped them off one by one at the appropriate time [and cast them] down to the earth [below]
[§401]* Now, those three doctrines, through which Providence established these three works of separation, it did not remove them all at once; but it set them aside one by one at the appropriate state of development [GAP-17] down to the natural mind [GAP-18].

[§402] In this way the universe of the [ ... ... ] his vessels [ ... ] these vessels like [ ... ]
[§402]* In this way the entire system of the [GAP-19] its containing forms [GAP-20] these containing forms like [GAP-21].

[§403] The use of the matter, and the management of these vessels that were swept out and discharged from above to earth, have brought an advantage and benefit of twelve great deeds. Afterwards, they were swept from the earth and pushed outside to the edges of the worlds and to the extremity.
[§403]* The use of these substances of evil, and the governance of these containing forms that were swept out and expelled from the interior mind down to the natural, have brought an advantage and benefit of twelve complete divine works. Afterwards, they were swept from the natural mind and pushed outward to the boundaries of all states and to the uttermost extremity.

[§404] The first deed is this: So that evil-doing does not increase in the universe, neither in the creations nor in the trees [nor in all flesh, for]tself up in three things.
[§404]* The first work is this: So that the doing of evil does not increase throughout the system of the mind, neither in the things it has formed nor in its perceptions [GAP-22 — it has set i]tself up as a threefold containment.

[§405] The [first is / ...]
[§405]* The [GAP-23: first is —]

[§406] The second is error [ ... ... ] if [the vessel of water] had not been swept down from above, but rather the waters existing up till now in the rulers above [ ... / ... the] torrents that would flow [down] and the droplets that would [drip] down, so lust would have sprouted [and multiplied]n the trees and in the fruits as well as all flesh
[§406]* The second is the wandering of the understanding [GAP-24] — if [GAP-25: REVIEW the containing form of falsified truth] had not been swept down from the interior, but rather the falsified truths existing up till now within the ruling persuasions of the interior mind [GAP-26] — the torrents of falsity that would pour down [GAP-27] and the infiltrations that would [GAP-28] down — then corrupt desire would have sprouted [GAP-29] in the perceptions and in the works as well as in every form of external life.

[§407] if the vessel of darkness had not been swept out and thrown down to the earth, nor swept from the earth and cast outside the region of the worlds into this pit; again the drops of darkness, which will drip down from it, and the torrents that will continue to flow down from above to the earth and assume the trees and all flesh, would have multiplied
[§407]* If the containing form of falsity had not been swept out and cast down to the natural mind, nor swept from the natural mind and cast outside the region of all mental states into this abyss — then again the drops of falsity, which would drip down from it, and the torrents that would continue to pour from the interior to the natural mind and overwhelm the perceptions and every external form of life, would have multiplied beyond measure.

[§408] if the fire were in the rulers of the worlds of the heavens, and had not been swept out and cast down to the earth, and swept from the earth and shut up inside the vessel of fire that surrounds the worlds; [again] this fire [would have poured down from] above through the torrent [ ... the torrent ... ] below;
[§408]* If the infernal desire were still in the ruling persuasions throughout the states of the interior mind, and had not been swept out and cast down to the natural mind, and swept from the natural mind and shut up inside the containing form of infernal love that surrounds all the states of life — [GAP-30] this infernal desire [GAP-31] from the interior through the torrent of influx [GAP-32 — the torrent of influx] into the natural;

[§409] and the dark fire would have multiplied in the tree and in fruit and in all flesh.
[§409]* And the dark fire of self-love would have multiplied in every perception and in every work and in all external life.

[§410] this single evil that was purified by three archetypes [ ... / ... ]
[§410]* This single evil that was purified through three archetypal forms of divine truth [GAP-33].

[§411] The second deed: Al[l] the heavens [ ... / ... ] of the sediment and the pollution [ ... ... ] so that [ ... / ... ] surpasses in them [ ... ]
[§411]* The second work: All the interior states of the mind [GAP-34] from the residue and the contamination of evil [GAP-35] so that [GAP-36] what surpasses in them [GAP-37].

[§412] [The third]: The heavens and the firmaments [ ... / ... b]urden [and were] relieved from the weight-carriers [ ... ] and it ceased being heavy. This rich one [ ... ... ] above.
[§412]* [GAP-38: REVIEW The third]: The interior states and the structural frameworks of truth [GAP-39 — the b]urden [GAP-40] relieved from those faculties that had borne the weight [GAP-41] and it ceased being oppressive. This abundance [GAP-42] in the interior.

[§413] The fourth: That they might purify and cleanse their dwelling places [ ... / ... ]
[§413]* The fourth: That the dwelling places of the spirit might be purified and cleansed — those interior states where spiritual life resides [GAP-43].

[§414] The fifth: Because the greatness and the richness that dwells in these ships, as it [fills them] you might find them living in these ships, purified and holy.
[§414]* The fifth: Because the greatness and the abundance of good that dwells within these doctrinal forms, as it [GAP-44] — you might find them living in these doctrinal forms, purified and consecrated.

[§415] The sixth: That you might find the mixed, incomplete, and unconfined temples above at rest and purified; since rest will reach them and pass through them and purify them.
[§415]* The sixth: That you might find the mingled, incomplete, and unbounded forms of worship in the interior mind brought to rest and purified; since the peace of heaven will reach them and pass through them and purify them.

[§416] The seventh: [That the] nature alone of the vessels, which will advance and extend to their places and sites [ ... / ... ] in [ ... ]
[§416]* The seventh: [GAP-45] the inherent nature alone of the containing forms, which will advance and extend to their proper places and appointed states [GAP-46] in [GAP-47].

[§417] The eighth: That great enclosures and walls would be made, and confine everything.
[§417]* The eighth: That great protective boundaries and walls of truth would be established, and confine everything within order.

[§418] The ninth: When the apostles [reach] them, they shall return to the universe and give a sign of [the end and] the dissolution of the universe.
[§418]* The ninth: When the divine truths sent forth [GAP-48] to them, they shall return to the system of the mind and give a sign of [GAP-49] the final dissolution of the entire natural order.

[§419] The tenth deed: That they would become releasers and purifiers of everything at the end of the universe like the fire [ ... / ... ] burns [all things]
[§419]* The tenth work: That they would become the releasers of good and purifiers of all things at the end of the age, like the refining fire of love [GAP-50] that burns away [GAP-51].

[§420] The eleventh deed: [That they] will make a crossing [to the light] and the entire ruling-power [shall be cast out]
[§420]* The eleventh work: [GAP-52] there will be a passage [GAP-53] and the entire dominion of falsity [GAP-54].

[§421] to the outer land, for they were not envious of this [ ... ] a man who is richer than them; because the aeons of light are outside [it, in] the place [of rest and] of peace, the place of honour and grace, the place where the joy of the householders who dwell in it shall never pass away nor recede.
[§421]* To the outermost region, for they bore no jealousy against this [GAP-55] — a mind that possesses greater abundance of good than they; because the eternal states of wisdom lie beyond [GAP-56] the state [GAP-57] of peace, the state of honor and of grace, the state where the joy of those who dwell in heavenly love shall never pass away nor diminish.

[§422] The twelfth: That you might find all the deeds of loss hidden from the aeons of light, because of the wall of fire that surrounds the entire building; and these unclean things are displayed (only) in its [interior]
[§422]* The twelfth: That you might find all the works of spiritual loss concealed from the eternal states of wisdom, because of the wall of the fire of divine love that surrounds the entire created order; and these impure things are displayed only within its [GAP-58].

[§423] There was a need for these twelve great deeds because the vessels that were discharged and swept out from the firmaments to the earth, and were also swept from the earth to the {extr[emity) ... / ... ] the three words of life watch over them that [ ... / ... ar]e set over them in great power [and authority] that they might watch over them at the last time; because of this, they are firmly set thus in these fetters
[§423]* There was a necessity for these twelve great divine works, because the containing forms that were expelled and swept out from the interior structures of truth down to the natural mind, and were also swept from the natural mind to the {extr[GAP-59: emity —] the three essential words of divine life watch over them so that [GAP-60 — the powers that ar]e set over them in sovereign authority [GAP-61] that they might watch over them in the final state; because of this, they are firmly bound within these restraints forever.

[§424] When the Father of Life discharged these three vessels from above, they of water and of darkness and of fire, did he perhaps discharge them to their pits at the edge of the firmament? Or did he discharge them first to this earth, and then he swept them from the earth and threw them to their places?
[§424]* When the Divine allowed these three containing forms of evil to descend from the interiors — those of corrupted falsity, of spiritual blindness, and of self-love — did the Divine perhaps cast them directly into the hells at the boundary of the interior mind? Or did the Divine first bring them into conscious awareness within the natural mind, and only then sweep them out and confine them in their appointed places?

[§425] when the Father of Life was about to discharge these three vessels he poured the three upon this earth, one by one at its (appointed) hour and season. He discharged them and cast them down to the earth.
[§425]* When the Divine was about to remove these three containing forms of evil, it poured all three into the natural mind, one by one at the ordained time and stage of development. It allowed them to descend and settle into conscious awareness.

[§426] Then he bailed and scooped them from this earth in these three garments, they of wind and of water and of fire, which swathed his body. He poured and made them flow into the three pits [at the edge of the firmament]hich he had [es]tablished during the construc[tio]n of the worlds [And when] they [were swept away, yet] something remained behind on the face of the earth from these three vessels, so that you can find their mark and type displayed in the universe.
[§426]* Then the Divine drew them out from the natural mind by means of three exterior truths — those of rational understanding, of genuine truth, and of purified love — which clothed the Divine presence itself. The Divine caused them to flow into the three confined regions of hell [GAP-1] which had been established during the formation of the spiritual world [GAP-2] they [GAP-3] yet something remained behind within the natural mind from these three forms of evil, so that their trace and character can be found displayed throughout the whole order of the mind.

[§427] The element that is left in water is the pungency and the salinity of this salty inner sea that surrounds the worlds.
[§427]* The element that remains from corrupted falsity is the bitterness and corrosiveness of the pervasive sensory falsity that encompasses all the states of the mind — the acrid quality of the outermost knowledge of appearances through which everything interior is distorted.

[§428] And the remnant that is left from the darkness is this black mountain that exists in the surroundings of the sunrise. For when the sun would shine over the world no sunlight is visible there, because the darkness that gushes from it manifests above it.
[§428]* And the remnant left behind from spiritual blindness is that obstinate elevation of self-intelligence that persists near the very source of illumination. For when Divine wisdom shines into the mind, no light of truth is visible in that state, because the falsity from evil that pours forth from self-intelligence manifests above it and obscures it.

[§429] And the remnant that remains behind from the fire, upon the earth, are all the mountains and islands from where fumes of fire gush up and are visible. For these things are the remnant of the great vessel, the vestige that was conjoined with this fire that comes up from the earth; together with the mountains and hills [and the] places where no fruit sprouts on the trees [for the fire is] greater than that place from the limb and [ ... / ... ] the trees shall not bear fruit in that place.
