[§429]* And the remnant that remains from self-love, within the natural mind, comprises all the elevations of pride and isolated states of self-will from which exhalations of evil desire rise up and become manifest. For these are remnants of the great containing form of infernal love, the vestige that was conjoined with the concupiscence ascending from the natural mind — together with the states of pride and self-exaltation [GAP-4] states where no good works spring forth from any perception [GAP-5] greater than that state from the limb and [GAP-6] perceptions shall not bear forth good works in that condition.

[§430] about the vessels that surround the universe, and why they were not discharged from above into the ditches prepared for them (straightaway); but were firstly poured upon the earth, and afterwards were swept from the earth and cast down into their pits?
[§430]* Concerning the containing forms of evil that encompass the whole mind, and why they were not removed directly from the interiors into the hells prepared for them at once, but were first poured into the natural mind, and only afterward swept from the natural mind and cast down into their confined places.

[§431] When the Father of Life discharged these vessels from above upon the earth, he poured them out for this reason: so that also the water and the darkness and the fire that exist in these three earths would be drawn and ascend towards they that had come down from above, and the earths would be purified and cleansed. Due to this he poured these vessels down to the earth, of water and of darkness and of fire, so that also the water and darkness and fire that exist in the earths would be drawn up towards they that had descended from above.
[§431]* When the Divine allowed these containing forms to descend from the interiors upon the natural mind, it poured them forth for this reason: so that also the corrupted falsity, the spiritual blindness, and the self-love already existing within the lower natural states would be attracted and drawn upward toward that which had descended from the interiors, and the natural states themselves would be purified and cleansed. For this purpose the Divine brought these containing forms into conscious awareness — of corrupted falsity, of spiritual blindness, and of self-love — so that the corrupted falsity, the spiritual blindness, and the self-love already embedded in the natural states would be drawn toward that which had descended from within.

[§432] Now, first of all, he discharged the water, the waste, the poison of the rulers who are above. He cleansed the firmaments of the [water that weighed them down] he poured down the dark waters. [Just as the] waters fell from heaven to earth, so also the waters below in the earths were drawn and came up from below towards the waters that had come from above to the earths. The waters [were] purified of the dark poison that exists in them. Then he swept them outside and made them a great vessel and a wall of sea that surrounds the universe, and on which the ships sail.
[§432]* First of all, the Divine removed the corrupted falsity — the waste and the poison of the ruling evils that dominated the interiors. The Divine cleansed the interior states of the [GAP-7: corrupted falsity]; it poured down the dark falsities. [GAP-8: Just as the] falsities descended from the interior mind into the natural mind, so also the falsities lodged below in the natural states were attracted and rose from below toward the falsities that had come from the interiors into conscious awareness. The falsities [GAP-9: were] purified of the dark poison of evil within them. Then the Divine swept them outward and made of them a great containing boundary — a wall of outermost sensory knowledge that encompasses the entire mind, and upon which doctrinal systems may navigate.

[§433] After the water, he discharged the darkness. He purified the firmaments and dried from them the darkness with which they are weighed down. He discharged it down to the earth. Just as he had purified the firmaments, so also he purified the earths. The darkness that is in the earths was drawn up towards the darkness that had been discharged from above. He swept them both out and shut them in that [pit] of darkness [ ... / ... ] the water.
[§433]* After the corrupted falsity, the Divine removed the spiritual blindness. It purified the interior states and dried from them the falsity from evil with which they were weighed down. It brought this blindness into the natural mind. Just as it had purified the interior states, so also it purified the natural states. The falsity from evil residing in the natural states was drawn upward toward the falsity from evil that had been discharged from the interiors. Both were swept out together and shut within that [GAP-10] region of spiritual blindness [GAP-11] the corrupted falsity.

[§434] And he discharged the fire from heaven to earth, [and at] that time again the fire that was shut [in] the earth below was drawn and came up towards its kind. Fire was added to fire. Afterwards, he swept it out and cast it into the outer underworld, which [he had constructed for] them outside the universe.
[§434]* And the Divine brought the self-love down from the interior states into the natural mind, [GAP-12: and at] that time again the self-love that had been confined [GAP-13: in] the natural states below was attracted and rose toward its own kind. Evil desire was added to evil desire. Afterward, the Divine swept it out and cast it into the outermost hell, which [GAP-14: had been constructed for] them beyond the whole order of the mind.

[§435] In the same way that he purified the firmaments above as a place for the light-givers to journey [ ... / ... ] so also he purified the earths below and swept from them the fire and the darkness and the water [and cast them out] He has purified them for the ascent of these three powers, they of the wind and the water and the fire, which ascend before the Porter and traverse the earths.
[§435]* In the same way that the Divine purified the interior states above to become a place through which truths of illumination could journey [GAP-15] so also the Divine purified the natural states below and swept from them the self-love, the spiritual blindness, and the corrupted falsity [GAP-16]. The Divine has purified them for the ascent of the three living powers — those of rational understanding, genuine truth, and purified love — which ascend before the Guardian of the threshold and traverse all the natural states from lowest to highest.

---

### Translator's Note on the Overarching Process

The entire chapter describes what Swedenborg calls *vastation* and *purification* in regeneration. The central teaching — that evils cannot be cast directly from the interiors into the hells but must first be brought into conscious awareness in the natural mind — corresponds precisely to the doctrine that no evil can be removed unless it is first seen, acknowledged, and resisted in the external man. The descent of like toward like (evil drawing out evil of its kind) reflects the law that latent hereditary evils are activated and made manifest through temptation, so that they may be identified, separated, and removed. The three stages — corrupted falsity first, then spiritual blindness, then self-love last — follow the Swedenborgian order of reformation: falsities of the understanding are addressed before the deeper evils of the will, and self-love, being the deepest root, is removed last and cast into the outermost hell.

[§436] After the Living Spirit constructed things and the worlds, he came up and set himself upon this earth. He gave a sign to the King of Glory, who turned the three wheels and caused the wheels that are found [to turn] up above all the mountains; he spread forth the wind to them, he breathed on them, he stirred them up, he washed the earth with the waters. And the waste and the accretion of the three vessels, they of water and of Darkness and of fire, which had been discharged upon the earth, he gathered them and deposited them in the sea that is within the walls and the vessels; because of this sea waters are salty. For they have received salt and bitterness from the washing out and cleansing of the three vessels.

[§437] When the Father of Life came, rising in his ascent to the heights, he discharged the wheel of the stars; he washed it out and loosened all the sediment that was in it. He cast it down to this great inner sea like [the stones] of the mountain. And that waste, the sediment of the washing out of the wheel, he cast it down and mixed it in with the sea waters. Its own thought collected it together; and the thought gathered the waste together in this way. It made it fast and fixed it [in the sea]

[§438] The sea giant had stamped upon him the seal of the seconds and the hours, the seal of the days and months and years, the impression of the stars and the signs of the zodiac [and the five stars] He came into existence from them and was discharged from their power and cast into the sea. The images and the seals and aspects and doctrines and counsels of [the wheel of the sphere] were sealed upon the body of that giant, because he is the residue of them all. Consequently, each star that will shine, and each sign of the zodiac that will turn: of one he shall be its inducement, and of another its confirmation.

[§439] He was discharged into the sea like this, the way a cloud might discharge rainwater into the midst of the sea. They compelled him totally through the action of the power and the helmsmanship of the angels. He was swept down to the mill, the place where he was constructed in the setting up of the universe; and the sea also conceived him [and bore him] for its part [and formed him] it being his chief sculptor and chief craftsman. It sculpted him in a likeness, he being changed more than the other residue of the sea bodies.

[§440] This matter is comparable with a craftsman who might take the stamped impressions of many coins and cast them on the fire, and mould them [into a single] image. These thousands of impressions and thousands of shapes [are perfected] and make a single body [from them all]

[§441] This is also the case with the sea giant, who too was moulded and sculpted by the power of the lust inside him from many doctrines and counsels that belong to the residue of the wheel of the sphere.

[§442] The archetype of the giant was constructed for three things.

[§443] The first: That the sphere would be relieved and purified of the burden present in it. In the same way that the ten firmaments were purified and relieved of the burden of the three vessels; so was it also done to the sphere, that it too might be relieved and purified of the burden and the waste inside it.

[§444] The second: That he might add a fetter and a prison to the wheel and the signs of the zodiac that are bound to it, so that when they wish to go up above, you can find the sea giant dragging them back as by an iron fetter, and they are unable to go up; and when the powers of the wheel would take a decision on a guileful thing, and it enters their mind to come down to the earth, they are not able to descend because of this stake that is nailed to the wheel upon the [midst] of the firmaments. This is one. However, this other one, because of its sleepless watchers that are set over it, it is turned all the time.

[§445] The third thing: That the wind and the water and the fire that gush from the circuit of the sea, and the rivers that flow upon the face of the earth discharging into the sea, and the rainwaters that come to ground, and the waste and rebirth that come down to it; that sea will be a hostel and gathering place for all of these.

[§446] As for all the power and the life that comes down to the sea, and in the breath that the sea giant will breathe and draw it into him that he might disturb the sea [there comes a] fullness and a [stirring] becomes between his movement [and his power to] purify; but the sea [is cleansed thereby]

[§436–§446]* **¶2**

After the Divine operative power had established the forms and states of the regenerating mind, it descended and fixed its attention upon the external natural mind. It communicated its purpose to the Divine truth governing from celestial good, which set in motion the three progressions of spiritual influx and caused these progressions, which are established [GAP-1] above all elevated states of love, to operate. It extended spiritual influx toward them, breathed the life of the spirit into them, roused them into activity, and purified the natural mind with truths. And the impurities and accumulations from the three containing forms — those of corrupted truth, of falsity from evil, and of perverted love — which had been discharged into the natural mind: these the Divine operative power gathered together and deposited them into the general mass of sensual knowledge that lies within its boundaries and containing structures. For this reason the general body of natural knowledge is adulterated. It has received bitterness and corruption from the washing-out and cleansing of those three containing forms.

---

**¶3**

When the Divine source of all spiritual life arose in its elevation to the inmost celestial state, it released and purified the revolving cycle of knowledges; it washed it clean and loosened all the residual falsity that was within it. It cast this residue down into the great interior mass of sensual knowledge, like [GAP-2] from an elevated state of love. And that impurity — the sediment from the purification of the cycle of knowledges — it precipitated and mingled with the body of natural understanding. The inherent intentionality of that residuum drew it together of itself; the proprial will consolidated the falsities in this way. It rendered them firm and fixed them [GAP-3].

---

**¶4**

Upon this composite selfhood formed from the general body of sensual knowledge there had been stamped the determination of every particular state and every intermediate state, the determination of every full state and every successive state and every completed period of regeneration, the character of all knowledges and all the general dispositions of spiritual life [GAP-4]. It came into existence from these accumulated states and was separated out from their collective power and cast into the general mass of natural understanding. The representations and determinations and appearances and doctrines and intentions of [GAP-5] were sealed upon the very form of that composite selfhood, because it is the sediment of all those states together. Consequently, every particular knowledge that illuminates and every general disposition that revolves through its cycle: to one this selfhood will be its inducement, and to another its confirmation.

---

**¶5**

This composite selfhood was precipitated into the general mass of natural knowledge in the same way that an obscured state might release truth-become-falsity into the midst of sensual understanding. It was wholly constrained through the operation of Divine power and the governance of celestial ministers. It was swept down into the process of reduction and breaking — the state where it had been formed in the original ordering of the mind. And the general body of natural knowledge also received and gestated it [GAP-6] for its own part [GAP-7], being the primary shaping and formative power over it. It shaped this selfhood into an image, this selfhood being transformed beyond any other remnant form within the mass of natural knowledge.

---

**¶6**

This process is comparable to a formative power that might take the fixed determinations of many distinct knowledges and subject them to the fire of self-love, and fuse them [GAP-8: REVIEW — into a single unified] form of selfhood. These innumerable determinations and innumerable configurations [GAP-9: REVIEW — are brought to completion] and constitute a single composite selfhood [GAP-10].

---

**¶7**

This is likewise the case with the composite selfhood formed from the general body of sensual knowledge, which was shaped and formed by the force of concupiscence within it, drawn from the many doctrines and intentions belonging to the residuum of the entire revolving cycle of spiritual states.

---

**¶8**

The primary pattern of this composite selfhood was established to serve three purposes.

---

**¶9**

The first: that the entire revolving cycle of spiritual states might be relieved and purified of the burden of evil within it. In the same way that the tenfold expanse of interior understanding was cleansed and freed from the weight of the three containing forms of corrupted truth, falsity, and perverted love, so likewise was it done to the revolving cycle — that it too might be relieved and purified of the oppressive evil and the impurity within it.

---

**¶10**

The second: that this composite selfhood might serve as a restraint and a confinement upon the revolving cycle of knowledges and the general dispositions bound to it, so that when these attempt to rise into the interior mind, the composite selfhood in the natural mind is found dragging them back as by an unyielding bond, and they are unable to ascend. And when the powers of that revolving cycle take a determination upon some deceptive course, and it enters their intention to descend into the external natural mind, they are unable to descend because of this fixed point of restraint that is fastened to the cycle upon the {GAP-11} of the expanses of interior understanding. This is the one function. However, the other: because of the vigilant celestial guardians appointed over it, the cycle is kept in continual revolution.

---

**¶11**

The third purpose: that the spiritual influx, the truth, and the love that pour forth from the circuit of the general body of sensual knowledge — and the particular streams of understanding that flow upon the surface of the natural mind and discharge into it — and the truths from heaven that descend to the external plane — and the impurities and regenerated states that come down into it — that general body of natural knowledge will be a receptacle and gathering place for all of these.

---

**¶12**

As for all the spiritual force and vitality that descends into the general body of natural knowledge, and the spiritual reception that the composite selfhood draws into itself by its breathing so as to agitate the mass of sensual understanding — [GAP-12] a plenitude and a [GAP-13] arises between its activity [GAP-14] to purify; but the general body of sensual knowledge [GAP-15].

[§447] Understand also this other (matter): Before these vessels were discharged and swept down from heaven, first three ditches were fashioned and constructed for them, in which they would be bound and fettered.
[§447]* Understand also this further matter: Before the containing forms of evil were expelled and cast down from the higher states of the mind, three confining states of restraint were first fashioned and established to receive them, states within which they would be held bound and prevented from spreading.

[§448] Before the sea giant was discharged from the sphere, the Father of Life constructed a place for [him i]n this great sea that is within the walls, whereon the ships sail, and where the fullness shall [be gathered from it]
[§448]* Before the great persuasion of falsity was expelled from the ordering arrangement of heaven, Divine Providence prepared a place for it within that vast body of natural knowledge contained within the boundaries of doctrine, upon which systems of teaching travel, and where the fullness of divine order shall [GAP-1] — that is, where the completion of all truth's purpose shall ultimately prevail over it.

[§449] Also, before the [beast] was displayed to the worlds [and] the earth [ ... ... f]ell to the sea and its thought shaped it, the Father of Life constructed a place for it in [the depths of the earth] below the four mountains, above the mountains of the heavens above [it] that [as] a great plain it digs in Matter in the midst of the worlds. When the Adamant of Light was sent to it, he hastened after it to that place, smote it and threw it between mountains in the place made ready for it [and he] is set fast upon it till the end of the universe.
[§449]* Also, before the [GAP-2] was made manifest to the states of the mind [GAP-3] the natural mind [GAP-4] descended into that body of natural knowledge and its own deliberation gave it form, Divine Providence prepared a place for it in [GAP-5] the lowest regions beneath the four elevated states of celestial good, above the elevated states of the interior heavens above [GAP-6] that [GAP-7] a vast open state it excavates within the substance of the unregenerate will amid the states of the mind. When the indestructible truth of divine wisdom was sent against it, that truth pursued it swiftly to that place, struck it and cast it down between elevated states of good into the place made ready for it [GAP-8] and it is held fixed there until the final consummation of all spiritual ages.

[§450] Before the waste and dirt of Darkness were swept out from creation, gehennas were constructed in which the waste might be covered till the dissolution of the universe.
[§450]* Before the refuse and impurity of falsity from evil were purged out from the regenerating mind, infernal states of containment were prepared in which that spiritual refuse might be buried and sealed away until the final dissolution of the old order.

[§451] Before the rebels rose up, they whose corps rebelled in the ruling-power above and below, they [were cast] outside, and also the tyrants in whose heart it lay [to ty]rannise amongst the murk and the clouds and winds and spirits and storms; for them were constructed in the great outer sea seven hostels, places whence they are thrown into the depths and the darkness [outside]
[§451]* Before the evils that rebel against divine order rose up — those whose entire will opposed the governance of both the interior and exterior mind — they [GAP-9] were thrust outward; and also the dominant evils in whose interior will it lay to tyrannize amid the obscurities of understanding, the darkened states of thought, the turbulent influxes of falsity, the disordered spiritual agencies, and the violent upheavals of the natural mind — for all of these, seven complete states of confinement were prepared within the vast outermost expanse of exterior falsity, places from which they are cast into the deepest states of spiritual blindness and privation of all truth [GAP-10].

[§452] Also, before the abortions [fell down from heaven] to the earth, there was set apart [a place for them]: they are gathered in and dwell therein.
[§452]* Also, before the incomplete and perverted spiritual formations [GAP-11] descended into the natural mind, there was set apart [GAP-12] a designated state of containment: they are gathered into it and remain confined therein.

[§453] Again, before the watchers rebelled and came down from heaven, a prison was fashioned and constructed for them in the depths of the earth, below the mountains.
[§453]* Again, before those who had possessed spiritual perception from on high rebelled and fell from their elevated state, a confining state of restraint was fashioned and established for them in the deepest regions of the natural mind, beneath even the elevated affections of the external person.

[§454] Before the children of the giants were born, they who had [no] knowledge of righteousness in them nor divinity, thirty-six cities were assigned and constructed for them wherein the children of [the giants would] live; they who would come to beget from each other, they who shall spend ten hundred years alive.
[§454]* Before the derivative persuasions born of monstrous self-intelligence came into being — they who had [GAP-13: REVIEW no] knowledge of genuine good within them nor anything of the Divine — a complete system of thirty-six doctrinal structures was designated and established for them, within which the offspring of [GAP-14: REVIEW monstrous self-intelligence would] dwell; those derivative falsities that would propagate endlessly from one another, and that would persist through a full and complete state of spiritual duration.

---

*Note on method:* The consistent pattern of this chapter — that containing states are always prepared *before* the evils they must hold — expresses the universal spiritual law that Divine Providence foresees every possible descent into evil and prepares the means of its restraint prior to its emergence. No falsity or evil enters the field of the mind without a corresponding boundary already established by the Divine to limit and ultimately contain it.

[§455] Before night and day were differentiated in the universe, the Third [Am]bassador was summoned. And afterwards, he was sent to this void and outer waste that he might purify the light, and separate night and day from each other.
[§455]* Before states of obscurity and states of enlightenment were distinguished from one another throughout the whole mind, a third divine proceeding was called forth from the Divine itself. And afterwards this power of discernment was sent into the emptiness and spiritual desolation of the external mind, so that it might purify wisdom from every contamination of falsity, and separate the state of spiritual darkness from the state of spiritual illumination, each from the other.

[§456] Before the light was released and freed and ascended through this zone, the Father of Life made beautiful for it the ships of light. He constructed them so that it might be purified in them, and be sculpted and fashioned in good order.
[§456]* Before wisdom was released from its bondage, and freed, and elevated through the successive degrees of the mind, the Divine Source of all spiritual life prepared for it vessels of truth made luminous and beautiful. These containing forms were ordered according to divine design, so that wisdom might be refined within them, and shaped and brought into perfect spiritual form according to heavenly order.

[§457] [Bef]ore Sin had multiplied and had made a kingdom in the flesh, before the acquitted and the condemn[ed] were set apart from each other, the Judge [w]as sent forth and revealed. His throne was created and placed [i]n the living atmosphere, so that he would be a just Judge, so that there the righteous and the sinners would be set apart and tested and separated from each other.
[§457]* Before evil had proliferated and established its dominion in the natural and corporeal mind, before those confirmed in good and those confirmed in evil were set apart from each other, the divine power of judgment was sent forth and made manifest. The seat of its authority was established and placed within the living sphere of spiritual life, so that it would render just discernment, so that there those who live from truth and good and those who live from falsity and evil would be set apart, and examined, and separated from each other.

[§458] Again, [befo]re mankind had been engendered, some were named ['rich' and] others 'poor' on earth [according to what is written] upon them. Before they die, they who will die are marked out by the stars and the signs of the zodiac in the sphere. They are appointed for them; in them are their births. And their root is bound up with their zodiacal signs; and they are compelled by them and brought to an equal judgement in accordance with their deeds and their sins.
[§458]* Again, before human minds had been brought into being, some were designated [GAP-1] as having abundance of knowledges and truths, and others as lacking them in the natural mind, [GAP-2] according to what was inscribed upon them. Before they undergo spiritual death, those who will perish inwardly are already determined by the fixed spiritual laws and the ordained states of influx in the order of providence. These states are appointed for them; within these states lie their spiritual origins. And their inmost nature is bound up with the spiritual conditions into which they were born; and they are governed by these conditions and brought to an impartial judgment in accordance with their works from love and their falsities from evil.

[§459] Again, before the universe is dissolved, [and the] entire earth, the destroyers were appointed and instructed for its dissolution.
[§459]* Again, before the entire spiritual dispensation is brought to its end, [GAP-3] and the whole natural mind with it, the powers of vastation were appointed and ordained for the dissolution of that state.

[§460] And before the ne[w] aeon was [built and] made be[auti]ful and marked out, [the] Great Builder [w]as summoned, the first architect and the angels who are with him, who would build and make beautiful the new aeon.
[§460]* And before the new spiritual state was [GAP-4] established and perfected in divine order and delineated according to its design, [GAP-5] the Divine Power that creates anew was called forth — the primary divine wisdom and the heavenly societies united with it — who would build and perfect the new heaven.

[§461] The wheel of the stars is a great mighty thing! Also, it is one among the four great things; since the whole ruling-power is assigned to four parts.
[§461]* The ordered cycle of all knowledges of good and truth is a great and mighty spiritual reality. It is one among the four fundamental domains of the mind, for the whole of dominion over spiritual states is distributed among four parts.

[§462] The first part is the powers who occur in the ten firmaments that exist above the wheel, and the ruling-power that is bound in them, as it is imprisoned within them in the heavens that are above.
[§462]* The first domain is the spiritual forces that dwell within the ten ascending degrees of heavenly consciousness that exist above the cycle of knowledges, and the power of evil that is held captive within them, imprisoned and restrained in those celestial states that are above.

[§463] The second part is the eight earths that are below; the four that are composite, and the four places of darkness. The second part extends from this earth, upon which mankind walks, towards the underside.
[§463]* The second domain is the eight natural and infernal states that lie below: the four that are of mixed quality, and the four states of pure falsity from evil. This second domain extends from the conscious natural mind, in which mankind dwells and walks, downward into the nether regions.

[§464] The third part is the walls and the vessels that surround the worlds. They are the four mountains and the three vessels; with what is in them, and the ruling-power that is trapped within them, and the powers who are masters over them.
[§464]* The third domain is the boundaries and the containing doctrinal forms that surround the spiritual worlds. These are the four elevated states of good and the three containing forms of doctrine, together with all that is held within them, and the power of evil that is confined within their structures, and the spiritual forces that exercise mastery over them.

[§465] The fourth part is the sphere of the stars that is appointed with great diligence, as it is ordered in a [great arrangement with]any [ordinances]; that of the Father of Life, [and that of the Mother of] the living.
[§465]* The fourth domain is the cycle of knowledges that was arranged with great providential care, as it is ordered in a [GAP-1: manifold spiritual arrangement encompassing] many [GAP-2: governing principles and correspondences]; the arrangement proceeding from the Divine Good that gives life, [GAP-3: REVIEW — and from the Divine Truth that brings forth] all living states.

[§466] Its [ordering was made with] very [great care] with diligence, because of the great and cruel powers, and the titanic figures, [who are bound] in it; they being suspended from it. So, all the first-born and the leaders of all the rulers, they who were existing in the three parts, these were garnered. They were collected together, seized, bound and assigned on this wheel. Each was apportioned by these three parts. They were distributed, entwined, and weighted against their companions of the same size.
[§466]* Its [GAP-4: structure was fashioned with] very great [GAP-5: providential precision] and care, because of the immense and merciless evils and the titanic forms of hereditary falsity [GAP-6: REVIEW — that are] bound within it, they being suspended from the cycle itself. Thus all the firstborn and chief evils, and the leaders among all the ruling falsities — those that had existed distributed throughout the three other domains — were gathered up. They were collected together, seized, bound, and assigned upon this cycle of knowledges. Each was apportioned out of those three domains. They were distributed, intertwined, and counterbalanced against their counterparts of equal force.

[§467] Still, for its part, the wheel is equal to them in its strength; the powers of the enmity who are bound on it. Indeed, they were suspended on the circling wheel because of their cruelty; so that, due to the wind and the motion in which they go round, they comprehend not the place wherein they are set. For if they had been bound in the heavens, the heavens would not have had the strength to support them. Again, if they had been assigned on the earths, on the fixtures that are below, they also would not have had the strength to support them. Rather, they were suspended from the wheel of the circling stars; so that the power and the light that should come up from below can be received, and given to the heavenly ones above them. Conversely, whatever will fall from above, whether cold or hot, the wheel of the sphere will also take it and cast it out upon the entire earth, upon all the wood.
[§467]* Nevertheless, for its part, the cycle of knowledges is equal in strength to those hostile powers that are bound upon it. Indeed, they were suspended upon the turning cycle because of the ferocity of their evil, so that, through the influx of spiritual life and the perpetual change of state in which they are carried round, they cannot comprehend or fix their awareness upon the condition in which they are held. For if they had been confined in the celestial states, those heavenly degrees would not have had the strength to bear them. Again, if they had been assigned upon the natural states, upon the fixed lower foundations, those also would not have had the strength to support them. Rather, they were suspended from the revolving cycle of knowledges, so that the wisdom and the truth that ascend from lower states can be received and transmitted to the celestial states above them. Conversely, whatever descends from above — whether states devoid of charity or states of consuming self-love — the revolving cycle also receives it and distributes it upon the whole of the natural mind, upon all perception.

[§468] Behold, the ordinance of the wheel is in this form. For the powers who are entwined on the wheel are like this: a king mastering his kingdom by this mighty legionary force of all the leaders of the kingdom; and with all the armour of the soldiery that is collected together for him; and also with the mass of the property of the kingdom; and the beautiful lustful aspect of the women who are collected together for him; together with [all the] property [of the land] and those cattle [and beasts] that have been distributed about among his retinue. Yet, if you ask about that king, he is matched with all his retinue. His retinue, for its part, is matched against his entire kingdom. Judgement shall come forth from him, vindication and condemnation, to his entire kingdom; by his wisdom, skill and strength.
[§468]* Behold, the spiritual order of the cycle is in this form. For the evil powers that are intertwined upon the cycle are like this: a ruling love governing its entire domain by the mighty combined force of all the governing principles of that domain; and with all the truths arrayed for combat and defense that are gathered to it; and also with the whole mass of spiritual possessions belonging to that domain; and the alluring beauty of the affections that are gathered to it; together with [GAP-7: all the] possessions [GAP-8: of natural good] and those natural affections [GAP-9: and desires] that have been distributed among its attendant states. Yet, if you inquire about that ruling love, it is matched in power with all its attendant states. Its attendant states, for their part, are matched against the whole domain. Judgment goes forth from it — vindication and condemnation — to its entire domain, by its wisdom, its skill, and its strength.

[§469] Now, just as for this king, who is matched against the measure of his entire kingdom by his wisdom, skill and strength; so also is it for the powers who are bound on the sphere as compared to the ruling-power, and the powers who exist in the entire zone. Like the king towards his retinue; so too is it that this totality is gathered to the sphere, to seven leaders, to twelve signs of the zodiac. As all the cities are gathered to the king, again they gather to the leaders of the universe. All that is done in the universe above and below: the battles and the disorders and the captivity and the hunger and the lust and the property; so shall they increase and diminish through these leaders. They are the motivaters of the whole created order.
[§469]* Now, just as this ruling love is matched against the measure of its entire domain by its wisdom, skill, and strength, so also is it for the evil powers that are bound upon the cycle of knowledges as compared to the dominion and the spiritual forces that exist throughout the whole zone of creation. As the ruling love stands toward its attendant states, so too all that exists is gathered to the cycle — to seven governing principles, to twelve fundamental states of the complete spiritual round. As all systems of doctrine are gathered to the ruling love, again they gather to the governing principles of the spiritual universe. All that is done in the spiritual world above and below — the conflicts and the disorders and the captivities and the deprivations and the concupiscences and the possessions — all these increase and diminish through these governing principles. They are the movers of the whole created order.

[§470] There are three conduits that exist through the entire zone, from the abyss to its heights.
[§470]* There are three pathways of influx that exist through every degree of the mind, from its lowest sensory darkness to its highest celestial states.

[§471] The first conduit is the root of all the powers above, they that exist in all the heavens. They are cast off and fettered on the earths below; because the earth below is the 'stripping off', the garment and the body of the powers that are above the heavens.
[§471]* The first pathway is the root of all the interior powers above — those that dwell in every celestial degree. They are cast out and bound fast into the natural levels below; for the natural mind is the "stripping away," the outermost truth-covering and corporeal vessel of the celestial powers that exist above in the interior heavens.

[§472] For when the Father of Life constructed the heavenly worlds, he divested them of their bodies, [he] threw them to the earths below.
[§472]* For when Divine Love ordered the celestial states, he separated from them their outermost corporeal forms, [GAP-1: and the Divine creative act] cast those forms down into the natural levels below.

[§473] So, the upper worlds are of the soul and the spirit; but the worlds below are of the body and the carcass [of the powers above] Now as for this, he [bound a root from] the powers of heaven; he sealed it on their bodies and their carcasses that are on the earths, so that when life comes up from the fullness of these earths, it can be entirely drawn up to the heights in the roots of what is heavenly, that are fastened in their bodies. And also, all life can be purified in that place.
[§473]* Thus the interior states belong to the will and the understanding; but the outermost states belong to the corporeal form and the lifeless vessel. [GAP-2: …] Now as for this, he [GAP-3: …] the powers of the interior heavens; he sealed a bond of correspondence upon their corporeal forms and lifeless vessels that are in the natural levels, so that when the good of life rises from the fullness of these natural states, it can be entirely drawn upward into the heights through the roots of what is celestial that are fastened in those corporeal forms. And also, all the good of life can be purified in that degree.

[§474] However, the waste that will be swept from the purified one who ascends to the heights will be let fall to the ground through the conduits of these conduits, and will be poured down [to the depths] and thrown to the underside.
[§474]* However, the falsity and evil that will be separated out from the one who is purified and ascends to the heights will be let fall to the natural ground through the descending channels of these pathways, and will be poured down [GAP-4: …] and cast to the underside — the infernal degree.

[§475] This is the first conduit, which comes from the powers of the heavens to their bodies and their carcasses that are upon the earth; also, from their bodies and [their] carcasses that are on the earths to the powers of the heavens.
[§475]* This is the first pathway of influx, which extends from the celestial powers to their corporeal forms and lifeless vessels upon the natural level; and likewise from their corporeal forms and [GAP-5: their] lifeless vessels in the natural degree back up to the celestial powers of the interior heavens.

[§476] The second conduit is the one that comes from the temples and dwellings and cities that exist in the heavens down to the ground, to the five shapes of tree that grow upon the earth. The life comes up from the trees to the temples and cities. Life too, and the residue of the heavenly ones, comes down to the trees in a conduit.
[§476]* The second pathway is the one that descends from the doctrinal forms, habitations of good, and systems of truth that exist in the interior heavens downward to the natural level, to the five orders of perception that grow upon the natural mind. The good of life ascends from perceptions up to the doctrinal forms and systems of truth. And likewise, the good of life together with the residual substance of the celestial beings descends to perceptions through this same influx pathway.

[§477] And the third conduit is all the powers and householders who dwell in all the firmaments, whose roots come down from them to the five worlds of flesh that creep upon the earth, being fixed to one another. Now, the power and the life that shall be gathered in from the worlds of flesh are dispersed among them in various shapes. The powers above shall draw them to the upper side through conduits.
[§477]* And the third pathway is all the affections and governing loves who dwell in every rational degree — whose roots descend from them to the five orders of proprial will that creep upon the natural mind, being bound to one another. Now, the interior power and the good of life that shall be gathered from the orders of proprial will are dispersed among them in diverse forms. The interior powers above shall draw them to the higher degree through influx pathways.

[§478] [The was]te too, and the lust and the evil-doing and the anger that will be greater in the powers of [heave]n, shall be poured to the ground through their various conduits. They shall be discharged upon mankind and the other remaining animals. When what is heavenly will wash the waste and the stench and the poison down on the creations of the flesh below, in their turn the creations shall be greater in lust and anger and evil-doing against each other through the action of their fathers who are on high.
[§478]* Yet the falsity, the concupiscence, the evil-doing, and the wrath that grow more intense in the perverted powers of the interior [heave]ns shall be poured down to the natural level through their various channels. They shall be discharged upon the human proprium and upon all the remaining animal affections. When what is interior washes its corruption and its spiritual stench and its poison down upon the corporeal creations of the proprial will below, those creations in their turn shall grow more powerful in concupiscence, wrath, and evil-doing against each other — through the inflowing action of their originating powers on high.

[§479] Still, look at the wheel of the stars! It has no [root in this earth], but its roots are bound in the totality. The wheel of stars, then, receives life through the power and the life that ascend from the earths through conduits and the firmaments above. It (also) takes life from those conduits that receive up to the firmaments and the heavenly worlds above it, it takes life out from that place.
[§479]* Yet consider the revolving cycle of natural knowledges! It has no [GAP-6: root in the natural mind], but its roots are bound into the totality of all things. The cycle of knowledges, then, receives life through the interior power and the good of life that ascend from the natural levels through influx pathways and the rational degrees above. It also takes life from those channels that receive upward to the rational degrees and the celestial states above it — it draws life out from that place.

[§480] In that the wheel has no root in this earth, from where did the stars and signs of the zodiac find this authority? They became masters over these five fleshes and five trees. Every thing, if it can be reduced from the earth, the heavenly powers above draw it through a conduit. Also, every thing that a person will attain, whether it be wealth that comes into his possession or poverty that will accrue to him, or his sickness and health; he attains it through the signs of the zodiac and the star under which he shall be born.
[§480]* In that the cycle of knowledges has no root in the natural mind, from where did the knowledges and the signs of ordered doctrine find this authority? They became masters over the five orders of proprial will and the five orders of perception. Every element of good, if it can be extracted from the natural level, the celestial powers above draw it through an influx pathway. Also, every condition a person will attain — whether it be abundance that comes into his possession or deprivation that will accrue to him, or his sickness and his wholeness — he attains it through the signs of ordered doctrine and the particular knowledge under which his natural mind was formed at birth.

[§481] the wheel has no root therein. It is set aright in its position. For when the Living Spirit, the Father of Life, constructed the heavens and the firmaments above, he bound their roots into the earths below; in their carcasses that he had stripped from them. Yet, when he constructed the wheel, [he] did not bind its root into the earths, but he bound the root of the wheel by the sea giant.
[§481]* The cycle of knowledges has no root therein. It is established in its own position. For when the Divine Proceeding — the Divine Love in its creative function — ordered the interior heavens and the rational degrees above, he bound their roots into the natural levels below, into those corporeal forms that he had stripped from them. Yet when he ordered the cycle of knowledges, [GAP-7: the Divine Proceeding] did not bind its root into the natural levels, but rather he bound the root of the cycle to the monstrous aggregate of natural falsities.

[§482] And when [the] Ambassador came, he displayed his image and purified and brought the light out from all [the] heavens. Matter, however, which is present in all the rulers, spurted out against the image of the Ambassador. It went up in order to reach it, but reached it not. It dropped back and fell to the [under]side. Now, when it had fallen from above, it separated out into three parts.
[§482]* And when [GAP-8: the] Divine Human came forth, he displayed his image and purified and brought the light of wisdom out from all [GAP-9: the] interior heavens. But the evil of the proprium, which is present in all the ruling loves, erupted against the image of the Divine Human. It rose up seeking to reach it, but could not reach it. It fell back and dropped to the infernal underside. Now, when it had fallen from the heights, it separated out into three parts.

[§483] The first part fell to the wheel; another part fell to the earth; an[other part fell to the se]a. [By means] of the part that fell to the sphere, [the r]oot was bound in the trees and flesh. Therefore, because of this, they received authority and became masters in the five worlds of the flesh and the five worlds of the tree.
[§483]* The first part fell into the cycle of knowledges; another part fell to the natural mind; an[other part fell to the aggregate of natural fals]ities. [GAP-10: By means] of the part that fell to the revolving cycle, [the r]oot of evil was bound into perceptions and proprial will. Therefore, because of this, the knowledges received authority and became masters in the five orders of proprial will and the five orders of perception.

[§484] Through the nature that came down [upo]n the wheel the root was bound in the trees. Also, by means of the abortions that came to the ground, another root was bound in the fleshes.
[§484]* Through the nature of evil that descended [upo]n the cycle of knowledges, the root of domination was bound into perceptions. Also, by means of the perverted goods that failed to come to spiritual birth and that fell to the natural level, another root was bound in the orders of proprial will.

[§485] Therefore, because of this, the stars and signs of the zodiac received authority over the trees and the fleshes: through the root of Sin that fell down; and [the ro]ot of the abortions that were aborted there, they fell to the ground.
[§485]* Therefore, because of this, the knowledges and signs of ordered doctrine received authority over perceptions and proprial states: through the root of self-love that fell down, and through [the ro]ot of the perverted goods that were aborted there — they fell to the natural ground.

[§486] However, the[se] other conduits that [the] Living Spirit has bound in the e[a]rths, they that the soul of the elements comes up through to the heavens; those (conduits) are not bound to the wheel. Rather, they are plundered from them by the powers on the wheel, that hang from it.
[§486]* However, the[se] other influx pathways that [GAP-11: the] Divine Proceeding has bound in the n[atur]al levels — those through which the interior essence of the elemental affections ascends to the celestial states — those pathways are not bound to the cycle of knowledges. Rather, they are plundered from them by the powers upon the cycle that hang from it.

[§487] The first is the one that comes from the firmaments to the earths, and the one that comes from the wheel to the sea giant. That is only one. So, the other root is the one that comes [fr]om [the temples] to the trees. The third is bound by means of the abortions in the f[leshes]; also in the fleshes the fathers of the abortions who are in the heavens [by their roots] are bound and twined, and the [root] that [is bound in them]
[§487]* The first is the one that descends from the rational degrees to the natural levels, and the one that descends from the cycle to the monstrous aggregate of falsities — that is one single pathway. So, the other root is the one that comes [fr]om [GAP-12: the doctrinal forms] to perceptions. The third is bound by means of the perverted goods in the proprial st[ates]; also in the proprial states the originating powers of the perverted goods who are in the interior heavens [GAP-13: …] are bound and intertwined, and the [GAP-14: …] that [GAP-15: …]

[§488] about the conduits, the one that is greater than its [partn]er [is so] as more life and soul is drawn [through it] than its partner.
[§488]* Concerning the influx pathways, the one that is greater than its [count]erpart [GAP-16: …] as more good of life and interior essence is drawn [GAP-17: …] than its counterpart.

[§489] great [is the root of] the earth from which much life ascends [to the heights] the Father of Life has drawn them. He has fastened them from the powers [above and] he has bound them in the earths. (These conduits) are the roots of the ent[ire] ruling-power that the life of the living soul is drawn up through, it coming up from the whole earth, from all the mountains, fields and islands.
[§489]* Great [GAP-18: …] the natural level from which much good of life ascends [GAP-19: …] Divine Love has drawn them. He has fastened them from the interior powers [GAP-20: …] he has bound them into the natural levels. These influx pathways are the roots of the ent[ire] governing order through which the good of the living interior essence is drawn upward, ascending from the whole natural mind, from all elevated states of celestial perception, open fields of understanding, and isolated truths amid the obscurity of the sensory level.

[§490] After that first great root is the root of the trees, which is bound to the temples and the buildings above. For there also is much great life coming up from the tree and the entire Cross, being drawn up from them to the upper side like a great host.
[§490]* After that first great root comes the root of perceptions, which is bound to the doctrinal forms and the structural truths above. For there also is much great life ascending from perceptions and the entire cross of suffering — that is, the good dispersed and bound throughout nature — being drawn upward from them to the interior degree like a great host.

[§491] What, however, is lesser [than these] of their conduit [through which] he takes up [life from] the roots [bound in] the earths, they [are present in] the whole earth in all the worlds. Again, these of the trees are present in the whole earth, whether small or great; but the root of mankind [and the fleshes] is not present in the whole earth, other than only this world of the south. Therefore, even now these fleshes do not make [their dwelling] in the entire world of the south [but rather] there is a small part of them in [the region] of the upper side of the world of the south in [being lesser] in number than the [trees]
[§491]* What, however, is lesser [GAP-21: …] of their influx pathway [GAP-22: …] he draws upward [GAP-23: …] the roots [GAP-24: …] the natural levels, they [GAP-25: …] the whole natural mind in all the degrees. Again, those of perceptions are present throughout the whole natural mind, whether small or great; but the root of the human proprium [GAP-26: …] is not present in the whole natural mind, other than only in this realm of the external will. Therefore, even now, these proprial states do not [GAP-27: …] in the entire realm of the external will [GAP-28: …] there is a small part of them in [GAP-29: …] of the upper portion of the realm of the external will in [GAP-30: …] in number than the [GAP-31: …]

[§492] they are bound by their [roots and conduits] with one another. They stir one another, they draw life from one another through the action of the powers above, which stirs the nature that dwells in them all.
[§492]* They are bound by their [GAP-32: roots …] with one another. They stir one another; they draw the good of life from one another through the action of the interior powers above, which stirs the inherited nature dwelling in them all.

[§493] Now, when the Matter that is in them will be crushed and despoiled, then that Matter too shall [stir up] its members and overturn them and [devour] each other [and shall] stir [the nature in them]
[§493]* Now, when the evil of the proprium that is in them will be crushed and despoiled, then that evil also shall [GAP-33: …] its constituent members and overturn them and [GAP-34: …] each other [GAP-35: …] stir [GAP-36: …]

---

### Note on the overall spiritual sense

This chapter describes the complete architecture of influx between the interior (celestial-spiritual) and the exterior (natural-corporeal) degrees of the human mind. Three reciprocal pathways connect what is highest to what is lowest:

**The first** links celestial affections directly to their outermost sensory-corporeal forms — the pathway by which life ascends from the body of the natural mind and by which purified good is drawn inward while falsity and evil are cast downward.

**The second** links doctrinal structures (temples, cities) to perceptions (trees) — the pathway of understanding, by which truth-as-known rises into truth-as-organized.

**The third** links governing rational loves to proprial will (flesh) — the pathway by which the selfhood's life is gathered, refined, and either elevated or, through corrupt influx from perverted interior states, made worse.

The *cycle of knowledges* (the zodiacal wheel) is a special case: it has no root in the natural mind proper but gained dominion over both perceptions and proprial will when the evil of the proprium, recoiling from the manifestation of the Divine Human, fragmented and fell into three degrees — contaminating the cycle of natural knowing, the natural mind itself, and the aggregate of sensory falsities. This is the origin of fatalistic dominion: natural knowledge rules over perception and self-will *only through the root of inherited evil*, not through any legitimate correspondence with the Divine order.

[§494] How does the wheel circle over the conduits, which extend from the heavens to the earths, and it not cut them? Or why shall the conduits not get entangled in with the wheel as it circles and drives [onward through the u]niverse?
[§494]* How does the cycle of Providence revolve through the channels of influx, which extend from interior heavenly states down into exterior natural states, without severing them? And why do the channels of influx not become entangled with the revolving progression of states as it turns and drives [GAP-1] through the whole of the created spiritual world?

[§495] The reason that the conduits shall not be cut, for they are not cut, is because they are spiritual. Thus the conduits are like the waters through which ships sail. You shall find the prow that is in position in the front part of the ship divides the waters, casting them back and forth. So, when it parts the waters and that ship opens up the water with its cleaving prow, immediately and without delay the waters mingle with each other again behind the ship; and the path of the ship shall not be discernible in the midst of the waters.
[§495]* The reason the channels of influx are not severed — for indeed they cannot be severed — is that they are of spiritual substance. The channels of influx are like truths through which systems of doctrine pass. One finds that the leading intellectual faculty, positioned at the fore of a doctrinal system, penetrates into truths, distinguishing them to one side and the other. And when that faculty parts the truths, and the doctrinal system opens its way through truth by means of its penetrating understanding, immediately and without delay the truths flow together and reunite behind the doctrinal system; and the passage of doctrine leaves no disruption discernible in the body of truth itself.

[§496] Just as when a bird flies up in the air, for its path is not visible in the air, and in the way of mankind, in the sun within the conduits. They also shall not be tangled up in them, and neither shall you find their path through the air known.
[§496]* In the same way, when a thought rises into the rational atmosphere of the mind, its path cannot be traced in that atmosphere. And so it is with the human will and understanding, moving within the Divine love and wisdom that operates through the channels of influx. They also are not entangled in those channels, nor shall their passage through the rational sphere be found to leave any mark.

[§497] This is also the case with the wheel. It turns through the conduits and the air. The wheel shall not cut the conduits, nor are the conduits themselves entangled in the wheel.
[§497]* So it is likewise with the cycle of Providence. It revolves through the channels of influx and through the rational atmosphere of the mind. The cycle of Providence does not sever the channels of influx, nor are the channels of influx themselves entangled in its revolution.

---

*Note on the whole passage:* The teaching is that Divine influx — flowing continuously from interior states to exterior states — is of such spiritual nature that no cyclical operation, no progressive change of state, no passage of doctrine or thought through it, can interrupt or deform it. It restores itself perfectly, as truths restore their unity after being distinguished by understanding. The spiritual substance of influx is self-healing; Providence moves through it without trace or harm.

[§498] This one, a name is given to him in the world. For people pronounce it with their mouths: 'god', 'rich one', and 'angel'. Who are they? Who are the gods? Or who are the rich ones? Or who are the angels? Who are these [three] archetypes?
[§498]* To this reality a quality is attributed in the natural mind. For people confess it outwardly with their speech: "Divine good," "abundance of truth," and "receptive form of wisdom." But what are these in themselves? What is Divine good in its essence? What is the abundance of truth? What is the receptive form of wisdom? What are these [GAP-1: three degrees of spiritual proceeding]?

[§499] There are these three archetypes [in the land of light, and there is no measure to] them!
[§499]* These three degrees of spiritual proceeding exist [GAP-2: in the realm of Divine wisdom, and there is no limit to] them.

[§500] So, the ones who are called 'god', are the gods whom the Father has summoned from himself. He has established them after the likeness of his greatness.
[§500]* Those realities called "Divine good" are the goods that the Divine Love brought forth from Itself. He established them according to the form of His own infinite goodness.

[§501] Conversely, these who are named 'rich [ones]', are the evocations of the first rich gods of the Father; because, when the Father had summoned (the first), they themselves summoned evocations. They called them 'rich'.
[§501]* Conversely, those that are named "abundances of truth [GAP-3: derived from good]" are the derivative states proceeding from the first goods of Divine Love; because when the Divine Love had brought forth the primary goods, those goods themselves brought forth derivative states of truth. These are called "abundances."

[§502] They too, the rich ones, have summoned their evocations. They call them 'angel'. These are the three archetypes who occur in the land of light. There is no measure to apply to them!
[§502]* These abundances of truth, in turn, brought forth their own derivative states, which are called "receptive forms of wisdom." These are the three degrees of proceeding that exist in the realm of Divine wisdom. They are infinite and cannot be delimited.

[§503] Other persons who are named 'god' are the emanations who have come from the Father; the evocations of the Father whom he summoned forth. They came out to the contest and humiliated the enmity.
[§503]* Other realities called "Divine good" are the emanations that have come forth from the Divine Love — the goods that He brought forth and sent out into spiritual combat, where they overcame the opposition of evil and falsity.

[§504] Conversely, the 'rich ones' are the evocations of these first living words. They too, the rich ones, have come and performed and fulfilled the pleasure of the greatness, in the worlds that are above and below.
[§504]* Conversely, the "abundances of truth" are derivative states proceeding from those first living Divine truths. These abundances of truth have likewise come forth and accomplished and fulfilled the purpose of the Divine, in both the interior and the exterior states of life.

[§505] On the other hand, the ones who are named the 'angels' are the evocations of the rich ones, who had come from the three living words. They have been sent in an embassy and an apostolate to this building. They have come to the entire divinity, which is established in silence and in hiddenness. And, also, they have come to all the souls who have been entangled in the enemy. They have brought them hope and confidence.
[§505]* And those called "receptive forms of wisdom" are the derivative truths proceeding from the abundances, which had come forth from the three living Divine truths. They have been sent as a mission and an apostolate into this constructed spiritual order. They have come to all that is Divine within the soul which dwells in stillness and in concealment — that is, to the inmost good not yet made manifest. And they have also come to all the affections that have been entangled in evil. They have brought to those affections hope and trust.

[§506] Again, they are called 'god', all the gods who belong to the household of the Great Spirit.
[§506]* Again, "Divine good" is the name given to all the goods belonging to the province of the Divine Proceeding as Life.

[§507] Conversely, the ones who are called 'angel', are all the angels who belong to the household of the Beloved of the Lights.
[§507]* Conversely, "receptive forms of wisdom" is the name given to all the truths that receive and transmit, belonging to the province of the Lord as the Beloved of all wisdoms.

[§508] The ones who are called 'rich', are all the rich ones who belong to the household of the glorious Ambassador.
[§508]* And "abundances of truth" is the name given to all the knowledges belonging to the province of the glorious Mediating Presence — the Lord as He stands between the Divine itself and the recipient forms below, communicating the fullness of truth into every degree of the spiritual order.

---

*Note on structure:* The teaching unfolds one doctrine through three applications — first as an eternal reality within the Divine wisdom itself (¶3–6), then as an active proceeding into spiritual combat and redemption (¶7–9), and finally as a living arrangement within the heavenly kingdom under three governing principles (¶10–12). This maps precisely to what Swedenborg calls the celestial, spiritual, and natural degrees of a single Divine proceeding — good begetting truth, truth begetting receptive forms, each degree infinite in itself.

[§509] When the [First] Man came forth against the ene[my, clothed in a] garment [against] the Darkness with the garment of [the] living fire. With that garment he withered the tumescence (?) of the enemy; he brought down i[t]s foolery and its vanity.
[§509]* When the [GAP-1] celestial good first proceeding from the Divine went forth into combat against the assault of [GAP-2] evil and falsity, clothed in an external truth [GAP-3] against the realm of falsity from evil, clothed in the truth-form of [GAP-4] celestial love itself — by that truth-form animated by love it withered the inflation of self-love in the adversary; it cast down the insanity and the emptiness of falsity.

---

**¶-3:** Now, celestial love alone was sufficient to subdue them. Yet, because celestial love in its proceeding was weakened through contact with what opposed it, the Divine adjoined to it four further truth-coverings: that of spiritual thought, that of natural truth, that of the understanding of wisdom, and that of the rational sphere of spiritual life. And why were these four given? They were given so that they would serve as supports to that divine principle which is celestial love; so that [GAP-5] falsity from evil would not [GAP-6] overwhelm celestial love and penetrate into it; and so that the destructive falsity and the opposition of evil would not sever it from its source.

[§510] Now, this one alone was sufficient for them. [Y]et, si[n]ce the living fire was weakened, because of this he added [to it another fou]r garments: that of the wind, that of the water, that of the light, and that of the a[i]r. And this, (why) has he given the four? He gave them s[o] th[a]t they would be helpe[r]s to t[h]at god, wh[ich i]s the living fire; so that [the enemy would] not with [in] the living f[ire], and penetrate it; and [the po]ison and the [enmi]ty cut it off.

[§511] While the fire received a bl[ow and] a wound in its body; yet still, [were weakened] all the powers of Da[rk]ness because of it [Yet also] It [was of] necessity [for its] brothers, to cause them to receive a blow and a woun[d to]o. The power of the en/emy mixed in with them. Rath[er, ex]cept [the air]. The Darkness did not mix in with it, nor was the burning of the enemy able to penet/rate it.
[§511]* While celestial love received a blow and a wound in its external form, yet still [GAP-7] all the powers of falsity from evil were restrained because of its sacrifice. [GAP-8] It [GAP-9] was by necessity [GAP-10] that its coordinate faculties also had to receive harm and wounding. The potency of evil was adulterated into them. However, except [GAP-11] the rational sphere of spiritual life — falsity from evil did not adulterate it, nor was the consuming fire of self-love able to penetrate into it.

[§512] Never-theless, even the one that remained behind without the wound, which is the air, it stripped off garment and power. It gave it to its [fo]ur brothers in its place; so that by that [ga]rment of the living air they would become strong, and [receive] profit, and endure, namely its other four brothers. And it [be]came for them [a spirit of] life.
[§512]* Nevertheless, even the one that remained behind without wound — which is the rational sphere of spiritual life — it relinquished its own truth-covering and its own power. It communicated these to its four coordinate faculties in its own stead, so that by the truth-form of the living rational sphere they would be strengthened, and [GAP-12] would receive spiritual benefit, and would endure — namely, its other four coordinate faculties. And it [GAP-13] became for them the inmost vivifying principle — their very life.

[§513] [Now as to what affects the] light, behold, at the beginning the Darkness was strong against it; [for] no disunity [or discord] exists in the light.
[§513]* [GAP-1] wisdom — behold, in the first state, falsity from evil was powerful against it; [GAP-2] no division [GAP-3] exists within wisdom.

[§514] [See then] how far [extends the lig]ht that came forth from [the Greatness] in that place in a [unity] alone [abiding in] the greatness of the light with one another for ever, and nor did one of them transgress the command and order that his brother had entrusted to him forever.
[§514]* [GAP-4] how far [GAP-5] the wisdom that proceeded forth from [GAP-6] in that state in a [GAP-7] alone [GAP-8] the fullness of wisdom in mutual conjunction forever, nor did any single truth transgress the order and arrangement that its coordinate truth had committed to it from eternity.

This passage describes the interior nature of divine wisdom: every truth that proceeds from the Divine dwells in perfect mutual conjunction with every other, and no truth departs from the order that belongs to it by influx from its kindred truth. In wisdom there is no self-contradiction, because all truths regard one end and flow from one origin.

[§515] However, the Darkness, the enemy, was doomed and overcome; because of the arising of disharmony it was divided within itself. Even the kingdom was divided within itself; [and] ev[en] the [powers, who had] left their abyss behind and come up fo[llow-ing] it, were doomed because of the disunity that was present in all of them. The Darkness was destroyed, and its other powers were humiliated
[§515]* However, falsity from evil, the adversary, was destined to perish and to be overthrown; because of the arising of internal disharmony it was divided against itself. Even the ruling principle of evil was divided against itself; [GAP-9] ev[en] the [GAP-10] agencies of evil, which had risen out of their infernal depth and ascended in pursuit of it, were destined to perish because of the self-contradiction present in all of them. Falsity from evil was destroyed, and its remaining agencies were brought into subjection.

This passage sets forth the essential law: evil and falsity contain within themselves the cause of their own dissolution. Because falsity does not regard one end but many competing self-loves, it cannot sustain internal unity. Every dominion built upon evil divides against itself, for each selfish affection within it seeks its own supremacy. The agencies of evil that rise out of the lowest infernal states carry this disunity inherently, and therefore their assault upon wisdom necessarily fails — not because wisdom exerts force against them, but because falsity, having no internal coherence, collapses under the weight of its own contradictions. Wisdom endures because all its truths are in mutual love; falsity perishes because all its forms are in mutual enmity.

[§516] At the time when the First Man was sent to the war in the first garment of [living fire], which he gave to the first nature that exists [...] in that single garment. [By it] he hunted Matter, the thought of death, [its rulers] and all its other powers.
[§516]* At the time when the Divine Human was sent forth into spiritual combat clothed in the first encompassing truth of [GAP-1] ardent divine love, which He imparted to the primal state of the will that exists [GAP-2] within that single encompassing truth — [GAP-3] He laid bare and captured the proprium, the very intention of spiritual death, [GAP-4] and all its subordinate faculties of evil.

[§517] When the First Man came up from the abyss, [...] the Living Spirit brought the rulers whom he had hunted [from every] element, that [substance] of [the land of] darkness, that he might construct from them this universe and fashion everything appropriate for the greatness. From that time on, the place of the storehouses of the elements [was left behind, and] he destroyed and laid waste the [land and] all that were in it.
[§517]* When the Divine Human ascended out of the deepest hell, [GAP-5] the Divine Truth proceeding from Good brought forth the ruling falsities that He had captured [GAP-6] as constituent states, those [GAP-7] of [GAP-8] the realm of falsity from evil, so that from them this spiritual world might be constructed and all things ordered according to the Divine design. From that time onward, the reservoirs in which evils and falsities had been stored [GAP-9] — He destroyed and laid waste the [GAP-10] and all that had been contained within them.

[§518] Just like a marsh, of which the fish and birds are snared in a net, and only the waters are left behind and the reeds and the plants that grow in it, this is also the case with the storehouses of death. The [First] Man snared their counsels and [their k]in that were [in them], and the storehouses were left behind in their [dwelli]ng place.
[§518]* Just as a stagnant mingling of truth and falsity, from which the natural affections and the thoughts are captured and drawn out by the net of discernment, and only the residual corrupted truths and the lowest natural perceptions rooted in that state remain behind — so it is with the reservoirs of spiritual death. The [GAP-11] Divine Human captured their deliberate intentions of evil and the derivative evils that were [GAP-12] contained within them, and the reservoirs were left behind in their own proper state.

[§519] So then, the case of the land of darkness is like this, as is that of the storehouses that were in it.
[§519]* Thus the condition of the realm of falsity from evil is such, and so too the reservoirs that existed within it.

[§520] Just as the body of man has many limbs in it, [the land of darkness] has [storehouses from] its beginning [like] all [the limbs] that hang from the great head of the [body]. A single root is the root of them all, because they all hang from the great head that is on top of the body. Should a limb be cut from the tip of [the]se small [h]eads, the great head that is on top of them [al]l stays in its place the way that it is [and still] you find this person alive. Al[l] these sm[all h]eads are firmly set in it [...]
[§520]* Just as the organized form of a person's life contains many particular faculties within it, so [GAP-13] the realm of falsity from evil contains [GAP-14] reservoirs of evil from its very origin — [GAP-15] all [GAP-16] the particular faculties that depend from the one supreme ruling love of self, which is the [GAP-17] governing principle of the whole organized evil. A single origin is the origin of them all, because they all depend from that supreme ruling self-love which presides over the whole form. Should any particular faculty be severed from the extremity of [th]ese lesser ruling evils, the supreme ruling love that presides over them [al]l remains in its place just as it is — [GAP-18] and one finds that the organized evil still lives. Al[l] these subordinate ruling evils are firmly rooted within it. [GAP-19]

[§521] Wh[en] they will cut from them [the limbs of] all of them, these little [heads] shall [become] the corpses, because they might see [that they] be firmly set [...] in the body, which they joined wi[th it ...]
[§521]* Wh[en] they shall sever from them [GAP-20] all of them, these lesser [GAP-21] ruling evils shall [GAP-22] become as states devoid of all spiritual life, because they might perceive [GAP-23] that they are firmly established [GAP-24] within the organized form of evil, with which they have been conjoined. [GAP-25]

[§522] In exactly this way, it is also the case that the storehouses of darkness are the same, the things from which the [darkness gushed] forth.
[§522]* In precisely this manner, the reservoirs of falsity from evil are the same — the sources from which [GAP-26] falsity from evil poured forth into existence.

---

*Note on method:* The "marsh" image (¶3) is among the most precise correspondences in this passage — stagnant water being truth that does not flow from its Divine source, mingled with the natural growths (reeds, plants) of the unregenerate external mind. The "body with many heads" image (¶5–6) corresponds to the Swedenborgian teaching that all particular evils are organized under a single ruling love of self, as members under a head, and that removing subordinate evils does not destroy the ruling love until that ruling love itself is overturned.

[§523] At the time when the F[ir]st [Man went down] he gave the leadership to his five [sons. Then the] Living Spirit and the Mother of Life [came down] as the entire ruling-power is entwined and crucified [in the Darkness, and] they were poured out and mixed with each other, the light with the Darkness and the Darkness with the light.

[§524] At that time the Living Spirit called one of his sons, one rich with power. He gave to him the leadership of the garments. Now, he himself [received the command] of [the garments, and] he hung them and set them up [by the command] of the Father of Life [who sent him. And] he set up all things [in the heights, and] he opened [out the] ten firmaments [and he set] the wheel of the stars [above] them and the [luminaries].
