[§525] The Living Spirit is like this: Just as [a builder] and an architect who might build a dwelling for the king, and divide it up into separate houses and [separate rooms, and provides] everything that is required for beauty [in every room] and he cleans them. And also the stones [he shapes] them for beauty and loveliness, and he arranges them and makes them so that should the king come and inhabit that palace, he might glorify him.

[§526] Furthermore, the Father of Life resembles a wise and skilled carpenter [who finds a piece of wood] before it was worked [or shaped, and] before it was established and made beautiful and [useful. This] carpenter [knows what it shall become, and] works in it, but [it is] in him in his [thought and] in his understanding, and he brings the saw and the adze, and cuts it into separate pieces [and he trims] it and he pulls the [roughness from] it with the plane and he smooths it. He shall make many [kinds of] wooden implements, arranging them shape by shape; he cuts and produces wooden implements from it, wooden implements of different kinds, (such as) dishes, bowls. This wood, that [had no form] since previously it had not been worked before it was sealed by [the ski]ll and wisdom of this dextrous carpenter. He cut it up into different sorts of implements useful to his master.

[§527] So is it also with the Father of Life. In his wisdom he has [formed all things] in limbs [and built the] palace [and placed all things] in it or in a [vessel] useful [to him. He made] the ships of light [and] all the other works [that he established] in the [universe] so that when the great king comes, he who is the Third Ambassador, who [shall come to it] he might dwell in this universe [and purify it]

[§528] [And all things shall] live. The light, however, that is in it [he who is] wise, and he takes it up to the land; but the waste and the Darkness will be gathered and thrown down to the abyss.

[§523–§528]* **¶1**

At the time when the Divine Human as it first proceeded forth [GAP-1: into the natural and sensual degrees of creation] entrusted governance to the five essential faculties of the spiritual mind [GAP-2: through which it operates — and when] the Divine Rational Principle and the Celestial Matrix of all affectional life [GAP-3: together descended into the lower degrees] — for the whole dominion of divine order had become interwoven with natural and sensual states, and the good within it was bound fast as upon a cross [GAP-4: within the realm of opposition] — all things were diffused and commingled with one another: wisdom with falsity and falsity with wisdom.

---

**¶2**

At that time the Divine Rational Principle summoned one of its subordinate faculties, one abundantly endowed with spiritual power, and granted it governance over the external truths that clothe the interior life. Now this faculty itself [GAP-5: received its commission] of [GAP-6: the ordering of all outward forms, and] suspended them in their proper arrangement and established them [GAP-7: according to the design] of the Divine Providence [GAP-8: from which all order descends, and] set all things into their rightful stations [GAP-9: within the spiritual cosmos, and] opened [GAP-10: the successive degrees of] the ten discrete planes of spiritual order [GAP-11: through which influx descends, and arranged] the revolving system of knowledges of good and truth [GAP-12: so that each knowledge might illuminate according to its station among] them, and the [GAP-13: whole fabric of the ordered mind was established].

---

**¶3**

The Divine Rational Principle operates in this manner: Just as [GAP-14: a master builder] and an ordering intelligence might construct a dwelling-place for the Lord Himself, and divide it into distinct faculties and chambers of the mind, and [GAP-15: distinct degrees of reception,] providing everything that is needful for the form of heavenly good [GAP-16: within each faculty,] and purifies them — and also the foundational natural truths [GAP-17: are shaped and fitted among] them for the form of good and its beauty — and arranges and perfects them so that when the Lord comes and inhabits that mind as His dwelling, it might glorify Him through its reception of His life.

---

**¶4**

Furthermore, the Divine Providence resembles a wise and skilled former of the natural mind [GAP-18: who perceives the unformed natural good] before it was wrought upon [GAP-19: by any ordering truth,] before it was established and made beautiful and [GAP-20: brought into the form of use. This wise] former of the natural [GAP-21: already perceives what each capacity may become, and] works within it; yet [GAP-22: the finished form does not yet exist in the material, but exists] in Him, in His [GAP-23: foresight and] in His understanding. And He brings the power of discrimination and the instrument of analysis, and separates the undifferentiated natural good into distinct states [GAP-24: suited to their several uses,] and draws forth the [GAP-25: rough and uneven qualities, and smooths] it with the refining power and makes it even. He fashions many [GAP-26: distinct] forms receptive of divine influx, ordering them according to their kind; He distinguishes and produces vessels of reception from it — vessels of differing kinds, such as containing forms for nourishment and containing forms for sustenance. This natural good, which [GAP-27: was formerly shapeless and without use,] since previously it had not been wrought upon before it was sealed by the skill and wisdom of this dextrous Divine Providence — He distinguished it into different forms of use, each one serviceable to its Lord.

---

**¶5**

So it is also with the Divine Providence. In His wisdom He has [GAP-28: formed and ordered all things] in their organic members [GAP-29: and arranged every faculty of the] mind as His dwelling [GAP-30: placing each good and each truth] within it, whether in a [GAP-31: greater or lesser capacity of] use [GAP-32: — and He has fashioned] the vessels that convey wisdom across the expanse [GAP-33: and] all the other works of spiritual formation [GAP-34: He has completed and set in order] within the [GAP-35: whole of creation] — so that when the great Lord comes, He who is the Third Proceeding of the Divine — the Redemptive Presence — who [GAP-36: enters into all that has been prepared for Him,] He might dwell within this ordered creation as within His own [GAP-37: kingdom, and fill it with His life.]

---

**¶6**

[GAP-38: And all things so ordered shall] live. The wisdom, however, that is within the creation [GAP-39: He who is] wise gathers up and elevates to its proper heavenly state; but the refuse and the falsity from evil shall be collected together and cast down into the hell from which it arose.

[§529] There are some among us telling us that we [know and perceive] that the (Ambassador) consents about [tha]t f[igure] of flesh; he who shall be fashi[oned in the flesh] at another time. And next, he says: [The Ambassa]dor displayed his image in the un[iverse]. The rulers and the powers of the universe saw h[is image], and they formed their shapes after his likeness, who are Adam and Eve. Behold, you see that god con[sen]ts to its happening; because of this, he has displayed his image to the rulers; and they constructed [mankind after his likeness.] If he was not consenting to humankind, he would not have uncovered his image, nor they fashioned mankind after his likeness!
[§529]* There are some among us who say that we [GAP-1: perceive (?)] that Divine Truth proceeding consents to the formation of that [GAP-2: merely natural and corporeal state] at another time. And further, the teaching says: Divine Truth displayed the form of the Divine Human throughout the natural mind. The self-derived faculties and the ruling powers of the natural mind perceived the form of the Divine Human, and they fashioned their own will and understanding after its likeness — which are the voluntary and the intellectual of the merely natural self. See, then, that the Divine [GAP-3: permitted these faculties to construct their external forms]. If Providence had not permitted the natural human to come into existence, the Divine would not have uncovered the form of the Divine Human, nor would the self-derived faculties have fashioned the natural self after that likeness.

[§530] Regarding the Ambassador, the occasion when he came displaying his image, he [d]id not come so th[a]t he might reveal his image to the ru[l]ers in the universe. Rather, he came and displayed to the universe be[cause of h]is soul and his son, who is [mixed in] every [place], that he might enable him to live [again] and make for him a release; and to free (the soul) from every bond and all the fetters, in which it is entangled and bound.
[§530]* Regarding Divine Truth proceeding — when it came forth manifesting the form of the Divine Human, it did not come so that it might reveal its form to the self-derived faculties of the natural mind. Rather, it came and manifested itself throughout the natural plane because of the entrapped divine life and the proceeding truth within, which is [GAP-4: bound within] every [GAP-5: state of the natural mind], so that it might enable that divine life to live [GAP-6: and be restored], and bring about its liberation — and to free the entrapped divine essence from every bondage and all the constraints of falsity and evil in which it is entangled and held captive.

[§531] The rulers and the authorities, in that they had no form in [the likeness of] him, they lusted for his image. They reflected upon themselves, that [they] had no [form] like him in their creation. They sealed hi[s im]a[g]e in their heart, within their soul. Afterward[s, they] built it according to his likeness; they formed Adam and [Ev]e. They sealed the image of the exalted one in their [soul]. So, they [co]pied with an imitation; but they have not [copied i]n truth! Rather, just as [an image that is seen in water,] it is made as these, for [i]t came [from the likeness] but it is not l[ik]e him in truth. [Again, so too] is this figure of ma[nkind. They copi]ed it against the image of the exalted one [but not in truth]
[§531]* The self-derived faculties and the ruling powers of the natural mind, in that they possessed no genuine spiritual form in [GAP-7: relation to] the Divine Human, lusted after its image. They reflected upon themselves and perceived that [GAP-8: they] had no [GAP-9: form or being] like the Divine Human in anything they had produced. They impressed the form of the Divine Human upon their will, within their interior life. Afterward they constructed an external likeness; they formed the voluntary and intellectual faculties of the natural self. They sealed the form of the Exalted Divine within their [GAP-10: interior life and will]. And so they copied it as an imitation; but they have not copied it in truth! Rather, just as [GAP-11: a reflection in a distorted mirror] is made to resemble the original, for it came [GAP-12: from the appearance of the original] but it is not like the original in genuine reality. [GAP-13: In the same way] is this formation of the natural human. They copied it against the form of the Exalted Divine [GAP-14: yet it remains merely an appearance without interior life].

[§532] Similarly the [t]ree, for while it is like the [image, it is] not [copi]ed in truth.
[§532]* Similarly the perception of truth — for while it appears like the [GAP-15: genuine perception of wisdom, it is] not a true copy in reality.

[§533] This matt[er is comparable to] this: As a great, fre[e] woman [who dwells] within her [palace, and she is] rich in wealth [and many] po[ss]essions; she is also beautiful in her beauty, and is virtuous in her modesty. She walks in a free and reputable position. The fame of her beauty has spread in every c[i]ty. She sur[passes every woman] Behold h[er] chamber [is closed to every] one who is mad, because [of] the nobles and the great ones. Al[so], they lust for her beautiful face. They did not [reach] her, because she is hidden in her palace [and guarded] within in a [secret place. None could] meet that woman [except through] his so[n]; or rather the head [covering of]hat garment. This free woman shall [not willingly] leave the cha[mber] behind her and come to the street [and uncover] her head [and her veil] and she reveals her [face and her beauty to all] because of her beloved broth[er]. This wom[an], on whom no man ever looked, nor did they ever see her fa[ce], as she neither desires nor rejoices [in outward display. She would not] leave her chamber behind her, and come in the midst of mankind [that all might see her] and everyone view her. The hones[t] men and the nobles, even the servants too, and the [lowly ones] look at her.
[§533]* This matter corresponds to the following: As a noble, celestial affection of truth [GAP-16: dwells] within her [GAP-17: interior states of wisdom], rich in the goods of heaven [GAP-18: and possessing abundance of] spiritual goods; she is also beautiful in the beauty of genuine truth, and is virtuous in her innocence. She moves in a free and honored spiritual state. The fame of her spiritual beauty has spread through every province of the mind. She sur[GAP-19: passes all other affections in radiance]. Behold, her interior dwelling [GAP-20: is concealed from all external approach, accessible to no] disordered desire, because [GAP-21: of the protection afforded by] the elevated and noble faculties. Also, the self-derived powers lust after the beauty of her truth. They did not [GAP-22: attain to] her, because she is hidden within her interior state [GAP-23: of wisdom, sheltered] within a [GAP-24: sacred enclosure. None could] encounter that celestial affection [GAP-25: except through] the proceeding truth; or rather the governing wisdom — [GAP-26: that is, the covering of] external truth. This celestial affection of truth shall [GAP-27: not willingly] leave the interior state behind her and come forth into the external and natural plane [GAP-28: uncovering] her governing wisdom [GAP-29: and her inmost nature], and she reveals her [GAP-30: innermost truth and her spiritual beauty] because of her beloved — the divine good within. This affection of truth, upon whom no merely natural faculty ever gazed, nor did they ever perceive her true nature, as she neither desires nor delights in [GAP-31: external display — would not willingly] leave her interior dwelling behind her, and come into the midst of the natural faculties [GAP-32: where every power might perceive her]. And so all behold her. The upright faculties and the noble powers, even the lowest servile faculties, and the [GAP-33: most external natural capacities] look upon her.

[§534] In accord with this simile: Again, [this] too [is] how [it is that] the Ambassador becomes [manifest in the universe] as this free woman [came forth, not] according to wantonness, in pride, nor [because she co]nsented to the outer world, she [came forth from her chamber with grief], she revealed her face and her [beau]ty [to all] but only due to the constraint of grief [about] her [bel]ov[ed one].
[§534]* In accord with this correspondence: Again, [GAP-34: this] too [GAP-35: is] the manner in which [GAP-36: the proceeding] Divine Truth becomes [GAP-37: manifest in the natural mind] — as this celestial affection of truth [GAP-38: comes forth, not] according to vain self-display, nor in the pride of self-derived intelligence, nor [GAP-39: because she] consented to the external natural world. She [GAP-40: came forth with reluctance], and she revealed her innermost truth and her spiritual beauty [GAP-41: to all who perceive], but only due to the constraint of love and grief [GAP-42: about] her beloved — the divine life entrapped in the natural.

[§535] Now, this too is the way of the Third Ambassador, when he came and display[ed his] image with a show towards all the powers. [He did] not [co]me so that all the rulers and the author[it]ies would sculpt a form [after h]is likeness. R[ather], he came because of his son being crucified in the totality; [fo]r he would release him, free him, and rescue him from oppres-sion. Yet, when the rulers looked o[n] him, [th]ey lusted for his image. They fashioned after his [like]ness Adam and Eve, without the consent of the gre[at]ness.
[§535]* Now, this too is the way of Divine Truth in its third proceeding, when it came and displayed the form of the Divine Human as an appearance before all the powers of the natural mind. [GAP-43: It did] not come so that all the self-derived faculties and ruling powers would form a merely natural likeness after the Divine image. Rather, it came because the proceeding divine truth was crucified — oppressed and rejected — throughout the totality of the natural mind; and it came that it might release that truth, free it, and rescue it from oppression. Yet when the self-derived faculties perceived the form of the Divine Human, they lusted after it. They fashioned after its likeness the voluntary and intellectual faculties of the natural self — the merely human will and understanding — without the consent of the Divine itself.

[§536] For, [a]s with Adam and Eve, whom the rulers fashioned and [se]aled after the image of the exalted one, so too this [i]s how a manifold divinity and many powers and angels [also received] the stamp of that image, which was displayed to them. For, in the majesty of the l[ig]ht and (the image's) love, they glowed with their desire and they were undone. Th[ey] were eager for their lust; like a m[an who looks] to the majesty of a lusted after face, and [is undone] and he is eager for the [fire] of the desire.
[§536]* For, as with the will and understanding of the natural self, which the self-derived faculties fashioned and impressed after the form of the Exalted Divine, so too is this how a manifold array of derivative states, and many subordinate powers, and messenger-faculties [GAP-44: received] the impression of that Divine form which was displayed to them. For in the splendor of divine wisdom and in the love that radiated from it, they burned with their desire and were undone. They were consumed by their cupidity — as when a faculty of the mind gazes upon the splendor of a longed-for truth, and [GAP-45: is overwhelmed], and it is consumed by the {GAP-46} urgency of that desire.

[§537] This is [als]o the case with those powers, when they looked on that majestic and beautiful [imag]e, they () [lusted for] a first image. They received the stamp of th[at image], which was displayed to them. They copied [its appearance, but that]ich th[ey co]pied out in a likeness, it being made [into outward forms, not in truth. And] the ones that they had put forth, which had received a [stamp of this im]age; but inward even to the rocks [and the stones of the earth,] which occur from place to place. The [stamp] of that image, which was displayed in the universe [spread to all, and] they received a stamp, it being revealed there, and an impress.
[§537]* This is also the case with those derivative powers: when they looked upon that majestic and beautiful form of the Divine Human, they [GAP-47: were seized with longing and sought to reproduce] the original form. They received the impression of that Divine image which was displayed to them. They copied [GAP-48: its appearance; but that wh]ich they copied out as a likeness was made [GAP-49: only into external and derivative imitations] — the forms they had produced — which had received an impression of this Divine image; and this impression extended inward even to the lowest sensory ultimates [GAP-50: and to the most fixed natural states] which occur from plane to plane of the mind. The {GAP-51} imprint of that Divine form which was displayed throughout the natural mind [GAP-52: pervaded all levels, and] they received its impression — it being revealed there as an image stamped upon the memory.

[§538] Now, this new birth that they begot in a new [form] in the world, is Adam and Eve. [There is not a] form in their world since the beginning like it; because that [im]age [occ]urs not in the worlds b[el]ow.
[§538]* Now, this new spiritual birth that these faculties begot in a new [GAP-53: formation] in the natural plane is the will and understanding of the regenerating human self. [GAP-54: There is not a] form in the lower planes of the mind since the beginning that is like it; because that form of the Divine Human does not naturally occur in the states below.

[§539] Not all the figures that have been fashioned were fashioned by birth from displayed intercourse. Rather, there are other figures fashioned [in another way]
[§539]* Not all the spiritual formations that have been produced were produced through the birth that arises from a displayed conjunction of truth and affection. Rather, there are other formations produced [GAP-55: by entirely different processes].

[§540] One of them is the sea [gi]ant, the 'sweeping' from the sphere, which the [Father] of Life discharged into the sea. Its own fire and thought fashioned it [in] a figure.
[§540]* One of them is the monstrous falsity of the sensory plane — the "sweeping away" that descends from the outermost boundary of the natural mind — which the [GAP-56: Divine Good as the source of all spiritual life] discharged into the gathering of falsities. Its own self-love and self-derived reasoning fashioned it [GAP-57: in]to a spiritual form.

[§541] The second figure is the one [that] fell to the earth. It made the sea its womb. Its own l[us]t became its sculptor. It alone fashioned it to a nature, which is the root of death. Yet, when it came up from the sea, it [rose up] that it might destroy the works [of the Father] of Life. They immediately sent against it the Adamant of Light, the great 'instruction of vigour'. He humbled it in the districts of the north. He has trampled underfoot and set h[im]self upon it, until the end of the world.
[§541]* The second formation is the one [GAP-58: that] fell into the natural plane. It made the realm of falsities its womb — the matrix from which it took shape. Its own cupidity became its fashioner. It alone shaped itself into a nature, which is the root of spiritual death. Yet when it arose from the realm of falsities, it [GAP-59: came forth with the intent] to destroy the works of good [GAP-60: that proceed from Divine] Good. Immediately there was sent against it the Indestructible Truth from Divine Good — the great instruction of spiritual strength and vigor. This truth subdued that monstrous evil in the states of spiritual obscurity in the north. It has trampled underfoot and set itself upon that evil nature, holding it in subjection until the consummation of the age.

[§542] [The] third figure is the nature that fell on wh[at] is dr[y. It] fashioned the tree and set itself there. [This is the] new figure that was fashioned [from within, and it]isplays.
[§542]* [GAP-61: The] third formation is the evil nature that fell upon the good of the natural mind — that which is firm ground. It fashioned itself into a false perception and embedded itself there. [GAP-62: This is] the new formation that was fashioned [GAP-63: from within, and it] manifests itself outwardly as though it were genuine.

[§543] So, in accord with the essence they [are of]ness; but yet th[ey] did not come with the King of Dark[ness to the wo]rld. Rather, their fashioning [came] about [by other means, and] they are composite and have been displayed in the world.
[§543]* So, in accord with their essential nature, these formations [GAP-64: derive from the realm of] falsity and evil; yet they did not come with the ruling self-love — the sovereign principle of all evil — into the natural world. Rather, their formation [GAP-65: came] about [GAP-66: through secondary processes]; they are composite productions of evil and falsity that have been displayed and made manifest in the natural mind.

[§544] All [these a]bortions, amongst them are Az and his consort Jeh and the ones whom they have served [ ... ], they are the ones that shaped Adam and Eve. How did they find this beautiful image laid over their shape, even though when it was displayed to their fathers they were not in existence? They never saw the image of the Ambassador! And how did they take the seal of the image of the Ambassador? They added it to [the] shape of Adam and Eve.

[§545] The Sin that spurted out from the rulers, which is Matter, shot up [tow]ards the image of the Ambassador. It was cut off from [tha]t place and came down to the earth, for they did not accept [it wi]thin the firmaments. When it came do[wn to] the earth, it formed the tree. It was established within the wood and formed the fruits. And when [the] abortions fell [to] the ground, to the earth, at the [second] time [ ... / ... ]

[§546] [When it came up] fruits, it assumed the[m] and spoke in the ruler, their leader. He says to his companions: Come! Give me your light, and I will construct for you an image aft[er] the likeness of the exalted one. What he said, they did: they gave it to him, and he constructed [the image].

[§547] The Sin that s[purted out ... / ... ] which is the Matter that saw the im[age of the Ambassa]dor and that formed the tre[e and was established i]n [i]t; afterwards it came up in the [and assumed them]. It went in to the rulers [and] they formed Adam and Eve after the [likeness of the] exalt[ed one]. Through the energy of the Sin that had s[een the image of the] Ambassador, it went into the (rulers) [through the fruits. It] assumed them and they [t]oo sculp[them. And they] begat each of them according to his destiny [ ... ]; one he resembles, one he does not resemble.

[§548] The moulder placed in the form of Adam and Eve limbs, outside and within, for perception and activity. He was apportioned house by house. For everything that his perceptions and elements will receive externally there are internal storehouses and repositories and cavities; and what is received in to them is stored in them. Whenever they will be questioned about what is deposited in their internal storehouses, they bring out what they have received within and give it to the questioner who requested it of them.

[§549] In this way his faculty [ ... ] outer [limbs] to look at [and receive] every type with [in ... ]

[§550] Also [the fa]culty of the eyes [has] houses and cavities and repositories and stores within, so that every image it might see, whether good or evil, whether loveable or detestable or lustful, it can receive into its storehouses and repositories. Also, when the faculty of the eyes is pleased to send out the image that it saw and took in, it can go in to its storehouses [at the] time and think and seek [after it] and it brings it out [and] gives it to the questioner who requested it and the one who wan[t]ed it—whether it be something from lu[and desire, or a]n image of love [or ... / ... something] hateful. And thus shall that faculty (of the eyes) produce and do what it does in each category.

[§551] The faculty of the ears has its own storehouses also. Every sound it might receive, whether good or evil, shall be taken in and placed in its houses and inner repositories, and it is guarded in its [places] for a thousand days. After a thousand days, if someone comes and asks that faculty about the sound that it heard [at] this time and took into its storehouses, immediately it [sh]all g[o] into its repositories and seek and review and search after this word, and send it out from where it was first put, the place in which it was kept.

[§552] [In] like order, the faculty of scent [s]hall function just as that of the eyes and that of the auditory organs. Every [o]dour it shall smell it shall take in [to it] and [d]eposit in its inner storehouses. Every time it will be [a]sked by a questioner, it shall go in [to its repositories] and [search its] storehouse and remember [ ... ] only these things.

[§553] However, even the mouth and the tongue within it, and the taste organ, have a faculty dwelling in them. Again, that faculty too, of taste, has thus cavities and repositories set apart for it. It too receives these tastes and gathers them in. A[nd] at any moment when someone will ask of a taste, if [it has received it] it shall send it out and remember tha[t] taste. It shall snare and give even the mar[k of] th[a]t taste; give its memory to the qu[est]ioner who asks for it.

[§554] Again, the fac[ul]ty of touch by the hands is also s[o]: When it might touch, touch shall [receive i]ts memory. And it [takes] it in to its inner repository [until] som[eo]ne will ask this faculty for the memory. [Immediately], it shall go in again and bring out the memory of this touch that it made, and give it to whoever asks for it.

[§555] And the faculty of the heart that rules over them all is much the most like this. Every thing that these five faculties will receive and put in store for the faculty of the heart it shall receive and guard. Any time that they will ask for their deposit it shall send out and give every thing that they gave to it.

[§556] For the counsel of the heart is like the king. All the ad[v]isers of his city and the leaders of his a[r]my are placed with him. Any time if his genera[ls] will entreat him for whatever they wish, he shall give them the ad[v]ice and opinion by which they move.

[§557] Furthermore, it is like a cellarman: [Eve]ry thing that will be entrusted to it by them, and given to it, it shall receive and put in separate vessels and places. Any time [ ... / ... ]

[§558] [It shall find it] in the place that it put it.

[§559] This also is the way of the faculty of the body. The vision that the eyes will see and give to it, and it receives it in, it shall bring out again from the storehouses wherein it had placed it; and give it to whoever asks for it.

[§560] Again, [the] sound that the ears will hear and give to it, and it receives it from them. At any time when someone will ask it for that sound, it shall bring out and give the sound of the ears to whoever has asked it for that sound. It shall bring out and give the sound of the ears to whoever has asked.

[§561] Again, [the] faculty of smell in this same pattern; and that of taste is of the same [ty]pe. The touch by the han[ds ... / ... ] they shall bring it out in to [ ... / ... ] And whenever they will as[k it] for it, it shall bring it out and gi[ve it].

[§562] Indeed, watchmen are a[t] these doors guarding them, and bolts are fastened on the doors at the hands of the guards that guard them!

[§563] Just like watchmen guarding a camp with towers and watch-posts. As the holding rooms come out from there and the bolts are upon the gates, the guards control the bolts of the gates while they sit at the entrance [of] the watchposts. So, someone who belongs to their city as a fellow [cit]izen of them, belonging to their country, for him they shall open [the ga]te and receive him in by their will. However, the foreigner who comes they shall prevent, and leave him standing at the gate. They shall not yield to let him enter. Again, another one whom they will receive in, with great trouble before [they] take him in, you shall find many holding rooms [ ... / ... ] that [ ... ] there are a mass [ ... / ... ] them. The number of d[oor] bolts [upon the gates] are [many] Whenever someone will wish [ ... ] the bolts at his hand, opens the gate and brings out whoever is hidden within.

[§564] This body too is like the mighty camp. And the gates of the camp with their guards are like the orifices and organs of the body. Now, the orifices of the body are of sight, hearing and smell; and they that send out words. There are many sentinels and a mass of guards placed over the limbs of the body, guarding their orifices. And the faculty in it is the directive of the body, the queen of the entire camp; so that whenever it wishes it can open, or when it wishes it can shut.

[§565] However, if and wh[en] Wahman comes, it shall tu[rn i]n to the orifices of the body. The rulers, who [a]re the door gua[rds], shall h[in]der and restrain it from en[ter]ing. They shall bind the circu[it] of the body so that the foreign[er] can not enter it.

[§566] Nevertheless, Wahman by its wisdom and awe and diligence shall hum[ble] the guards who are set at the body's orifices. When it finishes humbling them it shall take fr[o]m them bolts to all the orifices of the body. Now, the orifices that had been opened before to the parades of lust, to receive in the sights of pleasure, to transfer the [wor]ds of the practice of li[f]e's concerns, to receive in the t[astes] of the different foods and unclean meals together with the sin therein; whoever stops receiving th[is] in, his heart and mind shall follow after.

[§567] By the power of Wahman that has suffused him and lived in the body he has humbled Lust, the goddess of the body.

[§568] [S]imply, all orifices in similar fashion were opening at one time to wickedness, but now have opened to the good. Sin was ruling in the body this time, doing anything it liked, having none in command.

[§569] Yet, [n]ow, authority has been taken from the Sin in the org[ans] of the body. The Living Spirit has become lord over all its limbs. It has bound them with a chain of peace, and sealed them with the seal of truth. It has opened the orifices of the body to the good, and because of this the good enters them through the ears and the eyes. It dwells in the heart. The H[o]ly Spirit reigns. It does all that it pleases. It annuls [the] will of [S]in, the begin-ning and the end. [In contrast], the will of the [Ho]ly Spir[it] comes about, and the purpose of Wahman.

[§544–§569]* **¶1**

All these falsities devoid of genuine spiritual life — among them the ruling concupiscence and its corrupting seduction, and the hereditary tendencies they served [GAP-1: ...] — these are the powers that shaped the human will and understanding. How did they discover the beautiful form of the Divine Human impressed upon the mind's constitution, even though when it was first displayed to the originating evils from which they derived, these falsities did not yet exist? They never directly perceived the form of Divine Truth as revealed. And how did they appropriate the stamp of the Divine Human? They impressed it upon [GAP-2: REVIEW the] constitution of the human will and understanding.

---

**¶2**

The concupiscence that emanated from the ruling self-loves, which is the proprium itself, surged upward toward the form of Divine Truth as it was revealed. It was severed from that interior state and descended into the natural mind, for it was not received within the rational structures. When it descended into the natural mind, it generated a system of perception. It established itself within that natural knowledge and produced works from it. And when [GAP-3: REVIEW the] falsities devoid of genuine life fell [GAP-4: REVIEW into] the natural mind, into the external plane, at the [GAP-5: REVIEW subsequent] stage [GAP-6: ...]

---

**¶3**

[GAP-7: ... within the] works produced, the proprium assumed them and spoke through the ruling self-love, their chief. He says to his fellow evils: Come, yield to me your stolen wisdom, and I will fashion for you a form after the likeness of the Divine. What he commanded, they performed: they yielded their appropriated light to him, and he fashioned [GAP-8: ...].

---

**¶4**

The concupiscence that [GAP-9: emanated ...], which is the proprium that perceived the form of Divine Truth revealed and that generated the system of perception and established itself within it — afterwards it ascended through the [GAP-10: works produced ...]. It entered into the ruling self-loves [GAP-11: ...] and they fashioned the will and understanding after the [GAP-12: REVIEW likeness of the] Divine. Through the force of the concupiscence that had perceived the form of Divine Truth, it entered the ruling evils [GAP-13: REVIEW through the works. It] assumed them and they also shaped [GAP-14: ...] they generated each according to his spiritual destiny [GAP-15: ...]; some bear the Divine likeness, some do not.

---

**¶6**

The formative power placed within the will and understanding faculties both external and internal, for reception and for activity. These were apportioned state by state. For everything that the perceptive capacities and constituent elements receive from without, there are interior memory-stores and repositories and receptive chambers; and what is received into them is preserved therein. Whenever they are called upon to yield what has been deposited in their interior stores, they bring forth what they received within and deliver it to the inquiring faculty that sought it of them.

---

**¶7**

In this way its capacity [GAP-16: ...] external [GAP-17: REVIEW faculties] to perceive [GAP-18: ...] every kind of thing [GAP-19: ...]

---

**¶8**

Also the capacity of intellectual sight [GAP-20: REVIEW possesses] organized states and receptive chambers and repositories and memory-stores within, so that every form it may perceive — whether of truth or falsity, whether in accord with love or abhorrent or belonging to concupiscence — it can receive into its memory-stores and repositories. Also, when the capacity of intellectual sight is moved to send forth the form that it perceived and took in, it can enter into its memory-stores [GAP-21: REVIEW at the] appointed state and reflect and seek [GAP-22: ...] and it brings it forth [GAP-23: REVIEW and] delivers it to the inquiring faculty that sought it, and to whatever desired it — whether it be something from concu[GAP-24: piscence ... or a] form of love [GAP-25: or ... something] abhorrent. And thus shall that capacity of intellectual sight produce and accomplish what it does in each category.

---

**¶9**

The capacity of obedient reception has its own memory-stores also. Every doctrine it may receive, whether of truth or falsity, shall be taken in and placed in its organized states and interior repositories, and it is preserved in its [GAP-26: ...] through a fullness of states. After the fullness of states has passed, if inquiry comes to that capacity concerning the doctrine that it received [GAP-27: REVIEW at] that time and took into its memory-stores, immediately it shall enter into its repositories and seek and review and search after this truth, and bring it forth from where it was first deposited, the state in which it was preserved.

---

**¶10**

[GAP-28: REVIEW In] like manner, the capacity for perceiving interior quality shall function just as that of intellectual sight and that of obedient reception. Every sphere of affection it shall perceive, it shall take in [GAP-29: REVIEW to itself] and deposit in its interior memory-stores. Every time it is called upon by an inquiring faculty, it shall enter [GAP-30: ...] and [GAP-31: ...] its memory-store and recall [GAP-32: ...] only these things.

---

**¶11**

However, even the faculty of communication and the power of articulation within it, and the capacity of experiential discernment, have a governing power dwelling in them. Again, that capacity of experiential discernment likewise has receptive chambers and repositories set apart for it. It too receives these experiential perceptions and gathers them in. And at any moment when inquiry is made concerning a particular experience, if [GAP-33: ...] it shall send it forth and recall that experiential perception. It shall capture and yield even the distinguishing character of that experience; give its memory to the inquiring faculty that seeks it.

---

**¶12**

Again, the capacity for direct appropriation through action is also so: when it makes contact, that contact shall receive its impression in memory. And it [GAP-34: REVIEW takes] it into its interior repository [GAP-35: REVIEW until] someone will inquire of this capacity for the memory. [GAP-36: REVIEW Immediately], it shall enter again and bring forth the memory of this contact that it made, and give it to whatever inquires for it.

---

**¶13**

And the capacity of the will that rules over them all is much the most like this. Every thing that these five capacities receive and store for the will, it shall receive and guard. At any time that they inquire for their deposit, it shall bring forth and yield every thing that they gave to it.

---

**¶14**

For the deliberation of the will is like a sovereign. All the counselors of his domain and the commanders of his forces are placed with him. At any time if his generals entreat him for whatever they seek, he shall give them the direction and purpose by which they act.

---

**¶15**

Furthermore, the will is like a steward of interior goods: every thing that will be entrusted to it by the faculties, and given to it, it shall receive and place in separate containing forms and organized states. Any time [GAP-37: ...]

---

**¶16**

[GAP-38: ...] in the state where it placed it.

---

**¶17**

This also is the way of the governing capacity of the external person. The perception that the understanding has seen and given to it, and it receives it in — it shall bring it forth again from the memory-stores wherein it had placed it, and give it to whatever faculty asks for it.

---

**¶18**

Again, [GAP-39: REVIEW the] doctrine that the capacity of obedient reception has heard and given to it, and it receives it from them. At any time when inquiry is made for that doctrine, it shall bring forth and yield the doctrine received through obedience to whatever has asked. It shall bring forth and yield the doctrine received through obedience to whatever has inquired.

---

**¶19**

Again, [GAP-40: REVIEW the] capacity for perceiving interior quality follows this same pattern; and that of experiential discernment is of the same kind. Direct appropriation through act[GAP-41: ion ...] they shall bring it forth into [GAP-42: ...] And whenever inquiry is made, it shall bring it forth and yield it.

---

**¶20**

Indeed, governing affections are stationed at these openings guarding them, and resistances are fastened upon the openings under the authority of the affections that guard them!

---

**¶21**

Just as governing affections guard an ordered spiritual community with elevated doctrinal states and posts of vigilance. As the antechambers of deliberation proceed from there and the resistances are upon the entrances, the governing affections control the resistances of the entrances while they remain stationed at the threshold [GAP-43: REVIEW of] the posts of vigilance. So, whatever belongs to their own spiritual community as a fellow member, belonging to their domain — for this they shall open the entrance and receive it in willingly. However, the foreign element that comes they shall prevent, and leave it standing at the entrance. They shall not yield to let it enter. Again, another element that they will eventually receive — with great difficulty before [GAP-44: REVIEW they] take it in — you shall find many antechambers of deliberation [GAP-45: ...] that [GAP-46: ...] there are a multitude [GAP-47: ...] of them. The number of resistances [GAP-48: ...] are [GAP-49: ...] Whenever someone shall wish [GAP-50: ...] the resistances at his authority, opens the entrance and brings forth whatever is concealed within.

---

**¶22**

This external natural person is likewise the mighty ordered community. And the entrances of the community with their governing affections are like the openings and faculties of the external person. Now, the openings of the external person are those of intellectual sight, obedient reception, and perception of interior quality; and those that send forth communications. There are many vigilant affections and a multitude of governing powers stationed over the faculties of the external person, guarding their openings. And the capacity within is the directive of the external person, the sovereign of the entire ordered community; so that whenever it wills it can open, or when it wills it can close.

---

**¶23**

However, if and when Divine Wisdom descends, it shall turn toward the openings of the external person. The ruling evils, who are the guardians of the entrances, shall hinder and restrain it from entering. They shall bind the circuit of the external person so that the element foreign to their dominion cannot enter it.

---

**¶24**

Nevertheless, Divine Wisdom by its truth and holy awe and persistent striving shall humble the governing evils that are set at the openings of the external person. When it finishes humbling them it shall take from them the resistances to all the openings of the external person. Now, the openings that had been opened before to the processions of concupiscence, to receive in the appearances of selfish delight, to convey the communications belonging to the concerns of merely natural life, to receive in the appropriations of various falsities and impure goods together with the evil within them — whoever ceases receiving these, his will and understanding shall follow after the Divine.

---

**¶25**

By the power of Divine Wisdom that has suffused him and dwelt within the external person, he has humbled Concupiscence, the ruling love of the natural self.

---

**¶27**

Simply, all openings of the mind in similar fashion had once been opened to evil, but now have opened to good. Evil had been ruling in the external person at that time, doing whatever it willed, having no authority above it.

---

**¶28**

Yet now, authority has been taken from the Evil in the faculties of the external person. The Divine Good proceeding has become lord over all its capacities. It has bound them with the bond of charity, and sealed them with the confirmation of divine truth. It has opened the openings of the external person to good, and because of this, good enters through the faculties of obedient reception and intellectual sight. It dwells in the will. The Divine Truth proceeding reigns. It accomplishes all that it purposes. It annuls [GAP-51: REVIEW the] intention of Evil, from the first state to the last. [GAP-52: REVIEW In contrast], the purpose of the Divine Truth proceeding comes about, and the end purposed by Divine Wisdom.

[§570] about Adam, the first man. When he was fashioned, how did they sculpt him? Or, how did they beget him? Rather, is his begetting like the begetting that is brought forth today, amongst humankind; or not? Does a distinction exist between his birth and the one that they bring forth today?
[§570]* Concerning the Most Ancient Church, the first spiritual state of humanity. When it was constituted inwardly, how was its spiritual character formed? How was its life generated? Was the manner in which spiritual life arose in that earliest church the same as the manner in which spiritual life is received among people today, or not? Does a distinction exist between how that primordial perception of good and truth came into being and how regeneration proceeds now?

[§571] Now, no[te] that Adam is mighty in his stature, and great is his size. Even his years are many! He spent many years living in the world. And he is not the only one we have found to be mighty; but all the others who were begotten in his generation, many are their years too. Still, these people who are born today, their stature has been diminished in power; even their years are lessened. Also, why is the birth today, of [the]y who are born, altered compared with that of these first [generations, from] place to place? See, even the stars and signs of the zodiac continue in their positions. Why now have the age and the years of these last ones diminished, and he has also become smaller, compared to these ancient ones who belong to these first generations?
[§571]* Observe that the Most Ancient Church was immense in spiritual capacity, and the fullness of its wisdom was vast. Its states of spiritual life were also abundant — it endured through many states while dwelling in the natural world. Nor was it alone in its greatness; all who were regenerated in that era likewise possessed abundant and enduring spiritual states. Yet those who are born into spiritual life today have been diminished in capacity; their states of interior life have grown fewer. Moreover, why has the manner of regeneration today — among those now born into the spirit — become altered compared with that of these first [GAP-1: …] from one state to another? Behold, the knowledges of spiritual truth and the fixed arrangements of celestial order continue in their positions. Why then have the fullness and enduring states of these later ones diminished, and why has the human being become lesser in spiritual stature, compared to those ancients who belong to the earliest spiritual generations?

[§572] They increased, have turned, and diminished; because there are five types of authorities and leaders appointed in the sphere of zodiacal signs, and the heavens above it. They have names they are ca[lled] by: the first name is the year; the sec[on]d is the month; the third is the day; the fou[rth] is the hour; the fifth is the moment. Now, these five positions, and these five houses, exist in the sphere and the heavens. These places have five powers, and they are made masters over them. The master of the year exists; the master of the month exists; the master of the day exists; the master of the hour exists; the master of the moment exists. In turn one is above his companion; and in turn the one above is made the master over the one below him.
[§572]* Those states increased, turned through their cycle, and diminished — because there are five degrees of governing dominion and ruling principles appointed within the ordering of spiritual knowledge and in the heavens above it. They bear designations by which they are known: the first is the complete state of celestial life; the second is the state of spiritual change; the third is the state of natural illumination; the fourth is the state of particular influx; the fifth is the state of momentary perception. These five degrees and these five interior dwelling-places exist within the spiritual order and the heavens. These dwelling-places possess five governing powers, and those powers are set as rulers over them. The ruler of the complete celestial state exists; the ruler of the state of spiritual change exists; the ruler of natural illumination exists; the ruler of particular influx exists; the ruler of momentary perception exists. Each in turn stands above its companion; and in turn the higher is made ruler over the lower beneath it.

[§573] So, mankind is begotten, and [the ani]mals. They are born in these powers. And, thus, these powers have received authority since the beginning of creation till the end of the world. Now, in the beginning of the generations the powers who were masters over the years reigned. Therefore, because of this, the offspring born in their generations and their lifetime: their years were found to be greater. They attain a long life span in the generation of Adam, and [tha]t of Sethel his son, and that of the ones who came after him.
[§573]* In this way, new states of understanding are generated, and new affections likewise. They come into being under these governing powers. And so these ruling principles have received dominion from the beginning of creation until the consummation of the age. In the beginning of the spiritual generations, the powers that governed the complete celestial states held sway. Therefore, because of this, the spiritual offspring brought forth in their generations and under their life: their states of interior life were found to be the greatest. They attained enduring spiritual life in the era of the Most Ancient Church, and in that of its successor perception — its son — and in that of all who came after them.

[§574] After they ceased to reign, namely the powers who are masters over the years; the powers who are masters over the months themselves reigned. Like the months, for they are less than the years; again this [is] also the case with the offspring w[ho were] born in them. Their life span is less [than the life span of the ones born] in the generation of the powers who master the years!
[§574]* After they ceased to reign — namely the governing principles that ruled the complete celestial states — the powers that govern the states of spiritual change themselves took dominion. As the states of spiritual change are lesser than the complete celestial states, so again [GAP-2: REVIEW — this diminishment] is also the case with the spiritual offspring who were brought forth under them. Their duration of spiritual life is less [GAP-3: REVIEW — than the duration of those brought forth] in the era of the powers that governed the complete celestial states.

[§575] When the authority of the powers who master the months was completed; the powers of the days received authority after them. Therefore, like the days, for they are less than the months, this also is the case with the offspring who were born in them. Their life span is less than the life span of the ones born in the powers who are masters over the months. Again, even so the offspring who were born in the hours, and of the moments. Their conduit is less than that of the months and the days.
[§575]* When the dominion of the powers governing the states of spiritual change was completed, the powers of natural illumination received authority after them. Therefore, as the states of natural illumination are lesser than the states of spiritual change, so also is this the case with the spiritual offspring brought forth under them. Their duration of interior life is less than the duration of those born under the powers that govern the states of spiritual change. Again, even so with the spiritual offspring brought forth under particular influx and under momentary perception — their channel of spiritual life is less than that of the states of spiritual change and the states of natural illumination.

[§576] The life span of the people has drawn in to nothing. Their days have diminished. Their years have become less; because the life and the ligh[t that] was in the world in these first generations was more than that of today. Indeed, because of this have they diminished in their stature. Their life span has become less for them.
[§576]* The duration of spiritual life among people has contracted to almost nothing. Their states of illumination have diminished. Their interior states have grown fewer — because the good and the wisdom that were present in the world during those first spiritual generations were more abundant than what remains today. Indeed, because of this they have diminished in their spiritual capacity. The duration of their interior life has become less for them.

[§577] By what sort of birth was the entire offspring of mankind made from one man and one woman, Adam and Eve?
[§577]* By what manner of spiritual generation was the entire race of derivative goods and truths brought forth from one understanding and one will — the Most Ancient Church in its intellectual and volitional aspects?

[§578] For the manner of these first generations is that they are richer in their light and purer in stature. Even their years are greater. Thus, again, even their offspring is much more than the offspring of this tim[e]. They were spending a longer time [being fashioned] in [the w]omb of their mother. Afterwards, they [brought forth] a number in a single womb. There are times five were brought forth from a single womb, and times six were brought forth from [a] single womb! There are some lesser than this; again, some greater than this. Indeed, because of this the offspring of Eve multiplied, and of her children. The earth was filled by reason of them alone.
[§578]* For the character of those earliest spiritual generations is that they are richer in their wisdom and purer in spiritual capacity. Their interior states are more abundant. Thus again, their spiritual offspring — the goods and truths they produce — are far more numerous than the spiritual offspring of this present time. They dwelt a longer time [GAP-4: …] within the interior state of the will that bore them. Afterwards, they [GAP-5: …] a multitude from a single interior state. There are times when five distinct goods and truths were brought forth from a single state of love, and times when six were brought forth from [GAP-6: REVIEW — a] single state of love. Some were fewer than this; again, some greater. Indeed, because of this the spiritual offspring of the primordial will multiplied, and of all the derivative affections proceeding from it. The external mind was filled by reason of them alone.

[§579] For, they whom they beget today, in these last generations, they stand diminished and maimed. And, indeed, one by one do they bri[ng t]hem [forth] from a single womb. Hardly two, or [l]ess or more. They are even made ugly in their aspect, being diminished in their size, and feeble in their limbs.
[§579]* For those whom the spirit begets today, in these last generations, they stand diminished and impaired. Indeed, only one by one are they brought [GAP-7: REVIEW — forth] from a single interior state. Scarcely two, or fewer, or slightly more. They are even rendered lacking in spiritual beauty, being diminished in their capacity, and feeble in their powers of action.

[§580] The first who grieves is the first Mother of Life. She is sad because of her children who are set in affliction, for they were conjoined with the Darkness and the [poi]son. They have been bound with the entire ruling-power. For what was she grieving, [wa]tching over them taking these afflictions? Whenever she might [s]ee them and how suffering is brought upon them, she shall grieve and be unhappy on account of them.
[§580]* The first who grieves is the inmost Divine Love from which all spiritual life proceeds. She is in sorrow because the living affections that proceed from her have been plunged into states of temptation and vastation, for they were conjoined with falsity and the persuasions of evil that corrupt the interiors. They have been held captive under the entire dominion of self-love. On what account does that Divine Love grieve, watching over them as they undergo these assaults of spiritual combat? Whenever she perceives their state and how the violence of temptation is brought upon them, she shall grieve and remain in holy sorrow on their account.

[§581] [The s]econd one who is troubled of heart is the First Man, whose [great]ness and kingdom is established in the li[ght] ship [of the moon]. And he too is troubled of heart because of his five sons who are set in the midst of danger. For any time he may see that they are given trouble, always he might hear the sound of their weeping as they cry out because of the blows and wounds of the enemy who assaults them daily; and as he sees them like this constantly, he shall g[ri]eve and mourn on account of his [s]o[n]s.
[§581]* The second who is troubled in his inmost is the Divine Celestial Principle, whose sovereign sphere and dominion is established in the vessel of Divine Wisdom [GAP-1: — the understanding illuminated by heavenly light through which that dominion operates]. And he too is troubled in his inmost because of his five essential faculties of spiritual perception, which are set in the midst of peril from the hells. For at any time he may perceive that they are beset by assault, continually he receives the interior cry of their anguish as they call out because of the injuries and violations inflicted by the evil that attacks them in every state; and as he perceives them thus without ceasing, he grieves and mourns on account of the goods of perception that proceed from him.

[§582] The garments, the sons of the First Man, wept bitterly three times. Then they were silent. There is a fourth time of weeping, for they will weep at the end. And then their crying can stop, and they will never again weep from this time on.

[§583] Now, the first time of their weeping is when they had seen the darkness, the enemy being about to burst out against them. They understood everything that would happen to them. They were aware of the one that would rise and come upon them, but by necessity they hastened to him and fought with him. They mastered him, they detained him. They bound him with their body and their limbs, they overcame his death and his fire and his darkness.

[§584] [The] second time that they wept is [when the]ir father, the First Man, [ascend]ed from the [abyss] of darkness and left them behind; that is the time when he stripped [them off] and left them below. Indeed, when th[ey] s[aw] how the First Man had risen out of the abyss and [l]eft them behind, and that they were abandoned in the enmit[y, th]ey wept and were sore of heart. Still, what could they do, wishing [to go up after him? Yet by necessity they gave] their heart to every affliction. They [remained b]ehind and grew [s]ick under the pressure of the weighty burden of all the works, until the end time.

[§585] [The] third time that they wept is when the Living Spirit stripped off the three garments of activity, placing them beneath all things. He gave them a sign to lift their heads and rise to the heights from that place; and to pass and journey through all the earths until they should reach the land of tranquilli[t]y and peace, the place of rest prepared for them. So, when they had seen him, that he had separated himself from them, while they themselves remained behind after him and had not ascended; at that moment when they stayed behind these three garments wept. After their crying, they turned their [hear]t to all the heights that were concealed from them, and were of one mind. For [they would] soar up and come to the heights; and pass over all things until the time that was ordained for them all [should come] at the end of the worlds.

[§586] [The f]ourth time when they will weep is when the [S]tatue will be taken up on the last day, and they will weep [for] the souls of the liars and blasphemers; for they may give [them no help] because their limbs have been severed [and given to the powers] of the darkness. And also [th]ose souls, when the Statue will go up and they are left alo[n]e, they will weep in that they will rema[in] behind in affliction for ever. For they wi[ll be cut off] and separated from the Last Statue. An[d it is] a necessity to take these souls who are ready for loss as retribution for the deeds that they have done. They go i[n to] this [darkne]ss and are bound with the Darkness; just as they desired it and loved it, and placed their treasure with it. At that very moment, when the Last Statue rises up, the[y wi]ll weep. And then they will scream out loud because they will be severed from the company of this great Statue. And they remain behind for ever. This great weeping [and separation] is terrible, it occurs in front of the souls and [the spirits] who are ready for loss in accordance with their deeds. And they [will] weep with that crying, they will never be silent; because they will not attain rest from this time on.

[§587] So, they too are the garments of these elements. This fourth time of weeping is heart-rending for them. It is much worse for them than these other three times of weeping before. This fourth time the weeping is not for themselves. Rather they weep and are heartsore for these soul[s] who will be cut off from peace, because rest will not come about for them, to allow them to rest from torment for ever.

[§582–§587]* # The Four Griefs of the External Truths

**¶2.** The external truths that proceed from the Lord's inmost celestial nature — the truths that clothe the Divine Human — passed through three states of deep anguish. After the third they came to rest. Yet there remains a fourth grief, for they will grieve at the final consummation. And only then shall their lamentation cease, and they shall never again pass through such anguish from that time onward.

**¶3.** The first grief arose when they perceived the oncoming falsity from evil — the hostile power about to erupt against them. They had full perception of everything that would befall them. They were aware of the nature of the evil that would rise and assail them, yet by the necessity of divine providence they advanced toward it and entered spiritual combat with it. They prevailed over it; they held it fast. They confined and restrained it within the very forms of their own exterior being, within the structures through which they operate. They overcame its spiritual death, its self-love, and its falsity.

**¶4.** [GAP-1] The second grief came when [GAP-2] their origin, the Lord's inmost celestial nature, ascended out of the deepest state of falsity and evil, and left the external truths behind — that is, the moment when He divested Himself of them [GAP-3] and left them in the lower degrees. Indeed, when they perceived how the Divine Celestial had risen out of that abyss and departed from them, and that they were left behind amid the opposition of evil, [GAP-4: …the external truths surrendered] their will to every suffering. They remained behind and grew infirm beneath the pressure of the immense burden of all the labors of regeneration, until the final state.

**¶5.** [GAP-5] The third grief came when the Divine Proceeding stripped away the three external truths of active operation and placed them beneath all other degrees. He gave them an influx — a sign — to lift their perception upward and ascend toward interior states from that lowest place, and to pass through every natural state of the mind until they should arrive at the celestial state of tranquility and peace — the state of rest prepared for them. Yet when they perceived that He had separated Himself from them, while they themselves remained behind and had not ascended, at that moment these three operative truths grieved deeply. After their lamentation, they turned their will toward all the interior celestial states that had been concealed from them, and they came into unanimous accord. For [GAP-6] they were to ascend and reach the interior heights, and pass beyond all natural states until the appointed time that was ordained for them all [GAP-7: …to be fulfilled] at the consummation of all spiritual ages.

**¶6.** The fourth grief shall come when the Grand Human Form — the collective image of all regenerated good and truth — is taken up at the last judgment, and the external truths will grieve [GAP-8] over the souls of those who are in falsity from profanation and blasphemy against the Divine; for they may offer [GAP-9: …no further means of restoration] because those souls' living connections to the Divine Human have been severed [GAP-10: …and given over to] falsity from evil. And those souls likewise — when the Grand Human ascends and they are left alone — they will grieve because they will remain behind in torment forever. For they will be cut off and separated from the completed Divine Human Form. And it is an immutable consequence that these souls who are destined for spiritual death receive the retribution corresponding to the deeds they have done. They pass into that falsity from evil and are bound permanently with it — just as they desired it and loved it and invested their ruling love in it. At the very moment when the completed Divine Human rises up, they will grieve. And then they will cry out in anguish because they will be severed from the communion of this great Divine Human Form. And they remain behind forever. This great anguish [GAP-11: …of final separation] is terrible; it unfolds before the souls and [GAP-12: …the spirits] who are destined for spiritual death in accordance with their deeds. And they [GAP-13] will grieve with that grief and will never be silent, because rest shall not come to them from that time onward.

**¶7.** Thus these external truths belong to these very constituent principles of the created order. This fourth state of grief is the most heart-rending for them. It is far worse than the three preceding states. For in this fourth grief, the external truths do not grieve for themselves. Rather, they grieve and are stricken in will on account of those souls who will be permanently severed from peace — because rest shall never come about for them, so as to release them from torment for eternity.

[§588] Just as the corporeal body has limbs that are hidden, so it also has others that are visible. They can be seen upon it, they can be grasped and receive blows and wounds, whilst others can neither be grasped nor receive blows.

[§589] So it is also with this entire greatness, as we resemble it. Some of it is hidden, whilst some of it is visible; some of it is far away from the blows and suffering, whilst some of it receives blows and wounds.

[§590] Now, thus the directive of the body that lives within, hidden in the storehouses inside the body and unseen. So it is also how the Father, the God of truth, exists; as he too is hidden in his kingdom, not displayed before this outer desolation.

[§591] Thus, too, the body has two eyes that are visible and seen, and are even the body's source of illumination; so there are also these two light ships that shine and are seen in the entire universe and the whole of creation. The light has never been hidden, just like the two eyes of the body.

[§592] The living soul too is like this; it is established in apparent silence. In its apparent silence it is grasped and receives blows from these five fleshes, which destroy (the soul) and strike it. It can be likened to the mystery of the corporeal body, as it can be grasped and mastered, can receive blows and wounds.

[§593] ⟨p.152⟩ Also, thus this tongue that speaks, proclaims, reprimands and reveals about everything happening in this body, inside and outside, also the considerations and bodily knowledges. The tongue, which is outside, makes it manifest. It also proclaims about the honour and shame of the body. And it speaks, unveiling the pain of the body that is sick from its sores.

[§594] This is also what the Splendour is like, who shall be sent from the greatness. He manifests and reveals about everything, both the external and the internal, both what is above and what is below. Be it about the outer aeons that are hidden and not displayed, or about the ships of light that are seen: he is the one who unveils about them. He is also the one who reveals about their glory, and the greatness that dwells in them.

[§595] And regarding this beating and wounding of the living soul, Jesus is the one who reveals it. He also preaches about (the soul) and its peace. He also reveals about its cleansing and healing.

[§596] How great is the power of the garment of the waters? How far shall [it extend]? The source from which all these waters burst forth, how great will it be?
[§596]* How great is the power of the external form that contains divine truth? How far shall [GAP-1] its influence extend into the states of the mind? The divine origin from which all these truths burst forth — how immeasurable must that source be?

[§597] [At] the time when the First Man assumed the elements, and stood [fi]rm against the first enmity that had come about in the Darkness, he defied these great and powerful rulers. They are immeasurable, greater than the extent or size of thousands and ten thousands of parasangs. At the start, when he spread the fight against them, he gave them fire and wind; and afterwards he gave the garment of the waters to the rulers of death. So they, the rulers, drank in and consumed those waters. Many [waters] poured forth from the garment of waters [from which] the rivers flow. The mouth of the rulers [devoured] those [waters], the warriors of destruction [and they] laboured and brought them forth.
[§597]* [GAP-2] In the state when the Celestial Principle of Good clothed itself with the fundamental goods and truths, and stood firm against the primal enmity that had arisen within falsity from evil, it confronted those great and dominant ruling evils. They are beyond all comprehension, exceeding in magnitude and reach any degree the mind can reckon. At the beginning, when it waged the spiritual combat against them, it brought against them the fire of divine love and the breath of spiritual understanding; and afterward it gave the external containment of divine truths over to the ruling evils that produce spiritual death. So those ruling evils absorbed and consumed those truths, perverting them inwardly. Many [GAP-3] truths poured forth from the external vessel of divine truth [GAP-4] and doctrinal streams proceeded outward. The reception of the ruling evils [GAP-5] those [GAP-6], the aggressive falsities of destruction [GAP-7] labored and brought them forth from within themselves as corrupted forms.

[§598] There were seven (rivers) flowing in to the mouth of each one of them, as they shall not [suffice] to a single stomach, to their fill; because in turn the descent to its stomach shall burn them with its fire and heat. Those rulers were like the [depths] of [the sea]; they were not emptied; rain and [rivers throughout] their existent days.
[§598]* There were seven streams of truth flowing into the reception of each one of them, yet they could not [GAP-8] be brought to satiation within a single interior, to their fullness — because in turn the descent of truth into their interior was consumed by the burning of self-love and its concupiscence. Those ruling evils were like the [GAP-9] of [GAP-10]; they were never emptied of their craving; an influx of truth and [GAP-11] persisted throughout the whole duration of their existence.

[§599] The measure of that river was expanded seven times, as they flow [out] of the body of the [living] waters [until] now.
[§599]* The measure of that stream of truth was multiplied sevenfold to fullness, as they flowed [GAP-12] through the external nature of the [GAP-13] truths [GAP-14] in the present state.

[§600] [This] is how great a struggle, since it has [overcome] these creatures of destruction, the mighty giants. Behold, they are entwined, bound, and [sealed] in every place. They are hidden, not revealed before the eyes of every flesh that exists on the land of death [and darkness]. These rulers [shall be] revealed. The entire universe of flesh will be destroyed and dissolved [with them]; all pass away [and perish]. They will be destroyed with their angry voice, which is mightier than the thunder.
[§600]* [GAP-15] Such is the magnitude of the spiritual combat, since it has [GAP-16] these monstrous persuasions from evil, the towering falsities of self-intelligence. Behold, they are entangled, restrained by divine order, and [GAP-17] held fast in every region of the mind. They are hidden, not made manifest before the perception of any natural-sensory being that dwells upon the plane of spiritual death [GAP-18]. These ruling evils [GAP-19] are made manifest only at last. The entire proprial selfhood will be destroyed and dissolved [GAP-20]; all of it passes away [GAP-21]. It will be destroyed by their raging power, which exceeds in force even the proclamation of divine truth in its ultimates.

[§601] this small stream is reckoned [as nothing] before the vastness of these waters. For only one river from thousands of rivers of water, from the secrets that are not revealed, is the river Tigris upon the earth.
[§601]* This small rivulet of natural knowledge is reckoned as nothing [GAP-22] before the immensity of these divine truths. For the river Tigris upon the natural plane of the mind is but one single stream among thousands upon thousands of streams of truth — drawn from the arcana that remain unrevealed to natural comprehension.

[§602] Three great rocks, which exist, were appointed by the greatness. They were set in these outer worlds.
[§602]* Three foundational truths, which exist from eternity, were ordained by the Divine itself. They were established in the external degrees of the mind — in the outermost planes where spiritual life takes fixed form.

[§603] So, the first rock is the [Pillar] of Glory, the Perfect Man, which was summoned [by the] glorious Ambassador. He set it in this zone; [and he] brought forth its base (?) from the earth to heaven. It bore up under the [en]tire uni[verse], and became first of all the weight-carriers in [it]s powers. In its firmness it has been set upright, and has made fast every [t]hing above and below.
[§603]* The first foundational truth is the [GAP-1] sustaining power of divine wisdom made manifest — the Divine Human in its fullness — which was called forth [GAP-2] by the divine mediating presence that communicates celestial things to lower degrees. The Divine established this truth in a specific degree of spiritual life; [GAP-3] it extended its foundation from the lowest natural plane upward to the celestial. It sustained the whole order of spiritual states, and became the primary supporting truth among all [its] powers. In its confirmation it has been made upright and immovable, and has secured every reality both interior and exterior.

[§604] [The] second great and glorious rock is the new earth of light, which was set upon the earth of Darkness. It has squashed the fastenings of the five storehouses of death. It has established firmness, with great firmness, over and beyond the earth of Darkness. It has flattened, crushed and fixed the root of death.
[§604]* [GAP-4] The second great and glorious foundational truth is the regenerated natural mind illuminated by wisdom, which was established over the natural mind in its state of falsity from evil. It has broken the bonds of the five sensory faculties through which spiritual death enters. It has confirmed the dominion of truth, with absolute confirmation, over and beyond the mind's state of falsity. It has subdued, destroyed, and rendered powerless the deepest origin of spiritual death.

[§605] [The] third rock is the great thought, which was summoned from Jesus, the glorious one. It has stood firm, with a true fixity, for ever.
[§605]* [GAP-5] The third foundational truth is the divine perception, which proceeded from Divine Truth itself in its glory. It has been established, with an unchangeable confirmation, to eternity.

[§606] [These] are the three great rocks, they that have come forth in great confidence, in three great and honoured places. They were appointed and distributed in three great places of these [self]same worlds. They were made fast according to the will of the greatness.
[§606]* [GAP-6] These are the three foundational truths, which have come forth in absolute certainty, into three exalted and sacred degrees of the mind. They were ordained and distributed across three essential planes of these same spiritual states. They were confirmed according to the will of the Divine itself.

[§607] What is love? [Love is the Father.][He has been revealed to] each [aeon], calling it 'love'.
[§607]* What is Divine Love itself? [GAP-1: the spiritual definition or essence that initiates the inquiry] [GAP-2: a further unfolding of that essence, likely how it is perceived or received] each [GAP-3: state of spiritual life, or each being that partakes of it], recognizing it as 'Divine Love.'

[§608] Love is the Father of Greatness, who dwells in his glorious land. The entirety of the divine has been revealed [there]in! These two are a single living body, the Father and his love; because he has given himself for every thing, as he exists in his aeons. Indeed, due to this, they called the Father, the Lord of the totality, 'love'. For he gave the victory to his aeons and his limbs.
