[§608]* Divine Love is the Divine Itself, the Infinite Source of all good, which abides in its own celestial kingdom. The whole of the Divine nature is made manifest within that kingdom. These two are one indivisible life: the Divine Itself and its Love — for the Divine Esse and Divine Love are not two but one. Because the Divine has given itself forth into all things through its proceeding, as it exists within its own infinite states of being, therefore they recognized the Divine, the Lord of all that exists, as Love Itself. For it has granted dominion and life to all its proceeding states and to all that is conjoined with it as its very members.

[§609] What, conversely, is hate? Hate is the first death that welled up. It revealed the land of Darkness. This one called 'hate', because it has ruined all its perdition's offspring; it sinned even against a power foreign to it, and ruined it. Due to this they shall call it 'hated'; for it has caused destruction, both wounding in flesh, and what is not its own.
[§609]* What, conversely, is the opposite of Divine Love? Hatred is the primal spiritual death that welled up from the proprium. It brought into manifestation the entire realm of falsity and evil. It is called abomination because it has consumed and destroyed all the offspring of its own perdition; yet it assaulted even that Divine Good which is utterly foreign and apart from it, and profaned it. Therefore they call it the abominated — for it has wrought devastation both within the natural will, and upon what does not belong to it: the Divine things it invaded and corrupted.

[§610] Today again it causes wounding and destruction in all flesh. It kills them all with diverse deaths; these that are displayed today in every thing, in every type. Even the Cross of Light, the living soul, likewise too it ruins and kills them; as it did at the start. Indeed, due to this have they called it 'hated', this one from the hated; the envy from the envy; the devil from the devil; the bad that came about from the bad.
[§610]* Even now this self-love and falsity from evil continues to bring devastation and ruin upon every natural will. It extinguishes spiritual life through manifold forms of spiritual death — those that are made evident in every state of existence and in every kind of being. Even the Divine Truth in its suffering — the living good held captive within the human spirit — this evil likewise profanes and extinguishes, just as it did from the very origin of the fall. Therefore they recognize it as the abomination proceeding from the abomination, the self-love born of self-love, the infernal issuing from the infernal, the evil that comes forth from evil in unbroken hereditary succession.

[§611] Three great things that are distinct were revealed in Adam the first human. Indeed, due to this, he was found to be better and more outstanding than the conjoined powers that are in heaven and on earth.
[§611]* Three essential spiritual qualities, each distinct, were made manifest in the Most Ancient Church — the earliest celestial humanity. Because of these, that humanity was found to be nobler and more excellent than all other organized forms of reception, whether in internal states or in external states.

[§612] The first: The image of the exalted one was placed upon him. The creators and moulders of his body sealed him after the shining form of the image that had been displayed to them above. Indeed, due to this, the image of Adam was found to be surpassingly beautiful beyond any conjoined power above and below.
[§612]* The first: The likeness of the Divine itself was received into the inmost will. The hereditary and natural powers that shaped the external form configured it according to the radiant pattern of divine wisdom and love that had been revealed to them from the celestial degree. Because of this, the representative form of that earliest humanity was found to surpass in beauty every other organized receptacle of life, whether in internal or in external states.

[§613] The second thing: Adam was shaped in his structure through the light of the first-born of heaven and [earth, and through] the five sons. Indeed, due to this, his structure was found to differ from the structure of even the other creations; [sin]ce the form of all creations is different. As for Adam, the formation of his soul fits over the correct distribution of the elements. Therefore, he has intelligence surpassing that of the other creations and beasts.
[§613]* The second: That earliest humanity was organized in its interior arrangement through the wisdom proceeding from the firstborn emanations of divine good and [GAP-1] divine truth — the five discrete faculties of spiritual perception. Because of this, its interior arrangement was found to differ from the arrangement of all other living forms; for the constitution of every other living form is different. But in the case of the earliest humanity, the configuration of the interior spiritual life perfectly corresponds to the true ordering of goods and truths. Therefore, that humanity possesses rational understanding surpassing all other living forms and natural affections.

[§614] The third are the teachings and counsels and the seal of all the powers above and below. The creators, who set him in order, gathered them and sealed them in him. Adam and his consort Eve became a dwelling and home for the signs of the zodiac and the stars; and the months, the days and the years. For the seal of the entire universe is stamped upon Adam. Indeed, due to this, heaven and earth moved because of him; trouble and disorder arose on his account between they that are good and they that are evil. So, the good induce him to life because of their image and shape placed upon him; while the [wi]cked were drawing him to death, so that they would have power through him and they would conceive the kingdom, and through him humiliate the entire universe.
[§614]* The third comprises all doctrines, all interior guidance, and the representative correspondences of every spiritual force in internal and external states. The formative powers that ordered this humanity gathered these and inscribed them within it. The earliest celestial humanity and its conjoined intellectual faculty became a dwelling and habitation for all knowledges of good and truth in their ordered cycles — and for all states of faith, all states of love, and all complete periods of spiritual life. For the correspondence of the entire spiritual world is inscribed upon that earliest humanity. Because of this, internal and external states were profoundly stirred on its account; conflict and disorder arose between those influences that are from heaven and those that are from hell. The heavenly influences draw that humanity toward spiritual life, because of the divine likeness and form received within it; while the [inf]ernal powers were drawing it toward spiritual death, so that they might exercise dominion through it, and seize sovereignty, and through it profane all things of the Lord's kingdom.

[§615] The rulers determined and formed Adam so that they would be masters through him and conceive the kingdom because of seven things.
[§615]* The powers of the proprium — self-derived will and hereditary evil — determined and formed the natural human so that they would be masters through it and seize sovereignty, for seven complete reasons.

[§616] The first: He became the enlightener in their creation; instead of the light of the image of the Ambassador who had displayed himself to them above. For they all desired the likeness of the image of the Ambassador. Their heart was drawn after it.
[§616]* The first: That natural human became a vessel of reflected wisdom amid the forms they had generated — in place of the true wisdom radiating from the likeness of the Divine Human, who had revealed Himself to them from the celestial degree. For all those proprial powers craved to possess the likeness of the Divine Human. Their will was captivated by it.

[§617] The second: So that they would scoop up the light through him and restrain and obstruct it from its ascent to the heights.
[§617]* The second: So that through the natural human they might absorb and appropriate divine wisdom, and restrain and obstruct it from ascending to its celestial origin.

[§618] The third: So that because of the image of the Ambassador that is placed upon him the entirety of the divine that is above and below would be humiliated; on account of the likeness of the splendour, of the beauty of his superiority. For he is superior in his appearance to all creatures.
[§618]* The third: So that, by means of the divine likeness received into the natural form, the entirety of what is the Lord's in internal and external states would be profaned — on account of the reflected glory, the excellence and beauty of that divine superiority. For the natural human is superior in outward appearance to all other living forms.

[§619] The fourth: He is a seal-ring and a new impression, so that all births who will be begotten might be brought forth after his seal.
[§619]* The fourth: That natural human is a generative pattern and a new representative form, so that all subsequent states of life brought forth might be produced according to its imprint.

[§620] The fifth: That might be displayed through him all the skil[ls] of the powers, they above and below, secret and manifest; and that they might determine through him the totality, and every thing be revealed in him.
[§620]* The fifth: That through it all the capacities of every spiritual force — internal and external, hidden and manifest — might be displayed; and that through it they might govern all things, and every spiritual reality be made manifest within it.

[§621] The sixth: That there would come to be a great protection for them through the image of the exalted one that was placed upon him; for as the heavenly would [not harm them, nor] even rulers; and no evil thing would be done to them on account of Adam.
[§621]* The sixth: That there would come to be a powerful defense for them through the divine likeness that was received into the natural human; for inasmuch as heavenly powers would [GAP-2] even infernal rulers would be restrained; and no assault of falsity could be carried out against them on account of the divine form borne by that earliest humanity.

[§622] The seventh: That he might become chief and king and master over all creatures; and they might conceive the kingdom through him.
[§622]* The seventh: That the natural human might become sovereign, ruling power, and master over all affections, perceptions, and living forms; and that the proprial powers might seize complete dominion through it.

[§623] the greatness of the sun and its divinity: how was it fashioned after the likeness of the first greatness?
[§623]* The immensity of Divine Wisdom and its divinity: how was it formed after the likeness of the Divine Itself?

[§624] it is the gate of life and the vessel of peace, to this great aeon of light [from which it came forth]. it is the gate of the souls' departure
[§624]* It is the entrance into spiritual life and the containing form of heavenly peace, belonging to this great heavenly state of wisdom [GAP-1: — the inmost heaven from which all illumination proceeds]. It is the passage through which the interiors of the spirit are liberated.

[§625] Every day, would this great light-giver shine its light, it shall do seven good things for them.
[§625]* In every state of illumination, this great Divine Truth shining forth into the mind accomplishes seven full works of good for those who receive it.

[§626] The first good thing that it does for them is its light, which it shines upon them. It opens the eyes of all mankind. They see underfoot as they walk by it. It takes away the blindness of the night, which made them blind with its darkness.
[§626]* The first good that it accomplishes is its wisdom, which it pours into the understanding. It opens the intellectual sight of all who are in the human state. They perceive the path of life as they walk by its guidance. It removes the ignorance of the state of spiritual darkness, which had closed their understanding with its falsity.

[§627] The second: When (the sun) is come to the world, with its coming it shall bring calm and peace to the entire world. With its coming it shall also take away from them all the fear and trembling, which their heart is filled with the whole night; for it shall take away the darkness present by its light, and sweep it out. Also, this is how it extinguishes fear by its calm.
[§627]* The second: When Divine Truth enters the mind, by its coming it brings heavenly tranquility and peace to the whole of the interior life. By its coming it also removes from them all the anxiety and dread with which their will was filled during the entire state of darkness; for it dispels the falsity that was present by means of its wisdom, and sweeps it away. And in this same manner it extinguishes spiritual fear by its peace.

[§628] The third is its vigil; because when (the sun) comes to the world all sleeping mankind shall arise and awake. And the watchmen too shall cease from watching. The guards who sit at the gates of camps, they break vigil at the watchposts, because of its light that has shone upon them in the world.
[§628]* The third is its awakening; because when Divine Truth enters the mind, all who were in spiritual torpor arise and become conscious. And the truths that had served as guardians during the state of obscurity are released from their vigilance. The protective truths stationed at the entrances of the ordered life — they cease their watch at the outposts of defense, because of the wisdom that has shone upon them within the mind.

[§629] The fourth: It shall nourish, and give strength, taste and scent, to the trees and fruits and vegetables, and all the herbs, and the flowers and grasses, which are upon the entire earth.
[§629]* The fourth: It nourishes, and gives power, interior perception of good, and perception of truth, to all perceptions and their works, and to all natural knowledges and every form of understanding, and to the first openings of spiritual thought, and to the most general natural truths, which exist upon the whole of the natural mind.

[§630] The fifth: When (the sun) shines in all the world, the evil reptile and the sharp-toothed beasts that are filled of wickedness, they shall flee to hiding in their dens.
[§630]* The fifth: When Divine Truth illumines the whole of the interior life, the evil lusts that creep in the lowest parts of the mind, and the predatory selfish affections that are filled with malice — they flee into concealment within their hidden interiors.

[§631] The sixth: When it shines its light, the wound of any person is eased from pain. Its light shall melt away the evil arts of every doer of wickedness.
[§631]* The sixth: When it shines its wisdom, the spiritual injury of any soul is eased of its suffering. Its truth dissolves the persuasions of falsity employed by every agent of evil.

[§632] The seventh: (The sun) shall display and manifest to the world the sign of the splendour of the aeons of light, from which it has come out; it here in this world being their sign. It brings light to the whole of creation.
[§632]* The seventh: Divine Truth displays and makes manifest to the mind the sign of the glory of the heavenly states of wisdom, from which it has proceeded; it being, here within the natural mind, their representative. It brings illumination to the whole of what has been created in the spirit.

[§633] it has all wickednesses therein. Every occasion, would the night fall, it shall do seven evils in the world.
[§633]* The state of spiritual darkness contains within itself all evils. In every instance when the state of falsity descends, it accomplishes seven evils within the mind.

[§634] The first wickedness that the night shall do in the world is the darkness, with which it shall fill the entire world; because when the sun sinks from the world and draws to it its rays, at that instant the shadow of the night shall spread out over the whole world. The eyes of the people fill with blackness. They neither see nor wonder with their eyes, because of the darkness of the night which shall fill the whole world.
[§634]* The first evil that the state of spiritual darkness accomplishes in the mind is falsity, with which it fills the entire interior life; because when Divine Truth withdraws from the mind and gathers to itself its particular truths, at that instant the shadow of the state of ignorance spreads over the whole of the interior life. The understanding of the soul fills with obscurity. People neither perceive nor apprehend with their intellectual sight, because of the falsity from evil which fills the whole of the mind.

[§635] The second evil thing that the night shall do is its fear; because when its shadow looms forth, its fear and its trembling shall possess all of mankind. The entire world shall fill of the night.
[§635]* The second evil that the state of spiritual darkness accomplishes is its dread; because when its obscurity looms forth, its anxiety and its trembling take hold of all who are in the human state. The entire interior life fills with the darkness.

[§636] The third: When the night falls, all creatures take up revolt. They rebel in their heart, to do wickedness and perdition.
[§636]* The third: When the state of falsity descends, all affections and thoughts take up rebellion. They turn in their will against Divine order, to commit evil and spiritual destruction.

[§637] The fourth: The heaviness of sleep shall lay upon them, and they fall asleep; they slumber and doze, and they are like these corpses in the night.
[§637]* The fourth: The heaviness of spiritual torpor lays upon them, and they fall into unconsciousness; they become insensible and stupefied, and they are like those who are spiritually dead in the state of darkness.

[§638] The fifth is its ugliness; again, when the night would fall, the form of the people shall be hidden in the darkness and the ugliness of the night. The beauty and the form of men and women shall not be displayed before one another, because of its darkness and its ugliness, which it shall spread out over all their forms.
[§638]* The fifth is its deformity; again, when the state of falsity descends, the spiritual form of the soul is hidden in the obscurity and the ugliness of that state. The beauty and the form of the celestial and the spiritual — the masculine understanding and the feminine will — are not displayed to one another, because of the falsity and the deformity which it spreads over all their forms.

[§639] The sixth occasion is when the night would fall on the world. At that instant evil people shall come out to do wickedness; the adulterers, and the robbers and poisoners. The evil beasts shall come out, and all the reptiles that are filled with wickedness, from their dens. And they stalk in the night. Also, the demons and fiends find power to do wickedness in the night. Again, the blows and buffets and wounds and discharges shall torment people in the night; because in it the whole of evil shall find strength.
[§639]* The sixth occasion is when the state of spiritual darkness descends upon the mind. At that instant evil states of life come forth to do wickedness: the profaners of good, and the thieves of truth, and those who corrupt truth with falsity. The predatory evil affections come forth, and all the lusts that creep in the lowest parts of the mind, filled with malice, from their hidden interiors. And they prowl in the state of darkness. Also, the influences from hell and the infernal spirits find power to work evil in the state of falsity. Again, the assaults and violences and injuries and effusions of evil torment the soul in the darkness; because in it the whole of evil finds its strength.

[§640] The seventh: The night shall display the sign of the Darkness, its father. It has arisen out of its essence; because this night that came out of this first Darkness has been displayed in the world. Note this night, the shadow of this first Darkness, the one that is entwined and bound in all things above and below.
[§640]* The seventh: The state of spiritual darkness displays the sign of Evil itself, its origin. It has arisen out of the essence of evil; because this state of falsity that came forth from the primordial Evil has been displayed in the mind. Observe this state of spiritual darkness — it is the shadow of the primordial Evil, the one that is interwoven and bound into all things of the interior and the exterior life.

[§641] There are five archetypes occur in the sun, it making them [manifest to all] as it finds the world.
[§641]* There are five essential qualities that manifest in Divine Wisdom, which it produces [GAP-2: — impressing them upon the interior forms of the mind] as it encounters the states of the world.

[§642] The first is its light; for it illumines by its light the world, and all the creatures who exist in it.
[§642]* The first is its illumination; for it enlightens by its truth the whole of the mind, and all the affections and thoughts that exist within it.

[§643] The second is its beauty; because when it shines, it shall flood beauty and loveliness upon all creatures and everything.
[§643]* The second is its beauty; because when it shines, it floods spiritual form and loveliness upon all affections and thoughts, and upon everything.

[§644] The third is its peace; for, again, when the sun would shine on the world, all people shall receive affection through it, and give peace to one another.
[§644]* The third is its peace; for, again, when Divine Truth illumines the mind, all who are in the human state receive mutual love through it, and extend peace to one another.

[§645] The fourth is the life of the living soul, which it shall release from all the bonds and fetters of heaven and earth.
[§645]* The fourth is the liberation of the living spiritual essence, which it releases from all the bonds and constraints of what is interior and what is exterior.

[§646] The fifth: It gives a strength to the elements; and also it gives a scent and a taste to the entire Cross of the Light.
[§646]* The fifth: It gives power to the fundamental constituents of spiritual life; and also it gives perception of truth and interior perception of good to the whole of the Divine Life that is scattered and bound within natural forms.

[§647] its light illumines more than all lights that occur in the world. Also, its beauty is more beautiful than all the beauties of mankind. Again, its peace is more surpassing than all the powers and guards who exist in the world. Its releasing action, by which it releases the living soul, is a full day ahead of all releasing actions! The strength that it gives to its limbs is a great strength, being mightier than all strengths!
[§647]* Its wisdom illumines more than all lesser lights of understanding that exist in the mind. Also, its spiritual beauty is more beautiful than all the forms of merely human thought. Again, its peace surpasses all the powers and protections that exist in the mind. Its liberating power, by which it frees the living spiritual essence, is an entire state ahead of all other liberating actions! The strength that it gives to its members is a great strength, being mightier than all other powers!

[§648] Once again, there are another three archetypes made apparent by the sun, in respect of the mystery of the first greatness.
[§648]* Once again, there are another three essential qualities made apparent by Divine Wisdom, with respect to the mystery of the Divine Itself.

[§649] The first is the filling up of the disk of its ship; because its ship has filled up every season, and shall not wane at all, the way that the ship of the moon wanes. This continuous filling, by which it is filled, displays the mystery of the Father, the great greatness; from whom all the powers and the gods have come forth. He shall never wane, nor shall lack ever exist in him!
[§649]* The first is the perpetual fullness of its containing form; because its vessel is filled in every state, and never diminishes at all, in the way that the containing form of faith from natural truth wanes and fluctuates. This unceasing fullness, by which it is filled, displays the mystery of the Divine Love, the infinite Divine Itself; from whom all the powers and the celestial beings have proceeded. He never diminishes, nor does any lack ever exist in Him!

[§650] The second: (The sun's) light surpasses the light of all the stars, and all the lights that occur in the universe; corresponding to the mystery of the light of the Father; for his light surpasses and is greater than the light of his aeons.
[§650]* The second: The wisdom of Divine Truth surpasses the light of all particular knowledges of good and truth, and all the illuminations that exist throughout the created universe; corresponding to the mystery of the light of the Divine Love; for His wisdom surpasses and is greater than the illumination of all His heavenly societies.

[§651] The third: This sun is very high above all else. It surpasses the height of all the mountains and hills that exist upon the entire earth, corresponding to the mystery of the Father of Greatness; for he is high above all the household of his light-earth. Also, because the Father himself is high above all the heights and the mountains of his light-earth. He is 'filled up' in his image [all] the time and every hour from eternity to eternity!
[§651]* The third: This Divine Wisdom is very high above all else. It surpasses the elevation of all the states of celestial love and spiritual love that exist upon the whole of the regenerate mind — corresponding to the mystery of the Divine Itself; for He is elevated above all the household of His heaven. Also, because the Divine Itself is exalted above all the elevations and the states of love within His heaven. He is perpetually full in His own image [GAP-3: through] all states and every moment from eternity to eternity!

[§652] The mystery of the first great light is the sun, which is this second light-giver, the one that comes daily to the world. It displays all the marks of its graces, displaying and signifying on its own behalf that it is good, out of the essence of the good. It came from the good Father and appeared in this world.
[§652]* The mystery of the first great illumination from the Divine is this Divine Wisdom, which is the derived light-bearer, the one that comes in every state of life into the mind. It displays all the marks of its Divine goods, manifesting and signifying on its own behalf that it is good, out of the essence of the Good. It proceeded from the Divine Love and appeared within the natural mind.

[§653] this night that is made of fear exists after the mystery of the Darkness. Behold too the mystery of its essence! It displays it in the world in all these wickednesses. It displays this mystery daily in the world.
[§653]* This state of falsity that is constituted of dread exists after the pattern of Evil itself. Observe also the mystery of its essence! It displays itself in the mind through all these evils. It displays this mystery of its nature perpetually in the life of the world.

[§654] Two great mysteries are apparent in this shining sun, at its rising and setting. Now, at the moment when it rises and is visible in the universe, all creatures of flesh shall lift up their heads fr[om their sleep] towards the splendour of the sun, and open their doors. While at evening time, when the sun sinks from the universe, all people from every place and even all beasts shall go in to their dark hiding places. The population of the entire universe shuts their doors and dark night overcomes them. They go inside and stay within the dark night in their hiding places.
[§654]* Two profound arcana are disclosed through divine love and wisdom — its influx into the mind and its withdrawal. At the moment when divine wisdom flows in and becomes manifest throughout the whole of the mind, all natural affections rouse themselves from spiritual torpor toward the splendor of that wisdom, and open the interiors of the mind to receive it. But when divine perception withdraws from the mind's awareness, all thoughts in every region of the understanding, and even all affections of the will, retreat into obscure states of falsity. The whole mind closes its interiors, and the state of falsity from evil overcomes it. All things of thought and affection remain confined within that spiritual darkness in their states of concealment.

[§655] Very great is this mystery revealed by the sun! For when the sun rises and [be]come[s] visible at the beginning of the day all people open their doors, and all of humanity and every creature emerges onto the ear[th] to destroy this Light Cross that is established i[n] silence.
[§655]* Exceedingly great is this arcanum revealed through divine wisdom! For when divine truth flows in and becomes manifest at the first state of illumination, all the interiors of the mind open, and every faculty of thought and every affection descends into the external natural mind — there to assault the Divine Good and Truth that stands fixed in a state of patient silence within the natural plane.

[§656] This pertains to the mystery of the First Man, because when he came out from the grea[t]ness he shone forth over all the children of Darkness. At that mome[nt] all the children of Darkness came forth from their storehouses, its powers and its armies, abandoning their caves and their black abyss. They destroyed the [outer] body of his five sons, who were swathed over hi[s b]o[dy, in t]hat first struggle.
[§656]* This pertains to the arcanum of the Primordial Celestial Human, because when that divine principle proceeded from the Infinite itself, it shone forth over all the states of falsity from evil. At that moment all falsities from evil came forth from their interior repositories — organized evils and marshaled falsities — abandoning their deepest hells and their abyssal states of profanity. They devastated the [GAP-1: the exposed exterior truths constituting] the outward form of the five faculties of celestial perception, which were the protective truths enveloping the Divine Human's external nature, in that first state of spiritual combat.

[§657] Again, when the sun sinks from the universe and sets, and all people go in to their hiding places and houses and conceal themselves; this also pertains to the mystery of the end, as it presages the consummation of the universe. For, when all the light will be purified and redeemed i[n] the universe at the last, the collector of all things, the Last Statue, will gather in and sculpt it/self. It is the last hour of the day, the time when the Last Statue will go up to the aeo[n of] light. The enemy too, death, will go in to bondage; to the prison of the souls of the de[ni]iers and blasphemers who loved the darkness. They will go in with it to bondage and the dark night overcomes th[em]; and thenceforth this name 'light' will not shine upon them.
[§657]* Again, when divine perception withdraws from the mind's awareness and sets, and all thoughts retreat into their states of obscurity and conceal themselves — this also pertains to the arcanum of the final consummation: it prefigures the ultimate completion of all spiritual states. For when all wisdom and truth have been fully separated from falsity and liberated throughout the whole mind at the last, the gathering principle of all regenerate goods — the Ultimate Divine Human Form — will collect all truths and goods and compose itself into one perfect likeness. It is the final state of the church's spiritual day, the time when the Ultimate Divine Human Form will ascend into the eternal state of divine love and wisdom. The adversary also — spiritual death itself — will be confined to bondage: to the infernal state appointed for those who denied and profaned the Divine, who loved falsity and evil. They will enter with it into that confinement, and the state of utter falsity from evil overcomes them; and thenceforth the very quality called "wisdom" will never illuminate them again.

[§658] Behold the two mysteries made visible daily in the sun, which is this splendrous light-giver.
[§658]* Behold the two arcana made manifest perpetually through divine love — which is the resplendent source of all spiritual illumination.

[§659] This fire, which burns and is apparent today in the world, it does five works.
[§659]* This love, which consumes and is manifest today in the natural mind, performs five spiritual operations.

[§660] The first work that it does is this: It incinerates and burns wood, and everything that shall be placed upon it.
[§660]* The first operation it performs is this: It consumes and devours natural good and proprial merit, and everything that is submitted to its power.

[§661] The second: It separates four aspects of the wood, which are these: fire and light, ash and smoke.
[§661]* The second: It separates four distinct qualities from that natural good — namely, love itself and wisdom, dead residue of the merely natural, and the obscurity of falsity rising from what is consumed.

[§662] The third: It gives a taste and an aroma to whatever will be cooked on it. It also gives heat and cheer to whoever will be close to it.
[§662]* The third: It bestows interior perception and spiritual pleasantness upon whatever is prepared and matured through its activity. It also imparts the warmth of charity and spiritual delight to whoever draws near to its presence.

[§663] The fourth work that [it] does: All craftsmen shall work the utensils that they will fabricate in it.
[§663]* The fourth operation that [GAP-1: love/desire acting as formative power] performs: All who fashion spiritual capacities shall shape the containing forms that they bring into being through its activity.

[§664] The fifth: It shall display light, and the people see by [it].
[§664]* The fifth: It shall manifest wisdom openly, and people perceive truth by means of [GAP-2: love's illumination].

---

**Note on the structure:** The passage presents love as the single animating force behind five distinct spiritual processes: *consuming* what is merely natural; *distinguishing* interior qualities from exterior residues; *preparing* the interiors for genuine perception; *forming* the vessels or capacities of the mind to receive good and truth; and finally, *illuminating* understanding so that wisdom becomes visible. This sequence mirrors the regenerative order — love first breaks down the proprium, then separates, then warms, then shapes, and at last gives sight.

[§665] the twelve signs of the zodiac that are set in the sphere. How are they determined? Or to what place do they belong, according to their essence? Or else again, these five stars that circle in them, from whence are they? Or why has the demiurge appointed them as the authorities and leaders? He is the great constructor who has appointed the entire creation; and he has bound and fixed them on the sphere.

[§666] Whether these twelve signs of the zodiac that are fixed on the sphere, or these five stars that circle upon them; those and these are rulers, all of them, according to their essence. They are all enemies and rivals to each other ⟨one oppresses another, they threaten each other⟩. They plunder one another by the action of the great craftsman who has formed all the worlds. He has gathered [them] and bound them on the sphere above, which circles constantly. Now, while it circles and tumbles them, so they shall do everything that they desire, above and below, according to their pleasure. Nevertheless, a guardian is over them, a debt-collector above them; compelling, extorting, and taking what is theirs away from them!

[§667] Now, this is how it can rightly be understood. They were [s]eized from the five worlds of Darkness, and bound on the sphere. Two zodiacal signs were taken per world. Gemini and Sagittarius belong to the world of smoke, which is the mind. In contrast, Aries and Leo belong to the world of fire. Taurus, Aquarius and Libra belong to the world of wind. Cancer and Virgo and [P]isces belong to the world of water. Capricorn and Scorpio belong to the world of Darkness. These are the twelve rulers of depravity, the ones that wicked[ness] shall not [leave] For they cause all the evil and [corruption in the wo]rld, whether in tree or in flesh.

[§668] And understand also about these five stars, the leaders: from where were they formed? The star of Zeus (Jupiter) was generated from the world of smoke, which is the mind. In contrast, Aphrodite (Venus) came about from the world of fire. And then Ares (Mars) belongs to the world of wind. Hermes (Mercury) belongs to the world of water. Cronos (Saturn) belongs to the world [of] Darkness.

[§669] And also, the two ascendants are counted to fire and desire, the dry and the moist. They are the father and the mother of all these.

[§670] And also, the sun and the moon, which are counted in with them as a rule ⟨[counte]d to their kind, but why did they count them in with them?⟩ so that they would reign over them. You will discover they subjugate them at every moment.

[§671] These seven we have named, the five stars and the two ascendants: They are the doers of wickedness that do every wicked and bad act in every land, the whole of it, above and below, in every creature, in the dry and the moist, in tree and flesh.

[§672] [They] are distributed, appointed on four sides, three per angle in these four places. And they are fixed to this revolving sphere. Aries, Leo and Sagittarius, they three belong to a single side. In contrast, Taurus, Capricorn and Virgo, these other three belong to another side. And then, Gemini, Libra and Aquarius belong to another side. Scorpio, Pisces and Cancer belong to another corner. Now, they are placed like this, appointed to these four parts, and distributed on the sphere.

[§673] So, when the side of Aries, Leo and Sagittarius will be plundered by the guardian who is over it, who extorts from it and the leaders who move upon it; at that instant shall affliction strike all the four-footed creatures below. However, when [the side] of Taurus, Virgo and Capricorn will be plundered; [affliction] shall at once befall the herbs, together with the vegetables and all the fruits of the trees. Yet again, when the side of Scorpio, Pisces and Cancer will be plundered; scarcity shall befall the waters upon the earth, and drought be from place to place. Conversely, should the side of Gemini, Libra and Aquarius be plundered; deformity and stuntedness shall befall the form of mankind from place to place.

[§674] these twelve signs of the zodiac: They were fashioned from the five worlds of Darkness, and bound on the sphere.

[§675] the other five, the stars: They too came about from the five worlds of the land of Darkness.

[§676] the [t]wo ascendants: They are set to the mystery of fire and desire. They are the dry and the moist, the father and the mother.

[§677] the sun and the moon: They are strangers to them. Still, because of the constraint of the arrangement, in that the thing receives light, and as they belong and plunder them; because of this they were counted in with them in relation to the calculation of the number. For, the sun and the moon are from out of the greatness, not belonging to the stars and the signs of the zodiac.

[§665–§677]* **¶1.** The twelve fundamental genera of ruling falsity and evil that are fixed within the outermost circuit of the natural mind — how are they ordered? To what region of spiritual life do they belong according to their origin? And again, these five principal evil affections that move among them — from what source do they arise? And why has Divine Providence appointed them as governing authorities within that external sphere? It is the Lord as Divine Orderer who has arranged the whole of creation; and He has constrained and fixed even these evils within the revolving natural mind, so that they cannot act beyond their appointed bounds.

---

**¶2.** Whether these twelve genera of ruling evil fixed upon the outermost natural mind, or these five principal evil affections that circulate among them — all of them, according to their origin, are powers that seek dominion. They are all enemies and adversaries to one another: each evil oppresses every other, each threatens and assaults the rest. They plunder and consume one another through the ordering of Divine Providence, who has formed all states of life into arrangement. Providence has gathered [GAP-1: the spiritual sense requires that Providence collected or assembled all these hostile evils together] and bound them upon the outermost circuit of the mind, which revolves ceaselessly through alternating states. Now, as this cycle turns and agitates them, they act in all things according to their own nature, in interior and exterior regions alike, each following its own desire. Nevertheless, a guardian stands above them — a sovereign executor over them — compelling, extracting, and taking from them whatever good and truth they hold captive.

---

**¶3.** Now this is how it can rightly be understood. These twelve ruling evils were seized from the five genera of hell, and constrained upon the outermost natural mind. Two genera of evil were taken from each infernal origin. The evils of duplicitous reasoning and far-reaching persuasion belong to the hell of obscurity — which is the corruption of the intellectual faculty. In contrast, the evils of aggressive self-assertion and domineering pride belong to the hell of self-love. The evils of stubborn falsity, of disseminated false doctrine, and of deceptive judgment belong to the hell of empty persuasion. The evils of possessive attachment, of corrupted innocence, and of immersion in sensuality belong to the hell of concupiscence. And the evils of cold scheming and of venomous destruction belong to the hell of profound spiritual ignorance. These are the twelve governing principles of depravity — those which wickedness shall not [GAP-2: the spiritual sense requires a term for overcoming, releasing, or being separated from — that depravity cannot escape or relinquish]. For they cause all evil and [GAP-3: the spiritual sense requires "corruption" or "disorder throughout every state of the mind"] whether in perception or in the living will.

---

**¶4.** And understand also concerning these five ruling evils, the principal authorities: from what origin were they formed? The ruling evil that counterfeits wisdom was generated from the hell of obscurity — which is the corruption of the intellectual faculty. In contrast, the ruling evil that counterfeits celestial love came into being from the hell of self-love. And then the ruling evil that counterfeits spiritual power belongs to the hell of empty persuasion. The ruling evil that counterfeits truth and knowledge belongs to the hell of concupiscence. The ruling evil that counterfeits authority through utter negation belongs to the hell [GAP-4: the spiritual sense confirms this is the hell] of profound spiritual ignorance.

---

**¶5.** And also, the two originating principles are assigned to self-love and concupiscence — the active and the receptive, the will's evil and the understanding's falsity. These are the father and the mother of all these derivative evils.

---

**¶6.** And also, Divine Good and Divine Truth, which are reckoned within the same system as a governing principle — reckoned as belonging to their kind; yet why were they counted among them? — so that they would reign over them. For upon close examination one discovers that Divine Good and Divine Truth subjugate all these evils at every moment.

---

**¶7.** These seven that we have named — the five ruling evils and the two originating principles — they are the agents of wickedness that perpetrate every corrupt and destructive act in every state of the mind, throughout its entirety, in interior and exterior regions, in every living form, in the will and the understanding, in perception and in the active life of affection.

---

**¶8.** [GAP-5: The spiritual sense requires "These twelve ruling evils" or "They"] are distributed and appointed to four quarters of the mind, three to each quarter in these four orientations. And they are fixed upon this revolving outermost sphere of the natural mind. The evils of aggressive self-assertion, domineering pride, and far-reaching persuasion — these three belong to a single quarter. In contrast, the evils of stubborn falsity, cold scheming, and corrupted innocence — these other three belong to another quarter. And then the evils of duplicitous reasoning, deceptive judgment, and disseminated false doctrine belong to another quarter. Venomous destruction, immersion in sensuality, and possessive attachment belong to yet another quarter. Now they are arranged in this manner, appointed to these four orientations of the mind, and distributed upon the revolving circuit.

---

**¶9.** So when the quarter of aggressive self-assertion, domineering pride, and far-reaching persuasion is plundered by the Providence that governs it — that extracts captive good from it and from the ruling evils that move upon it — at that instant affliction shall strike all natural affections of the will below. However, when [GAP-6: the spiritual sense requires "the quarter" or "the side"] of stubborn falsity, corrupted innocence, and cold scheming is plundered, [GAP-7: the spiritual sense requires "affliction" or "devastation"] shall at once befall all natural knowledges, together with external goods and all the works proceeding from perception. Yet again, when the quarter of venomous destruction, immersion in sensuality, and possessive attachment is plundered, scarcity shall befall the truths within the natural mind, and spiritual drought shall spread from state to state. Conversely, should the quarter of duplicitous reasoning, deceptive judgment, and disseminated false doctrine be plundered, distortion and arrested development shall afflict the properly human rational form, from state to state.

---

**¶10.** These twelve governing evils: they were fashioned from the five genera of hell, and constrained upon the outermost revolving natural mind.

---

**¶11.** The other five — the ruling evil affections: they too came into being from the five genera of the realm of spiritual darkness.

---

**¶12.** The two originating principles: they are set to the arcanum of self-love and concupiscence. They are the active and the receptive — the will's evil and the understanding's falsity — the father and the mother of all derivative evils.

---

**¶13.** Divine Good and Divine Truth: they are strangers to these evils and share nothing of their origin. Still, because of the necessity of Divine order — in that every created thing receives its life and light from the Lord, and because Divine Good and Divine Truth are present even within the realm of evil in order to govern and extract what is captive — because of this they were reckoned within the same system in relation to the complete account of spiritual governance. For Divine Good and Divine Truth proceed from the Divine Itself, and do not belong to the ruling evils or to the genera of falsity.

[§678] This whole universe, above and below, reflects the pattern of the human body; as the formation of this body of flesh accords to the pattern of the universe.
[§678]* The whole of the spiritual world, in its celestial heights and its natural depths, is arranged according to the form of the Grand Human; and the constitution of every regenerating person accords, degree by degree, to the order of the spiritual world.

[§679] Its head is like the first-fruits of the five garments. And, from its neck down to the site of its heart, it resembles the pattern of the ten firmaments. And again, the heart accords to the wheel of the rotating sphere. And, from its heart down to its intestines (?), is like this atmosphere that extends from the sphere down to the earth. The [male] part of the body corresponds to this great earth. And also, from its intestines (?) down to its loins is like the three earths. And also, its shinbones to the pattern of the space in which the Porter stands. Its footsoles to the great earth upon which the Porter is stood; and the four fastenings that are under his feet. Its liver to the vessel of fire. Its flesh to the vessel of darkness. Its blood to the vessel of water.
[§679]* The inmost wisdom of the person is like the first perfections of the five external truths that clothe interior good. From the outermost boundary of that inmost degree down to the seat of the will, the person's constitution follows the pattern of the ten discrete degrees of heavenly order. The will itself accords to the living center around which the whole cycle of spiritual life revolves. From the will downward to the faculties of appropriation, the constitution is like the intermediate influx that extends from the spiritual cycle down to the natural plane. The [GAP-1: the generative natural faculty] of the person corresponds to the outermost natural plane. And from the faculties of appropriation down to the seat of generative power is like the three degrees of natural order. The sustaining structures of the lowest natural degree follow the pattern of the ground on which the foundational upholding power stands. The very base of the natural person corresponds to the outermost plane upon which that sustaining power rests, and to the four restraints upon infernal forces beneath. The organ of purification corresponds to the containing form of love's fire. The proprium — what is from self — corresponds to the containing form of falsity from evil. The vivifying spiritual life corresponds to the containing form of flowing truth.

[§680] the small body corresponds to the macro-cosmos in its firmaments, in its orderings, in its mountains, its walls and its vessels
[§680]* Thus the individual human spirit corresponds to the whole spiritual world in its heavenly degrees, in its orderings, in its states of elevation, its boundaries of protection, and its containing forms.

[§681] There exist five great camps in this great outer zone, of which the five sons of the Living Spirit are masters.
[§681]* There exist five principal provinces of spiritual governance in the great external order of the heavens, over which the five divine powers proceeding from the Lord's living operation have authority.

[§682] So, in the first watch-station above, of which the Keeper of Splendour is master, on the battlement of all the works, is found his authority over the three heavens. Then the ones below him [are under the authority of] the great King of Honour.
[§682]* In the first and highest station of spiritual guardianship, over which the guardian of celestial wisdom presides — upon the summit of all works of divine order — is found his governance over the three inmost heavens. Then those below him [GAP-2: are under the authority of] the great governing power of spiritual honor.

[§683] The second watch, of which the great King of Honour is master, his own authority (extends) over the seven firmaments beneath him.
[§683]* The second station of guardianship, over which the governing power of spiritual honor presides — his own governance extends over the seven heavenly degrees of truth beneath him.

[§684] The third watch, of which the Adamant [is master, his authority] reigns from the firmament down below to the [earth]. [And he] invigorates, according to his authority, the sphere and the worlds of the atmosphere; and the other four worlds that are appointed on this earth.
[§684]* The third station, over which the unyielding power of divine truth [GAP-3: presides, whose governance] extends from the heavenly degree below down to the [GAP-4: natural plane]. [GAP-5: This power] quickens, according to its governance, the revolving cycle of spiritual life and the intermediate states of influx between heaven and the natural; and the other four degrees of order that are established upon the natural plane.

[§685] The fourth watch, over which the King of Glory has power, is the three wheels. And his authority lies over the three earths upon the head of the Porter.
[§685]* The fourth station, over which the Lord's power of glorification has dominion, consists of the three revolving cycles of purification. And its governance extends over the three natural degrees resting upon the foundation of the sustaining power.

[§686] The fifth watch is the watch over which the Porter is authoritative. Again, he is master, according to his authority, over this great earth he stands upon, and the four fastenings under his feet.
[§686]* The fifth station is the one over which the foundational sustaining power holds authority. It governs, according to its province, the outermost natural plane upon which it rests, and the four restraints upon infernal forces beneath.

[§687] Affliction has come about in these five watches from time to time.
[§687]* Spiritual temptation and assault from evil have befallen each of these five provinces of governance in successive states.

[§688] So, in the watch of the Keeper of Splendour, Sin desired to spurt up towards the image of the Ambassador. Yet, they barred it from that place, and it fell back down in shame.
[§688]* In the province of the guardian of celestial wisdom, self-love from evil desired to rise up and profane the divine image presented by the Lord's representative. Yet the powers of order barred it from that height, and it fell back down into its own state of disgrace.

[§689] Again, an earthquake and treachery occurred in the watch of the great King of Honour, which the watchers who had arisen at the time and had [sinned] they who were sent came down, until they were humbled.
[§689]* Again, a fundamental upheaval and betrayal of trust occurred in the province of the governing power of spiritual honor, in which the rebellious intelligences who had risen up at that time and had [GAP-6: transgressed the bounds of their order] — those who were cast forth descended, until they were brought into subjection.

[§690] Also, in the watch of the Adamant, the abortions came to the earth and fashioned [the] figure of flesh.
[§690]* Also, in the province of unyielding divine truth, premature falsities and evils descended to the natural plane and fashioned [GAP-7: the] form of the proprium — the selfhood of the external person.

[§691] Again, in the watch of the King of Glory, a commotion happened in these three earths above the Porter. The pathway was obstructed for the ascent of these three wheels; that of wind, that of water, and that of fire.
[§691]* Again, in the province of the Lord's glorifying power, a violent disturbance occurred in the three natural degrees above the sustaining foundation. The pathway was obstructed for the ascending purification of the three cycles: that of natural affection, that of truth, and that of love.

[§692] Also, in the watch of the Porter, the fastenings beneath were laid bare and strained their chains. A great earthquake befell that place.
[§692]* Also, in the province of the foundational sustaining power, the restraints upon infernal forces beneath were exposed and strained against their bonds. A great upheaval of the natural foundation took place.

[§693] [Like] these five watch-stations, which exist in this great [outer zone as these five] camps: this is also the case with this body. There are another five camps there, and Wahman is watching over them, and the new man who is with him.
[§693]* [GAP-8: Just as] these five stations of guardianship exist in the great [GAP-9: external spiritual order as five] provinces: so it is also with the individual person. There are another five corresponding provinces within, and divine intelligence keeps watch over them, together with the new spiritual person who is being formed from within.

[§694] The configuration of his face, which is above his body: the mystery of the Keeper of Splendour, who rules over the watch above the zone.
[§694]* The configuration of the face, which is above the whole person: this is the spiritual reality of the guardian of celestial wisdom, who governs from the station above all external order.

[§695] His heart: the sign of the great King of Honour, who subdues the seven heavens.
[§695]* The will, which is the inmost center: this is the sign of the governing power of spiritual honor, who brings the seven heavenly degrees of truth into subjection.

[§696] His genitals: the mystery of the Adamant of Light, the one who subdues Matter.
[§696]* The generative faculty: this is the spiritual reality of the unyielding power of divine truth, which subdues the proprium and all that is merely natural.

[§697] The stomach and the fire that is in him: the likeness of the King of Glory, the one who turns the wheels, sending the life above.
[§697]* The appropriating faculty and the love that burns within it: this is the likeness of the Lord's glorifying power, which turns the cycles of purification and sends the living essence upward.

[§698] The ruling-power that is beneath in his feet: the Porter, the one who subdues with his footsoles the abysses below.
[§698]* The governing power that resides in the lowest natural degree of the person: this is the sustaining foundation, which holds in subjection beneath itself the deepest abysses of evil.

[§699] Also, the teaching of wisdom, which circulates in that body: to the likeness of the Virgin of Light, who goes up and comes down in this zone, above and below.
[§699]* Also, the teaching of divine wisdom, which circulates through the whole constitution of the person: this is the likeness of the Virgin of Light — pure divine truth — which ascends and descends through all degrees of spiritual order, from the heights to the depths and back again.

[§700] love and joy, faith and truth: they are to the likeness of these two light ships. For, the living soul should go up in them and become free through them; and it ascends from the abysses below and arrives at the heights above.
[§700]* Love and joy, faith and truth: these are the likeness of the two vessels of divine light. For through these — the vessel of celestial good and the vessel of spiritual truth — the living soul is carried upward and set free; it ascends from the deepest natural states below and arrives at the celestial heights above.

[§701] There exist four worlds in this body of the flesh, and there exist countless times seven rulers in the body of the four worlds!
[§701]* There exist four discrete degrees within the constitution of the person, and within these four degrees there are innumerable governing affections that rule the person's life in every particular!

[§702] So, the first world is from its neck up; and also the seven rulers who are in this upper world are these: the two organs of sight, and the two of hearing, and the two of smell. The other one is the mouth, which is the organ of taste.
[§702]* The first degree encompasses from the outermost boundary of inmost wisdom upward — the celestial degree. The seven governing faculties within this highest degree are these: the two organs of intellectual sight, the two of obedient perception, and the two of interior discernment. The remaining one is the mouth, which is the faculty of discriminating truth from falsity.

[§703] The second world is from its neck down to its entire upper torso. And also, the seven rulers who are in this second world are: its two arms that accord to the pattern of its two ears, which occur in its first world above; and its two breasts that are to the pattern of its organs of smell; and the two eyes of its heart; and the gullet of the stomach between the two breasts, which in the chest is the pattern of the mouth.
[§703]* The second degree extends from the boundary of inmost wisdom down through the whole region of the interior will. The seven governing faculties within this second degree are: the two active powers that correspond to the two faculties of obedient perception in the first degree above; the two faculties of interior discernment in this degree that correspond to the organs of subtle perception above; the two interior eyes of the will; and the channel of appropriation between these two, which within the interior region corresponds to the faculty of discrimination in the first degree.

[§704] Once more, there exist seven rulers in the third world of the body, which are these: the fat, the lung, the spleen, the liver, the gall, and its two kidneys.
[§704]* Furthermore, there exist seven governing faculties in the third degree of the person's constitution, which are the interior organic functions: the faculty that stores abundance, the faculty that receives the breath of spiritual life, the faculty that purifies natural affections, the faculty that purifies love, the faculty that separates bitter falsity from truth, and the two faculties of interior elimination.

[§705] Also, the fourth world below. There are another seven there of this same kind: its two buttocks (?), and its two testicles, and its two loins; and the member out of which the seed comes, that begets all the forms.
[§705]* Also, in the fourth and lowest degree: there are another seven of the same kind — the two supporting foundations, the two repositories of generative potential, the two seats of procreative power; and the member through which the interior seed of truth issues forth, begetting all derivative forms of life.

[§706] Aries and Taurus, Gemini, Cancer, Leo, Virgo, Libra, Scorpio, Sagittarius, Capricorn, Aquarius and Pisces; these are the twelve signs of the zodiac that occur in the worlds above, on the sphere. They are interlocked and bound on this turning wheel, the one in which they are [set], fixed, and planted. And, regarding all these twelve signs of the zodiac, this is how they were appointed and made leaders [in their order] on the sphere beneath the heaven that is the wheel of the stars. Now, they are appointed like this, they are thus, so that you can find the head of [each one] and you find the tail too [in the one set against it]
[§706]* The twelve fundamental states of spiritual progression — which in the celestial vocabulary are called Aries, Taurus, Gemini, Cancer, Leo, Virgo, Libra, Scorpio, Sagittarius, Capricorn, Aquarius, and Pisces — these are the twelve governing principles that exist in the heavenly order above, upon the great revolving cycle. They are interlocked and bound upon this turning wheel of providence, in which they are [GAP-10: ordered], fixed, and rooted. And regarding all these twelve governing principles, this is how they were appointed and established as ruling states [GAP-11: of spiritual order] upon the revolving cycle beneath the heaven of the inmost degree. Now, they are arranged in this way so that one can trace the beginning of [GAP-12: any spiritual progression] and find also its completion [GAP-13: in its corresponding opposite state].

[§707] Again, they occur like this, these that we have recited, one after another in this body. They are counted by order and number from the head to the feet. Its head is Aries. Its neck and its shoulders are Taurus. Its two arms Gemini. Its upper torso is Cancer. Its stomach is Leo. Its belly is Virgo. The vertical spine and its intestines (?) are Libra. Its genitals Scorpio. Its loins are Sagittarius. Its knees are Capricorn. Its shinbones are Aquarius. The soles of its feet are Pisces.
[§707]* Again, these twelve states of spiritual progression, which we have set forth one after another, exist correspondingly in the person. They are counted in order and degree from the inmost to the outermost. The inmost wisdom is Aries. The capacity to receive and convey truth is Taurus. The two active powers of understanding are Gemini. The interior will that contains is Cancer. The central love that governs is Leo. The faculty of discrimination and purification is Virgo. The upright structure of moral order and the processes of interior sorting are Libra. The generative power in its hidden potency is Scorpio. The seat of procreative and propelling force is Sagittarius. The sustaining structure that bears weight is Capricorn. The supporting vessels of the lower natural degree are Aquarius. The very foundation upon which the person rests in the natural world is Pisces.

[§708] Behold, these are also distributed one after another. They exist in this body, as if turned to the side and bent to the pattern of the twelve signs of the zodiac. They too are thus, arranged one against another, head to tail, as they occur on the wheel [from below] up.
[§708]* Behold, these twelve states are also distributed one after another. They exist within the person as if turned to one side and bent to mirror the pattern of the twelve governing principles in the heavens. They too are arranged in complementary opposition, beginning joined to end, as they occur upon the revolving wheel [GAP-14: of providence from below] upward.

[§709] Also, they are counted by order and number of the body and all its members. So, we have proclaimed that these are turned to the side, bent, and spread out; because from its head down to its hip shall count six to its left, and another six to its right.
[§709]* Also, they are counted in the order and number of the whole person and all its faculties. We have declared that these states are turned to the side, bent, and spread out, because from the inmost down to the center there are six to one side, and another six to the other.

[§710] The six that are down its right are these: the right temple is Aries; its right shoulder is Taurus; its right arm is Gemini; its right rib-cage is Cancer; its stomach is Leo; the right side of its reproductive organs are Virgo.
[§710]* The six that descend along the right — the side of good — are these: the right aspect of inmost wisdom is Aries; the right capacity for receiving truth is Taurus; the right active power of understanding is Gemini; the right portion of the containing will is Cancer; the central governing love is Leo; the right aspect of the generative faculty in its purity is Virgo.

[§711] the other six which are to its left, in contrast they come from its hip to its head: its left [hip] is Libra; its left rib-cage is Scorpio; its left breast and left kidney is Sagittarius; its [left] elbow is Capricorn; its left shoulder is Aquarius; its left temple is Pisces. This is how the creator of the body himself has appointed them. He has [set] them in order and arranged them, one against its companion, head to tail.
[§711]* The other six, which are to the left — the side of truth — ascend in contrast from the center back to the inmost: the left [GAP-15: aspect of moral discernment] is Libra; the left portion of the containing will is Scorpio; the left interior faculty of warmth and purification is Sagittarius; the [GAP-16: left] active power of understanding is Capricorn; the left capacity for receiving truth is Aquarius; the left aspect of inmost wisdom is Pisces. This is how the one who forms the person's spiritual constitution has arranged them. He has [GAP-17: established] them in order and disposed them, each facing its counterpart, beginning joined to completion.

[§712] There exist many powers in this body. They are the house-dwellers who are made the leaders in it. There are eight hundred and forty times ten thousand rulers made chiefs in the human body! They are distributed and set firm, quartered according to house. The count and number of their houses is two hundred and ten times ten thousand.
[§712]* There exist innumerable powers within the person's constitution. These are the indwelling affections and persuasions that have been established as governing forces within. There are affections and dominant states beyond reckoning that have been made rulers within the human form! They are distributed and fixed in place, each quartered according to the faculty it inhabits. The count of their dwelling-places is vast beyond measure.

[§713] When all these rulers come creeping and moving within the body, they will meet one another; and they shall beset and destroy one another, and [make war] in them there. When [the affliction grows until] they shall erupt from the body of the person who [will] die; and make putrid boils and sores and burning wounds in the body. Either they might have made him sick within; or else they might come forth upon his outer side. Those wounds shall first be squeezed and discharge their liquid, and empty out their pus, until they are squeezed [clean of their impurity] and the suppuration forced out from the wound. Afterwards, their scars shall close over; and they are healed and obtain relief from the places that came out upon them.
[§713]* When all these governing affections begin to stir and move within the person, they encounter one another; and they assault and seek to destroy one another, and [GAP-18: wage conflict] among themselves within. When [GAP-19: the assault intensifies until] they erupt from the constitution of the person who [GAP-20: is undergoing] spiritual death; and they produce corrupt eruptions of evil, open wounds of falsity, and burning afflictions of self-love within the person. Either these evils make the person sick in the interior will; or else they break forth upon the external life for all to see. Those eruptions of evil must first be pressed and forced to discharge the falsity within them, and to empty out the corruption of their infection, until they are thoroughly pressed [GAP-21: clean of every impurity] and the putrefaction of falsity is driven out from the wound. Afterward, the openings in the person's spiritual fabric close over; and the person is healed and obtains relief from the states of evil that had broken forth upon them.

[§714] In what way shall the elements be gathered in, from one to another?
[§714]* In what manner shall the scattered faculties and qualities of the spirit be progressively interiorized, each degree being received into the next more interior degree?

[§715] The light shall be gathered in to the fire, and the fire itself gathered in to the water, and the water gathered in to the wind, and the wind gathered to the air, and the air gathered to the answer, and the answer gathered to the summons. Also, the summons to the purified Mind, which is the intellectual. The intellectual to the Pillar of Glory, [the Pillar of Glory to the First Man, the Man to the Ambassador, the Ambassador to] the aeons of greatness.
[§715]* The wisdom of the understanding shall be received into the love of the will, and the love of the will itself shall be received into living truth, and living truth received into the influx of the spirit, and the influx of the spirit into the sphere of celestial life, and the sphere of celestial life into the responsive perception awakened by the Divine, and that responsive perception into the Lord's calling itself. The Lord's calling then flows into the regenerated understanding, which is the purified rational mind. The purified rational mind is elevated into the Divine Truth that connects all degrees of heaven and earth — [GAP-1: the Divine Truth proceeding is received into the Divine Human, the Divine Human into the Lord's mediating presence that goes forth, and the Lord's mediating presence into] the infinite states of the Lord's celestial kingdom.

---

**Translator's note:** The entire sequence describes regeneration as a concentrating return — every exterior faculty being gathered into its more interior origin, degree by degree, until what was scattered through the natural mind is wholly received back into the Divine. The chain mirrors what Swedenborg calls discrete degrees: natural understanding → spiritual affection → celestial truth → the Divine Human → the Divine Itself. The "Pillar of Glory" resists simple translation but reads most clearly as the *Divine Truth proceeding* — the ordered axis through which all ascent passes, corresponding to the Word as the structural means connecting the lowest natural degree to the Lord Himself.

[§716] what then are these 'great garments'? Or again, this you sometimes call the 'rags', what are those 'rags'? Or the 'worn and torn apart'? Or what are the 'images and the tattered clothes'? Or what is this name: 'light element'? Or conversely, what is the 'Cross of Light'? Or why named otherwise: the 'counsel of life'? Or what is the 'soul that is slain'; being killed, oppressed, and murdered in the enemy? What are all these names?
[§716]* What then are these "great external truths"? Or again, what you sometimes call the "degraded truths" — what are those degraded truths? Or the "devastated and fragmented truths"? Or what are the "semblances and remnants of truth in their ruined state"? Or what is this name, "the essential substance of wisdom"? Or conversely, what is the "suffering of wisdom bound in temptation"? Or why is it named otherwise, "the Divine calling toward life"? Or what is "the interior life that is destroyed" — annihilated, subjugated, and consumed within the power of evil? What are all these spiritual states?

[§717] how have they changed with all these names and become set in altered forms
[§717]* How has the one wisdom been differentiated into all these designations and taken on these altered conditions of existence?

[§718] The garments that are named 'great garment' are the five intellectuals that make perfect the body of the Pillar of Glory, the Perfect Man. They were purified by the coming of the Ambassador.
[§718]* The external truths named "great" are the five faculties of the understanding that complete the form of the ascending structure of Divine Truth — the fully regenerated Divine Human. These were purified by the descent of the Divine into manifestation.

[§719] In contrast, the ones named 'rag': the rags are the devoured enlightening power. It is compounded and entangled in the body of the universe in the rulers who are above, who exist in all the firmaments. Also, its shine is that which lights up and is displayed in the stars of the chain and the powers of the course. And likewise, these rags that are entangled in these three earths below, above the head of the Porter [and also] on the earth that is under the Porter. These are named the 'rags', because [they are] set [apart in every place]
[§719]* In contrast, those named "degraded truths": the degraded truths are the devoured illuminating wisdom. It is compounded and entangled in the general form of the created order, within the ruling falsities that dominate from the interior, which persist throughout all the doctrinal structures that separate one degree from another. And its radiance is that which shines forth and is displayed in the knowledges that are bound in their fixed series and in the faculties that govern the natural progression. And likewise, these degraded truths that are entangled in the three natural degrees below — above the sustaining principle at the lowest foundation [GAP-1] — upon the natural plane that lies beneath the sustaining principle. These are named "degraded truths" because [GAP-2] established [GAP-3]

[§720] However, the 'great worn and torn apart garments', which we have proclaimed, is this entangled living power. It is reaped, cut, and devoured. It is overpowered by the five worlds of the flesh, which creep as they move from place to place.
[§720]* However, the "great devastated and fragmented truths" which we have set forth — this is the entangled living influx of the Divine. It is harvested, severed, and consumed. It is overpowered by the five corporeal domains of the proprium, which insinuate themselves as they advance from state to state.

[§721] On the other hand, [these] we have termed the 'images and the great tattered clothes' [they] are the three great garments of the wind, the fire, and the water. These the Living Spirit cloaked upon his body. With them he set in order things in the universe. With them also he emptied the vessels. He swept them out and laid them bare in front of the Porter at the foundation of all things.
[§721]* On the other hand, [GAP-4: REVIEW these] we have termed the "semblances and great remnants of truth in their ruined state" [GAP-5] are the three encompassing external truths of understanding, of love, and of truth itself. These the Lord's operative Divine Truth clothed upon His external form. With them He set all things in the spiritual world into order. With them also He emptied the containing forms of their falsities. He swept them clean and laid them bare before the sustaining principle at the foundation of all things.

[§722] Conversely, '[the ele]ments', we have called them this name, are this power that remains in all things below. It rises up in the wombs of all the earths, gathering together, and pouring forth upon all things.
[§722]* Conversely, "the essential substances of wisdom" — we have called them by this name — are the living power that remains within all things in the natural degree. It rises through the receptive states within all natural conditions, gathering itself together and pouring forth upon all things.

[§723] And then, the '[Light] Cross' [we have termed] this bound 'light power'. It [is spread in] all [things] upon the earth, in the dry and the moist.
[§723]* And then, the "[GAP-6: REVIEW Light] suffering of wisdom in temptation" — [GAP-7: REVIEW we have termed] this bound "illuminating power of the Divine." It [GAP-8] all [GAP-9] upon the natural plane, in what is without good and in what is conjoined with good.

[§724] Also, the one named the 'counsel of life' is the summons and the obedience. It moves in all these powers that we have named. It awakens them, gives them ease, and drives them to their movement.
[§724]* Also, that which is named "the Divine calling toward life" is the summons from the Divine and the obedient response of the human will. It moves within all the faculties and powers we have named. It awakens them, brings them peace, and impels them toward their proper function and activity.

[§725] The one named 'the soul that is slain', which is killed, oppressed and murdered; it is this power of fruit, vegetables and seeds. They shall be threshed, plucked and cut down; and they give nourishment to the worlds of the flesh. Further: the wood should it be dry, and the garment should it become ragged, will perish. Again, it is [a pa]rt of this entire soul that is slain and killed; since also even this whole part of it they kill [and devour]
[§725]* That which is named "the interior life that is destroyed" — which is annihilated, subjugated, and consumed — this is the living power within the goods of works, the natural goods of use, and the interior truths still in their seminal state. These shall be threshed out, plucked away, and cut down, and they give sustenance to the corporeal domains of the proprium. Furthermore: the perception that has lost all its living good, and the external truth that has become wholly devastated — these shall perish. Again, this is a portion of the entire interior life that is destroyed and annihilated; since even this whole portion of it they destroy [GAP-10]

[§726] For all these names [that we have proclaimed] are a single [power] since the beginning, it [was one] as it enlightens; but they separated into all these parts in this first contest. They became set in all these altered forms, and these many names.
