Of course, if now all these varieties are laid bare, and stripped of all these appearances, and parted from all these names; they will gather together and make a single form, and a single name, unaltered and unchangeable for ever in the land of their first essence, from which they were sent forth against the enemy.
[§726]* For all these designations [GAP-11] are a single [GAP-12] since the beginning, it [GAP-13] as it illuminates all things; but they were separated into all these distinct conditions in the first great temptation-combat. They became established in all these altered forms and received these many names. Of course, if now all these varieties are laid bare and stripped of all these outward appearances and parted from all these designations, they will gather together and constitute a single form and a single name — unaltered and unchangeable forever — in the realm of their first Divine essence, from which they were sent forth against the power of evil.

[§727] [The] entirety of envy is this first nature that occu[red] in the worlds of darkness.
[§727]* [GAP-1: REVIEW The] whole of the lust to appropriate what belongs to divine good is this primordial proprium — the original self-derived will — that arose in the states of falsity from evil.

[§728] In this envy that [existed] in it, the First Man [swallowed] its poiso[n and] its evil-doing. His garment[s] were [seized and] it cut them and drew them; [ ... ]
[§728]* In this lust to appropriate, which [GAP-2: ...] within it, the Divine Good descending into combat [GAP-3: ...] its persuasive falsity [GAP-4: ...] its active working of evil. The external truths that clothed the Divine Good were [GAP-5: ...] — the falsity from evil severed and stripped them away; [GAP-6: ...]

[§729] It envied the first image of the Ambassador, which he displayed to it in the heights abov[e], i[n] his form that it saw. It established the seal and the image of flesh, and put the impression of t[h]at image on flesh, its very own birth.
[§729]* It lusted to possess the first manifestation of the Divine Wisdom sent forth, which the Lord as Divine Truth displayed before it from the celestial states above, in the form of divine order which it perceived outwardly. From this perception, evil fashioned a counterfeit likeness and an imitation form within what is merely corporeal and natural, impressing the semblance of that divine form upon its own proprium — a thing generated entirely from its own self-derived will.

[§730] [The th]ird time it envied Adam, the fir[st hum]an. It seized a power from him, and bore it forth from a [rib of] of Adam's [flesh].
[§730]* A [th]ird time it lusted to appropriate what belonged to Adam — the Most Ancient Church, the celestial human in its earliest state. Evil seized a living faculty from that church, and brought forth from it something derived from the [GAP-7: REVIEW innermost natural proprium of] the celestial human's [GAP-8: REVIEW self-derived will] — appropriating the very proprium that had little life of its own, and generating from it a counterfeit form of the will separated from celestial good.

[§731] The [holy] living f[ire] is present i[n] eight places.
[§731]* The [GAP-1] living divine love is present in eight distinct states of spiritual order.

[§732] The first place wherein it is, is this [light] land [where it is present and] exists. It is established beneath [it, and is ut]terly beyond the totality. It is the foundation and basis of that great earth, and the consideratio[n th]at is beneath them all.
[§732]* The first state in which it dwells is the [GAP-2] ground of wisdom [GAP-3] in which it abides and has its being. It is established as the most interior principle [GAP-4], utterly beyond the whole of created order. It is the foundation and supporting ground of the entire regenerate will, and the deepest interior perception that underlies all other states.

[§733] The second place wherein it is, is the Father, the God of truth; because [the Fathe]r is established in his great, mighty consideration. So, [firs]t in the Father. The other powers that came from him were revealed through him, and (the fire) too came w[i]th them [and was revea]led by them.
[§733]* The second state in which it dwells is the Divine Itself, the Divine Good of Divine Truth; because the Divine Itself is established in its infinite and omnipotent interior perception. Thus, first within the Divine Itself. All the derivative divine faculties that proceeded from the Divine were made manifest through it, and divine love too came forth with them and was revealed through their proceeding.

[§734] The third [place wherein it is], is the First Man, h[e] wh[o was clothed in the five sons as his] armour. Again, it is this fire [ ... ... ] on the body of the Perfect Man.
[§734]* The third [GAP-5] state in which it dwells is the Celestial Human — the most ancient spiritual form — who [GAP-6] was clothed in protecting truths. Again, it is this divine love [GAP-7] resting upon the external form of the fully regenerate will.

[§735] [The fourth is this fir]e that was purified from all the [light] powers, [which is i]n the outer wall, in the ship of the day.
[§735]* The fourth is this divine love that was separated and purified from all the [GAP-8] faculties of wisdom, which dwells in the outermost boundary of spiritual order, in the containing form of illuminated understanding.

[§736] [The fifth i]s this fire that is in the light-giver of the night [and dwells] within the water.
[§736]* The fifth is this divine love that abides in the containing form of faith in states of spiritual obscurity, [GAP-9] dwelling within truth itself.

[§737] [The] sixth is this f[i]re that is purified from the totality. The Living [Spirit] swathed it upon his body, over the summons and the obedience. He has established many things by it. He went down and stripped himself of it before the Por[t]er.
[§737]* [GAP-10] The sixth is this divine love that is purified and distinguished from all derivative forms. The Divine Truth Proceeding [GAP-11] clothed its own external form with this love, over and above both the calling forth and the reception. It ordered and established all things through this love. It descended into lower natural states and divested itself of this love before the Guardian of the threshold between spiritual and natural degrees.

[§738] The seventh is this living fire that is of the new earth, and is the consideration. For it is the first foundation in the new earth, it they built anew [ ... ... ] the first course, that is a crown for the entire earth. T[hey] are below the totality, over the entire earth of da[rkne]ss [that is] in it, in the [ ... ] whole [earth]
[§738]* The seventh is this living divine love belonging to the regenerated natural mind, and it is interior perception itself. For it is the primary founding principle in the regenerated will, upon which all things were built anew [GAP-12] as the first ordering truth, which is the governing crown over the whole natural mind. These principles lie beneath the whole of spiritual order, yet reign over the entire natural mind that had been dominated by falsity from evil [GAP-13] within it, throughout the [GAP-14] whole [GAP-15] of its extent.

[§739] [The] eigh[th] is the fire that shall be revealed in this [last time upon every] person, and it signifies fear and war. Furthermore it [ ... ] it displays and manifests its sign in [its ter]ror. It shall be r[ev]ealed in these two light ships.
[§739]* [GAP-16] The eighth is the divine love that shall be made manifest in the final [GAP-17] state of the human spirit, and it signifies spiritual temptation and combat. Furthermore it [GAP-18] displays and makes evident its presence through the upheaval of judgment. It shall be revealed through both containing forms of celestial and spiritual illumination.

[§740] This i[s the way] that the holy living fire exists in these [eight places].
[§740]* This is the manner in which holy living divine love exists throughout these [GAP-19] eight states of spiritual order.

---

*Note on method:* "Consideration" (Coptic rendering of a Persian/Aramaic contemplative term) consistently translates as **interior perception** — the deepest reflective faculty by which spiritual realities are apprehended. The "ships" of day and night correspond to **containing forms** (vessels) of understanding in illumination and faith in obscurity, respectively. The "Porter" corresponds to the **guardian principle** at the threshold between discrete degrees — the boundary between the spiritual and natural minds where what is higher must divest itself to pass into what is lower. This descent-and-divestiture pattern closely mirrors what Swedenborg describes as the accommodation of influx between degrees.

[§741] the summons and the obedience
[§741]* Concerning the divine calling and the reciprocal acknowledgment of the human will.

[§742] Consider the summons, which was sent from the Living Spirit in the beginning. He sent it to the First Man. It is a peace letter and greeting that he wrote and sent to his brother, in which all the tidings are written down, together with everything it will bring about and establish by that summons. That summons, the First Man [received, and all that is ordained to happen]
[§742]* Consider the divine calling, which proceeded from the Lord as Divine Truth in the beginning. He directed it into the celestial mind that had descended into spiritual combat. It is an influx of divine peace and conjunction that He inscribed with all providential truths and sent to the human will as to one conjoined to Him in origin — in which all things of the Lord's providence are contained, together with everything that calling will accomplish and bring into spiritual order. That divine calling, the celestial mind [GAP-1: is destined to receive and undergo according to providence.]

[§743] The answer that was sent from the First Man came to the heights, to the Living Spirit. That is also a letter, which was sent from the First Man to the Living Spirit, in which all the tidings are written down; and every war and the struggles that he had waged. It did [make known] in the answer that the First Man [had written down and made known] everything that had happened and everything [that would happen], all that had been done and whatever is made ready to be done by him. The Living Spirit knew all the wars and the dangers and the contests that the rulers had waged against the First Man.
[§743]* The reciprocal acknowledgment that ascended from the celestial mind came into the interior and celestial states, to the Lord as Divine Truth. That also is a communication of interior states, which was sent from the celestial mind to the Lord, in which all the states of temptation are laid open; and every spiritual combat and the struggles that had been endured against evils and falsities. It did [GAP-2: set forth and make manifest] in the reciprocal acknowledgment that the celestial mind [GAP-3: disclosed and laid bare] everything that had been undergone in temptation and everything [GAP-4: REVIEW: that remained yet to be endured], all that had been accomplished through combat and whatever is prepared yet to be accomplished by it. The Lord as Divine Truth perceived all the temptations, and the spiritual perils, and the interior conflicts that the ruling evils and dominant falsities had waged against the celestial mind.

---

*Note on method:* "Rulers" corresponds to dominant evils and their falsities — the loves of self and the world that govern the natural mind and assault the celestial. "Brother" corresponds to conjunction through mutual good, reflecting that the Lord and the celestial human share one origin in Divine Good. The "letter" in both directions is the medium of spiritual communication: influx descending, acknowledgment ascending — the two movements that constitute all conjunction between the Divine and the human.

[§744] For the light-givers that are in the heavens shall be revealed with these two aspects, in the aspect of love and the aspect of fear. They display these two aspects of the powers above that are in the heavens, because of their soul that passes through the totality. For in these two appearances, the aspect of love that is beauty and sublimity, together with the other aspect of confrontation that is in them, they shall purify in them the powers above.

[§745] The soul that assumes the body, when Wahman will come to it, shall be purified by the power of wisdom and obedience, and it is cleansed and made a new man. There is no trouble in (the soul), nor confusion nor disturbance.

[§746] However, when a disturbance will arise for him and he will be troubled, this disturbance shall go into him in [many ways], first through his birth-signs and his difficult stars that [are upon him, and] they turn over him and stir him and trouble him with lust and anger and depression and grief, as he wills. Also, as he wills, the powers of heaven shall trouble him through their roots, to which he is attached.

[§747] Again, there are times you shall find the powers of heaven are peaceful and tranquil for him, and it comes to [pass that] a difficult part comes into him by the nourishment that he has eaten, or else indeed in some [drink] or in the water that they have drunk. Again, trouble and confusion and anger will increase in him, and lust multiplies upon him together with depression and grief; because of the nourishment of the bread he has eaten and the water he has drunk, which are full of bothersome parts, a vengeful counsel. They shall enter his body, [mixed in] with these foods, and they even become joined in with the wicked parts of the body and the Sin that is in him; transferring the anger and the lust and the depression and the grief, these wicked thoughts of the body.

[§748] should you be troubled [know that it comes to you from] them through the conduits and the roots of they above [so that you may recognise] the flood that comes into you.

[§749] There are times, however, if you shall find that the nourishment that comes into you is pure [and clear, and full of good parts], so it is greater in light and life while the waste is less in it, and the evil-doing is diminished from it. Even the atmosphere above is still, being purified through the [ ... ... ] also the image is calmed from [its disturbance, and what] increases in you through those good parts of the food that come into you. And they find you at ease and tranquil, as you are well governed in your abodes because of the living part, which is greater on account of the light of these justified souls that are in it. The ones that have perfected their deeds. Their dues have ceased, while their soul is carefree [because] they have been allied with these living souls that [are p]resent in you. Due to this you shall find them in you, calm in tranquillity, and they come from you without trouble; and you are found flourishing of body. Even your deeds are determined, being well constructed after their manner, and your doctrines are in order, as your words [are true, and] your soul is carefree within you, ascending like a bird.

[§750] when [your bo]dy is troubled, you shall be troubled by the conduits of they below, the difficult parts that come into you by food and the act of eating. Again, when the conduits above are calmed from you, even your food that has come into you is clear and pure. Your heart shall be found ordered in its place, your counsels at peace from disturbance.

[§745–§750]* **¶6.** The interior spiritual life, when it is conjoined to the external natural mind, receives influx of Divine Truth from the Lord; whereupon it is reformed through the power of wisdom received and obedience rendered, and it is purified and made into a regenerate person. In this state there is no spiritual conflict within the interior life, no confusion of understanding, and no disturbance of the will.

---

**¶7.** Yet when temptation arises and the person is thrown into spiritual combat, this disturbance enters into the interior life in [GAP-1] successive degrees — first through hereditary evil inclinations and adverse tendencies from birth, which [GAP-2] revolve upon the person and agitate and disturb through concupiscence and hostility toward truth and spiritual desolation and anguish, according to what the person's own will permits. Moreover, according to what the will permits, the societies of the spiritual world exert their influence through the interior connections to which the person is bound.

---

**¶8.** Again, there are times when the influx from the spiritual societies is at rest and in order for the person, and it comes to pass that [GAP-3] a harmful element enters the interior life through the goods and truths that have been appropriated from external life, or indeed through some [GAP-4] particular form of external experience, or through the truths that have been received into the understanding. Then conflict and confusion and hostility to good increase within, and concupiscence multiplies together with desolation and anguish — because the goods of the will that were appropriated and the truths of the understanding that were received are full of admixtures of evil and falsity, constituting a vindictive purpose from the proprium. These enter the external natural mind, [GAP-5: REVIEW mixed in] together with what is appropriated, and they become conjoined with the evil tendencies already present in the natural mind and with the hereditary evil that dwells therein — exciting hostility toward good, concupiscence, spiritual desolation, and anguish, which are the evil cogitations proceeding from the unregenerate selfhood.

---

**¶9.** Should you be cast into spiritual combat, [GAP-6] these evils act through the channels of influx and the interior connections of the spiritual societies above, [GAP-7] so that the overwhelming influx of falsity that enters into you may be recognized as such.

---

**¶10.** There are times, however, when the goods and truths appropriated into your life are genuine [GAP-8] and unmixed, so that there is more wisdom and spiritual life within them while the falsity and evil in them is less, and the destructive tendency is diminished. Even the spiritual sphere surrounding you is at rest, being purified through the [GAP-9] activity of the Divine order upon it. Also the external form of the mind is calmed from [GAP-10] its former agitation, and what increases in you does so through those genuine goods that are appropriated into the interior life. And they find you in a state of peace and spiritual rest, well-governed in your interior states, because the living spiritual element within predominates — on account of the light of wisdom from those perfected goods and truths that reside within. These are the goods and truths that have completed their spiritual uses. Their obligations of restitution have ended, and the interior spiritual life is free from anxiety [GAP-11] because they have been conjoined with those living goods and truths that are present within you. On account of this you find them within you, dwelling in tranquility, and they proceed from you without conflict; and you are found flourishing in your external life. Your works also are rightly ordered, being well formed according to their proper nature, and your doctrines of understanding are arranged in due sequence, as your expressions of truth [GAP-12] correspond to their interior source, and your interior spiritual life rises freely within you, ascending like rational thought toward the Lord.

---

**¶11.** When your external natural mind is in conflict, you are disturbed through the channels of influx from the lower natural degree — the harmful elements that enter through the appropriation of external goods and truths. Conversely, when the channels of influx from the spiritual are at peace toward you, and the goods and truths you have appropriated are clear and genuine, then your will is found rightly ordered in its place, and the deliberations of your understanding are free from the disturbance of falsity.

[§751] This [liv]ing sou[l] that is set in mixture; for it wishes to [as]cend and go to the house of its people, but it kn[ow][s a]nd understands that it has no open door in all the powers of heaven and earth. For they are its oppression that [presses upon] it everywhere. Indeed, it has no open door except the sun and the moon themselves, the light-givers of the heavens [ ... / ... ] they become the place of rest for it. And they become a door that opens for it [in] the coming forth, and (the soul) comes out through them to the country of the household of i[ts] people.
[§751]* This interior spiritual life that remains implanted within the person where good and evil, truth and falsity, are commingled — for it yearns to be elevated and to return to the heavenly society to which it belongs by its inmost love — yet it perceives and comprehends that there is no open passage through any of the faculties of the internal or external mind. For these faculties are its confinement, which [GAP-1] press upon it from every direction. Indeed, it has no open passage except through Divine Love and Divine Truth themselves, the illuminating principles of the spiritual mind, [GAP-2] which become the state of celestial peace for it. And they become the means by which the interior degrees are opened for it [GAP-3: REVIEW in] the liberation from the natural state, and the spiritual life passes through them into the kingdom of heaven where its kindred affections dwell.

[§752] The light-givers of the heavens have no place of rest amongst all the powers of heaven; except for the lan[d] of light, that has indeed been theirs for ever.
[§752]* Divine Love and Divine Truth, the illuminating principles of the spiritual mind, have no permanent state of peace among any of the created faculties of the internal person; except in the Divine itself, which has been their own dwelling from eternity.

---

*Note on the structure:* The passage describes the condition of spiritual life trapped in the mixed state of the unregenerate mind. Every faculty — natural and spiritual — is closed by the proprium. Only the Lord's own Love (sun) and Truth (moon), acting as vessels of influx from above, can provide rest and open the interior degrees, allowing that life to pass out of confinement into heaven. ¶8 then states the principle behind this: Divine Good and Divine Truth originate in no created thing, but solely in the Lord's own infinite being — they merely pass *through* the mind's faculties without residing in them.

[§753] Fifteen paths are drawn out in the zone. Four paths are pure and belong [to the] light, leading up to life. Eight other paths are [mixed], leading above from that place. The light shall go [u]p and become free through them; be purified and go in [to the] ships. However, the waste is separated and thrown [dow]n to transmigr[a]tion and the other three paths of [waste are] discharged to the gehennas.
[§753]* Fifteen ways of spiritual progression are established within the intermediate state of the human mind. Four of these ways are uncorrupted and belong [GAP-1] to divine wisdom, ascending toward spiritual life itself. Eight further ways are [GAP-2] of mingled quality — partly of good, partly of evil — rising upward from that intermediate condition. Through these, whatever is of wisdom ascends and is set free; it is purified and passes in [GAP-3] to the doctrinal vessels that carry truth to the heavenly kingdom. But what is refuse — the residue of falsity and evil — is separated out and cast down into further states of bondage, and the remaining three ways of [GAP-4] spiritual refuse are expelled into the hells.

[§754] [The three] paths that belong to waste, the ones that are drawn to the gehennas, [a]re drawn from the fleshes. O[n]e of them is the appetite for [lawl]essness, that of people and of all flesh. The second is the [destruction of] all bodies, which is slaughter, [with] which all flesh is consumed. The third is the damaging action, which [harms] the Cross of Light and every body; together with the rest of the error and the blasphemy that wounds the gods.
[§754]* [GAP-5] The three ways that belong to what is spiritually refuse, those that are drawn toward the hells, originate from what is merely corporeal and sensual in the will. The first of these is the lust for what violates divine order — the concupiscence that rules in human beings and in every sensual affection. The second is the [GAP-6] destruction that pervades all outward forms of life, which is the annihilation of good and truth — [GAP-7] by which every natural affection is consumed and laid waste. The third is the act of profanation, which [GAP-8] assaults the divine truth suffering within natural forms, and every external vessel of spiritual life — together with all remaining falsity and blasphemy that wounds the divine attributes themselves.

[§755] Th[ese th]ree paths lead to the gehennas. Every person shall follow after hi[s] de[e]ds, whether to life or indeed to death.
[§755]* These three ways lead into the hells. Every soul proceeds according to the quality of its life and its works — either toward spiritual life, or else toward spiritual death.

[§756] The four elements [are what is] purified. From the beginning they are holy ones, living ones, and enlighteners. However, when they undertood [the mingling] in that first struggle, the waste was [mixed] in with them, and the darkness of the enemy was mixed with them. And when they were come into the body, stripping off these polluted garments, they set them in the flesh. Their pollutions, which they have stripped off, shall be displayed in a [form] and become a danger to the body.
[§756]* The four fundamental faculties of the spiritual mind — love, wisdom, truth, and perception — [GAP-1: REVIEW are what is] undergoing regeneration. From their origin in the Divine they are holy, alive with spiritual life, and illuminating with heavenly light. However, when they entered into [GAP-2: …] the first spiritual combat of temptation, the corruption of evil was [GAP-3: REVIEW intermingled] with them, and the falsity that proceeds from hell became conjoined with them. And when these faculties descended into the external natural mind, putting off the corrupted external truths that had clothed them, they deposited those corruptions in the selfhood, the proprium. The falsities and evils that they have cast off shall be made manifest in a [GAP-4: …] and become a source of destruction to the external natural mind.

[§757] The discharge of this pollution, that [the fir]e shall disgorge and place in the body, is [blo]od. The discharge of the blood is anger. The discharge of anger is the humours.
[§757]* The corruption that love turned to self-love disgorges and deposits in the natural mind is the profaned life-force of perverted vitality. The emanation of that perverted vitality is the burning of wrath. The emanation of wrath is the disordered flowing states of the unregenerate mind.

[§758] Also, the emissions that the waters discharge from them are lust. The discharge of lust is bitterness. The emission of bitterness is f[e]ver.
[§758]* Likewise, the corruptions that perverted truths discharge from themselves are the concupiscences of disordered desire. The emanation of concupiscence is interior anguish and bitterness. The emanation of bitterness is the inflamed restlessness that consumes the natural mind.

[§759] The emission that light shall disgorge is flesh. The emission of flesh is gloom. [The] emission of gloom is obstinacy.
[§759]* The corruption that wisdom in its fallen state disgorges is the selfhood — the proprium. The emanation of the proprium is spiritual obscurity and darkness of understanding. [GAP-5: REVIEW The] emanation of that spiritual obscurity is hardened resistance against all divine influx — obstinacy of will.

[§760] The emissions that the tree shall disgorge, they are the voices of the winds of shame. The emission of winds of shame [is sorrow. The emission of sorrow is the tear]
[§760]* The corruptions that perception in its fallen state disgorges are the false persuasions arising from the pride of self-derived intelligence — the empty声 声ounds of vain reasoning rooted in shame before the Divine. The emanation of those false persuasions from self-intelligence [GAP-6: is …]

[§761] The emission of the [tear] is the [last] of these twelve emissions.
[§761]* The emanation of [GAP-7: REVIEW the grief of spiritual anguish] is the [GAP-8: …] completion and summation of these twelve corruptions flowing from the four faculties — three emanations proceeding from each, forming the full system of spiritual disorder in the unregenerate mind.

[§762] [Now] these four elements shall [disgorge, and] what are gathered in are [f]ound by the management of this soul food [that] enters the body. When they enter the body, they are cleansed and purified and established [i]n their living image, which is the new man. They shall live [again] and receive the Light Mind and be purified in their image; and they come forth, being cleansed and holy. They attain their first rest.
[§762]* [GAP-9: …] these four fundamental faculties shall [GAP-10: …] what is gathered inward is [f]ound through the providential ordering of the spiritual nourishment from the Word [GAP-11: REVIEW that] enters the external natural mind. When the goods and truths of that nourishment enter the natural mind, the faculties are purified and reformed and regenerated, and they are established in their true living spiritual form, which is the new person — the regenerated human being. They come alive [GAP-12: …] and receive the Divine Wisdom flowing into the understanding and the will, and they are purified in their original heavenly form; and they come forth from the natural mind, being cleansed and made holy. They attain their first state of heavenly peace and rest.

[§763] they shall be cleansed and go u[p to] the land of the living ones; but these that come to [the flesh] and pass through them and [ ... / ... ] in sins. Their end will occur in transmigration and spirit.
[§763]* They are purified and ascend into heaven, the state of the truly living; but those who come to [GAP-13: …] and pass through those states and [GAP-14: … / …] remaining in falsities and evils. Their end shall be a continual cycling through states of vastation, wandering without rest in the spiritual world.

[§764] it shall be released from five places and ascend over this great earth and [rise up] and it is revealed and seen in the atmosphere between.
[§764]* The captive wisdom shall be liberated from the five sensory faculties of the external mind and ascend beyond this vast natural plane of consciousness, and [GAP-1] and it becomes manifest and perceptible in the rational understanding that mediates between the celestial and the natural.

[§765] So, the cloud shall ascend from fire to the heavens towards the likeness of the Virgin of Light that she shall display to it. Its sign is the flashes that occur with lightning storms [and are seen. I]ts exchange [ ... ]. And they shall be purified by her towards [receiving] the light that she shall reveal [to it]. The rulers shall be released by the lightning storms and they are freed and sent.
[§765]* So the obscured truth shall rise from the faculty of love toward the celestial realm, drawn toward the form of Divine Wisdom in her purity, which she presents to attract it. The evidence of this is the sudden illuminations that occur during powerful revelations [GAP-2] its transformation [GAP-3]. And the captive goods shall be purified by her toward [GAP-4] the wisdom she shall unveil [GAP-5]. The ruling evil affections shall be separated out by these sudden illuminations, and they are dislodged and expelled.

[§766] However, [these] angels shall immediately be summoned to them, so that they will seize them, because they know that they shall never do good. Every place they will touch they shall make dead and a loss. Whenever the angels will make towards them and come beside [them], they shall flee in their constraint before them. They go into whatever they will meet upon and assume it, like a robber being abandoned and fleeing before one stronger than him, as he runs after him to arrest him.
[§766]* However, [GAP-6: REVIEW these] divine truths from the Lord shall immediately be dispatched toward them, so that they will seize them, because it is known that they shall never produce any good. Every faculty they contact they shall render spiritually dead and bring to ruin. Whenever the divine truths advance toward them and draw near [GAP-7], the evil affections shall flee under compulsion before them. They enter into whatever faculty they encounter and possess it — like a plunderer who is exposed and flees before one stronger than himself, as that stronger one pursues him to apprehend him.

[§767] Now, those rulers that shall be freed from these clouds and assume whatever they will meet upon [and go] into the [ ... / ... ] that they did not [seek] will seize this thing, whether indeed a tree to which they have found the way, or animals, or people. Simply, every place that they will assume they shall burn and destroy by the fire of their body. Namely, the rulers, [the] children of fire, they that shall be stripped of the fire.
[§767]* Now, those ruling evil affections that shall be dislodged from these obscured truths and possess whatever they encounter [GAP-8] into the [GAP-9] that they did not [GAP-10] will seize this faculty — whether indeed a perception to which they have found access, or natural affections, or the understanding and will of a person. Simply, every faculty they possess they shall consume and destroy by the fire of their self-love. Namely, the ruling evils, [GAP-11: REVIEW the] offspring of self-love, they that shall be stripped of the consuming love.

[§768] In contrast, [the] cloud that shall be raised up from water, and comes above towards the Virgin of Light; its sign is the thunder storms and the booms that shall come in the atmosphere in various forms and are heard. [The lives in it shall be purified] Again, there are times if the rulers that belong to them, which exist in the cloud, shall on occasion be constrained [in a] water [clou]d. And they flee from it and abandon it.
[§768]* In contrast, [GAP-12: REVIEW the] obscured truth that shall be raised up from natural knowledge and ascends toward Divine Wisdom in her purity — its evidence is the deep reverberations of divine power and the soundings that arise in the rational mind in manifold forms and are inwardly perceived. [GAP-13] Again, there are times when the evil affections belonging to these, which dwell within the obscured truth, shall on occasion be constrained [GAP-14: REVIEW in a] truth-bearing form of natural knowledge. And they flee from it and abandon it.

[§769] Now, [they] too are so, in that every place that they will [reach], they shall make frost and hail and snow. [They rui]n the seed-corn and the fruits and the plants; and they [shall destroy] every place [that] they will reach.
[§769]* Now, [GAP-15: REVIEW they] too are such, in that every faculty they will [GAP-16: REVIEW reach], they shall produce frozen falsity and hardened falsity and the coldness of spiritual death. They destroy the interior truths capable of producing good, and the works of charity, and the growing perceptions; and they [GAP-17] every faculty [GAP-18: REVIEW that] they will reach.

[§770] Again, [ang]els shall be summoned to them who seize them. Still, while the angels [ru]sh after them in order to seize them, they shall make this destruction.
[§770]* Again, divine truths shall be dispatched toward them to seize them. Still, while the divine truths pursue them in order to seize them, the evil affections shall work this destruction.

[§771] Also, [the cl]oud that will be raised up from wind ascends to the atmosphere between, towards the likeness of the Virgin that she shall display to it. Its sign is [the s]torm wind that shall blow with bitterness.
[§771]* Also, the obscured truth that will be raised up from spiritual thought ascends to the rational understanding, drawn toward the form of Divine Wisdom that she shall present to it. Its evidence is the forceful spiritual temptation that shall sweep through with severity.

[§772] So, in those winds and tempests shall the lives be purified. Again, the rulers will be stripped of the cloud of wind and they descend from it, safe in their image. All the rivers and the seas that they will reach and arrive at, they shall make waves in them and raise [them up] and they [overturn] the ships. They wreak destruction [in them] and they seize and bring them [to the deep].
[§772]* So, in those spiritual trials and upheavals shall the living essences of good be purified. Again, the ruling evil affections will be stripped from the truth-bearing form of thought, and they descend from it, intact in their own nature. All the flowing truths and the gathered bodies of natural knowledge that they reach and arrive at — they shall make turbulence in them and raise [GAP-19] and they [GAP-20] the doctrinal systems. They wreak destruction [GAP-21] and they seize and carry them [GAP-22].

[§773] Also the cloud, the one of light, immediately they will be stripped of it [and it shall come above towards the likeness of] the Virgin. She shall display its sign too [to it]. And its sign is this, in that you shall find it sailing in stillness and calm. The lives in it shall be cleansed in stillness and calm. Again, the rulers that will be stripped of it [in their image] that will come forth [from it] they shall put [ ... ] and they take [ ... / ... ] there [until] the angels seize them.
[§773]* Also the truth-form of wisdom itself — immediately the ruling evils will be stripped of it [GAP-23] Divine Wisdom. She shall display its evidence too [GAP-24]. And its evidence is this: that you shall find it proceeding in peace and heavenly rest. The living essences within it shall be purified in peace and heavenly rest. Again, the ruling evil affections that will be stripped of it [GAP-25] that will come forth [GAP-26] they shall impose [GAP-27] and they take [GAP-28] there [GAP-29] the divine truths seize them.

[§774] Again, the cloud that shall be raised up from the mingled air comes above towards [the likeness] of the Virgin. Its sign is this, in that [the lives] are purified in it in stillness and [rest. Again, these rulers] that will be stripped of that cloud and come from it; every place that they will find [and reach] they shall make it [like] one of [the dead] until [the hour when] they are seized and taken to their midst.
[§774]* Again, the obscured truth that shall be raised up from the mingled rational faculty ascends toward [GAP-30: REVIEW the form] of Divine Wisdom. Its evidence is this: that [GAP-31] are purified within it in peace and [GAP-32: rest … these ruling evils] that will be stripped from that truth-form and emerge from it; every faculty that they will find [GAP-33] they shall render it [GAP-34] one of [GAP-35] until [GAP-36] they are seized and taken to their confinement.

[§775] Their strippings, that they shall leave behind them in various places they will have reached, whether the produce of the trees or the offspring of the fleshes, they shall be taken midst [ ... ] wither away. They shall inflict great dangers, but again those rulers also the angels shall send to their midst. They bring the light from them and cast the demons down to these seven outer ditches of the great sea; which is the vessel of the waters that are outside the world.
[§775]* The residues of evil they leave behind in the various faculties they have reached — whether the corrupted works produced through perception, or the offspring generated through the merely corporeal will — these shall be gathered into [GAP-37] and wither away. They shall inflict grave spiritual dangers; but again, those ruling evils also shall the divine truths consign to their confinement. They extract the wisdom from them and cast the falsities born of evil down into the seven outermost depths of the vast body of natural knowledge — which is the containing form of the knowledges that lie outside the regenerate mind.

[§776] To what place do those seven ditches go down?
[§776]* To what state do those seven depths descend?

[§777] Those ditches and the [paths go down] until they reach the earths below [to the] feet of the Porter.
[§777]* Those depths and the [GAP-38] until they reach the lowest natural planes below [GAP-39] the foundation of the Guardian who keeps the boundary.

[§778] [ ... ] for sometimes these angels shall be sent to them, to seize the rulers. Whenever the rulers [ ... / ... ] for the angels rush after them. They beseech them [ ... ] they that are given the command. However, [t]hose [rul]ers shall be freed from them alone and assume [the tree] and [the fle]sh. They are consumed by this offspring [ ... / ... ] tree and that of flesh. The angels shall set a [watch] over them, that they might guard them until the hour when [those r]ulers will be stripped of tree and [flesh; a]nd they go up above. They shall be gathered [together] above, and they are poured out [again] in their first for[ms and im]ages in which they first were. They take the light from them, which had remained behind in them. And they too are cast down to these seven outer [path]s that are in the great sea; the place to where these former ones [were c/as]t. They shall reach their midst [ ... ] and the light that is in them is taken.
[§778]* [GAP-40] For at times these divine truths shall be sent toward them, to seize the ruling evils. Whenever the ruling evils [GAP-41] for the divine truths pursue them. They implore them [GAP-42] they that are given the authority. However, those ruling evils shall be released from them alone and possess [GAP-43] and the corporeal will. They are consumed by the offspring they generate [GAP-44] perception and that of corporeal will. The divine truths shall set a [GAP-45] over them, that they might guard them until the hour when those ruling evils will be stripped from perception and corporeal will, and they ascend above. They shall be gathered [GAP-46] above, and they are poured forth [GAP-47] into their original [GAP-48: forms … the] states in which they first existed. They extract the wisdom from them, which had remained within them. And they too are cast down into the seven outermost ways that are in the vast body of knowledge — the state to where the former ones were cast. They shall reach their confinement [GAP-49] and the wisdom that remains in them is taken.

[§779] And they are cast to the outer prison by the angels, when the command is given to them through the power of the Virgin of Light. She has authority over the entire zone, and cleanses the life that is in it.
[§779]* And they are cast into the outermost prison by the divine truths, when the authority is given to them through the power of Divine Wisdom in her purity. She has dominion over the entire intermediate realm between the celestial and the natural, and purifies every living good that is within it.

[§780] Three earths exist in the universe, and are being toiled over. At first they [trouble] the farmer in them. Afterwards they bear [good] fruit to they who toil over them.
[§780]* Three degrees of the mind exist within the whole created order of the human being, and all three are undergoing regeneration. At first they [GAP-1] resist the Divine intelligence that labors within them, as unbroken ground resists the one who would sow. Afterwards they bring forth [GAP-2: REVIEW — genuine works of charity] to the Divine powers that labor upon them.

[§781] One of them is this great earth that people are living upon. They are ploughing in it [and digging], it with iron hoes, they cultivate, [making] the furrows, turning it over [from its place] Afterwards they harvest fruit from [it in its season]
[§781]* One of these is the outermost natural mind, upon which all merely sensory and worldly thought resides. The Divine works to break open this hardened natural plane [GAP-3], penetrating it with truths on the lowest natural level — for iron implements correspond to the hard discipline of natural truth applied by effort — and orders it into receptive arrangements [GAP-4: REVIEW — forming orderly states capable of receiving seeds of interior truth], overturning its former self-derived orientation [GAP-5]. Afterwards good works are gathered from [GAP-6: this reformed natural mind], which now yields what it could not yield before.

[§782] The second field is the living soul, the holy one. It is mixed in the entire zone, above and below. The farmers who toil over it are these two great light-givers, which journey through the heavens. They are toiling and cultivating it, so that they will draw it towards their aspect. And it leaps up and comes to the heights. It goes upwards, out from every place wherein it is, and it adds to them.
[§782]* The second degree is the interior spiritual mind, the living and sacred plane of consciousness. It is dispersed and intermingled throughout every region of existence, in higher states and in lower. The intelligences that labor upon it are the two great principles of Divine love and Divine wisdom, which proceed through all the interior heavens of the mind. They labor and cultivate this spiritual plane so that they may draw whatever is receptive of good and truth toward their own likeness. And the spiritual mind, so vivified, rises up and ascends to elevated states of perception. It goes upward, liberated from every condition of mixture and captivity in which it had been held, and in ascending it augments the heavenly kingdom — adding itself to what is Divine.

[§783] All these animals that you see, and also the rest of the [crea]tures [upon the face] of the earth, confirm them in three likeness[es of birth]: some from [the] abortions. Others, however, form their own selves in their shape [and nature].

[§784] Also, their nourishment is thre[efold: the] light in them, if they shall wish [to eat it]. [Some of them] they eat see[ds and] fr[uit and grass]; they eat seeds and fruit. The [nourishment fills] it entirely, and they [bear within them] the three archetyp[es] from their nature and the [whole of] the time when they are on the ea[rth in this world]; they are seized [and bound] in their [bodies]. The Ambassador [purifies] all powers [of the darkness].

[§785] [Now] it [formed them], first [of all]. Plant them generally to this name: shoots and grass. It formed the bad, bitter trees, the reeds and the thorns and the [thistles] and these others that are like them.

[§786] For that thought belongs to the flesh-eating rulers. Still, these [that were] planted (belong to) grass and gourds, since they are in their essence. Nevertheless, [this t]ho[ugh]t that came down from the rulers, as they are in fruit and grass first and shall not be planted in flesh and blood, it formed the trees that shall give nourishing fru[it], as their gourds are sweet.

[§787] Again, this thou[ght] that came down from the powers that eat m[ea]t and also eat grass and gourds, it has formed the trees that shall not produce any nourishment. Their fruits belong neither to good nor evil.

[§783–§787]* # Spiritual Translation

## ¶2

All these affections that are perceived in the mind, and also the remaining living states [GAP-1] of the external natural mind — these are established according to three modes of spiritual formation [GAP-2]: some arise from [GAP-3] abortive and incomplete spiritual births. Others, however, take on their own form according to their inherent spiritual quality [GAP-4].

## ¶3

Also, their spiritual sustenance is thre[GAP-5: efold — the] wisdom dwelling within them, if they shall desire to receive it [GAP-6]. [GAP-7] They take in interior truths and the good works produced from them [GAP-8]; they receive interior truths and goods of use. The [GAP-9] it entirely, and they [GAP-10] the three fundamental spiritual patterns inherent in their nature and the [GAP-11] the period during which they dwell in the external natural mind [GAP-12]; they are held captive [GAP-13] in their [GAP-14]. The Divine Emissary [GAP-15] all governing loves [GAP-16].

## ¶4

[GAP-17: REVIEW N] it [GAP-18], first [GAP-19]. Establish them generally under this designation: nascent perceptions and elementary natural knowledges. It formed the evil, falsified perceptions — the hollow semblances of truth, the falsities that wound and pierce — and the [GAP-20] and all others that resemble them.

## ¶5

For that intention belongs to the ruling loves of self that devour living affections. Still, these [GAP-21: REVIEW that were] established pertain to elementary natural knowledges and simple natural goods, since they exist within their own essential nature. Nevertheless, that intention which descended from the ruling loves — insofar as it manifests first in works of use and natural knowledges and has not yet been implanted into what is corporeal and of the proprium — it formed the perceptions that yield nourishing goods of use, just as their natural goods are genuinely sweet and life-giving.

## ¶6

Again, that intention which descended from the governing loves that devour living affections and also consume natural knowledges and simple natural goods — it has formed the perceptions that yield no spiritual nourishment whatsoever. Their works belong neither to genuine good nor to manifest evil.

---

**Translator's note:** The threefold structure throughout concerns three degrees of spiritual formation determined by the quality of the ruling love from which they descend: (1) loves oriented toward devouring affections (pure self-love) produce perceptions bearing only falsity and harm; (2) loves that also feed on natural knowledges and simple goods produce perceptions bearing neither good nor evil — spiritually barren states; (3) loves that operate within natural knowledges *before* descending into the corporeal proprium produce perceptions that yield genuinely nourishing goods. The "abortions" of ¶2 are spiritual states that never reach full formation because the ruling love is too disordered to carry them to completion.

[§788] What is the virgin that is named 'virginal'? Or the [one that] is called 'continent', what is it? Or by what category is the [vi]rgin called 'virginal'? Or [the co]ntinent, which is given this name, by what category is it named '[c]ontinent'?
[§788]* What is the state of wisdom that is called "pristine"? Or the [GAP-1: state of wisdom that] is called "restored" — what is it? By what principle is pristine wisdom recognized as such? And restored wisdom, which is given this name — by what principle is it known as "restored"?

[§789] What is called 'virginal' is assigned to the mystery of the aeons of greatness. And they called it 'virginal' by the category of all the light that did not [taste] of death, nor come to the enemy's war.
[§789]* What is called "pristine" belongs to the mystery of the infinite degrees of Divine order. It is called "pristine" by virtue of all the wisdom that never [GAP-2: underwent] spiritual death, nor descended into the conflict that falsity wages against good.

[§790] However, what is called 'continent' occurs to the mystery of the light that came and was mixed with the darkness. It was defiled in bodies [of the creatures] and went up to the light. [They] called [it 'co]ntinent'.
[§790]* However, what is called "restored" pertains to the mystery of the wisdom that descended and became intermingled with falsity. It was defiled in corporeal and sensory states [GAP-3: through its captivity in external nature] and yet ascended again toward its celestial origin. [GAP-4: Therefore they] called it "restored."

[§791] Furthermore, the holy vir[gin]als [are of such a nature that, even] should they come out against the en[emy and come to the war, they would not] taste death, nor [be defiled].
[§791]* Furthermore, the holy pristine wisdoms [GAP-5: are of such a nature that even if] they should go forth against the [GAP-6: assault of falsity, they would] not undergo spiritual death, nor [GAP-7: suffer contamination from evil].

[§792] On the other hand, what is called 'continent' [is this li]ght that mixed with the darkness, was purif[ie]d, came [up f]rom all the creatures, and was confirmed in the great[ness]. They called it 'continent' since it tasted [of the l]ust; but afterwards was cleansed from it and [become an] holy one.
[§792]* On the other hand, what is called "restored" is that wisdom which became intermingled with falsity, was purified, ascended out of all created and sensory states, and was confirmed in the Divine order. It is called "restored" because it tasted of self-love and concupiscence; but afterward it was cleansed from these and [GAP-8: became a] holy state of good.

[§793] Furthermore, what is called ['vi]rginal' is this light that was purified by the ima[ge] of the [A]mbassador. It wen[t up and] attained [the] he[ights. It was] confirmed in the image of the gods.
[§793]* Furthermore, what is called "pristine" is that wisdom which was purified by the manifestation of the Divine Truth sent forth into the world. It ascended and attained [GAP-9: the] interior celestial states. It was confirmed in the forms of Divine good and truth.

[§794] Again, what is [called] 'continent' is the remainder of this light th[at] remained behind, from what was cleansed. It goes up and comes down in transmigration. Al[so], there is a part [of] it that shall come and attain the form of [hum]an [fl]esh. Again, that one too shall be chosen in the flesh, [accept] the hope, and become an holy one. And they name it 'continent'.
[§794]* Again, what is [GAP-10: called] "restored" is the remaining portion of that wisdom which was left behind after what had been purified ascended. It rises and descends through successive states of reformation. Also, there is a portion [GAP-11: of] it that enters into and assumes the condition of the natural and corporeal person. Again, even that portion shall be chosen while still in the natural state, [GAP-12: receive] the interior hope of salvation, and become a holy state of good. And they name it "restored."

[§795] Furthermore, what is named 'virginal' is Wahman, should he come and assume the images [of] the ele[ct]. However, on the other hand, what is called 'contin[ent]' is the new man, should it be purified from this old man. And it is cleansed and strips off the Sin that is compounded with it, and it becomes a continent one.
[§795]* Furthermore, what is named "pristine" is the Divine influx of interior understanding when it descends and takes on the interior forms [GAP-13: of] those who are in genuine good. However, on the other hand, what is called "restored" is the regenerate will, when it has been purified from the old proprium — the inherited and accumulated selfhood. It is cleansed and strips off the evil that was compounded with it, and it becomes a restored state of holiness.

[§796] And also, what is called 'virgi[n]al' in the flesh, is a man if he has [ne]ver joined himself to woman, has not been defiled by intercourse. However, [i]n contrast, what is called 'continent', is the man who has a wom[a]n in the world. Afterwards, he cleanses himself from her [and] renounces her. And because of this he [is purified] and he becomes an holy contin[en]t one.
[§796]* And also, what is called "pristine" in the natural person is the state of one who has never conjoined his understanding with the love of self and the world, and has not been defiled by that conjunction. However, in contrast, what is called "restored" is the state of one who was conjoined with worldly affection and natural desire. Afterward, he purifies himself from that affection [GAP-14: and] renounces it entirely. And because of this he [GAP-15: is separated from falsity] and becomes a holy and restored one.

[§797] there is a dragon with fourteen heads, and it is gathered in and [hidden in its] den with five refuges.
[§797]* There exists a ruling falsity of the selfhood possessing fourteen powers of dominion, and it is gathered inward and [GAP-1] concealed within a hidden interior state furnished with five containing forms in the natural mind where it takes refuge.

[§798] Again, this is how this earth [is likened] to it, as it is accounted a corporeal dragon [of desire] the dragon is this doctrine [and teaching] of the flesh [and its thought] also the fourteen heads that are distributed over the dragon are these.
[§798]* Again, this is the manner in which the natural mind [GAP-2] pertains to this falsity, inasmuch as it is recognized as the persuasion of the proprium — a corporeal falsity ruling from self-love [GAP-3]. This ruling falsity is the very doctrine [GAP-4] of self-derived life, the intelligence of the selfhood [GAP-5]. And the fourteen powers of dominion that are distributed throughout this ruling falsity are these:

[§799] The seven sense organs on the head of the body are the two of sight by which one sees; the two of sound by which one hears; the two of scent by which one smells; also the tongue [of taste] by which one selects, receiving there the different tastes that occur in every form.
[§799]* The seven natural faculties of perception belonging to the external mind are: the two of intellectual apprehension, by which one discerns; the two of obedience and reception, by which one perceives what is communicated; the two of interior discernment, by which one detects what is spiritually agreeable or repugnant; also the faculty of discrimination [GAP-6], by which one selects, receiving there the various qualities of delight and aversion that arise in every form of experience.

[§800] These are the seven heads of the dragon, the seven sense organs that are on the upper part of the person; [and seven are] below. The seven of the torso are these: two [hands]; the two [feet] ruler [they correspond to] the seven heads that are above; so with the ones below they total and make fourteen heads of the dragon.
[§800]* These are the seven powers of the ruling falsity — the seven faculties of natural perception that belong to the interior rational degree of the person; [GAP-7] and corresponding to these are seven belonging to the exterior sensual degree below. The seven of the exterior natural mind are these: two [GAP-8]; the two [GAP-9] governing faculty [GAP-10] — corresponding to the seven powers of dominion that operate interiorly above; so together with the ones that operate in the sensual degree below, they complete and constitute fourteen powers of dominion belonging to the ruling falsity of the selfhood.

[§801] Conversely, the five dens about which they have spoken, where the dragon is congregated and protrudes, are these: the first is the tongue; the second is the lungs; the third is the heart; the fourth is the spleen; the fifth is the blood that dwells in them. Again, if it shall look out from them [and reveal its likeness and its observation above and below, it wages war by] its fourteen heads.
[§801]* Conversely, the five hidden recesses of which it has been taught, where the ruling falsity is concentrated and from which it extends itself, are these: the first is the faculty of outward expression and communication; the second is the faculty of spiritual thought and interior respiration; the third is the will, the seat of love; the fourth is the faculty that mediates the purification or corruption of affections; the fifth is the living truth — or its profanation — that flows through all of them. Again, if this falsity should project itself outward from these interior recesses, [GAP-11: REVIEW — and manifest its character and exercise its perception both into the interior rational and exterior sensual degrees, it wages spiritual combat through] its fourteen powers of dominion.

[§802] the dragon, which is the thought of the body
[§802]* — this ruling falsity, which is the self-derived thinking of the proprium.

[§803] the dragon that exists with its fourteen heads and five dens, wherein it is concealed and hidden. It does through them [all its evil deeds, fulfilling its will and its des]ire all the time.
[§803]* — the ruling falsity that exists with its fourteen powers of dominion and five concealed interior states, within which it is hidden and harbored. It accomplishes through these recesses [GAP-12: its contin­ual] craving and self-will at every moment.

---

*Note on the overall structure:* The teaching describes the complete anatomy of self-derived intelligence — the proprium's own "doctrine of the flesh." The fourteen heads are not mere natural organs but the full set of cognitive and sensory faculties through which the selfhood exercises dominion over the whole person, interior and exterior alike. The five dens represent the deepest containing forms of the natural mind — expression, thought, will, affective mediation, and the vital medium that connects them all — within which evil love conceals itself and from which it wages its war against regeneration.

[§804] When the person is above water and looking down [into the wat]ers, if he shall see his face reflected why shall you find his head turned upside down and also his feet upwards? Yet, when he stares down into the waters, his face shall not be visible, nor can he see it there?
[§804]* When the understanding stands above natural truth and directs its perception downward into that truth, if it beholds its own interior quality reflected there, why does it find that what is highest in wisdom has become lowest, and what is lowest and most external has been raised to the uppermost place? And yet, when the understanding gazes fixedly into natural truth, its own essential character is not made manifest, nor can it perceive itself there directly.

[§805] [And] this: The face of people and the shape of trees shall appear turned upside down in the water as this is signified in [the] mystery of the summons, when it was sent down to the worlds of Darkness towards the First Man. Sin[ce the summons came down] in this way it cast itself down [to the depths] like a person [who] plunges headlong into water; so it is also with the summons. It cast itself headlong to the worlds below, with strength and diligence.
[§805]* [GAP-1] This also: The interior quality of human understanding and the forms of perception appear inverted in natural truth, and this is what is signified in [GAP-2] the hidden law of the divine call — when divine truth was sent down into the realms of falsity toward the primordial celestial human. Sin[GAP-3: ce divine truth descended,] in this manner it cast itself downward [GAP-4: into the lowest states of the natural mind] like a person [GAP-5] who plunges headlong into natural truth; so it is also with the divine call. It cast itself with its inmost things foremost into the realms below, with power and urgency.

[§806] And this too: Shall the person lift his head up from the water, his face shall not be visible to him, being bent below. This characterises the mystery of the obedience that ascended from below, from with the First Man his father to the Living Spirit.
[§806]* And this too: Should the understanding lift itself up out of natural truth, its interior quality is not visible to it in what lies below, being turned away from the natural plane. This expresses the hidden law of the will's responsive ascent, which rose from the lowest states — from the primordial celestial human, its origin — upward toward the Divine Life itself.

[§807] The face of people and of animals and of trees is visible in the water, hanging upside down. This occurs to the mystery of the stars and the zodiac, which hang upside down and are visible in the great sea. For the face of people and beasts and all trees hang on the root of the stars and the zodiac, being begotten from them. Just as their fathers, who are spread out above, hang upside down; so it is also that the mystery of their fathers is being revealed to them whenever they stare down into the water. This is, that you shall find their shapes inverted.
[§807]* The interior qualities of understanding, of affection, and of perception are all visible in natural truth, suspended in inversion. This corresponds to the hidden law of interior spiritual knowledges and the celestial orders of influx, which hang inverted and are made visible in the vast aggregate of natural truths. For the interior qualities of understanding, of affection, and of all perceptions depend upon the origin-point of those spiritual knowledges and celestial orders, being begotten from them. Just as their spiritual origins, which are spread out in the higher realm, are themselves inverted in their descent — so it is that the hidden reality of those origins is revealed to their derivatives whenever the understanding directs itself downward into natural truth. This is the reason their forms are found in an inverted state.

[§808] Not only this, but [the] child who is born; as the ho[ur] approaches for him to be born, he hangs upside down and is born head first. Indeed, due to this, his mystery shall be revealed in these waters people stare down into.
[§808]* Not only this, but [GAP-6] every new spiritual state that comes into being — as the appointed moment approaches for it to enter the natural plane — it hangs inverted and is born with what is highest entering first into the lowest. Indeed, because of this law, its hidden spiritual nature is revealed in those natural truths into which the understanding gazes.

[§809] Behold, you will find the shadow of the person who walks along is joined to the earth all the time. If the person is troubled [and seeks to lift] his shadow up upon the earth [it is bound from] above. It is impossible for him to do [this, to part from his shadow while he remains] on the earth. See, this mystery is [a] great sign, signifying that the entire body came from the earth and had ascended from the abyss. Again, due to this, its shadow is joined on the earth, turned downwards, and hanging from above.
[§809]* Behold, you will find that the natural correspondence of a person who progresses through life is joined to the corporeal plane at all times. If the person is disturbed [GAP-7: and seeks to separate] his natural correspondence upward from the corporeal plane [GAP-8: he finds it bound] above. It is impossible for him to [GAP-9: sever that bond while he remains] in the corporeal plane. See, this hidden reality is [GAP-10] a profound sign, signifying that the entire external form originated from the corporeal plane and had been elevated from the deepest natural states. Again, because of this, its natural correspondence remains joined to the corporeal plane, tending always downward, yet suspended from the spiritual above.

[§810] This sustenance of various kinds that people gather in and eat, and enters the body; it shall be distributed to five productions.
[§810]* The manifold goods and truths that the human spirit receives inwardly and appropriates — taking them into the whole of the mind — these are distributed into five distinct spiritual productions.

[§811] The first product is this that shall issue from the person in rapture and rises up in the Mind, and it comes out from all his limbs, and is immeasurable.
[§811]* The first production is that which proceeds from the spirit as inmost blessedness and ascends into the interior understanding as wisdom; it flows forth from every faculty and capacity of the whole spiritual being, and is without measure or limit.

[§812] The second is this that shall issue from the person in voice and word.
[§812]* The second is that which proceeds from the spirit as the affection of truth expressed outwardly — as doctrine spoken and truth confessed.

[§813] The third is this that shall spring from them in strength and activity.
[§813]* The third is that which springs forth as the power of good in act — charity exercised through living use and service.

[§814] The fourth is what is engendered by the pleasure of lu[s]t in men and women.
[§814]* The fourth is that which is generated by the delight of self-love and concupiscence when the will and the understanding turn what they have received toward the proprium — toward selfish and merely natural ends.

[§815] The fifth is this that shall be formed and sculpted in the flesh, and be engendered and come from them. It is this corporeal creature; only its parents can recognise this creature that they beget. They are attentive to its desire and its thought and its [lo]ve every day, all the time. However, these other four forms they neither notice nor are concerned about; because they are not displayed.
[§815]* The fifth is that which is formed and fashioned in the outermost natural degree alone, and brought forth into the corporeal and sensual life. This is the merely external person as it appears in the world. Only the will and the understanding — the inward faculties that generated it — can recognise this outermost form they have produced. And they attend solely to its cravings, its natural reasoning, and its self-love, continually, at every moment. Yet the four interior productions — inmost blessedness, expressed truth, active good, and even the misdirected desires of the proprium — they neither perceive nor regard at all, because these are not made visible to the outward senses.

[§816] There is not [o]ne person [existing in] the world, standing firm [i]n mixture, for whom joy remains to the end.
[§816]* There is not a single mind [GAP-1: dwelling in] the condition of spiritual life, established amid the commingling of good and evil, for whom blessedness endures to the end.

[§817] Not only people, even the sun and the moon, the light-givers of the heavens, [do not suffer] nor are they oppressed, they know not [sorrow, being s]et firm in a summons and an [obe]dience. Nevertheless, they too, they do not rejoice completely.
[§817]* Not only individual minds — even divine love and divine faith, the illuminating powers of the interior heavens, [GAP-2: ...] nor are they oppressed; they know not [GAP-3: ...] established in divine vocation and obedience to heavenly order. Nevertheless, even they do not possess complete joy.

[§818] So, one: Their eyes stare at their enemy, they [perceiv]e him and also look at his ugly shapes. They [s]ee him, that he is alive and established and is king above and below, in secret and manifest. He does every thing that he wants according to his wil[l].
[§818]* The first cause: Their understanding gazes upon evil itself; they perceive it and behold its deformed appearances. They see that it is active and entrenched and exercises dominion over both interior and exterior states of the mind, in what is hidden and what is manifest. It accomplishes everything that proceeds from its own self-love.

[§819] [The s]econd: They perceive the living soul, they see [i]t and that it is ensnared and set in a great [fetter] above and below, in the tree and in the flesh, [burdened] with every oppression. It is being pressed, drawn near to [and] sliced and eaten as it comes up and down; from above below and from below above. It [is] despoiled and moved from body to body.
[§819]* The second cause: They perceive the divine good dispersed within all things; they see it captive and held in a great [GAP-4: bondage] throughout both interior and exterior states, within perceptions and within the corporeal nature, [GAP-5: ...] with every form of violation. It is profaned, drawn toward [GAP-6: and] torn asunder and consumed by evil as it passes between interior and exterior states — from the higher into the lower and from the lower toward the higher. It [GAP-7: is] stripped of its life and transferred from one external containment to another.

[§820] Now, because of their living soul that is loved by them and honoured of them, when they see it in oppression th[ey can] not [re]joice completely.
[§820]* Now, because of the divine good within all things, which is beloved to them and held sacred by them — when they behold it in bondage to evil, they cannot attain complete blessedness.

[§821] So, one about the soul; and one about their enemy whom they see ruling [over] what is not his own. He is proud and satisfied with a wealth that is not his own. Indeed, due to this, they do not rejoice in total; but bear up and swallow their heart until they cleanse the living soul and take it from the enemy's hands. Then they obliterate the death that is their enemy and they gather it in [and fetter it in the lump] for ever and ever.
[§821]* Thus one grief concerns the captive divine good; and the other concerns evil itself, which they see exercising dominion [GAP-8: over] what does not belong to it. Self-love is exalted and glutted upon goods and truths it has usurped. Indeed, for this reason they do not rejoice in fullness, but endure in patient love and contain their inmost grief until they purify the divine good and liberate it from the power of evil. Then they annihilate spiritual death, which is evil itself, and they gather it together [GAP-9: and confine it within a single fixed mass] for all eternity.

[§822] When they will chain their enemy in the bond at the [las]t, then they will rejoice and no longer grieve from this t[ime]. Also, they become the very same as they were from the [beginning] for ever!
[§822]* When at the final state they confine evil permanently within its own bondage, then they will enter into complete blessedness and grieve no more from that time onward. And they are restored to the very same divine order in which they existed from the [GAP-10: beginning] unto eternity.

[§823] The production of the word t[hat comes] from the mouth and is heard by the [ears]. [The mouth shapes it] and the throat draws it up and the tongue spreads it out and the teeth cut it and the lips g[at]her it! The word shall come forth through the power of th[ese five] members and be heard outside.
[§823]* The formation of truth as it proceeds from the interior will and becomes perceptible to the [GAP-1] receptive understanding of others. [GAP-2] The deeper volition draws it upward from within, and the understanding unfolds it into intelligible form, and the natural rational faculty distinguishes and defines it into discrete ideas, and the outermost sensory comprehension gathers it into completeness. Thus truth comes forth through the operation of these five successive degrees of the mind and is perceived in the external life.

[§824] Simila[rly] the coin: One shall pour it out and an [othe]r beat it and another trim it as it is turned, and a[nother] put the stamp on it and another wipe it in the sieve (?). [Behold], these five craftsmen shall shape and beautify [their] coin, and it comes amongst mankind. It becomes a posse[ssion] to be received and given.
[§824]* In like manner, a truth of genuine spiritual worth: One agency pours forth the initial influx from within, and another shapes it through the labor of reformation, and another refines and orders it as it is turned through successive states, and another impresses upon it the authentic image of the Divine, and another purifies it by separating what is genuine from what is counterfeit. [GAP-3] Behold, these five successive spiritual operations shape and perfect [GAP-4] their truth of communicable worth, and it enters into the life of the spiritual community. It becomes a living good capable of being received into the understanding and given forth through charity.

[§825] This is also the case with the [wor]d, as it is formed and embellished by five [member]s. It comes forth and is heard by the ears [of] others.
[§825]* This is also the nature of truth proceeding from the Divine: it is formed and perfected through five successive degrees of the interior life. It comes forth and is perceived by the [GAP-5] receptive faculties of others.

---

*Note: The fivefold structure in both images — members of speech and craftsmen of the coin — corresponds to the same reality: truth descends through five discrete degrees from its origin in the will toward its outermost communicable form. The "coin" — a unit of value that circulates — is truth rendered into a form that can serve the mutual exchange of spiritual life, which is charity itself.*

[§826] This seed grain and this barley grain shall be formed and [nourished] and beautified by the five elements. Now, the warmth and the cold nourish the seed grain and the entire tree. So, just as these things nourish it [so also] it is destroyed [and consumed] through [great] hunger.
[§826]* This interior truth and this natural good of truth shall be formed and [GAP-1] and perfected by the five essential faculties of the spiritual life. Now, love and the absence of love sustain the interior truth and the whole perception from which it grows. So, just as these spiritual nourishments sustain it [GAP-2] it perishes [GAP-3] through [GAP-4] the deprivation that comes from the absence of all good.

[§827] In this way also righteousness gathers the five to it.
[§827]* In this way also the Divine Good operating through truth gathers the five essential faculties of the spirit unto itself and unites them.

[§828] In this way too, a lack and a shortfall shall occur from place to place through them; and they who have chosen it have gathered it in and beautified and attained it, while an affliction shall arise for it through those.
[§828]* In this way too, a spiritual deficiency and a privation of good shall arise from state to state through these faculties when they are separated; and those who have received the Divine Good by free choice have gathered it inward and perfected it and made it their own, while spiritual temptation and suffering shall arise for it through those who have refused.

[§829] People also shall be caused to stumble on their account, through the energy of Matter that dwells in them. It has poured its shadow over them, just as it shall also pour its shadow over the elements and destroy the fruits of all the trees.
[§829]* Minds also shall be led into falsity on account of these things, through the active power of the corporeal and self-loving nature that dwells within them. It has cast its obscurity over them — that is, the darkness of self-love has veiled their understanding — just as it shall also cast its obscurity over the essential goods and truths themselves and corrupt the works that proceed from all perceptions of truth.

---

*Translator's note:* The "five elements" in this oldest cosmological layer correspond to the five constituent faculties or powers of the regenerating mind — roughly: understanding, thought, reflection, will, and reasoning — which together compose the spiritual life. Their gathering by "righteousness" (¶3) describes the unification of these faculties under the Divine Good, while their dispersal (¶4–5) describes the fragmentation that occurs when the corporeal nature — here called "Matter" — imposes its shadow, which is the obscurity of self-love and falsity from evil, over the whole perceptive life.

[§830] [In eac]h one of these five garments that the [First] Man summoned there are five powers. They shall be counted to the [number of each] other, these five, and five times [five is] their number. However, at the time [when] the summons went down. The answer wished to ascend with it. And the summons with the obedience [constitute] twenty-five characteristics in their twenty-five limbs, [which] are the sons of the Man.
