The summons and [the obed]ience were raised to the heights through them.
[§830]* Within each of these five degrees of external truth that the [GAP-1] primordial celestial will called forth from within itself, there reside five interior faculties. These are to be reckoned together [GAP-2] one with another, these five, and five times [GAP-3] their full number. Yet at the time [GAP-4] the calling-forth of divine truth descended into lower states, the reception of that truth sought to ascend together with it. And the calling-forth of divine truth together with the will's receptive response [GAP-5] constitute twenty-five spiritual faculties in their twenty-five capacities of life, [GAP-6] which are the derivative states proceeding from the celestial will. The divine call and its willing reception were elevated into interior states through these faculties.

[§831] Just as their fa[ther] the living Man raised himself up through them, by these these other ones have set up all things a[bov]e and below. They fasted from their [foo]d, [w]hich is their own.
[§831]* Just as their origin, the living celestial will, raised itself into interior states through them, so by these same faculties every other spiritual reality has been ordered in both interior and exterior degrees. They emptied themselves of their own proper spiritual nourishment — that which belongs to them by nature.

[§832] So, from that time until [the time] of [the adv]ent of the Splendour, the glorious one who came [from the land of light and gave to them]heir food which is the [life] of sou[l], which is the summons and the obedience [that is] that spiritual food. They rele[ased them throughout] all these fifty days, which [means] this living food suffices for all of them. It filled them all with power and richness and life. Light [is] this living food that [in them] became rich.
[§832]* And from that state until [GAP-7] the state of the advent of Divine Wisdom made manifest, the glorious influx that came [GAP-8] bearing their spiritual nourishment, which is the [GAP-9] life of the interior spirit — namely, the divine call and its willing reception — [GAP-10] that sustaining truth and good. They released [GAP-11] throughout all these fifty complete states of spiritual process, which [GAP-12] this living truth-and-good suffices to sustain them all. It filled them all with spiritual capacity, with abundance of truth, and with the very life of heaven. Divine wisdom [GAP-13] this living nourishment from the Divine that [GAP-14] was made abundant.

[§833] [The light] exists [and comes] daily to the body of the person [and] there are two hundred and fifty thousand seals in it [And with them] there are another two hundred and fifty thousand rulers [and] they are cleansed and purified and enter [into the rest] by the voice, by the word, and by the [thought that grows] quiet with the silence at the time when his heart [is opened to the love] of God the Father.
[§833]* [GAP-1] There exists [GAP-2] continually within the composite of the natural mind of a person [GAP-3] there is an immense fullness of confirmed interior states sealed in order within it [GAP-4] there is a corresponding fullness of governing principles [GAP-5] these are purified from falsity and cleansed from evil, and they enter [GAP-6] by means of interior perception, by means of Divine truth revealed, and by [GAP-7] the stilling of self-derived thought in the peace that comes when the will [GAP-8] is opened to the influx of the Divine Love itself.

[§834] Also, the twelve [signs of the zodiac and their] rulers that belong to the Darkness shall be swept out outside in wandering and in transmigration [and in perdition, and] some of them [shall pass through] this transmigration, and others among them shall be brought back.
[§834]* Likewise, the twelve [GAP-9] governing falsities that belong to the domain of spiritual darkness shall be cast out from the interiors into the exteriors — into states of vastation and passage through successive judgments [GAP-10] certain among them [GAP-11: through] this process of vastation, and certain others among them shall be reclaimed and restored to spiritual order.

---

**Translator's note:** The "two hundred and fifty thousand" appearing twice suggests a mirrored fullness — one set of confirmed states belonging to order, one set of governing principles belonging to disorder — the dual structure of regeneration, in which what is of heaven is sealed inward and what is of hell is progressively subordinated. The "twelve rulers of Darkness" correspond to the complete structure of falsity from evil that must be removed in fullness before the interiors can be opened. The "voice, word, and silence" sequence traces the path of Divine truth from external hearing to interior reception to the cessation of the proprium — the deepest state, in which the will rests in the Divine without self-activity.

[§835] There are four aspects among mankind that perceive, as they perceive [all things] without these four that are hidden in them [no one can perceive, for] they belong [to the Living Soul]. So, [these four give perception to all mankind].

[§836] [The heat] that is in the [body] belongs to the fire. The darkness belongs to the abyss; but the light that perceives what is hidden in all these things, being clear, is the living air.

[§837] How shall the pupil of the eye perceive? While it perceives by day, yet come the night it shall not perceive. [For it is blind in the darkness] The pupil of the eye shall not [perceive] the mystery of the [eye] is the door [to what is] below [and to] the other four cardinal points. For in the [height] above. He has [established a] great [power and] they came down [to the] living [spirit]. The First Man came forth from it and knew all things.

[§835–§837]* # Spiritual Translation

## ¶1

There are four faculties within the human mind by which it receives spiritual perception, even as these faculties apprehend [GAP-1] — yet without these four interior capacities concealed within the soul [GAP-2] they pertain [GAP-3] to the whole of the mind's life. Thus, [GAP-4].

*Note: The "four aspects that perceive" correspond to the fourfold constitution of spiritual reception — the will, the understanding, the interior thought, and the conscience — which together form the complete organ by which the mind apprehends what flows in from the Divine. That they are "hidden" signifies they belong to the interior person, not the external natural mind.*

---

## ¶2

[GAP-5] that abides in the [GAP-6] belongs to the love of self. Falsity from evil belongs to the depths of hell; but the wisdom that discerns what is concealed within all these interior states, being pure and unclouded, is the living sphere of spiritual understanding that proceeds from the Divine.

*Note: Fire here signifies self-love, the ruling love of the proprium when turned away from the Divine. The abyss is the bottomless depth of evil from which falsities arise. The "light that perceives what is hidden" is Divine wisdom — the truth that penetrates into all states and lays bare their quality. The "living air" corresponds to the spiritual atmosphere of genuine understanding: the breath of life from the Lord flowing through the rational mind, by which all things are perceived in their true order.*

---

## ¶3

How shall the inmost faculty of understanding perceive? While it perceives in states of spiritual illumination, yet when the state of obscurity comes upon it, it cannot perceive. [GAP-7] The inmost understanding cannot [GAP-8] the interior truth of the [GAP-9] which is the means of entry [GAP-10] into the more external states [GAP-11] and into the other four fundamental orientations of the spiritual mind. For in the [GAP-12] which is celestial and interior, He possesses [GAP-13] the immensity [GAP-14] of the Divine order, and from those heights they descended by influx [GAP-15] as living [GAP-16] perceptions of truth. The Celestial Human — the most ancient perception of the Divine — proceeded from that interior origin and possessed complete knowledge of all spiritual realities.

*Note: The "pupil of the eye" is the deepest receptive center of intellectual sight — the point at which Divine light enters the understanding. "Day" and "night" are states of the church: illumination by faith grounded in charity, and the obscurity that follows when charity grows cold. The "door" is the aperture between the natural and spiritual degrees of the mind. The "four cardinal points" signify the total compass of spiritual orientation — celestial love, spiritual wisdom, natural truth, and sensory good — through which all influx is ordered. "The First Man" is not a historical figure but the primordial state of celestial perception: the most ancient church in which the human mind, still open to all interior degrees, received wisdom directly and knew all things by correspondence with the Divine.*

[§838] this bit of life and light that comes from [these three] vessels that surround the universe [and the] life comes up [to the light]
[§838]* This portion of good and wisdom that proceeds from [GAP-1: REVIEW these three] receptive forms that encompass the whole of spiritual creation [GAP-2] — the good of life ascends [GAP-3] toward its Divine origin.

[§839] [ ... ] b[u]t should the [life] ascend [through the] light of the rays that [come down] which are received, which shine and go in through these great open gates, through these doors, firmament by firmament.
[§839]* [GAP-4] but should the [GAP-5] rise upward [GAP-6] — the wisdom of truths proceeding from the Divine that [GAP-7] — which are received into the interiors, which illuminate and pass inward through the great openings of reception, through every entrance of admission, degree by degree through the successive planes of the mind.

[§840] For four [gates o]pen in each world, distributed at the four points of the compass, opening downwards to the vessels. So, due to this, the [light of the] rays shall go in through these openings and it shines on [that] place, and pulls through them the light and the little pi[llar (?) that ex]ists there.
[§840]* For four modes of reception open in each degree of spiritual life, ordered according to the four fundamental orientations of the will and understanding, opening downward toward the containing forms below. Therefore the [GAP-8: REVIEW wisdom of] truths proceeding from the Divine shall enter through these openings of reception and illuminate [GAP-9: REVIEW that] state, and draw upward through them whatever is good and whatever interior supporting connection [to higher degrees] exists there.

[§841] From that place [it] shall be poured [out to the] firmaments. It shall also be clarified in these firmaments [until] a part there is cleansed and it is [all] taken up with the living ones. However, its other part [of darkness] shall all [be cast] down.
[§841]* From that state [GAP-10: REVIEW the good and truth] shall be distributed [GAP-11: REVIEW throughout the] successive planes of the mind. It shall also be purified within these planes [GAP-12] — one part is refined and it is [GAP-13: REVIEW entirely] elevated into conjunction with those who dwell in states of genuine spiritual life. However, its other part — [GAP-14] what is impure and falsified — shall [GAP-15: REVIEW be cast] downward into separation and rejection.

---
*Note on ¶3:* The "four gates at the four points of the compass" correspond to the four cardinal faculties through which Divine influx is received at every discrete degree — love, wisdom, use, and their containing form — opening "downward" because influx always descends from interiors into exterior vessels. The "little pillar" resists full translation but reads best as the slender interior bond connecting a lower degree to a higher one — the living channel through which good ascends between planes.

[§842] Three images occur in the elect person.
[§842]* Three degrees of interior life exist within the person undergoing regeneration.

[§843] The first is the spiritual image, which is the new man, which Wahman shall form in him; and it goes into him and dwells in him.
[§843]* The first is the inmost spiritual degree, which is the regenerated will and understanding — the new state of life that Divine Wisdom forms within the person; it enters into the interiors and takes up its dwelling there.

[§844] The second image is the remnant and remainder of the new man, which is the psychic image that is bound in the flesh, as the old man [has persisted since the] times of this psychic image.
[§844]* The second degree is the remnant and residue of that regenerated state — the intermediate rational degree that remains bound within the proprium, as the unregenerate natural disposition persists [GAP-1: since the earliest formation] of this intermediate rational degree.

[§845] The other one is the corporeal image [that is] added to them all.
[§845]* The third is the outermost sensual degree, [GAP-2: which is] adjoined beneath them all.

[§846] These three images [exist in him] entirely. When it will [come into him] through the nourishment of food. If it shall sink [then it shall come] down to the corporeal image. [And] the corporeal, [that] is in it, shall divest itself of it and place it in the [psychic] image. [And] again it rises up and falls to the [spiritual] image; it shall divest itself of the psychical of the enemies, which [ ... ... ] anger. The lust [ ... ... ... ] with it.
[§846]* These three degrees of life [GAP-3: constitute the whole person] entirely. When it [GAP-4: receives influx] through the appropriation of good and truth from external sources — if it descends [GAP-5: it passes] down to the outermost sensual degree. [GAP-6: And] the sensual nature, [GAP-7: that] which is within it, divests itself of what does not belong to it and transfers it to the [GAP-8: intermediate rational] degree. [GAP-9: And] again it ascends and descends to the [GAP-10: spiritual] degree; it puts off the natural affections belonging to the evils, which [GAP-11: are] wrath. The concupiscence [GAP-12: is also separated] with it.

[§847] Then shall a light virgin come and reveal the spiritual image that is [there], which [is] the new man. That virgin acts as a guide. [She g]oes on before and it is extended to the heights above, and received into this spiritual image. And she [forms] it and adorns it with the new man [wi]thin. It is sealed with all the limbs of this light virgin who is present and dwells in the new man.
[§847]* Then a pure affection of Divine Wisdom comes forth and reveals the interior spiritual degree that is [GAP-13: present] there, which [GAP-14: is] the regenerated state of life. That pure affection serves as a guide. She goes before, and the living element is elevated toward the celestial degrees above, and received into this interior spiritual degree. And she [GAP-15: forms] it and adorns it with the regenerated will and understanding within. It is sealed and confirmed in all the faculties of this pure wisdom who is present and dwells within the regenerated state.

[§848] So, this is [h]ow this living limb shall be [puri]fied and live, the one that comes into the body of [the ri]ghteous one from without through the administration of food of various ki[nds], like this. The living soul shall be clea[n]sed entirely every [d]ay and traverse these three [im]a[ges].
[§848]* This, then, is how the living element of good and truth is purified and quickened — that which enters the life of the righteous person from without through the appropriation of goods and truths of various kinds — in this manner. The living interior will is cleansed entirely each day and traverses these three degrees.

[§849] So, it shall divest itself of the body, which is not its own, in the corporeal. It shall also divest itself of the souls that are not its own, these that are mixed with it in the psychical [image, which are] anger and desire and [bitter]-ness and foolishness and envy and strife; and these other wicked teachings that are not its own.
[§849]* It puts off the sensual externals that are not its own at the outermost degree. It also puts off the natural affections that are not its own — those that are commingled with it in the intermediate rational degree — [GAP-16: namely] wrath and concupiscence and [GAP-17: bitter]-ness and folly and envy and contention; and all those other falsities proceeding from evil that do not belong to it.

[§850] However, in [the] spiritual image itself it shall live and be joined with [lo]ng-sufferingness, the perfection of faith and love that reigns over them all. It is the virgin of light who robes the new man and who shall be cal[l]ed 'the hour of life'. S[he] is the f[irst], but s[h]e is also the last.
[§850]* However, in [GAP-18: the] interior spiritual degree itself, it lives and is conjoined with patience, the fullness of faith, and the love that reigns over all other states. It is the pure affection of Divine Wisdom who clothes the regenerated will and understanding, and who is called *the moment of true life*. She is the first origin, but she is also the final end.

---

**Translator's note:** The entire chapter describes a daily process of purification through three discrete degrees — sensual, rational, and spiritual — by which good and truth appropriated externally are progressively separated from the evils and falsities of the proprium. The "virgin of light" corresponds precisely to what Swedenborg calls the *affection of truth from good*: the leading influx from the Lord through the celestial degree that guides regeneration from first to last.

[§851] The Great Spirit, the Mother of Life, she claimed and prayed and besought and glorified and praised the first established one, who is the Father. She besought him an entreaty. She claimed of him a request. She received a great gift; she and the many powers with her.

[§852] She besought an entreaty on behalf of the First Man, because of how he had come forth and thrown himself down. He became distant from the Mother of Life. He separated from her and gave himself; in great affliction. He joined war on earth with the destruction and the grief and the weakness; in the worlds of Darkness; in the abysses made of fear; in the midst of the demons' ditch; amongst the powers of the devil!

[§853] Indeed, he is like a king standing in the midst of his enemies. And when the Mother of Life petitioned and prayed and glorified and praised the Father, the first established one, she did petition him that he might send a power; a protector and redeemer and helper for the son of God who is set in affliction.

[§854] Now, just as she had besought and interceded, so likewise was her prayer fulfilled and received into the presence of the Father of Greatness. He turned and gave to her her entreaty: the great power, the Living Spirit, the giver of ease. The Mother of Life and the Living Spirit came with great power to the borders of the territory. They brought the First Man up from that war and struggle, wherein he is set.

[§855] He gave himself as a grace for the Mother of Life. He became estranged and far from her, the one whom his enemies (place) in great affliction, the one who is in the land of Darkness since he became weak.

[§856] The Living Spirit grasped and took him in. He freed him. He set him right and gave him ease in the land of rest [and joy] the Living Spirit, the first giver of ease [he fulfilled] the will of these three. The welcomer [shall] find with the welcomer that [ease is given]

[§857] [He received] a great power [and the charity] of the loved one [for] the First Man who had been far away. He had become distant. She had besought on his behalf. He was set fast in affliction. (The Living Spirit) went to the place of destruction and weakness, the one that freed and redeemed him and gave him ease from the affliction. He set him right with rest and joy. He has himself become rested, with him for ever.

[§858] [These great ones came] and they built the universe and completed it with its firmaments, with its wheels, with the ships of light that are in the heights, with its fastenings, with its earths and its walls, with its vessels. They completed the universe and set it right by the fathers of light.

[§859] Then, the Great Spirit stood with the Beloved of the Lights, the Great Builder with the Living Spirit and the First Man. These five stood to prayer; they glorified and praised the Father, the first established one. They besought of him an entreaty. They claimed of him a request, that he might give them the power [and] the leader, a guide to all the things of activity that they had constructed. That he might come and purify the living soul that is set in affliction.

[§860] They prayed and besought their Father, and he received from them their prayer [and] he gave them their entreaty and their request [and] he summoned from him [a great power] which is the Third Ambassador. He, he came from the power of his greatness. He saw the entire soul, how it is entangled in a chain and set fast. It sees the things of activity that his brothers have constructed, that have come from the heights. Again, he saw the living soul, that it is entangled and set fast in a great torture. It is oppressed in the stink of the abyss that is joined with it.

[§861] And therefore, he, the Ambassador [was moved] in his charity. So, he revealed his way, before this underworld that is dug deep down, to free the living soul. He redeemed it from all the fleshes of death wherein it is pained. He gave it ease. He set it right in the place of rest and joy.

[§862] And again, he too, the Ambassador, he fulfilled the will of these three; the will of his brothers who had besought the Father for him. They claimed of him a request, together with the [will] of the Father, the one that [heard them and] he gave the entreaty that they had besought of him.

[§863] The third is the transferral of the living soul that he had freed. He released it and gave it ease from the affliction. He became king and first to the entire body.

[§864] the third entreaty when they besought him the third time [and he] displayed his image [to the light and] he hid his image from the [powers of darkness] he stood firm [in his power and] they revealed [the glory of] the light [to] the First Man [whom the archons] mock [and came] to him in [the height] which is exalted [in his] garment [and] they were freed [and came] to him [and raised] their voice [from] the body [in] prayer [and] it [did] beseech [for] the body [did] beseech [and] she besought [him] in [that they might] be free [through the] Ambassador [came] and also [gave them ease]

[§865] [He dwells] with the living ones and [rests] for ever [and] the first [Man was freed] from the [affliction and] he gave him the hope [of life]

[§866] Also, the Splendour perfected the will [of the Father and] the will [of the elements and] they found the light [and fulfilled] the will of the [elements] that he had [desired and] he gave them the power that is the Splendour. He granted the will of the elements. [And they rest in joy] for ever. This first [Man] he was humbled, he was made free [and] the [great]-ness is displayed to his enemies. He received the hope [of life to] reveal [through] the Splendour, the light power [of the Father] and the entreaty of the [fathers] of light [for ever]

[§851–§866]* **¶8.** The Divine Proceeding — the originating sphere of all spiritual life — ascended in ardent desire, in supplication, in glorification, toward the Infinite Divine Love and Wisdom in its first and primal establishment. This sphere of divine life brought before the Divine origin an act of deepest longing, a petition from the very root of heavenly order. And a great bestowal of new power was received: by that sphere itself, and by the multitude of heavenly affections of good and truth dwelling within it.

---

**¶9.** The supplication was on behalf of the Celestial Will — the primal state of heavenly good — because of how it had gone forth from the Divine and cast itself downward into lower states. It became remote from the sphere of divine life. It separated from her presence and surrendered itself freely, in great anguish, into spiritual combat within the natural mind — into devastation, grief, and impotence — into the realms of falsity from evil — into the deepest hells constituted by dread — into the very pit where evil persuasions dwell — among the organized forces of self-love and the love of the world.

---

**¶10.** Indeed, this celestial good is as a sovereign principle standing surrounded on every side by assailing evils and falsities. And when the Divine Sphere of Life brought her supplication, her glorification, her praise before the Divine Origin — the Infinite Love and Wisdom in its primal establishment — she petitioned that He would send forth a divine strength: a defender and liberator and sustainer for the heavenly good proceeding from the Divine, which is held captive in temptation-states.

---

**¶11.** And just as the petition had been raised in longing and intercession, so likewise was that desire received and fulfilled within the Divine Itself. The Infinite turned and granted the entreaty: the great divine power of reformation and restoration — the Living Strength, the bringer of heavenly peace. The Sphere of Divine Life and the Power of Restoration descended together with divine might to the outermost boundary where the spiritual order meets the infernal. They raised the Celestial Will upward out of that combat and struggle in which it was held fast.

---

**¶12.** The Celestial Will had offered itself as a free gift on behalf of the Sphere of Divine Life. It had become estranged and remote from her — it whom the forces of evil held in great torment — it who dwelt in the realm of falsity, having been rendered spiritually impotent.

---

**¶13.** The Power of Restoration seized hold of it and drew it inward. It liberated the celestial good. It set it in right order and brought it into the state of heavenly peace [GAP-1: the spiritual sense requires a statement that this peace was established in the divine sphere itself, perhaps naming its eternal quality] the Power of Restoration, the first bringer of heavenly peace [GAP-2: the spiritual sense requires a connecting passage showing how this act fulfills a prior divine purpose or unites with a higher origin] the unified will of these three divine powers. The one who receives into conjunction [GAP-3: the spiritual sense requires a description of the receiving function — the capacity to welcome the restored good into heavenly order] found together with that receiving capacity that [GAP-4: the spiritual sense requires a statement of what was discovered in the act of reception — likely the indwelling of divine peace or the unity of good and truth]

---

**¶14.** [GAP-5: the spiritual sense requires an introductory statement, likely about the restored celestial good or the fullness of the divine power] a great divine strength [GAP-6: the spiritual sense requires a qualifying phrase linking this power to its origin] of the beloved good [GAP-7: the spiritual sense requires a transitional phrase connecting the beloved to what follows] the Celestial Will which had been far from the Divine. It had become remote. The Sphere of Life had interceded on its behalf. It had been held fast in temptation-states. The Power of Restoration descended to the place of devastation and spiritual impotence — that same power which liberated and redeemed the celestial good and brought it heavenly peace from its anguish. It established it in right order, within rest and blessedness. And that restoring power has itself entered into eternal repose, conjoined with it forever.

---

**¶17.** [GAP-8: the spiritual sense requires an identification of the agents who constructed the heavenly order — the united divine powers] and they established the entire order of the heavens and perfected it: with its separations between interior and exterior states, with its progressions of spiritual life, with the vehicles of divine truth that traverse the celestial heights, with its bonds of conjunction, with its natural foundations and its protecting boundaries of truth, with its containing forms of good and truth. They brought the whole heavenly order to completion and set it in right arrangement through the governing principles of divine wisdom.

---

**¶18.** Then the Divine Proceeding stood together with the Beloved of Divine Truths, the Great Architect of heavenly form with the Power of Restoration and the Celestial Will. These five divine powers stood united in ascending desire; they glorified and praised the Infinite Divine Origin in its primal establishment. They brought before it an entreaty. They claimed from it a request — that the Divine would bestow the governing power [GAP-9: the spiritual sense requires a phrase describing the nature of the power sought — a ruling and directing truth] the ruling principle, a guide over all the structures of heavenly order they had established — that this power might come forth and purify the interiors of the human spirit which are held in states of suffering and temptation.

---

**¶19.** They brought their petition before the Divine Origin, and the Origin received their ascending desire [GAP-10: the spiritual sense requires a phrase indicating reception and the Divine turning toward the petition — perhaps a statement that the prayer ascended and was accepted] the Divine granted them their entreaty and their request [GAP-11: the spiritual sense requires a transitional phrase, perhaps expressing the manner of the granting — with fullness, or in accordance with divine order] the Divine summoned forth from itself [GAP-12: the spiritual sense requires the identification of what was summoned — a new proceeding of divine truth in its mediating function] which is the Third Mediating Power of Divine Truth. This power came forth from the might of the Divine itself. It perceived the whole of the interior human spirit — how it is bound by persuasions of falsity, held immovable in chains of evil. It beheld the structures of heavenly order that its kindred powers had established, descending from the celestial states. And again it perceived the interiors of the spirit — that they are entangled and held fast in great torment, weighed down by the foulness of the deepest falsities from evil that have attached themselves to it.

---

**¶20.** And therefore this Mediating Truth [GAP-13: the spiritual sense requires a phrase indicating the quality from which this act proceeds — compassion, or divine mercy flowing from love] in its mercy, it disclosed its path of descent, before the lowest infernal states dug down to the uttermost depth, in order to liberate the interiors of the human spirit. It redeemed the spirit from every form of the proprium and selfhood that belongs to spiritual death, in which it suffered. It brought it heavenly peace. It established it in right order, in the state of rest and blessedness.

---

**¶21.** And again, this Mediating Truth also accomplished the unified purpose of these three — the purpose of its kindred powers who had petitioned the Divine Origin on its behalf. They had brought their request together with the [GAP-14: the spiritual sense requires a phrase indicating another element united in the petition — perhaps the will, the love, or the blessing] of the Divine Origin, the one that [GAP-15: the spiritual sense requires a verb-phrase indicating what the Origin did — perhaps "granted" or "sent forth in accordance with the petition"] the Divine bestowed the entreaty that had been sought.

---

**¶22.** The third act is the elevation and transference of the interiors of the human spirit that had been freed. The Mediating Truth released them and brought them peace from their anguish. It became the sovereign governing principle, the first and ruling truth over the whole of the external natural mind.

---

**¶25.** The third entreaty — when for the third time they brought their petition [GAP-16: the spiritual sense requires a description of what happened in response to this third petition — likely the It received the assured hope of eternal restoration [GAP-56: the spiritual sense requires an elaboration — the nature or scope of this hope] revealed [GAP-57: the spiritual sense requires a connecting phrase] the Radiant Fullness of Divine Truth, the power of divine wisdom [GAP-58: the spiritual sense requires a concluding phrase about this power] and the petition of the [GAP-59: the spiritual sense requires an identification — likely the heavenly powers or the divine sphere] of divine wisdom [GAP-60: the spiritual sense requires whatever concludes this passage — likely a statement of eternal fulfillment or glorification]

*Translator's note:* The three entreaties describe one continuous spiritual process: the descent of celestial good into temptation-states (first), its rescue by the reforming power of divine truth (second), and the purification of the captive human spirit through an ever-deeper proceeding of mediating divine truth (third). Each petition ascending to the Divine Origin and each power descending in answer corresponds to the successive stages by which the Lord, from His infinite love, liberates the interiors of the human mind from bondage to falsity and self-love, and restores them to heavenly order, peace, and blessedness. The entire drama is not cosmological but regenerative: it is the process of salvation enacted within the human spirit across its successive planes.

[§867–§868]* # Spiritual Translation

## ¶2

Why is it that when essential faculties are severed from the natural mind of a person, that person suffers spiritual pain? Yet if the outermost external knowledges upon the governing principle of the mind are stripped away, and the most external natural forms of one's power and one's lowest natural life are removed, one does not perceive their loss [GAP-1: ...] Some of these outermost things even now regenerate an[GAP-2: d ...] again. Just as the most external natural forms [GAP-3: ...] so the outermost knowledges shall be restored and [GAP-4: ...] sever a particular faculty of power, one in [GAP-5: ...] upon the essential faculties of the [natural mi]nd that you [GAP-6: ...] him [GAP-7: ...] since this stage of life to the [GAP-8: ...] the external mind ag[a]in.

*The teaching here distinguishes between what is ultimate and replaceable in the natural mind — mere outward forms of knowledge and the lowest natural coverings — and what constitutes an essential living faculty. The outermost things, being merely terminal forms, regenerate after removal because their spiritual root remains intact. But the deeper faculties, once destroyed, occasion real spiritual suffering because they participate in the interior life itself.*

## ¶5

Th[at the] natural mind is like the ground of reception, wh[ile these] essential faculties als[GAP-9: o ...] of it are like the perceptions rooted therein. There are varieties [GAP-10: ...] of perception one shall find growing in the natural mind. They aris[e] and are restored again and stand firm in the understanding. They have not [GAP-11: ...] Again, there are other perceptions that if they are destroyed shall not [GAP-12: be restored ...] and they grow, as they are li[GAP-13: ke ...] which shall be cut off and yet regenerate [GAP-14: ...] and [GAP-15: ...] they too that occur [GAP-16: ...] they shall be destroyed and not be restored, resembling [GAP-17: ... neither] regenerate nor shall they [GAP-18: ...] it [sh]all please him concerning [GAP-19: ...] and grows [GAP-20: ...] in what belongs to evil and the proprium [GAP-21: ...] from him. This selfish will that is corrupt [GAP-22: ...] within the regenerating will and they [GAP-23: ...] are in a state of spiritual infirmity.

*The passage classifies three orders of spiritual perception within the natural mind: those which, like grasses and renewable growths, spring back after being cut away — these are natural knowledges grounded in good; those which, once severed, cannot regenerate — these are deeper perceptions whose interior root has been destroyed; and a third condition, where the corrupt proprium (the selfish will belonging to the old nature) persists within the living, regenerating will, producing a state the teaching calls spiritual sickness — an incomplete regeneration in which evil and good coexist painfully in the same mind.*

[§870] The [sec]ond [kind] if he shall fall [into the darkness for a time] one [portion of light remaining for] one hour [in that middle place and] others who shall [linger between the light and the darkness] and they remain. They shall delay until they [are purified and then] he is freed, and they delay to come toward [the land of light]
[§870]* The second state [GAP-4: of this liberation] — if one shall fall into [GAP-5: a prolonged condition of spiritual obscurity] — a single [GAP-6: quality of interior life persisting] — one brief state of duration [GAP-7: within that intermediate condition] — others who shall [GAP-8: remain held in those intermediate states between falsity and emerging truth] — and they linger there. They are detained until they [GAP-9: pass through the fullness of their vastation and reformation] — the interior understanding is set free, and yet they are delayed in approaching [GAP-10: the state of genuine wisdom and conjunction with good].

[§871] which names shall be freed, as they [receive the light that shall] release them.
[§871]* — which essential spiritual qualities shall be liberated, inasmuch as they [GAP-11: come into correspondence with the truths that can act upon them and] release them from bondage.

---

**Note on the translation:** The passage describes, in its spiritual sense, the process by which interior understanding is separated from the dominion of the merely natural mind — not in a single act, but through successive states (first, second) of vastation and delay. "Names" correspond to the essential qualities of each mind's spiritual identity; their liberation depends upon truths received inwardly acting to dissolve the bonds of falsity. The extreme fragmentation resists certainty, but the structure clearly maps onto the progressive stages of spiritual liberation through intermediate states — what Swedenborg would recognize as the *world of spirits*, where minds linger until their ruling love is disclosed and they are either elevated or descend.

[§872–§873]* # Spiritual Translation

## ¶1

[GAP-1] upon [GAP-2] from [GAP-3] thus [GAP-4]

*This paragraph is almost entirely lacuna. The surviving connective words ("upon," "from," "thus") suggest a causal sequence — an influx descending from a higher spiritual origin into a consequent state — but the substance is lost. No confident spiritual reading can be given beyond that skeletal movement of influx.*

---

## ¶2

[GAP-5] the totality of these states [GAP-6] that they shall [GAP-7] seven complete degrees of the regenerating mind [GAP-8]. Again, he too, this very [GAP-9: individual spiritual intelligence] — in the season when [GAP-10] of divine wisdom [GAP-11] the whole of the spiritual world [GAP-12] is opened to interior perception [GAP-13] — receives into divine wisdom [GAP-14] entirely, according to the mode in which his spiritual life subsists [GAP-15] from [GAP-16] these remaining three degrees of interior order [GAP-17].

### Notes on the reading:

- **"all of them"** → the totality of spiritual states under discussion.
- **"seven people"** → seven signifies what is full and complete; "people" corresponds to discrete states or degrees of the mind undergoing regeneration — the complete series of spiritual development.
- **"this person"** → a particular spiritual intelligence or angelic form progressing through these states.
- **"of the light"** → of divine wisdom (light = truth proceeding from the Divine).
- **"the universe"** → the whole spiritual world, or the Grand Human in its fullness.
- **"is revealed"** → interior perception is opened; what was hidden in correspondence becomes manifest.
- **"take to the light"** → to receive into, or be conjoined with, divine wisdom.
- **"the way that he exists"** → the specific mode or quality of that intelligence's spiritual life — his ruling love and its form.
- **"these other three"** → three remaining discrete degrees (likely celestial, spiritual, and natural — the tripartite order of influx), distinguished from the sevenfold completeness of regeneration mentioned above. The "three" points to the essential structural degrees through which the Divine flows into created minds.

*The passage, despite its fragmentary state, appears to describe how a spiritual intelligence, at the appointed state of readiness, receives divine wisdom fully — and how that reception accords with three interior discrete degrees that constitute the architecture of the regenerating mind within the complete (sevenfold) process.*

[§875] This one, whom they called "approved" [he received this name because] they approved him in the midst of the ae[ons of] light. These numerous multitudes set him a[part], and he came forth against the enemy. They gave this name [to him on account of] this matter [ ... ... ] who are numerous, that are in the aeons of light and who [are without] measure or number.

[§876] This one, [whom they called] "righteous", because he is the first righteous one who had no sin amongst all of the aeons that belong to the light and the thought [by which] they knew him, so that [it was known becau]se he is the first [wh]o obe[y]ed his Father, who had sent him with five sons [into] perdition.
[§876]* This one, [GAP-8: REVIEW whom the heavenly societies called] "the one perfected in Divine Good," because he is the first being wholly in good who possessed no falsity among all the heavenly societies belonging to wisdom, and the interior perception [GAP-9] by which they recognized him — so that [GAP-10: it was known, becau]se he is the first who acted in perfect accord with the will of Divine Love, who had sent him forth with the five powers of the celestial mind [GAP-11] into the state of spiritual destruction.

[§877] This one also [whom they called] "only begotten", because he was established [alone to] pour [forth against the] enemy.
[§877]* This one also [GAP-12: REVIEW whom the heavenly societies called] "the sole-proceeding," because he was established [GAP-13] to pour forth [GAP-14] against the dominion of falsity from evil.

[§878] [the o]nly begot-ten also from gold and silver and all the [treasures] of the universe.
[§878]* The sole-proceeding is also distinguished from celestial good and spiritual truth and all the [GAP-15] forms of good and truth throughout creation.

[§879] [The F]irst Man, himself stood firm in the [midst] of all the worlds of Darkness. He did not love [the] worlds of Darkness, nor did he lust for them! Rather, h[is] thought and love for the household of his people is in h[i]m. Anything else that the enemy [did to them] with his violent de[e]ds. Behold, (the First Man) toils and struggles; so that (the enemy) would be separat[e from] them and be cast out from them. And he purifies them and ma[ke]s them f[r]ee, and makes them as they were from the beginning.
[§879]* The Celestial Human himself stood firm in the [GAP-16: REVIEW midst] of all the states of falsity from evil. He did not love [GAP-17: REVIEW the] states of falsity from evil, nor did he desire conjunction with them. Rather, his interior will and understanding remained wholly directed toward the goods and truths of his heavenly household. Whatever else the power of falsity from evil [GAP-18] wrought through its destructive operations — behold, the Celestial Human labors in spiritual combat so that falsity from evil may be separated from those goods and truths and cast out from among them. And he purifies them and restores them to spiritual freedom, and returns them to the celestial state they possessed from the beginning.

[§880] the two essences that are present at the beginning [and are opposed to one another:] the light and the darkness, that which is good and that which is evi[l], life and death.
[§880]* The two fundamental principles of all spiritual reality that subsist from the very origin [GAP-1] — wisdom and falsity, all that proceeds from divine love and all that proceeds from self-love [GAP-2], conjunction with the Divine and separation from it.

---

### Translator's notes:

**"the two essences"** — These are not substances in space but the two universal principles underlying every state of the human spirit: what flows from the Divine (love and its wisdom) and what flows from the proprium turned away from the Divine (self-love and its falsity).

**"at the beginning"** — Not a temporal origin but the *a priori* condition: these two principles are distinguished before any created state unfolds, as the very ground upon which all spiritual history proceeds.

**[GAP-1]** — The spiritual sense requires here a statement about the eternal separateness or mutual opposition of these two principles — that they face each other without mixture. Whatever natural-plane words once filled this lacuna likely expressed that primordial distinction.

**"light and darkness"** — The governing correspondence: wisdom (the form that divine love takes when received by understanding) and falsity (the inversion that arises when love of self occludes that reception).

**[GAP-2]** — Merely completes the word *evil*; the spiritual sense is unbroken: the principle of self-love and all that derives from it.

**"life and death"** — Spiritual life is the continuous influx of love and wisdom from the Divine into the will and understanding; spiritual death is the state in which that influx is refused, and the spirit subsists in its own darkness. These are not sequential events but permanent contraries.

## ¶5

The [GAP-1] and the great [GAP-2] and the great Divine Intellectual Principle that is [GAP-3] — this first regenerating state of the will that is with the [GAP-4] — thirty-six divine influxes constituting a fullness of spiritual operation [GAP-5] twenty-two divine influxes corresponding to every mode through which divine truth can be expressed [GAP-6] but [GAP-7] of the twenty-two modes of divine expression [GAP-8] — the seven holy foundational principles that sust[GAP-9: ain] — the encompassing natural cognition that bounds the whole spiritual form of the human [GAP-10] — four operative truths that are distributed to the [GAP-11] whole spiritual constitution, upholding the divine power [GAP-12] in the external and lowest degree, which is the natural foundation upon which all interior states of wisdom rest [GAP-13] — these other five divine faculties, the qualities proceeding from the Divine Human, they that [GAP-14] the whole spiritual constitution is established and made firm [GAP-15] — the divine calling forth and the will's answering reception [GAP-16] — the radiance of divine wisdom from the [GAP-17] — the Indestructible Truth proceeding from Divine Good [GAP-18] — they have become opposing falsities and hostile evils [GAP-19] — they lead and govern it.

---

## ¶6

The third degree also, [GAP-20] in the external and lowest plane, there are twelve complete principles of faith standing firm there. However, the full number of the twelve representative forms of spiritual truth [GAP-21] — the three fundamental supporting principles of divine power that [GAP-22] — foundational truths upholding the plane of conscious awareness [GAP-23] — the other three [GAP-24] — they are the external truths clothing interior goods, which the Divine Proceeding that orders all spiritual life [GAP-25] — He disposed them in that state of the mind's formation [GAP-26] — that cast them forth, together with [GAP-27] — which is manifested therein [GAP-28] — this [GAP-29] — brought to completeness before [GAP-30] — the great Guardian Principle governing passage between interior and exterior states [GAP-31] — within his governing power [GAP-32] — these are the three great [GAP-33] — against [GAP-34] — are in the midst of human minds [GAP-35] — perceives and acknowledges [GAP-36] — commits his inmost life to their keeping [GAP-37].

---

### Translator's Note

The spiritual architecture here describes how the Lord's influx sustains the regenerating human through three discrete degrees: the celestial (the Divine Mind and the Youth-will), the spiritual (the seven holy pillars and the five faculties from the Divine Human), and the natural (the twelve principles of faith and the three supporting truths). The "sea surrounding the universe" is the body of natural memory-knowledge that encloses and bounds the whole spiritual organism. The "Adamant of Light" — indestructible truth from divine good — stands as the principle that cannot be shattered even when surrounding states become hostile. The "great Porter" is the guardian truth that governs what may pass between the external and the internal human. The passage culminates in a doctrine of trust: whoever perceives these ordered principles and commits his inmost life to their governance is sustained by the whole divine arrangement.

[§883] What is the 'assent' and the 'amen'?
[§883]* What are affirmation and confirmation in the spiritual sense?

[§884] The assent and the amen have come about from these great ones of the powers. They were given the name as the assent belongs to the summons and the amen belongs to the answer.
[§884]* Affirmation and confirmation proceed from the principal faculties of the celestial mind. They received their designation because affirmation belongs to the divine calling, and confirmation belongs to the reciprocal response.

[§885] Just as the summons and the obedience are the ones that opened the portals of mercy and entreaty for the First Man, so they also became portals for the Mother of Life and the Father of the living. The First Man sought and found his redeemer. And also the Mother of Life and the Father of the living sought [and found] this runner, who came forth from them, who is the First Man; but the summons and the obedience became the portals for them.
[§885]* Just as the divine calling and the will's reception are the very states that opened the interior pathways of divine mercy and the soul's turning toward it for the primordial celestial will, so likewise they became pathways of receptivity for the divine truth that generates spiritual life and for the divine love from which all life proceeds. The primordial will in its captivity sought and found the liberating truth that could restore it. And likewise the generating divine truth and the originating divine love sought [GAP-1: *the spiritual sense requires here an expression of longing or searching toward*] this active proceeding principle that had gone forth from them — which is the primordial celestial will itself — and the divine calling and the will's reception became their pathways of access to it.

[§886] they have become portals for the aeons of greatness towards the First Man, and the soul that was conjoined with death. They became helpers [and saviours] of the First Man during his ascent. They gave him [back to] the Father of Life and the Mother of Life [who received him]. They are purifiers of the living soul, being helpers and bestowers of remembrance for it, be it either in [the water or] in the tree or in the creation of flesh [wherein it is bound]
[§886]* They became pathways of receptivity for the heavenly states of the divine order toward the primordial celestial will, and toward the spiritual life that had been conjoined with falsity and spiritual death. They became serving truths [GAP-2: *the spiritual sense requires an expression of aid or sustaining power directed toward*] the primordial will during its elevation and regeneration. They gave to it [GAP-3: *the spiritual sense requires here the content of what was restored — conjunction with, or access to*] the originating divine love and the generating divine truth [GAP-4: *the spiritual sense requires a completing clause — perhaps restoration of conjunction, or a reciprocal reunion*]. They are the purifying powers of the interior spiritual life, being serving truths and restorers of interior memory to it, whether [GAP-5: *the spiritual sense requires a domain — perhaps in a state of perception, or among knowledges*] in states of perception, or in the natural corporeal state [GAP-6: *the spiritual sense requires a completing thought — perhaps concerning the captivity of spiritual life within the material body*].
